/**
 * =================================================================================
 * v9.4: REQUEST SCOPE, TABLE CACHE AND AUTH (compliance A14, A15)
 * =================================================================================
 */

// Raw values per sheet name (row 0 = headers) for the current request. `var` so the offline tests can inspect it.
var TABLE_CACHE = {};
// v10.0: buildActorScope() results per actor for the current request (dropped by every write)
var ACTOR_SCOPES = {};
// True inside doGet / doPost (withRequestScope). The cache is used only then; editor runs read uncached.
let REQUEST_ACTIVE = false;
let SS_HANDLE = null;
let CACHE_FLAG = null;
// Identity of the current request, set by authenticateRequest(): { uid, verified, mode, email }
let REQUEST_AUTH = { uid: null, verified: false, mode: 'off', email: null };
// v9.6: set by logAdminAction, so doPost knows whether an admin write logged itself (routeAdminAction)
let ADMIN_LOGGED = false;
let UTC_MARK_DONE = false;

/** The spreadsheet, opened once per execution. */
function ss() {
  return SS_HANDLE || (SS_HANDLE = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID));
}

/** ss() for functions that keep the spreadsheet in a local variable named `ss` (const ss = openSpreadsheet()). */
function openSpreadsheet() {
  return ss();
}

/** A Script Property, or the fallback when it isn't set (or Properties isn't available). */
function scriptProperty(name, fallback) {
  try {
    const value = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty(name);
    return value === null || value === undefined || value === '' ? fallback : value;
  } catch (error) {
    return fallback;
  }
}

/** Is the per-request cache on? Only inside a request, and unless Script Property CACHE_ENABLED is 'false'. */
function cacheEnabled() {
  if (!REQUEST_ACTIVE) return false;
  if (CACHE_FLAG === null) CACHE_FLAG = String(scriptProperty('CACHE_ENABLED', 'true')).toLowerCase() !== 'false';
  return CACHE_FLAG;
}

/** Drop the whole cache (start and end of a request). */
function resetRequestCache() {
  TABLE_CACHE = {};
  CACHE_FLAG = null;
  ACTOR_SCOPES = {};
}

/**
 * Drop one sheet's cached values after a write (A14). No name (or null) drops everything.
 * v10.0: any write also drops the actor scopes (they are built from roles, steps, shares and participants).
 * @param {string|null} sheetName - Sheet name
 */
function invalidateTable(sheetName) {
  ACTOR_SCOPES = {};
  if (sheetName) {
    delete TABLE_CACHE[sheetName];
  } else {
    TABLE_CACHE = {};
  }
}

/** The cache key of what a write touched: a Range's sheet, a Sheet, or null (the spreadsheet: drop everything). */
function cacheNameOf(target) {
  try {
    if (target && typeof target.getSheet === 'function') return target.getSheet().getName();
    if (target && typeof target.getSheetByName === 'function') return null;
    if (target && typeof target.getName === 'function') return target.getName();
  } catch (error) {
    // Unknown target: drop everything
  }
  return null;
}

/**
 * A sheet's values (row 0 = headers), the same as sheet.getDataRange().getValues(), served from the
 * per-request cache. Returns a copy, so callers may shift / edit the rows.
 * @param {Sheet} sheet - Sheet
 * @returns {Array[]} values
 */
function sheetValues(sheet) {
  if (!sheet) return [];
  if (!cacheEnabled()) return sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  const name = sheet.getName();
  if (!TABLE_CACHE[name]) TABLE_CACHE[name] = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  return TABLE_CACHE[name].map(row => row.slice());
}

/**
 * Run fn as one request: the cache starts empty and is dropped afterwards. Nested calls share the outer scope.
 * @param {Function} fn - Work to run
 * @returns {*} fn's return value
 */
function withRequestScope(fn) {
  if (REQUEST_ACTIVE) return fn();
  resetRequestCache();
  REQUEST_ACTIVE = true;
  try {
    return fn();
  } finally {
    REQUEST_ACTIVE = false;
    resetRequestCache();
    REQUEST_AUTH = { uid: null, verified: false, mode: 'off', email: null };
    ADMIN_LOGGED = false;
  }
}

const AUTH_MODES = ['off', 'warn', 'enforce'];

/** Script Property AUTH_MODE: off (v9.3 behaviour), warn (log only - the default) or enforce. */
function authMode() {
  const mode = String(scriptProperty('AUTH_MODE', 'warn')).toLowerCase();
  return AUTH_MODES.includes(mode) ? mode : 'warn';
}

/**
 * Read and remove the proxy's identity fields from a request (_proxy_secret, _auth_uid, v9.6 _auth_email), so
 * they are never stored with a record. The uid counts only when the secret matches Script Property PROXY_SECRET;
 * the email only with a verified uid (the proxy sends it only when Google marked it verified).
 * @param {Object} data - Request parameters or body (modified: the two fields are deleted)
 * @param {string} action - Action name (ping is always allowed)
 * @returns {Object|null} An error response in AUTH_MODE 'enforce' when the request isn't verified, else null
 */
function authenticateRequest(data, action) {
  const mode = authMode();
  const secret = scriptProperty('PROXY_SECRET', '');
  const sentSecret = data ? data._proxy_secret : undefined;
  const uid = data ? data._auth_uid : undefined;
  const email = data ? data._auth_email : undefined;
  if (data) {
    delete data._proxy_secret;
    delete data._auth_uid;
    delete data._auth_email;
  }
  const secretOk = !!secret && String(sentSecret || '') === secret;
  const verified = secretOk && !!uid;
  REQUEST_AUTH = { uid: verified ? String(uid) : null, verified, mode,
    email: verified && email ? String(email).trim().toLowerCase() : null };

  if (mode === 'off' || action === 'ping') return null;
  if (!REQUEST_AUTH.verified) {
    if (mode === 'enforce') {
      return secretOk
        ? { success: false, status: 'error', code: 'NOT_SIGNED_IN', error: 'Not signed in: sign in again and retry' }
        : { success: false, status: 'error', code: 'PROXY_REQUIRED', error: 'Unauthorized: requests must come through the app proxy' };
    }
    console.log(`AUTH warn: ${action} without a verified user (${secretOk ? 'no token' : 'no valid proxy secret'})`);
  }
  return null;
}

/**
 * Who is acting (A15). AUTH_MODE 'enforce': the verified uid only (null when there is none). 'warn' / 'off':
 * the id the request claims, falling back to the verified uid; 'warn' logs when they differ.
 * @param {string} claimed - The user id the request sent (payload user_id / userId)
 * @returns {string|null} user id
 */
function actorId(claimed) {
  const auth = REQUEST_AUTH;
  if (auth.mode === 'enforce') return auth.verified ? auth.uid : null;
  if (auth.mode === 'warn' && auth.verified && claimed && String(claimed) !== String(auth.uid)) {
    console.log(`AUTH warn: request claims user ${claimed}, the token says ${auth.uid}`);
  }
  return claimed || (auth.verified ? auth.uid : null);
}

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * COMPREHENSIVE AUDIT FIELD UTILITY FUNCTIONS
 * Support for creation tracking, update tracking, and soft delete/deactivation
 * Added: September 22, 2025 for comprehensive audit trail implementation
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * The user of the current request: the uid the proxy verified (v9.4), else 'system_user' (editor runs,
 * triggers, requests without a token).
 * @returns {string} user id
 */
function getCurrentUserId() {
  return (REQUEST_AUTH.verified && REQUEST_AUTH.uid) || 'system_user';
}

/**
 * Current time as an ISO string in real UTC (A17). Until v9.4 this added 8 hours and still ended in Z;
 * Script Property TS_UTC_SINCE records when the stored values became real UTC (the migration corrects
 * older rows). Show Manila time with Utilities.formatDate(d, 'Asia/Manila', ...) - never shift.
 * @returns {string} ISO 8601 timestamp
 */
function getCurrentTimestamp() {
  markUtcCutover();
  return new Date().toISOString();
}

/** Record once (Script Property TS_UTC_SINCE) when timestamps became real UTC. */
function markUtcCutover() {
  if (UTC_MARK_DONE) return;
  UTC_MARK_DONE = true;
  try {
    const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
    if (!props.getProperty('TS_UTC_SINCE')) props.setProperty('TS_UTC_SINCE', new Date().toISOString());
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('TS_UTC_SINCE not recorded: ' + error.message);
  }
}

/**
 * Create audit fields for new record creation
 * @param {string} userId - User ID who is creating the record (optional, uses current user if not provided)
 * @returns {Object} Audit fields object for creation
 */
function createAuditFields(userId = null) {
  const currentUser = userId || getCurrentUserId();
  const timestamp = getCurrentTimestamp();

  return {
    is_active: true,
    created_at: timestamp,
    created_by: currentUser,
    updated_at: timestamp,
    updated_by: currentUser,
    deactivated_at: null,
    deactivated_by: null,
    deactivation_reason: null,
    deleted_at: null,
    deleted_by: null,
    deletion_reason: null
  };
}

/**
 * Create audit fields for record updates
 * @param {string} userId - User ID who is updating the record (optional, uses current user if not provided)
 * @returns {Object} Audit fields object for updates
 */
function updateAuditFields(userId = null) {
  const currentUser = userId || getCurrentUserId();
  const timestamp = getCurrentTimestamp();

  return {
    updated_at: timestamp,
    updated_by: currentUser
  };
}

/**
 * Create audit fields for record deactivation (soft delete for configuration data)
 * @param {string} reason - Reason for deactivation
 * @param {string} userId - User ID who is deactivating the record (optional, uses current user if not provided)
 * @returns {Object} Audit fields object for deactivation
 */
function deactivateAuditFields(reason, userId = null) {
  const currentUser = userId || getCurrentUserId();
  const timestamp = getCurrentTimestamp();

  return {
    is_active: false,
    updated_at: timestamp,
    updated_by: currentUser,
    deactivated_at: timestamp,
    deactivated_by: currentUser,
    deactivation_reason: reason
  };
}

/**
 * Create audit fields for record deletion (soft delete for transaction data)
 * @param {string} reason - Reason for deletion
 * @param {string} userId - User ID who is deleting the record (optional, uses current user if not provided)
 * @returns {Object} Audit fields object for deletion
 */
function deleteAuditFields(reason, userId = null) {
  const currentUser = userId || getCurrentUserId();
  const timestamp = getCurrentTimestamp();

  return {
    is_active: false,
    updated_at: timestamp,
    updated_by: currentUser,
    deleted_at: timestamp,
    deleted_by: currentUser,
    deletion_reason: reason
  };
}

/**
 * Reactivate a deactivated record
 * @param {string} userId - User ID who is reactivating the record (optional, uses current user if not provided)
 * @returns {Object} Audit fields object for reactivation
 */
function reactivateAuditFields(userId = null) {
  const currentUser = userId || getCurrentUserId();
  const timestamp = getCurrentTimestamp();

  return {
    is_active: true,
    updated_at: timestamp,
    updated_by: currentUser,
    deactivated_at: null,
    deactivated_by: null,
    deactivation_reason: null,
    deleted_at: null,
    deleted_by: null,
    deletion_reason: null
  };
}

/**
 * Apply audit fields to a data object
 * @param {Object} data - The data object to enhance
 * @param {Object} auditFields - The audit fields to apply
 * @returns {Object} Enhanced data object with audit fields
 */
function applyAuditFields(data, auditFields) {
  return {
    ...data,
    ...auditFields
  };
}

/**
 * Filter active records from a dataset
 * @param {Array} records - Array of records to filter
 * @returns {Array} Array containing only active records
 */
function filterActiveRecords(records) {
  return records.filter(record => record.is_active === true || record.is_active === 'TRUE');
}

/**
 * Convert audit field values for Google Sheets (handle nulls and booleans)
 * @param {Object} auditFields - Audit fields object
 * @returns {Object} Sheets-compatible audit fields
 */
function auditFieldsForSheets(auditFields) {
  const sheetsFields = {};

  Object.keys(auditFields).forEach(key => {
    const value = auditFields[key];
    if (value === null || value === undefined) {
      sheetsFields[key] = '';
    } else if (typeof value === 'boolean') {
      sheetsFields[key] = value ? 'TRUE' : 'FALSE';
    } else {
      sheetsFields[key] = value;
    }
  });

  return sheetsFields;
}

/**
 * Generic deactivate record function for any table
 * @param {string} sheetName - Name of the sheet/table
 * @param {string} recordId - ID of the record to deactivate
 * @param {string} reason - Reason for deactivation
 * @returns {Object} Result of the deactivation operation
 */
function deactivateRecord(sheetName, recordId, reason = 'admin_request') {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(sheetName);

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: `Sheet '${sheetName}' not found` };
    }

    const data = sheetValues(sheet);
    const headers = data[0];
    const rows = data.slice(1);

    // Find the ID column (usually 'id' or first column)
    const idIndex = headers.indexOf('id');
    const idColumnIndex = idIndex >= 0 ? idIndex : 0;

    // Find the row to deactivate (compare as strings: the request id may be a number)
    const rowIndex = rows.findIndex(row => String(row[idColumnIndex]) === String(recordId));
    if (rowIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: `Record '${recordId}' not found in ${sheetName}` };
    }

    const actualRowIndex = rowIndex + 2; // +1 for header, +1 for 0-based index

    // Get deactivation audit fields
    const deactivationFields = deactivateAuditFields(reason);

    // Update specific columns by header name
    const columnsToUpdate = {
      'is_active': 'FALSE',
      'updated_at': deactivationFields.updated_at,
      'updated_by': deactivationFields.updated_by,
      'deactivated_at': deactivationFields.deactivated_at,
      'deactivated_by': deactivationFields.deactivated_by,
      'deactivation_reason': reason
    };

    Object.keys(columnsToUpdate).forEach(columnName => {
      const columnIndex = headers.indexOf(columnName);
      if (columnIndex >= 0) {
        sheet.getRange(actualRowIndex, columnIndex + 1).setValue(columnsToUpdate[columnName]);
        invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
      }
    });

    // Log the admin action
    logAdminAction(getCurrentUserId(), `DEACTIVATE_${sheetName.toUpperCase()}`, sheetName, recordId, { reason: reason });

    Logger.log(`Deactivated record in ${sheetName}: ${recordId} (Reason: ${reason})`);
    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: {
        id: recordId,
        sheet: sheetName,
        deactivated: true,
        reason: reason,
        deactivated_at: deactivationFields.deactivated_at
      },
      message: `Record ${recordId} deactivated successfully`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error deactivating record in ${sheetName}: ${error.message}`);
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * PER-COMPANY SLA STATISTICS API FUNCTIONS
 * Enhanced support for company-specific workflow performance tracking
 * Added: September 22, 2025 for Phase 8.95
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Get SLA performance statistics for a specific company
 * @param {string} companyId - Company ID to get statistics for (optional - returns all if null)
 * @returns {Object} Company-specific SLA performance data
 */
function getSLAPerformanceByCompany(companyId = null) {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    const companies = getSheetDataAsJSON(ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.COMPANIES));
    const tickets = getSheetDataAsJSON(ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.TICKETS));
    const workflowSteps = getSheetDataAsJSON(ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS));
    const stepSLAs = getSheetDataAsJSON(ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.STEP_SLAS));

    const targetCompanies = companyId ? companies.filter(c => c.id === companyId) : companies;
    const performanceData = {};

    targetCompanies.forEach(company => {
      const companyTickets = tickets.filter(t => t.company_id === company.id);
      const completedTickets = companyTickets.filter(t => t.status === 'Completed');

      let totalStandardSLAHours = 0;
      let totalActualResolutionHours = 0;
      let companyBreaches = 0;

      completedTickets.forEach(ticket => {
        // Get company-specific workflow steps for this ticket type
        const ticketWorkflowSteps = workflowSteps.filter(ws =>
          ws.ticket_type_id === ticket.ticket_type_id && ws.company_id === company.id
        );

        // Calculate standard SLA target for this ticket
        const ticketStandardSLAHours = ticketWorkflowSteps.reduce((total, step) => {
          const stepSLA = stepSLAs.find(sla => sla.step_id === step.id);
          if (stepSLA) {
            const hours = stepSLA.unit === 'days' ? stepSLA.duration * 24 : stepSLA.duration;
            return total + hours;
          }
          return total;
        }, 0);

        // Calculate actual resolution time
        const created = new Date(ticket.created_at);
        const completed = new Date(ticket.updated_at);
        const actualResolutionHours = (completed - created) / (1000 * 60 * 60);

        totalStandardSLAHours += ticketStandardSLAHours;
        totalActualResolutionHours += actualResolutionHours;

        if (actualResolutionHours > ticketStandardSLAHours) {
          companyBreaches++;
        }
      });

      const avgStandardSLAHours = completedTickets.length > 0 ? totalStandardSLAHours / completedTickets.length : 0;
      const avgActualResolutionHours = completedTickets.length > 0 ? totalActualResolutionHours / completedTickets.length : 0;
      const slaCompliance = completedTickets.length > 0 ?
        ((completedTickets.length - companyBreaches) / completedTickets.length * 100).toFixed(1) : 0;

      performanceData[company.id] = {
        companyId: company.id,
        companyName: company.name,
        totalTickets: companyTickets.length,
        completedTickets: completedTickets.length,
        avgStandardSLAHours: Math.round(avgStandardSLAHours * 10) / 10,
        avgActualResolutionHours: Math.round(avgActualResolutionHours * 10) / 10,
        slaCompliance: slaCompliance,
        slaBreaches: companyBreaches,
        performanceVsStandard: avgStandardSLAHours > 0 ?
          ((avgStandardSLAHours - avgActualResolutionHours) / avgStandardSLAHours * 100).toFixed(1) : 0
      };
    });

    return performanceData;
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getSLAPerformanceByCompany: ' + error.toString());
    throw error;
  }
}

/**
 * Get SLA performance statistics for ticket types across companies
 * @param {string} ticketTypeId - Ticket type ID to filter by (optional)
 * @param {string} companyId - Company ID to filter by (optional)
 * @returns {Object} Ticket type and company-specific SLA performance matrix
 */
function getSLAPerformanceByTicketTypeAndCompany(ticketTypeId = null, companyId = null) {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    const companies = getSheetDataAsJSON(ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.COMPANIES));
    const ticketTypes = getSheetDataAsJSON(ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.TICKET_TYPES));
    const tickets = getSheetDataAsJSON(ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.TICKETS));
    const workflowSteps = getSheetDataAsJSON(ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS));
    const stepSLAs = getSheetDataAsJSON(ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.STEP_SLAS));

    const targetCompanies = companyId ? companies.filter(c => c.id === companyId) : companies;
    const targetTicketTypes = ticketTypeId ? ticketTypes.filter(tt => tt.id === ticketTypeId) : ticketTypes;
    const performanceMatrix = {};

    targetCompanies.forEach(company => {
      targetTicketTypes.forEach(ticketType => {
        const specificTickets = tickets.filter(t =>
          t.company_id === company.id && t.ticket_type_id === ticketType.id
        );

        if (specificTickets.length === 0) return;

        // Get company-specific workflow steps for this ticket type
        const companyWorkflowSteps = workflowSteps.filter(ws =>
          ws.ticket_type_id === ticketType.id && ws.company_id === company.id
        );

        // Calculate standard SLA target for this company's workflow
        const standardSLAHours = companyWorkflowSteps.reduce((total, step) => {
          const stepSLA = stepSLAs.find(sla => sla.step_id === step.id);
          if (stepSLA) {
            const hours = stepSLA.unit === 'days' ? stepSLA.duration * 24 : stepSLA.duration;
            return total + hours;
          }
          return total;
        }, 0);

        const completedTickets = specificTickets.filter(t => t.status === 'Completed');
        const avgActualResolutionHours = completedTickets.length > 0 ?
          completedTickets.reduce((sum, ticket) => {
            const created = new Date(ticket.created_at);
            const completed = new Date(ticket.updated_at);
            const hours = (completed - created) / (1000 * 60 * 60);
            return sum + hours;
          }, 0) / completedTickets.length : 0;

        const slaBreaches = completedTickets.filter(ticket => {
          const created = new Date(ticket.created_at);
          const completed = new Date(ticket.updated_at);
          const actualHours = (completed - created) / (1000 * 60 * 60);
          return actualHours > standardSLAHours;
        }).length;

        const performanceKey = `${company.id}_${ticketType.id}`;
        performanceMatrix[performanceKey] = {
          companyId: company.id,
          companyName: company.name,
          ticketTypeId: ticketType.id,
          ticketTypeName: ticketType.name,
          standardSLAHours: standardSLAHours,
          avgActualResolutionHours: Math.round(avgActualResolutionHours * 10) / 10,
          totalTickets: specificTickets.length,
          completedTickets: completedTickets.length,
          slaBreaches: slaBreaches,
          slaCompliance: completedTickets.length > 0 ?
            ((completedTickets.length - slaBreaches) / completedTickets.length * 100).toFixed(1) : 0,
          performanceVsStandard: standardSLAHours > 0 ?
            ((standardSLAHours - avgActualResolutionHours) / standardSLAHours * 100).toFixed(1) : 0
        };
      });
    });

    return performanceMatrix;
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getSLAPerformanceByTicketTypeAndCompany: ' + error.toString());
    throw error;
  }
}

/**
 * Get global SLA performance across all companies
 * @returns {Object} System-wide SLA performance metrics
 */
function getGlobalSLAPerformance() {
  try {
    const companyPerformanceData = getSLAPerformanceByCompany();
    const companies = Object.values(companyPerformanceData);

    if (companies.length === 0) {
      return {
        totalCompanies: 0,
        totalTickets: 0,
        totalCompletedTickets: 0,
        globalAvgSLACompliance: 0,
        globalAvgStandardSLAHours: 0,
        globalAvgActualResolutionHours: 0,
        totalSLABreaches: 0,
        bestPerformingCompany: null,
        worstPerformingCompany: null
      };
    }

    const totalTickets = companies.reduce((sum, c) => sum + c.totalTickets, 0);
    const totalCompletedTickets = companies.reduce((sum, c) => sum + c.completedTickets, 0);
    const totalBreaches = companies.reduce((sum, c) => sum + c.slaBreaches, 0);

    const weightedSLASum = companies.reduce((sum, c) =>
      sum + (c.avgStandardSLAHours * c.completedTickets), 0);
    const weightedActualSum = companies.reduce((sum, c) =>
      sum + (c.avgActualResolutionHours * c.completedTickets), 0);

    const globalAvgStandardSLAHours = totalCompletedTickets > 0 ?
      weightedSLASum / totalCompletedTickets : 0;
    const globalAvgActualResolutionHours = totalCompletedTickets > 0 ?
      weightedActualSum / totalCompletedTickets : 0;
    const globalSLACompliance = totalCompletedTickets > 0 ?
      ((totalCompletedTickets - totalBreaches) / totalCompletedTickets * 100).toFixed(1) : 0;

    const bestPerformingCompany = companies.reduce((best, current) =>
      (!best || parseFloat(current.slaCompliance) > parseFloat(best.slaCompliance)) ? current : best, null);
    const worstPerformingCompany = companies.reduce((worst, current) =>
      (!worst || parseFloat(current.slaCompliance) < parseFloat(worst.slaCompliance)) ? current : worst, null);

    return {
      totalCompanies: companies.length,
      totalTickets: totalTickets,
      totalCompletedTickets: totalCompletedTickets,
      globalAvgSLACompliance: globalSLACompliance,
      globalAvgStandardSLAHours: Math.round(globalAvgStandardSLAHours * 10) / 10,
      globalAvgActualResolutionHours: Math.round(globalAvgActualResolutionHours * 10) / 10,
      totalSLABreaches: totalBreaches,
      bestPerformingCompany: bestPerformingCompany,
      worstPerformingCompany: worstPerformingCompany
    };
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getGlobalSLAPerformance: ' + error.toString());
    throw error;
  }
}

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * MAIN API ROUTERS (doGet, doPost, doOptions)
 * These are the main entry points for the deployed Web App.
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Handles all GET requests. Used for fetching data.
 * v9.0: every case goes through createHandlerResponse (success: false + status: 'error' when the handler
 * reports a failure - before, doGet wrapped failures as success: true), the default case is loud, and
 * getWorkflowSteps / getSystemHealth are routed (the frontend sends both by GET).
 * v9.4: runs as one request (withRequestScope - table cache) and checks the proxy's identity first.
 * @param {object} e - The event object from the GET request.
 * @returns {ContentService.TextOutput} - A JSON response.
 */
function doGet(e) {
    if (!REQUEST_ACTIVE) return withRequestScope(() => doGet(e));
    // Safe parameter access with fallback
    const params = e && e.parameter ? e.parameter : {};
    const action = params.action || 'ping'; // Default to 'ping' if no action

    // v9.4 (A15): removes _proxy_secret / _auth_uid from params before anything logs or stores them
    const denied = authenticateRequest(params, action);
    if (denied) return createJsonResponse(denied);

    try {
      // v9.6: sign-in allow-list (accepts a pending invite on the first verified sign-in)
      const notAllowed = checkAllowList(action);
      if (notAllowed) return createJsonResponse(notAllowed);

      // Log for debugging
      console.log('doGet called with params:', params);

      // Handle the request based on action
      switch(action) {
        case 'ping':
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            message: 'API connection successful',
            serverTime: new Date().toISOString(),
            responseTime: 150,
            version: API_VERSION
          });

        case 'getCompanies':
          return createHandlerResponse(getCompanies(params));

        case 'getCompany':
          return createHandlerResponse(getCompany(params.companyId));

        case 'getRoles':
          return createHandlerResponse(getRoles(params));

        case 'getDropdownLists':
          return createHandlerResponse(getDropdownLists(params));

        case 'getDropdownOptions':
          return createHandlerResponse(getDropdownOptions(params.listId));

        case 'getTicketTypes':
          return createHandlerResponse(getTicketTypes(params));

        case 'getTickets':
          return createHandlerResponse(getTickets(params));

        case 'getUserPreferences':
          // v9.6 (A15): your own preferences - in enforce the verified uid, whatever user_id says
          const preferencesUser = actorId(params.user_id);
          if (!preferencesUser) {
            throw new Error('User ID is required for getting preferences');
          }
          return createHandlerResponse(getUserPreferences(preferencesUser));

        case 'getSLAPerformanceByCompany':
          return createHandlerResponse(getSLAPerformanceByCompany(params.company_id || null));

        case 'getSLAPerformanceByTicketType':
          return createHandlerResponse(getSLAPerformanceByTicketTypeAndCompany(params.ticket_type_id || null, params.company_id || null));

        case 'getGlobalSLAPerformance':
          return createHandlerResponse(getGlobalSLAPerformance());

        // Phase 10.0: User Management GET endpoints (v9.0: include_inactive for the admin lists)
        case 'getUserProfileTypes':
          return createHandlerResponse(getUserProfileTypes(params.company_id || null, String(params.include_inactive) === 'true'));

        case 'getRoleTypes':
          return createHandlerResponse(getRoleTypes(params.company_id || null, String(params.include_inactive) === 'true'));

        case 'getUserProfiles':
          return createHandlerResponse(getUserProfiles(params.company_id || null));

        case 'getCustomFields':
          // v9.1 (review R-07): the frontend sends ticket_type_id or entityTypeId / entityCategory
          return createDataResponse(getCustomFields(
            params.entity_type_id || params.entityTypeId || params.ticket_type_id || null,
            params.entity_category || params.entityCategory || 'ticket'));

        // v9.0: sent by GET from src/api/googleSheet.js readActions
        case 'getWorkflowSteps':
          return createDataResponse(getWorkflowSteps(params.ticket_type_id || null, params.company_id || null));

        case 'getSystemHealth':
          return createDataResponse(getSystemHealth());

        // v9.1 reads (src/api/googleSheet.js readActions)
        case 'getNextTicketNumber':
          return createDataResponse(peekTicketNumber(params.company_id, params.ticket_type_id));

        case 'getTicketType':
          return createDataResponse(getTicketTypeById(params.ticket_type_id || params.id));

        case 'getCustomField':
          return createDataResponse(getCustomFieldById(params.field_id || params.id));

        case 'getTicketLinks':
          return createDataResponse(getTicketLinksAction(params));

        case 'validateTicketDependencies':
          return createDataResponse(validateTicketDependencies());

        case 'checkCompanyLockStatus':
          return createDataResponse(checkCompanyLockStatus(params.id || params.company_id));

        case 'getCommentRequirements':
          return createDataResponse(getCommentRequirements(params.ticket_type_id, params.company_id || null));

        case 'getTicketHistory':
          // v10.0: a ticket the caller can't see answers "Ticket not found" (canViewTicket via ticketReadGuard)
          return createDataResponse(ticketReadGuard(params, params.ticket_id || params.id, 'getTicketHistory') ||
            getTicketHistory(params.ticket_id || params.id));

        // v10.0: the ticket types the New Ticket form may offer (decision 1)
        case 'getCreatableTicketTypes':
          return createDataResponse(getCreatableTicketTypes(params));

        // code-rules-allow A21 own inbox: only tickets the actor can approve now (an approver of the step sees them)
        case 'getTicketsForApproval':
          return createDataResponse(getTicketsForApproval(actorId(params.user_id))); // v9.4 (A15): own inbox

        // v9.4 read: companies + ticket types + approval inbox + counts in one call (reco P8, F5)
        case 'getDashboard':
          return createDataResponse(getDashboard(params));

        // v9.5 reads: SLA escalation, notifications, holidays
        case 'getSLAEscalationRules':
          return createDataResponse(getSLAEscalationRules(params));

        case 'getSLAEscalationEvents':
          return createDataResponse(getSLAEscalationEvents(params));

        case 'testSLAEscalations':
          return createDataResponse(testSLAEscalations(params));

        case 'getNotifications':
          return createDataResponse(getNotifications(params));

        case 'getCompanyHolidays':
          return createDataResponse(getCompanyHolidays(params));

        case 'getSLANotificationSettings':
          return createDataResponse(getSLANotificationSettings());

        // v9.2 reads
        case 'getStepConditions':
          return createDataResponse(getStepConditions(params.step_id || params.id));

        case 'getReportConfig':
          return createDataResponse(getReportConfig(params.ticket_type_id, params.company_id || null));

        case 'getReportData':
          return createDataResponse(getReportData(params));

        case 'getAdminActionLogs':
          return createDataResponse(getAdminActionLogs(params));

        case 'getTicketActionLogs':
          return createDataResponse(getTicketActionLogs(params));

        // v9.3 reads
        case 'getDropdownCompanyAssignments':
          return createDataResponse(getDropdownCompanyAssignments(params.dropdown_list_id || params.list_id));

        case 'getCompanySLARules':
          return createDataResponse(getCompanySLARules(params.company_id, params.ticket_type_id || null));

        case 'getTags':
          return createDataResponse(getTagsAction(params));

        case 'getTicketTags':
          return createDataResponse(ticketReadGuard(params, params.ticket_id, 'getTicketTags') || getTicketTagsAction(params.ticket_id));

        case 'searchTicketsByTags':
          return createDataResponse(searchTicketsByTagsAction(params));

        case 'getTicketShares':
          return createDataResponse(ticketReadGuard(params, params.ticket_id, 'getTicketShares') || getTicketSharesAction(params.ticket_id));

        // code-rules-allow A21 only tickets shared with the actor (a share in force is visibility, canViewTicket)
        case 'getSharedTickets':
          return createDataResponse(getSharedTicketsAction(actorId(params.user_id))); // v9.4 (A15)

        // v9.6 reads: invites (admin), company email settings (admin), ticket attachment links
        case 'getUserInvites':
          return createDataResponse(getUserInvites(params));

        case 'getCompanyEmailSettings':
          return createDataResponse(getCompanyEmailSettings(params));

        case 'getTicketAttachments':
          return createDataResponse(getTicketAttachments(params));

        default:
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: false,
            status: 'error',
            error: 'Unknown action: ' + action
          });
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('doGet Error:', error);
      return createJsonResponse({
        success: false,
        status: 'error',
        error: error.toString()
      });
    }
}

/**
 * Handles all POST requests. Used for creating or updating data.
 * v9.4: runs as one request (withRequestScope - table cache) and checks the proxy's identity first.
 * @param {object} e - The event object from the POST request.
 * @returns {ContentService.TextOutput} - A JSON response.
 */
function doPost(e) {
    if (!REQUEST_ACTIVE) return withRequestScope(() => doPost(e));
    // Safe parameter access
    const postData = e && e.postData ? e.postData.contents : '{}';

    try {
      const data = JSON.parse(postData);
      const action = data.action || 'ping';

      // v9.4 (review CR2-10): the proxy sends GET reads as POST bodies, so the shared secret never travels in a URL
      // query; _via_get routes them to doGet, which checks the identity itself
      if (data._via_get) {
        delete data._via_get;
        return doGet({ parameter: data });
      }

      // v9.4 (A15): removes _proxy_secret / _auth_uid from the body before anything logs or stores them
      const denied = authenticateRequest(data, action);
      if (denied) return createJsonResponse(denied);

      // v9.6: sign-in allow-list (accepts a pending invite on the first verified sign-in)
      const notAllowed = checkAllowList(action);
      if (notAllowed) return createJsonResponse(notAllowed);

      console.log('doPost called with action:', action, 'data:', data);

      // v8.3 (CR-03): writes hold the script lock so rebuildAllTablesApply() can't rewrite a tab under them
      if (POST_READ_ONLY_ACTIONS.includes(action)) {
        return routePostAction(action, data);
      }
      // v9.6 (S1 part 3): admin routes check the verified user first and always leave an admin log row
      const gate = checkAdminOnly(action);
      if (gate.denied) return createJsonResponse(gate.denied);
      if (ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS.includes(action)) {
        return withScriptLock(() => routeAdminAction(action, data, gate));
      }
      return withScriptLock(() => routePostAction(action, data));
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('doPost Error:', error);
      return createJsonResponse({
        success: false,
        status: 'error',
        error: error.toString()
      });
    }
}

/**
 * doPost actions that only read - they skip the script lock (A5: never list an action that writes).
 */
const POST_READ_ONLY_ACTIONS = ['ping', 'getCompanies', 'getTickets', 'checkDropdownPersistence',
  // v9.0 reads
  'canUserApprove', 'getStepApprovals', 'getStepApprovers', 'getWorkflowStep', 'getTicket',
  'getUsers', 'getUserProfile', 'getUserRoles', 'getUserGeneralRole',
  // v9.3: read-only, sent by POST because its ticket_type_ids list goes in the body
  'exportConfiguration'];

/**
 * Route one doPost action. Split out of doPost in v8.3 so writes run inside withScriptLock().
 * Errors are thrown to doPost, which returns them as { success: false }.
 * @param {string} action - Action name
 * @param {Object} data - Parsed request body
 * @returns {ContentService.TextOutput} JSON response
 */
function routePostAction(action, data) {
      // Handle the request based on action
      switch(action) {
        case 'ping':
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            message: 'API connection successful',
            serverTime: new Date().toISOString(),
            responseTime: 150,
            version: API_VERSION
          });

        case 'getCompanies':
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: getCompanies()
          });

        case 'createCompany':
          // Handle both payload formats: { action, payload } or { action, ...payload }
          const companyData = data.payload || { name: data.name, code: data.code };
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: createCompany(companyData)
          });

        case 'updateCompany':
          const updateCompanyData = data.payload || { id: data.id, name: data.name, code: data.code, user_id: data.user_id };
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: updateCompany(updateCompanyData)
          });

        case 'deleteCompany':
          const deactivationData = data.payload || {
            id: data.id,
            reason: data.reason || 'admin_request'
          };
          if (!deactivationData.id) {
            throw new Error('Company ID is required for deactivation');
          }
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: deactivateCompany(deactivationData.id, deactivationData.reason)
          });

        case 'reactivateCompany':
          const reactivationData = data.payload || {
            id: data.id
          };
          if (!reactivationData.id) {
            throw new Error('Company ID is required for reactivation');
          }
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: reactivateCompany(reactivationData.id)
          });

        case 'createRole':
          const roleData = data.payload || { name: data.name, company_id: data.company_id, role_type_id: data.role_type_id };
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: createRole(roleData)
          });

        case 'updateRole':
          const updateRoleData = data.payload || {
            id: data.id, name: data.name, company_id: data.company_id,
            role_type_id: data.role_type_id, is_active: data.is_active
          };
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: updateRole(updateRoleData)
          });

        case 'deleteRole':
          const roleDeletionData = data.payload || {
            id: data.id,
            reason: data.reason || 'admin_request'
          };
          if (!roleDeletionData.id) {
            throw new Error('Role ID is required for deletion');
          }
          // v9.0: deactivateRole() never existed - soft delete through deactivateRecord
          return createHandlerResponse(deleteRole(roleDeletionData.id, roleDeletionData.reason));

        case 'createDropdownList':
          // Simplified payload handling to match working company creation pattern
          const dropdownData = data.payload || {
            name: data.name,
            description: data.description || '',
            company_id: data.company_id || null,
            options: data.options || []
          };

          Logger.log('=== SIMPLIFIED DROPDOWN CREATION DEBUG ===');
          Logger.log('🔍 Raw request data keys:', Object.keys(data));
          Logger.log('📥 Final extracted payload:', JSON.stringify(dropdownData, null, 2));

          // Use the debug version for comprehensive validation and logging
          const creationResult = createDropdownListWithDebug(dropdownData);

          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: creationResult
          });

        case 'updateDropdownList':
          const updateDropdownData = data.payload || {
            id: data.id,
            name: data.name,
            description: data.description,
            company_id: data.company_id,
            options: data.options
          };
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: updateDropdownList(updateDropdownData)
          });

        case 'deleteDropdownList':
          const dropdownDeletionData = data.payload || {
            id: data.id,
            reason: data.reason || 'admin_request'
          };
          if (!dropdownDeletionData.id) {
            throw new Error('Dropdown list ID is required for deletion');
          }
          // v9.0: deactivateDropdownList() never existed - soft delete through deactivateRecord
          return createHandlerResponse(deactivateRecord(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_LISTS, dropdownDeletionData.id, dropdownDeletionData.reason));

        case 'createTicketType':
          const ticketTypeData = data.payload || { name: data.name, code: data.code, description: data.description, transaction_id: data.transaction_id, require_attachment_on_create: data.require_attachment_on_create, company_id: data.company_id };
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: createTicketType(ticketTypeData)
          });

        case 'updateTicketType':
          const updateTicketTypeData = data.payload || { id: data.id, name: data.name, code: data.code, description: data.description, transaction_id: data.transaction_id, require_attachment_on_create: data.require_attachment_on_create, company_id: data.company_id };
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: updateTicketType(updateTicketTypeData)
          });

        case 'deleteTicketType':
          const ticketTypeDeletionData = data.payload || {
            id: data.id,
            reason: data.reason || 'admin_request'
          };
          if (!ticketTypeDeletionData.id) {
            throw new Error('Ticket type ID is required for deletion');
          }
          // v9.0: deactivateTicketType() never existed - soft delete through deactivateRecord
          return createHandlerResponse(deactivateRecord(SHEETS.TICKET_TYPES, ticketTypeDeletionData.id, ticketTypeDeletionData.reason));

        case 'getTickets':
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: getTickets(data)
          });

        case 'createTicket':
          // v9.1: keep description / priority / customData (the old list dropped them)
          const ticketData = data.payload || pickDefined(data, ['title', 'description', 'priority', 'ticket_type_id',
            'requester_id', 'company_id', 'customData', 'custom_fields']);
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: createTicket(ticketData)
          });

        case 'recordLogin':
          const loginData = data.payload || {
            userId: data.userId || data.user_id,
            email: data.email,
            ipAddress: data.ipAddress
          };
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: logLogin(loginData)
          });

        case 'updateUserPreferences':
          const userPreferencesData = data.payload || {
            user_id: data.user_id,
            preferences: data.preferences
          };
          // v9.6 (A15): only your own preferences - in enforce the verified uid, whatever user_id says
          userPreferencesData.user_id = actorId(userPreferencesData.user_id);
          if (!userPreferencesData.user_id) {
            throw new Error('User ID is required for updating preferences');
          }
          if (!userPreferencesData.preferences) {
            throw new Error('Preferences data is required');
          }
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: updateUserPreferences(userPreferencesData.user_id, userPreferencesData.preferences)
          });

        case 'testMinimalDropdownCreate':
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: testMinimalDropdownCreate()
          });

        case 'checkDropdownPersistence':
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: checkDropdownPersistence()
          });

        // Phase 10.0: User Management POST endpoints
        // v8.3 (CR-07): these handlers return { status: 'error' } instead of throwing - createHandlerResponse
        // turns that into success:false. The frontend sends user_profile_type_id / role_type_id, not id.
        case 'createUserProfileType':
          const userProfileTypeData = data.payload || data;
          return createHandlerResponse(createUserProfileType(userProfileTypeData));

        case 'updateUserProfileType':
          const updateUserProfileTypeData = data.payload || data;
          return createHandlerResponse(updateUserProfileType(
            updateUserProfileTypeData.id || updateUserProfileTypeData.user_profile_type_id,
            updateUserProfileTypeData
          ));

        case 'deleteUserProfileType':
          const deleteUserProfileTypeData = data.payload || data;
          return createHandlerResponse(deactivateRecord(
            SHEETS.USER_PROFILE_TYPES,
            deleteUserProfileTypeData.id || deleteUserProfileTypeData.user_profile_type_id,
            deleteUserProfileTypeData.reason || 'admin_request'
          ));

        case 'createRoleType':
          const roleTypeData = data.payload || data;
          return createHandlerResponse(createRoleType(roleTypeData));

        case 'updateRoleType':
          const updateRoleTypeData = data.payload || data;
          return createHandlerResponse(updateRoleType(
            updateRoleTypeData.id || updateRoleTypeData.role_type_id,
            updateRoleTypeData
          ));

        case 'deleteRoleType':
          const deleteRoleTypeData = data.payload || data;
          return createHandlerResponse(deactivateRecord(
            SHEETS.ROLE_TYPES,
            deleteRoleTypeData.id || deleteRoleTypeData.role_type_id,
            deleteRoleTypeData.reason || 'admin_request'
          ));

        case 'createUserProfile':
          const userProfileData = data.payload || data;
          return createHandlerResponse(createUserProfile(userProfileData));

        case 'updateUserProfile':
          const updateUserProfileData = data.payload || data;
          return createHandlerResponse(updateUserProfile(updateUserProfileData.user_id, updateUserProfileData));

        case 'createCustomField':
          const customFieldData = data.payload || data;
          return createHandlerResponse(createCustomField(customFieldData.entity_type_id, customFieldData, customFieldData.entity_category || 'ticket'));

        case 'setCustomFieldValue':
          const customFieldValueData = data.payload || data;
          return createHandlerResponse(setCustomFieldValue(customFieldValueData.entity_id, customFieldValueData.field_id, customFieldValueData.value, customFieldValueData.entity_category || 'ticket'));

        // User Management Setup endpoints (v8.3 (CR-05): safe to repeat - never clears data, seeds test data once)
        case 'initializeUserManagementTables':
          return createHandlerResponse(initializeUserManagementTables());

        case 'completeUserManagementSetup':
          return createHandlerResponse(completeUserManagementSetup());

        // v9.0: approval path. Keys per src/api/googleSheet.js (A7); responses unwrapped (createDataResponse)
        // so response.data is what the frontend method returns.
        case 'canUserApprove':
          return createDataResponse(canUserApproveAction(data.payload || data));

        case 'submitStepApproval':
          return createDataResponse(submitStepApproval(data.payload || data));

        // v9.5 writes: SLA escalation, notifications, holidays (admin checks inside, A15)
        case 'createSLAEscalationRule':
          return createDataResponse(createSLAEscalationRule(data));

        case 'updateSLAEscalationRule':
          return createDataResponse(updateSLAEscalationRule(data));

        case 'deleteSLAEscalationRule':
          return createDataResponse(deleteSLAEscalationRule(data));

        case 'runSLAEscalationsNow':
          return createDataResponse(runSLAEscalationsNow(data));

        case 'markNotificationsRead':
          return createDataResponse(markNotificationsRead(data));

        case 'createCompanyHoliday':
          return createDataResponse(createCompanyHoliday(data));

        case 'deleteCompanyHoliday':
          return createDataResponse(deleteCompanyHoliday(data));

        case 'addPhilippineHolidays':
          return createDataResponse(addPhilippineHolidays(data));

        case 'saveSLANotificationSettings':
          return createDataResponse(saveSLANotificationSettings(data));

        case 'getStepApprovals':
          const stepApprovalsQuery = data.payload || data;
          return createDataResponse(ticketReadGuard({ user_id: stepApprovalsQuery.user_id || data.user_id }, stepApprovalsQuery.ticket_id, 'getStepApprovals') ||
            getStepApprovals(stepApprovalsQuery.ticket_id, stepApprovalsQuery.step_id));

        case 'getStepApprovers':
          const stepApproversQuery = data.payload || data;
          return createDataResponse(getStepApprovers(stepApproversQuery.step_id));

        case 'setStepApprovers':
          const stepApproversData = data.payload || data;
          return createDataResponse(setStepApprovers(stepApproversData.step_id, stepApproversData.role_ids));

        case 'getWorkflowStep':
          const workflowStepQuery = data.payload || data;
          return createDataResponse(getWorkflowStepById(workflowStepQuery.step_id || workflowStepQuery.id));

        case 'getTicket':
          const ticketQuery = data.payload || data;
          const ticketQueryId = ticketQuery.ticketId || ticketQuery.ticket_id || ticketQuery.id;
          // v10.0: a ticket the caller can't see answers "Ticket not found" (canViewTicket via ticketReadGuard)
          return createDataResponse(ticketReadGuard({ user_id: ticketQuery.user_id || data.user_id }, ticketQueryId, 'getTicket') ||
            getTicketById(ticketQueryId));

        case 'updateTicket':
          const ticketUpdateData = data.payload || data;
          return createDataResponse(updateTicketWithValidation(ticketUpdateData.id || ticketUpdateData.ticket_id, ticketUpdateData));

        case 'updateTicketStatus':
          return createDataResponse(updateTicketStatusAction(data.payload || data));

        case 'getUsers':
          const usersQuery = data.payload || data;
          return createDataResponse(getUsersWithAccess(usersQuery.company_id || null));

        case 'getUserProfile':
          const userProfileQuery = data.payload || data;
          return createDataResponse(getUserProfileWithAccess(userProfileQuery.userId || userProfileQuery.user_id));

        case 'getUserRoles':
          const userRolesQuery = data.payload || data;
          return createDataResponse(getUserRoleList(userRolesQuery.userId || userRolesQuery.user_id, userRolesQuery.company_id || null));

        case 'getUserGeneralRole':
          const generalRoleQuery = data.payload || data;
          return createDataResponse(getUserGeneralRole(generalRoleQuery.userId || generalRoleQuery.user_id, generalRoleQuery.company_id || null));

        case 'assignUserRole':
          return createDataResponse(assignUserRoleAction(data.payload || data));

        case 'removeUserRole':
          return createDataResponse(removeUserRoleAction(data.payload || data));

        case 'bulkAssignRole':
          return createDataResponse(bulkAssignRole(data.payload || data));

        case 'replaceUserRoles':
          return createDataResponse(replaceUserRoles(data.payload || data));

        case 'updateUserRoleAssignment':
          const assignmentUpdateData = data.payload || data;
          return createDataResponse(updateUserRoleAssignment(assignmentUpdateData.assignment_id || assignmentUpdateData.id, assignmentUpdateData));

        case 'deleteUserRoleAssignment':
          const assignmentDeletion = data.payload || data;
          return createDataResponse(deleteUserRoleAssignment(
            assignmentDeletion.assignment_id || assignmentDeletion.id,
            assignmentDeletion.reason || 'Admin deletion'
          ));

        case 'getExpiringAssignments':
          const expiringQuery = data.payload || data;
          return createDataResponse(getExpiringAssignments(expiringQuery.days_ahead || 30));

        // v9.1 writes
        case 'generateTicketNumber':
          return createDataResponse(generateTicketNumberAction(data.company_id, data.ticket_type_id));

        case 'createWorkflowStep':
          return createDataResponse(createWorkflowStep(data.payload || data));

        case 'updateWorkflowStep':
          const stepUpdate = data.payload || data;
          return createDataResponse(updateWorkflowStep(stepUpdate.step_id || stepUpdate.id, stepUpdate));

        case 'deleteWorkflowStep':
          return createDataResponse(deleteWorkflowStep(data.step_id || data.id, data.user_id || null));

        case 'copyWorkflowSteps':
          return createDataResponse(copyWorkflowStepsFromCompany(data.ticket_type_id, data.source_company_id,
            data.target_company_id, data.user_id || null));

        case 'createDefaultWorkflow':
          return createDataResponse(createDefaultWorkflow(data.ticket_type_id, data.company_id, data.user_id || null));

        case 'updateCustomField':
          return createDataResponse(updateCustomField(data.field_id || data.id, data));

        case 'deleteCustomField':
          return createDataResponse(deleteCustomField(data.field_id || data.id, data.reason || 'Admin requested field deletion'));

        case 'createTicketLink':
          return createDataResponse(createTicketLinkAction(data.payload || data));

        case 'deleteTicketLink':
          return createDataResponse(deleteTicketLinkAction(data.link_id || data.id, data.reason));

        case 'lockCompany':
          return createDataResponse(lockCompany(data.id || data.company_id, data.reason, data.user_id || null));

        case 'unlockCompany':
          return createDataResponse(unlockCompany(data.id || data.company_id, data.reason, data.user_id || null));

        case 'saveCommentRequirements':
          return createDataResponse(saveCommentRequirements(data.payload || data));

        // v9.2 writes
        case 'saveStepConditions':
          return createDataResponse(saveStepConditions(data.payload || data));

        case 'saveReportConfig':
          return createDataResponse(saveReportConfig(data.payload || data));

        // v9.3 writes
        case 'assignDropdownToCompany':
          return createDataResponse(assignDropdownToCompany(data.dropdown_list_id, data.company_id, data.user_id || null));

        case 'assignDropdownGlobally':
          return createDataResponse(assignDropdownGlobally(data.dropdown_list_id, data.user_id || null));

        case 'removeDropdownCompanyAssignment':
          return createDataResponse(removeDropdownCompanyAssignment(data.dropdown_list_id, data.company_id || '', data.user_id || null));

        case 'updateDropdownCompanyAssignments':
          return createDataResponse(updateDropdownCompanyAssignments(data.dropdown_list_id, data.assignments, data.user_id || null));

        case 'createCompanySLARule':
          return createDataResponse(createCompanySLARule(data.payload || data));

        case 'updateCompanySLARule':
          return createDataResponse(updateCompanySLARule(data.rule_id || data.id, data.payload || data));

        case 'deleteCompanySLARule':
          return createDataResponse(deleteCompanySLARule(data.rule_id || data.id, data.user_id || null, data.reason));

        case 'exportConfiguration':
          return createDataResponse(exportConfiguration(data.payload || data));

        case 'createConfigurationBackup':
          return createDataResponse(createConfigurationBackup(data.payload || data));

        case 'importConfiguration':
          return createDataResponse(importConfiguration(data.payload || data));

        case 'setTicketTypeFeatures':
          return createDataResponse(setTicketTypeFeatures(data.payload || data));

        case 'createTag':

        case 'updateTag':

        case 'deleteTag':
          return createDataResponse(deleteTagAction(data.tag_id || data.id, data.user_id || null, data.reason));

        case 'assignTags':
          return createDataResponse(assignTagsAction(data.payload || data));

        case 'removeTags':
          return createDataResponse(removeTagsAction(data.payload || data));

        case 'shareTicket':
          return createDataResponse(shareTicketAction(data.payload || data));

        case 'revokeTicketShare':
          return createDataResponse(revokeTicketShareAction(data.payload || data));

        // v9.6 writes: invites + company email (ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS), attachment links (own rule inside)
        case 'createUserInvite':
          return createDataResponse(createUserInvite(data.payload || data));

        case 'resendUserInvite':
          return createDataResponse(resendUserInvite(data.payload || data));

        case 'revokeUserInvite':
          return createDataResponse(revokeUserInvite(data.payload || data));

        case 'saveCompanyEmailSettings':

        case 'sendCompanyTestEmail':
          return createDataResponse(sendCompanyTestEmail(data.payload || data));

        case 'addTicketAttachmentLink':

        case 'removeTicketAttachment':
