        case 'reportAttachmentLinkBroken':
          return createDataResponse(reportAttachmentLinkBroken(data.payload || data));

        case 'updateAttachmentLink':
          return createDataResponse(updateAttachmentLink(data.payload || data));

        default:
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: false,
            status: 'error',
            error: 'Unknown action: ' + action
          });
      }
}

/**
 * Handles OPTIONS requests for CORS preflight checks. This is essential for
 * allowing the frontend (e.g., on Vercel) to communicate with this API.
 * Google Apps Script requires proper CORS handling for cross-origin requests.
 * @param {object} e - The event object from the OPTIONS request.
 * @returns {ContentService.TextOutput} - An empty response with CORS support.
 */
function doOptions(e) {
  // Google Apps Script doesn't support setHeader chaining
  // For Web Apps, CORS is handled automatically when deployed with "Anyone" access
  return ContentService
    .createTextOutput('')
    .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.TEXT);
}

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * CORE BUSINESS LOGIC FUNCTIONS
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Create a ticket: number it, write it by header, count it against the company (locking the company code on
 * the first ticket, spec 3.3), log it, start its workflow and save its custom field values.
 * v9.1: header-driven row with description / priority / created_by (the positional row dropped them);
 * customData falls back to custom_fields; the id is max(id) + 1 so a deleted row can't cause a duplicate.
 * v9.4: the id comes from the 'ticket_id' sequence_counters row (A18 - no read of the whole tickets tab);
 * the requester is the verified user (actorId, A15).
 * @param {Object} payload - { title, description, priority, ticket_type_id, company_id, requester_id, customData }
 * @returns {Object} { id, ticketId, ticket_number, ticketNumber, workflow, customFieldErrors, message }
 */
function createTicket(payload, options = {}) {
  if (!payload) {
    throw new Error('Payload is required');
  }
  ['title', 'ticket_type_id', 'company_id', 'requester_id'].forEach(field => {
    if (!payload[field]) throw new Error(`Missing required field: ${field}`);
  });

  const ticketsSheet = ss().getSheetByName(SHEETS.TICKETS) || ensureTableSheet(SHEETS.TICKETS);
  const now = new Date();

  const ticketTypes = readTable(SHEETS.TICKET_TYPES);
  const companies = readTable(SHEETS.COMPANIES);
  const ticketType = (ticketTypes ? ticketTypes.records : []).find(t => sameId(t.id, payload.ticket_type_id));
  const company = (companies ? companies.records : []).find(c => sameId(c.id, payload.company_id));
  if (!ticketType) throw new Error(`Ticket Type with ID ${payload.ticket_type_id} not found.`);
  if (!company) throw new Error(`Company with ID ${payload.company_id} not found.`);

  // The verified user is the requester (A15) - except for a chained ticket, which keeps the parent's requester
  // (a target; the actor is the approver who completed the step) - review CR2-01
  const requesterId = options.requesterIsTarget ? payload.requester_id : actorId(payload.requester_id);
  // v10.0 (decision 1): only a user with a role on this ticket type in this company may create it (admins always).
  // A chained ticket is created by the workflow, not by the requester, so it isn't checked. Rollout as the v9.6 admin
  // gate: AUTH_MODE enforce refuses, warn logs and continues, off doesn't check.
  if (!options.requesterIsTarget && REQUEST_AUTH.mode !== 'off' && !canCreateTicket(buildActorScope(requesterId), company.id, ticketType.id)) {
    if (REQUEST_AUTH.mode === 'enforce') {
      throw new Error(`You can't create ${ticketType.name || 'this ticket type'} tickets in ${company.name || 'this company'}: ` +
        'you need a role on this ticket type there. Ask your administrator.');
    }
    console.log(`AUTH warn: createTicket by ${requesterId} without a role on ticket type ${ticketType.id} in company ${company.id}`);
  }
  const newTicketId = nextSequenceValue('ticket_id', highestTicketId);

  const ticketNumber = generateTicketNumber(company.code, ticketType.code);

  ticketsSheet.appendRow(rowFromRecord(sheetHeaders(ticketsSheet), {
    id: newTicketId,
    ticket_number: ticketNumber,
    title: payload.title,
    ticket_type_id: payload.ticket_type_id,
    requester_id: requesterId,
    status: 'New',
    current_step_id: '',
    step_due_date: '',
    created_at: now,
    updated_at: now,
    company_id: payload.company_id,
    description: payload.description || '',
    priority: payload.priority || 'medium',
    is_active: true,
    created_by: requesterId,
    updated_by: requesterId
  }));
  invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(ticketsSheet));

  // Spec 3.3: the company code locks on the first ticket, so existing ticket numbers stay valid
  const companyChanges = { ticket_count: (Number(company.ticket_count) || 0) + 1 };
  if (!isLockedFlag(company.code_locked)) {
    companyChanges.code_locked = true;
    companyChanges.code_locked_at = now.toISOString();
    companyChanges.code_locked_reason = 'first_ticket_created';
  }
  updateRecordFields(SHEETS.COMPANIES, company.id, companyChanges);

  logTicketHistory(newTicketId, requesterId, 'Created', 'Ticket submitted by user.');
  logTicketAction(newTicketId, requesterId, 'CREATE_TICKET', { newTicketData: payload });

  // v9.0: put the ticket on the first step of its workflow
  const workflow = startTicketWorkflow(newTicketId, requesterId);

  // v8.3 (CR-01): header-driven via setCustomFieldValue
  const customData = payload.customData && Object.keys(payload.customData).length ? payload.customData : (payload.custom_fields || {});
  const customFieldErrors = [];
  Object.keys(customData).forEach(fieldId => {
    const result = setCustomFieldValue(newTicketId, fieldId, customData[fieldId], 'ticket');
    if (result.status === 'error') {
      customFieldErrors.push(`${fieldId}: ${result.message}`);
    }
  });

  return {
    id: newTicketId,
    ticketId: newTicketId,
    ticket_number: ticketNumber,
    ticketNumber: ticketNumber,
    workflow: workflow.data,
    customFieldErrors: customFieldErrors,
    message: customFieldErrors.length > 0
      ? "Ticket created, but some custom field values were not saved."
      : "Ticket created successfully."
  };
}

/**
 * Fetches all tickets with their links.
 * v9.2: only active links count; chained_children / chained_parent carry the links a step created
 * (link_type 'chained') for the dashboard's parent/child view. children / parent keep every active link.
 * v10.0: only the tickets the caller may see (canViewTicket; design §4.2).
 * @param {object} params - Optional filters ({ status }) + user_id.
 * @returns {Array} - Ticket objects.
 */
function getTickets(params) {
  const ss = openSpreadsheet();
  const scope = requestScope(params && (params.user_id || params.userId), 'getTickets');
  const tickets = getSheetDataAsJSON(ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.TICKETS)).filter(t => canViewTicket(scope, t) !== 'none');
  const links = activeTicketLinks();
  // v9.4 (reco P7): index the links once instead of filtering all links for every ticket
  const childLinksOf = groupBy(links, l => String(l.parent_ticket_id));
  const parentLinksOf = groupBy(links, l => String(l.child_ticket_id));

  const ticketsWithLinks = tickets.map(ticket => {
    const childLinks = childLinksOf[String(ticket.id)] || [];
    const parentLinks = parentLinksOf[String(ticket.id)] || [];
    const chainedParent = parentLinks.find(l => String(l.link_type) === 'chained');
    return {
      ...ticket,
      children: childLinks.map(l => l.child_ticket_id),
      parent: parentLinks.length ? parentLinks[0].parent_ticket_id : null,
      chained_children: childLinks.filter(l => String(l.link_type) === 'chained').map(l => l.child_ticket_id),
      chained_parent: chainedParent ? chainedParent.parent_ticket_id : null
    };
  });

  if (params && params.status) {
    return ticketsWithLinks.filter(t => t.status === params.status);
  }
  return ticketsWithLinks;
}

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * COMPANY MANAGEMENT FUNCTIONS
 * =================================================================================
 */

function getCompanies(params = {}) {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    const companiesSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.COMPANIES);

    if (!companiesSheet) {
      initializeCompaniesSheet();
      return [];
    }

    const companies = getSheetDataAsJSON(companiesSheet);
    Logger.log(`Retrieved ${companies.length} companies`);
    return companies;
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error getting companies: ${error.message}`);
    throw new Error(`Failed to retrieve companies: ${error.message}`);
  }
}

function getCompany(companyId) {
  try {
    const companies = getCompanies();
    const company = companies.find(c => c.id === companyId);

    if (!company) {
      throw new Error('Company not found');
    }

    return company;
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error getting company ${companyId}: ${error.message}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

function createCompany(payload) {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    let companiesSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.COMPANIES);

    if (!companiesSheet) {
      companiesSheet = initializeCompaniesSheet();
    }

    // Validate payload exists
    if (!payload) {
      throw new Error('Payload is required');
    }

    // Validate required fields
    if (!payload.name || !payload.code) {
      throw new Error('Company name and code are required');
    }

    // Check if code already exists (among active companies only)
    const existingCompanies = getSheetDataAsJSON(companiesSheet);
    const activeCompanies = filterActiveRecords(existingCompanies);
    const codeExists = activeCompanies.some(c => c.code.toUpperCase() === payload.code.toUpperCase());

    if (codeExists) {
      throw new Error('Company code already exists');
    }

    // Create audit fields for new record
    const auditFields = createAuditFields();

    // Generate new company with comprehensive audit fields
    const newCompany = {
      id: generateId('comp'),
      name: payload.name.trim(),
      code: payload.code.toUpperCase(),
      code_locked: false,
      code_locked_at: null,
      code_locked_reason: null,
      ticket_count: 0,
      ...auditFields
    };

    // Add to sheet with all audit fields
    companiesSheet.appendRow([
      newCompany.id,
      newCompany.name,
      newCompany.code,
      newCompany.code_locked,
      newCompany.code_locked_at,
      newCompany.code_locked_reason,
      newCompany.ticket_count,
      newCompany.is_active,
      newCompany.created_at,
      newCompany.created_by,
      newCompany.updated_at,
      newCompany.updated_by,
      newCompany.deactivated_at,
      newCompany.deactivated_by,
      newCompany.deactivation_reason
    ]);
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(companiesSheet));

    // Log the action
    logAdminAction('CREATE_COMPANY', newCompany.id, newCompany);

    Logger.log(`Created company: ${newCompany.name} (${newCompany.code})`);
    return newCompany;
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error creating company: ${error.message}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

/**
 * Deactivate (soft delete) a company
 * @param {string} companyId - The company ID to deactivate
 * @param {string} reason - Reason for deactivation
 * @returns {Object} Result of the deactivation operation
 */
function deactivateCompany(companyId, reason = 'admin_request') {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    const companiesSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.COMPANIES);

    if (!companiesSheet) {
      throw new Error('Companies sheet not found');
    }

    // Find the row to deactivate
    const data = sheetValues(companiesSheet);
    const headers = data[0];
    const rows = data.slice(1);

    const rowIndex = rows.findIndex(row => row[0] === companyId);
    if (rowIndex === -1) {
      throw new Error('Company not found');
    }

    const actualRowIndex = rowIndex + 2; // +1 for header, +1 for 0-based index

    // Create deactivation audit fields
    const deactivationFields = deactivateAuditFields(reason);
    const sheetsFields = auditFieldsForSheets(deactivationFields);

    // Update the row with deactivation fields
    const currentRow = rows[rowIndex];
    const updatedRow = [...currentRow];

    // Update audit fields (assuming column positions match the schema)
    updatedRow[7] = sheetsFields.is_active; // is_active column
    updatedRow[10] = sheetsFields.updated_at; // updated_at column
    updatedRow[11] = sheetsFields.updated_by; // updated_by column
    updatedRow[12] = sheetsFields.deactivated_at; // deactivated_at column
    updatedRow[13] = sheetsFields.deactivated_by; // deactivated_by column
    updatedRow[14] = sheetsFields.deactivation_reason; // deactivation_reason column

    // Write the updated row back to the sheet
    companiesSheet.getRange(actualRowIndex, 1, 1, updatedRow.length).setValues([updatedRow]);
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(companiesSheet));

    // Log the action
    logAdminAction('DEACTIVATE_COMPANY', companyId, { reason: reason });

    Logger.log(`Deactivated company: ${companyId} (Reason: ${reason})`);
    return {
      id: companyId,
      deactivated: true,
      reason: reason,
      deactivated_at: deactivationFields.deactivated_at
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error deactivating company: ${error.message}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

/**
 * Reactivate a deactivated company
 * @param {string} companyId - The company ID to reactivate
 * @returns {Object} Result of the reactivation operation
 */
function reactivateCompany(companyId) {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    const companiesSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.COMPANIES);

    if (!companiesSheet) {
      throw new Error('Companies sheet not found');
    }

    // Find the row to reactivate
    const data = sheetValues(companiesSheet);
    const headers = data[0];
    const rows = data.slice(1);

    const rowIndex = rows.findIndex(row => row[0] === companyId);
    if (rowIndex === -1) {
      throw new Error('Company not found');
    }

    const actualRowIndex = rowIndex + 2; // +1 for header, +1 for 0-based index

    // Create reactivation audit fields
    const reactivationFields = reactivateAuditFields();
    const sheetsFields = auditFieldsForSheets(reactivationFields);

    // Update the row with reactivation fields
    const currentRow = rows[rowIndex];
    const updatedRow = [...currentRow];

    // Update audit fields (assuming column positions match the schema)
    updatedRow[7] = sheetsFields.is_active; // is_active column
    updatedRow[10] = sheetsFields.updated_at; // updated_at column
    updatedRow[11] = sheetsFields.updated_by; // updated_by column
    updatedRow[12] = sheetsFields.deactivated_at; // deactivated_at column (cleared)
    updatedRow[13] = sheetsFields.deactivated_by; // deactivated_by column (cleared)
    updatedRow[14] = sheetsFields.deactivation_reason; // deactivation_reason column (cleared)

    // Write the updated row back to the sheet
    companiesSheet.getRange(actualRowIndex, 1, 1, updatedRow.length).setValues([updatedRow]);
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(companiesSheet));

    // Log the action
    logAdminAction('REACTIVATE_COMPANY', companyId, { reactivated: true });

    Logger.log(`Reactivated company: ${companyId}`);
    return {
      id: companyId,
      reactivated: true,
      reactivated_at: reactivationFields.updated_at
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error reactivating company: ${error.message}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

/**
 * Update a company's name / code.
 * v9.1: the whole row is written by header (A1). Before, a positional 5-column write put created_at and
 * updated_at into code_locked / code_locked_at on every edit. A locked code can't change (spec 3.3) - unlock
 * it first (unlockCompany, admin override).
 * @param {Object} payload - { id, name, code, user_id }
 * @returns {Object} The updated company record
 */
function updateCompany(payload) {
  try {
    if (!payload) {
      throw new Error('Payload is required');
    }
    if (!payload.id) {
      throw new Error('Company ID is required for update');
    }
    if (!payload.name || !payload.code) {
      throw new Error('Company name and code are required');
    }

    const table = readTable(SHEETS.COMPANIES);
    if (!table) {
      throw new Error('Companies sheet not found');
    }
    const current = table.records.find(c => sameId(c.id, payload.id));
    if (!current) {
      throw new Error('Company not found');
    }

    const code = String(payload.code).toUpperCase().trim();
    const codeExists = table.records.some(c => !sameId(c.id, payload.id) && String(c.code || '').toUpperCase().trim() === code);
    if (codeExists) {
      throw new Error('Company code already exists');
    }
    if (code !== String(current.code || '').toUpperCase().trim() && isLockedFlag(current.code_locked)) {
      throw new Error('Company code is locked because tickets use it; unlock it first (admin override)');
    }

    const userId = payload.user_id || getCurrentUserId();
    const result = updateRecordFields(SHEETS.COMPANIES, payload.id, {
      name: String(payload.name).trim(),
      code: code,
      updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      updated_by: userId
    });

    logAdminAction(userId, 'UPDATE_COMPANY', 'company', payload.id, {
      before: { name: current.name, code: current.code },
      after: { name: result.after.name, code: result.after.code }
    });

    Logger.log(`Updated company: ${result.after.name}`);
    return result.after;
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error updating company: ${error.message}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

function deleteCompany(companyId) {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    const companiesSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.COMPANIES);

    // Find the row to delete
    const data = sheetValues(companiesSheet);
    let rowIndex = -1;
    let companyData = null;

    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (data[i][0] === companyId) {
        rowIndex = i + 1; // 1-based index
        companyData = data[i];
        break;
      }
    }

    if (rowIndex === -1) {
      throw new Error('Company not found');
    }

    // Delete the row
    companiesSheet.deleteRow(rowIndex);
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(companiesSheet));

    // Log the action
    logAdminAction('DELETE_COMPANY', companyId, { deletedCompany: companyData });

    Logger.log(`Deleted company: ${companyData[1]}`);
    return { message: 'Company deleted successfully' };
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error deleting company: ${error.message}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * ROLE MANAGEMENT FUNCTIONS
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Get roles. v9.0: deactivated roles are hidden unless params.include_inactive; rows written before
 * roles had an is_active column (blank) count as active.
 * @param {Object} params - { companyId | company_id, include_inactive }
 * @returns {Array} Role records
 */
function getRoles(params = {}) {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    let rolesSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ROLES);

    if (!rolesSheet) {
      rolesSheet = initializeRolesSheet();
      return [];
    }

    let roles = getSheetDataAsJSON(rolesSheet);
    const companyId = params.companyId || params.company_id;
    const includeInactive = String(params.include_inactive) === 'true';

    if (!includeInactive) {
      roles = roles.filter(r => isActiveFlag(r.is_active));
    }

    // Filter by company if specified (global roles have no company)
    if (companyId) {
      roles = roles.filter(r => sameId(r.company_id, companyId) || !r.company_id);
    }

    Logger.log(`Retrieved ${roles.length} roles`);
    return roles;
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error getting roles: ${error.message}`);
    throw new Error(`Failed to retrieve roles: ${error.message}`);
  }
}

/**
 * Create a role. v9.0: header-driven (A1) and accepts role_type_id.
 * @param {Object} payload - { name, company_id ('global' or blank = global), role_type_id }
 * @returns {Object} The new role
 */
function createRole(payload) {
  const ss = openSpreadsheet();
  const rolesSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ROLES) || ensureTableSheet(SHEETS.ROLES);

  if (!payload) {
    throw new Error('Payload is required');
  }
  if (!payload.name || !String(payload.name).trim()) {
    throw new Error('Role name is required');
  }

  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  const newRole = {
    id: generateId('role'),
    name: String(payload.name).trim(),
    company_id: payload.company_id === 'global' ? '' : (payload.company_id || ''),
    role_type_id: payload.role_type_id || '',
    is_active: true,
    created_at: now,
    created_by: getCurrentUserId(),
    updated_at: now,
    updated_by: getCurrentUserId()
  };

  rolesSheet.appendRow(rowFromRecord(sheetHeaders(rolesSheet), newRole));
  invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(rolesSheet));
  logAdminAction(getCurrentUserId(), 'CREATE_ROLE', 'role', newRole.id, { name: newRole.name, company_id: newRole.company_id });

  Logger.log(`Created role: ${newRole.name}`);
  return newRole;
}

/**
 * Update a role's name, company or role type. v9.0: reads the row and writes it back by header (A1).
 * @param {Object} payload - { id, name, company_id, role_type_id }
 * @returns {Object} The updated role
 */
function updateRole(payload) {
  if (!payload) {
    throw new Error('Payload is required');
  }
  if (!payload.id || !payload.name) {
    throw new Error('Role ID and name are required');
  }

  const changes = {
    name: String(payload.name).trim(),
    company_id: payload.company_id === 'global' ? '' : (payload.company_id || '')
  };
  if (payload.role_type_id !== undefined) changes.role_type_id = payload.role_type_id || '';
  if (payload.is_active !== undefined) changes.is_active = isActiveFlag(payload.is_active);

  const result = updateRecordFields(SHEETS.ROLES, payload.id, changes);
  if (!result) {
    throw new Error('Role not found');
  }

  logAdminAction(getCurrentUserId(), 'UPDATE_ROLE', 'role', payload.id, changes);
  Logger.log(`Updated role: ${changes.name}`);
  return result.after;
}

/**
 * Deactivate a role (v9.0: soft delete; roles are never hard-deleted - A4).
 * Before v9.0 the deleteRole route called deactivateRole(), which never existed, so every delete failed.
 * @param {string} roleId - Role ID
 * @param {string} reason - Deactivation reason
 * @returns {Object} deactivateRecord() result ({ status, data, message })
 */
function deleteRole(roleId, reason = 'admin_request') {
  return deactivateRecord(SHEETS.ROLES, roleId, reason);
}

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * DROPDOWN LIST MANAGEMENT FUNCTIONS
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Dropdown lists with their options and company access.
 * v9.3: each list carries assignments (live dropdown_company_assignments) and availability
 * ({ mode: global | companies | draft | legacy, company_ids }); with company_id only the lists that company
 * can use are returned (a draft list - assignments removed - is admin-only and never returned for a company).
 * @param {Object} params - { company_id } (optional)
 * @returns {Array} dropdown lists
 */
function getDropdownLists(params = {}) {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    let listsSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_LISTS);

    if (!listsSheet) {
      listsSheet = initializeDropdownListsSheet();
      return [];
    }

    const lists = getSheetDataAsJSON(listsSheet);
    const assignmentTable = readTable(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_COMPANY_ASSIGNMENTS);
    const assignmentRows = assignmentTable ? assignmentTable.records : [];
    const companyId = (params && (params.company_id || params.companyId)) || null;

    // v9.4 (reco P3, A16): read dropdown_options once and group by list (it was one tab read per list)
    const optionsTable = readTable(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_OPTIONS);
    const optionsByList = groupBy(optionsTable ? optionsTable.records : [], o => String(o.dropdown_list_id));

    const listsWithOptions = lists
      .map(list => ({
        ...list,
        options: optionsByList[String(list.id)] || [],
        assignments: liveDropdownAssignments(list.id, assignmentRows.filter(liveRecord)),
        availability: dropdownAvailability(list, assignmentRows)
      }))
      .filter(list => !companyId || dropdownAvailableTo(list.availability, companyId));

    Logger.log(`Retrieved ${listsWithOptions.length} dropdown lists`);
    return listsWithOptions;
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error getting dropdown lists: ${error.message}`);
    throw new Error(`Failed to retrieve dropdown lists: ${error.message}`);
  }
}

function getDropdownOptions(listId) {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    let optionsSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_OPTIONS);

    if (!optionsSheet) {
      optionsSheet = initializeDropdownOptionsSheet();
      return [];
    }

    const allOptions = getSheetDataAsJSON(optionsSheet);
    // v9.4 (A8): compare as strings - a numeric cell never === a string id, so those lists showed no options
    const listOptions = allOptions.filter(opt => sameId(opt.dropdown_list_id, listId));

    return listOptions;
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error getting dropdown options for list ${listId}: ${error.message}`);
    return [];
  }
}

function createDropdownList(payload) {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    let listsSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_LISTS);
    let optionsSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_OPTIONS);

    if (!listsSheet) {
      listsSheet = initializeDropdownListsSheet();
    }
    if (!optionsSheet) {
      optionsSheet = initializeDropdownOptionsSheet();
    }

    // Validate payload exists
    if (!payload) {
      throw new Error('Payload is required');
    }

    if (!payload.name) {
      throw new Error('Dropdown list name is required');
    }

    // Handle multi-company assignment
    let companyId = payload.company_id || null;

    // If multiple companies are specified, store as JSON array
    if (Array.isArray(payload.company_ids) && payload.company_ids.length > 0) {
      companyId = JSON.stringify(payload.company_ids);
    } else if (Array.isArray(payload.company_id) && payload.company_id.length > 0) {
      companyId = JSON.stringify(payload.company_id);
    }

    const currentTime = new Date().toISOString();
    const currentUser = getCurrentUserId();

    const newList = {
      id: generateId('dd'),
      name: payload.name.trim(),
      description: payload.description ? payload.description.trim() : '',
      company_id: companyId,
      is_active: true,
      created_at: currentTime,
      created_by: currentUser,
      updated_at: currentTime,
      updated_by: currentUser,
      deactivated_at: null,
      deactivated_by: null,
      deactivation_reason: null
    };

    // ENHANCED SHEET WRITING WITH VERIFICATION
    Logger.log('About to write to sheet - newList data:', JSON.stringify(newList, null, 2));
    Logger.log('Sheet name:', listsSheet.getName());
    Logger.log('Sheet ID:', listsSheet.getSheetId());

    const rowToAppend = [
      newList.id,
      newList.name,
      newList.description,
      newList.company_id,
      newList.is_active,
      newList.created_at,
      newList.created_by,
      newList.updated_at,
      newList.updated_by,
      newList.deactivated_at,
      newList.deactivated_by,
      newList.deactivation_reason
    ];

    Logger.log('Row data to append:', JSON.stringify(rowToAppend, null, 2));

    const beforeRowCount = listsSheet.getLastRow();
    Logger.log('Row count before append:', beforeRowCount);

    // Write to sheet
    listsSheet.appendRow(rowToAppend);
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(listsSheet));

    const afterRowCount = listsSheet.getLastRow();
    Logger.log('Row count after append:', afterRowCount);
    Logger.log('Row count difference:', afterRowCount - beforeRowCount);

    // Verify the write by reading back the last row
    if (afterRowCount > beforeRowCount) {
      const lastRow = listsSheet.getRange(afterRowCount, 1, 1, 12).getValues()[0];
      Logger.log('Verification - last row written:', JSON.stringify(lastRow, null, 2));
    } else {
      Logger.error('ERROR: Row count did not increase after appendRow!');
    }

    // Add options if provided
    if (payload.options && Array.isArray(payload.options)) {
      Logger.log('Adding options:', payload.options.length);
      payload.options.forEach((option, index) => {
        if (option.label && option.value) {
          const optionRow = [
            generateId('opt'),
            newList.id,
            option.label.trim(),
            option.value.trim(),
            option.parent_option_id || ''
          ];
          Logger.log(`Adding option ${index + 1}:`, JSON.stringify(optionRow, null, 2));
          optionsSheet.appendRow(optionRow);
          invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(optionsSheet));
        }
      });
    }

    // Log admin action
    try {
      logAdminAction('CREATE_DROPDOWN_LIST', newList.id, newList);
      Logger.log('Admin action logged successfully');
    } catch (logError) {
      Logger.error('Error logging admin action:', logError.message);
    }

    Logger.log('=== SHEET OPERATIONS COMPLETE ===');
    Logger.log(`Created dropdown list: ${newList.name}`);
    return newList;
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error creating dropdown list: ${error.message}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

function updateDropdownList(payload) {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    const listsSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_LISTS);
    const optionsSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_OPTIONS);

    // Validate payload exists
    if (!payload) {
      throw new Error('Payload is required');
    }

    if (!payload.id || !payload.name) {
      throw new Error('Dropdown list ID and name are required');
    }

    // Update the list
    const listData = sheetValues(listsSheet);
    let listRowIndex = -1;

    for (let i = 1; i < listData.length; i++) {
      if (listData[i][0] === payload.id) {
        listRowIndex = i + 1;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (listRowIndex === -1) {
      throw new Error('Dropdown list not found');
    }

    const currentTime = new Date().toISOString();
    const currentUser = getCurrentUserId();

    const updatedList = {
      id: payload.id,
      name: payload.name.trim(),
      description: payload.description ? payload.description.trim() : (listData[listRowIndex - 1][2] || ''),
      company_id: payload.company_id !== undefined ? payload.company_id : listData[listRowIndex - 1][3],
      is_active: listData[listRowIndex - 1][4] !== undefined ? listData[listRowIndex - 1][4] : true,
      created_at: listData[listRowIndex - 1][5] || currentTime,
      created_by: listData[listRowIndex - 1][6] || currentUser,
      updated_at: currentTime,
      updated_by: currentUser,
      deactivated_at: listData[listRowIndex - 1][9] || null,
      deactivated_by: listData[listRowIndex - 1][10] || null,
      deactivation_reason: listData[listRowIndex - 1][11] || null
    };

    listsSheet.getRange(listRowIndex, 1, 1, 12).setValues([[
      updatedList.id,
      updatedList.name,
      updatedList.description,
      updatedList.company_id,
      updatedList.is_active,
      updatedList.created_at,
      updatedList.created_by,
      updatedList.updated_at,
      updatedList.updated_by,
      updatedList.deactivated_at,
      updatedList.deactivated_by,
      updatedList.deactivation_reason
    ]]);
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(listsSheet));

    // Delete existing options
    const optionsData = sheetValues(optionsSheet);
    for (let i = optionsData.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      if (optionsData[i][1] === payload.id) {
        optionsSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
        invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(optionsSheet));
      }
    }

    // Add new options
    if (payload.options && Array.isArray(payload.options)) {
      payload.options.forEach(option => {
        if (option.label && option.value) {
          optionsSheet.appendRow([
            generateId('opt'),
            payload.id,
            option.label.trim(),
            option.value.trim(),
            option.parentValue || ''
          ]);
          invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(optionsSheet));
        }
      });
    }

    logAdminAction('UPDATE_DROPDOWN_LIST', payload.id, updatedList);

    Logger.log(`Updated dropdown list: ${updatedList.name}`);
    return updatedList;
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error updating dropdown list: ${error.message}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

function deleteDropdownList(listId) {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    const listsSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_LISTS);
    const optionsSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_OPTIONS);

    // Find and delete the list
    const listData = sheetValues(listsSheet);
    let listRowIndex = -1;
    let deletedListData = null;

    for (let i = 1; i < listData.length; i++) {
      if (listData[i][0] === listId) {
        listRowIndex = i + 1;
        deletedListData = listData[i];
        break;
      }
    }

    if (listRowIndex === -1) {
      throw new Error('Dropdown list not found');
    }

    // Delete options first
    const optionsData = sheetValues(optionsSheet);
    for (let i = optionsData.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      if (optionsData[i][1] === listId) {
        optionsSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
        invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(optionsSheet));
      }
    }

    // Delete the list
    listsSheet.deleteRow(listRowIndex);
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(listsSheet));

    logAdminAction('DELETE_DROPDOWN_LIST', listId, { deletedList: deletedListData });

    Logger.log(`Deleted dropdown list: ${deletedListData[1]}`);
    return { message: 'Dropdown list deleted successfully' };
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error deleting dropdown list: ${error.message}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * TICKET TYPE MANAGEMENT FUNCTIONS
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Get ticket types, optionally for one company (its own types plus global ones with a blank company_id).
 * v9.1: accepts company_id or companyId and compares ids as strings; before, the filter kept only types
 * whose company_id was exactly null, which a sheet cell never is, so global types disappeared.
 * @param {Object} params - { company_id | companyId }
 * @returns {Array} Ticket type records
 */
function getTicketTypes(params = {}) {
  try {
    const table = readTable(SHEETS.TICKET_TYPES);
    if (!table) {
      initializeTicketTypesSheet();
      return [];
    }

    const companyId = params.company_id || params.companyId || null;
    const ticketTypes = table.records.filter(tt => !companyId || !tt.company_id || sameId(tt.company_id, companyId));

    Logger.log(`Retrieved ${ticketTypes.length} ticket types`);
    return ticketTypes;
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error getting ticket types: ${error.message}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

function createTicketType(payload) {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    let ticketTypesSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.TICKET_TYPES);

    if (!ticketTypesSheet) {
      ticketTypesSheet = initializeTicketTypesSheet();
    }

    // Validate payload exists
    if (!payload) {
      throw new Error('Payload is required');
    }

    if (!payload.name || !payload.code) {
      throw new Error('Ticket type name and code are required');
    }

    const newTicketType = {
      id: generateId('tt'),
      transaction_id: payload.transaction_id || generateId('tr'),
      code: payload.code.toUpperCase().trim(),
      name: payload.name.trim(),
      description: payload.description || '',
      is_active: payload.is_active !== false,
      require_attachment_on_create: payload.require_attachment_on_create || false,
      company_id: payload.company_id === 'global' ? null : payload.company_id,
      created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    ticketTypesSheet.appendRow([
      newTicketType.id,
      newTicketType.transaction_id,
      newTicketType.code,
      newTicketType.name,
      newTicketType.description,
      newTicketType.is_active,
      newTicketType.require_attachment_on_create,
      newTicketType.company_id || '',
      newTicketType.created_at,
      newTicketType.updated_at
    ]);
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(ticketTypesSheet));

    logAdminAction('CREATE_TICKET_TYPE', newTicketType.id, newTicketType);

    Logger.log(`Created ticket type: ${newTicketType.name}`);
    return newTicketType;
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error creating ticket type: ${error.message}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

function updateTicketType(payload) {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    const ticketTypesSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.TICKET_TYPES);

    // Validate payload exists
    if (!payload) {
      throw new Error('Payload is required');
    }

    if (!payload.id || !payload.name || !payload.code) {
      throw new Error('Ticket type ID, name, and code are required');
    }

    // Find and update the ticket type
    const data = sheetValues(ticketTypesSheet);
    let rowIndex = -1;

    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (data[i][0] === payload.id) {
        rowIndex = i + 1;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (rowIndex === -1) {
      throw new Error('Ticket type not found');
    }

    const updatedTicketType = {
      id: payload.id,
      transaction_id: payload.transaction_id || data[rowIndex - 1][1],
      code: payload.code.toUpperCase().trim(),
      name: payload.name.trim(),
      description: payload.description || '',
      is_active: payload.is_active !== false,
      require_attachment_on_create: payload.require_attachment_on_create || false,
      company_id: payload.company_id === 'global' ? null : payload.company_id,
      created_at: data[rowIndex - 1][8],
      updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    ticketTypesSheet.getRange(rowIndex, 1, 1, 10).setValues([[
      updatedTicketType.id,
      updatedTicketType.transaction_id,
      updatedTicketType.code,
      updatedTicketType.name,
      updatedTicketType.description,
      updatedTicketType.is_active,
      updatedTicketType.require_attachment_on_create,
      updatedTicketType.company_id || '',
      updatedTicketType.created_at,
      updatedTicketType.updated_at
    ]]);
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(ticketTypesSheet));

    logAdminAction('UPDATE_TICKET_TYPE', payload.id, updatedTicketType);

    Logger.log(`Updated ticket type: ${updatedTicketType.name}`);
    return updatedTicketType;
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error updating ticket type: ${error.message}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

function deleteTicketType(ticketTypeId) {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    const ticketTypesSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.TICKET_TYPES);

    const data = sheetValues(ticketTypesSheet);
    let rowIndex = -1;
    let ticketTypeData = null;

    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (data[i][0] === ticketTypeId) {
        rowIndex = i + 1;
        ticketTypeData = data[i];
        break;
      }
    }

    if (rowIndex === -1) {
      throw new Error('Ticket type not found');
    }

    ticketTypesSheet.deleteRow(rowIndex);
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(ticketTypesSheet));
    logAdminAction('DELETE_TICKET_TYPE', ticketTypeId, { deletedTicketType: ticketTypeData });

    Logger.log(`Deleted ticket type: ${ticketTypeData[3]}`);
    return { message: 'Ticket type deleted successfully' };
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error deleting ticket type: ${error.message}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * USER PREFERENCES FUNCTIONS
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Retrieves user preferences by user ID.
 * @param {string} userId - The user's ID.
 * @returns {object} - The user's preferences or default preferences.
 */
function getUserPreferences(userId) {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    // v9.4 (A10): getSheetByName returns null for a missing tab - it never threw, so the catch never ran
    const preferencesSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.USER_PREFERENCES) || initializeUserPreferencesSheet();

    const data = sheetValues(preferencesSheet);

    // Look for existing user preferences
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (data[i][0] === userId) {
        // Found existing preferences, return them
        return {
          user_id: data[i][0],
          dark_mode: data[i][1] || false,
          timezone: data[i][2] || 'Asia/Manila',
          language: data[i][3] || 'en',
          email_notifications: data[i][4] !== false, // Default to true
          desktop_notifications: data[i][5] !== false, // Default to true
          dashboard_layout: data[i][6] || 'grid',
          items_per_page: data[i][7] || 20,
          auto_refresh: data[i][8] !== false, // Default to true
          refresh_interval: data[i][9] || 30000,
          created_at: data[i][10],
          updated_at: data[i][11]
        };
      }
    }

    // If no preferences found, return default preferences
    const defaultPreferences = {
      user_id: userId,
      dark_mode: false,
      timezone: 'Asia/Manila',
      language: 'en',
      email_notifications: true,
      desktop_notifications: true,
      dashboard_layout: 'grid',
      items_per_page: 20,
      auto_refresh: true,
      refresh_interval: 30000,
      created_at: null,
      updated_at: null
    };

    Logger.log(`No existing preferences found for user ${userId}, returning defaults`);
    return defaultPreferences;

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error getting user preferences: ${error.message}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

/**
 * Updates or creates user preferences.
 * @param {string} userId - The user's ID.
 * @param {object} preferences - The preferences to update.
 * @returns {object} - The updated preferences.
 */
function updateUserPreferences(userId, preferences) {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    // v9.4 (A10): getSheetByName returns null for a missing tab - it never threw, so the catch never ran
    const preferencesSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.USER_PREFERENCES) || initializeUserPreferencesSheet();

    const data = sheetValues(preferencesSheet);
    let rowIndex = -1;
    let existingPrefs = null;

    // Look for existing user preferences
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (data[i][0] === userId) {
        rowIndex = i + 1; // Sheet rows are 1-indexed
        existingPrefs = {
          dark_mode: data[i][1] || false,
          timezone: data[i][2] || 'Asia/Manila',
          language: data[i][3] || 'en',
          email_notifications: data[i][4] !== false,
          desktop_notifications: data[i][5] !== false,
          dashboard_layout: data[i][6] || 'grid',
          items_per_page: data[i][7] || 20,
          auto_refresh: data[i][8] !== false,
          refresh_interval: data[i][9] || 30000,
          created_at: data[i][10],
          updated_at: data[i][11]
        };
        break;
      }
    }

    const now = new Date();
    const updatedPrefs = {
      user_id: userId,
      dark_mode: preferences.dark_mode !== undefined ? preferences.dark_mode : (existingPrefs?.dark_mode || false),
      timezone: preferences.timezone || existingPrefs?.timezone || 'Asia/Manila',
      language: preferences.language || existingPrefs?.language || 'en',
      email_notifications: preferences.email_notifications !== undefined ? preferences.email_notifications : (existingPrefs?.email_notifications !== false),
      desktop_notifications: preferences.desktop_notifications !== undefined ? preferences.desktop_notifications : (existingPrefs?.desktop_notifications !== false),
      dashboard_layout: preferences.dashboard_layout || existingPrefs?.dashboard_layout || 'grid',
      items_per_page: preferences.items_per_page || existingPrefs?.items_per_page || 20,
      auto_refresh: preferences.auto_refresh !== undefined ? preferences.auto_refresh : (existingPrefs?.auto_refresh !== false),
      refresh_interval: preferences.refresh_interval || existingPrefs?.refresh_interval || 30000,
      created_at: existingPrefs?.created_at || now,
      updated_at: now
    };

    const rowData = [
      updatedPrefs.user_id,
      updatedPrefs.dark_mode,
      updatedPrefs.timezone,
      updatedPrefs.language,
      updatedPrefs.email_notifications,
      updatedPrefs.desktop_notifications,
      updatedPrefs.dashboard_layout,
      updatedPrefs.items_per_page,
      updatedPrefs.auto_refresh,
      updatedPrefs.refresh_interval,
      updatedPrefs.created_at,
      updatedPrefs.updated_at
    ];

    if (rowIndex > -1) {
      // Update existing row
      preferencesSheet.getRange(rowIndex, 1, 1, rowData.length).setValues([rowData]);
      invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(preferencesSheet));
      Logger.log(`Updated preferences for user ${userId}`);
    } else {
      // Create new row
      preferencesSheet.appendRow(rowData);
      invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(preferencesSheet));
      Logger.log(`Created new preferences for user ${userId}`);
    }

    // Log the admin action
    logAdminAction('UPDATE_USER_PREFERENCES', userId, {
      updatedFields: Object.keys(preferences),
      preferences: updatedPrefs
    });

    return updatedPrefs;

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error updating user preferences: ${error.message}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

/**
 * Initializes the user_preferences sheet with proper headers.
 * @returns {Sheet} - The initialized preferences sheet.
 */
function initializeUserPreferencesSheet() {
  // v8.3 (CR-04/CR-05): headers from TABLE_SCHEMAS; never clears a tab that already has data
  return ensureTableSheet(SHEETS.USER_PREFERENCES);
}

/**
 * Records a successful user login event.
 * @param {object} payload - Contains the user's ID and email.
 * @returns {object} - A success message.
 */
function logLogin(payload) {
  // v9.4 (A1, A18, A15): header-driven row, generateId id (was the row count), the verified user
  const userId = actorId(payload.userId);
  appendRecord(SHEETS.ADMIN_ACTION_LOGS, {
    id: generateId('log'),
    admin_user_id: userId,
    action_type: 'USER_LOGIN',
    target_entity: 'USER',
    target_id: userId,
    details: JSON.stringify({ email: payload.email, ipAddress: payload.ipAddress || 'N/A' }),
    timestamp: new Date()
  });
  return { message: "Login recorded." };
}

/**
 * Records a user-facing action in the ticket's history.
 * @param {number} ticketId - The ID of the ticket.
 * @param {string} userId - The ID of the user performing the action.
 * @param {string} action - The name of the action (e.g., 'Approved').
 * @param {string} comment - The user's comment.
 */
function logTicketHistory(ticketId, userId, action, comment) {
  // v9.4 (A1, A18): header-driven row with a generateId id (the row count repeated after a deleted row)
  appendRecord(SHEETS.TICKET_HISTORY, {
    id: generateId('th'),
    ticket_id: ticketId,
    user_id: userId,
    action: action,
    comment: comment,
    timestamp: new Date()
  });
}

/**
 * Records a detailed, system-level change for a ticket.
 * @param {number} ticketId - The ID of the ticket.
 * @param {string} userId - The ID of the user performing the action.
 * @param {string} actionType - A system-level action name (e.g., 'STATUS_CHANGE').
 * @param {object} details - A JSON object describing the change.
 */
function logTicketAction(ticketId, userId, actionType, details) {
  // v9.4 (A1, A18): header-driven row with a generateId id (the row count repeated after a deleted row)
  appendRecord(SHEETS.TICKET_ACTION_LOGS, {
    id: generateId('tal'),
    ticket_id: ticketId,
    user_id: userId,
    action_type: actionType,
    details: JSON.stringify(details),
    timestamp: new Date()
  });
}

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * UTILITY & HELPER FUNCTIONS
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Generates a unique, formatted ticket number.
 * Uses LockService to prevent race conditions when multiple users create tickets simultaneously.
 * @param {string} companyCode - The short code for the company.
 * @param {string} typeCode - The short code for the ticket type.
 * @returns {string} - The formatted ticket number.
 */
function generateTicketNumber(companyCode, typeCode) {
  // v8.3 (CR-03): shared re-entrant lock - a locked doPost write already holds it
  return withScriptLock(() => {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    const countersSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.SEQUENCE_COUNTERS);
    const data = sheetValues(countersSheet);
    const headers = data.shift();

    const sequenceName = `${companyCode}-${typeCode}`;
    const year = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), "Asia/Manila", "yyyy");

    let currentNumber = 0;
    let rowIndex = -1;

    for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
        if (data[i][0] === sequenceName) {
            currentNumber = parseInt(data[i][1], 10);
            rowIndex = i + 2; // +1 for 1-based index, +1 for header
            break;
        }
    }

    const nextNumber = currentNumber + 1;

    if (nextNumber > 99999999) {
        // In a real system, you might throw an error or handle the reset differently.
        // For now, we'll just log it.
        Logger.log(`Sequence ${sequenceName} has exceeded 99,999,999.`);
    }

    // Update the sheet with the new number
    if (rowIndex > -1) {
        countersSheet.getRange(rowIndex, 2).setValue(nextNumber);
        invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(countersSheet));
    } else {
        countersSheet.appendRow([sequenceName, nextNumber]);
        invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(countersSheet));
    }

    // Pad the number with leading zeros to 8 digits
    const paddedNumber = String(nextNumber).padStart(8, '0');

    return `${sequenceName}-${year}-${paddedNumber}`;
  });
}

/**
 * Next value of a sequence_counters row, under the script lock (v9.4, A18). The row is created on first use,
 * continuing after seed() (e.g. the highest existing id), so ids never repeat after a deleted row.
 * @param {string} sequenceName - sequence_counters.sequence_name
 * @param {Function} seed - Returns the last value already used, for the first call (optional)
 * @returns {number} the new value
 */
function nextSequenceValue(sequenceName, seed) {
  return withScriptLock(() => {
    const sheet = ss().getSheetByName(SHEETS.SEQUENCE_COUNTERS) || ensureTableSheet(SHEETS.SEQUENCE_COUNTERS);
    const data = sheetValues(sheet);
    const headers = (data[0] || []).map(h => String(h).trim());
    const nameCol = Math.max(headers.indexOf('sequence_name'), 0);
    const valueCol = headers.indexOf('last_number') === -1 ? 1 : headers.indexOf('last_number');
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][nameCol]) === sequenceName) {
        const next = (parseInt(data[i][valueCol], 10) || 0) + 1;
        sheet.getRange(i + 1, valueCol + 1).setValue(next);
        invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
        return next;
      }
    }
    const next = (Number(seed ? seed() : 0) || 0) + 1;
    sheet.appendRow(rowFromRecord(sheetHeaders(sheet), { sequence_name: sequenceName, last_number: next }));
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
    return next;
  });
}

/** The highest numeric ticket id so far: seeds the 'ticket_id' sequence once (v9.4). */
function highestTicketId() {
  const tickets = readTable(SHEETS.TICKETS);
  return (tickets ? tickets.records : []).reduce((max, t) => Math.max(max, Number(t.id) || 0), 0);
}

/**
 * True while this execution holds the script lock (Apps Script globals live for one execution).
 */
let scriptLockHeld = false;

/**
 * Run fn while holding the script lock (v8.3, CR-03).
 * doPost writes, ticket numbering and rebuildAllTablesApply() all use it, so the rebuild can't
 * rewrite a tab while a write is in flight. Re-entrant: a nested call runs fn without re-locking.
 * @param {Function} fn - Work to run
 * @param {number} timeoutMs - How long to wait for the lock before throwing (default 30s)
 * @returns {*} fn's return value
 */
function withScriptLock(fn, timeoutMs = 30000) {
  if (scriptLockHeld) {
    return fn();
  }

  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(timeoutMs);
  scriptLockHeld = true;
  try {
    return fn();
  } finally {
    scriptLockHeld = false;
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

/**
 * Converts a Google Sheet's data into an array of JSON objects.
 * @param {Sheet} sheet - The Google Sheet object.
 * @returns {Array} - An array of objects representing the sheet data.
 */
/**
 * Converts a Google Sheet's data into an array of JSON objects.
 * @param {Sheet} sheet - The Google Sheet object.
 * @returns {Array} - An array of objects representing the sheet data.
 */
function getSheetDataAsJSON(sheet) {
  if (!sheet) {
    Logger.log('Warning: getSheetDataAsJSON called with null/undefined sheet');
    return [];
  }

  try {
    const data = sheetValues(sheet);
    if (data.length < 2) return []; // No data beyond headers

    const headers = data.shift();
    return data.map(row => {
      let obj = {};
      headers.forEach((header, i) => {
        obj[header] = row[i] !== undefined ? row[i] : null;
      });
      return obj;
    });
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error in getSheetDataAsJSON: ${error.message}`);
    return [];
  }
}

/**
 * Creates a standard JSON response with proper error handling.
 * Google Apps Script Web Apps handle CORS automatically when deployed with "Anyone" access.
 * @param {object} response - The data to be stringified.
 * @returns {ContentService.TextOutput} - The final JSON response object.
 */
function createJsonResponse(response) {
  try {
    // Google Apps Script handles CORS automatically for Web Apps deployed with "Anyone" access
    const output = ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify(response))
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);

    return output;
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('createJsonResponse Error:', error);
    // Fallback response
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
      success: false,
      error: 'Response creation failed',
      details: error.toString()
    }))
    .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
  }
}

/**
 * JSON response for a handler that reports failure in its return value instead of throwing (v8.3, CR-07).
 * { status: 'error' } or { success: false } -> success: false plus a top-level status: 'error',
 * which makes the frontend's makeRequest() throw (src/api/googleSheet.js).
 * @param {Object} result - Handler return value
 * @returns {ContentService.TextOutput} JSON response
 */
function createHandlerResponse(result) {
  const failed = result && (result.status === 'error' || result.success === false);
  if (failed) {
    return createJsonResponse({
      success: false,
      status: 'error',
      error: result.message || result.error || (result.errors || []).join('; ') || 'Request failed',
      // v9.6: a handler's code (ADMIN_ONLY, ...) at the top level, where the frontend reads it
      ...(result.code ? { code: result.code } : {}),
      data: result
    });
  }
  return createJsonResponse({ success: true, data: result });
}

/**
 * Build a sheet row from a record by header name (v8.3, CR-01).
 * Unlike positional arrays it can't misalign when TABLE_SCHEMAS adds or moves a column.
 * @param {string[]} headers - The sheet's header row
 * @param {Object} record - Values keyed by header name (undefined/null -> '')
 * @returns {Array} Row values in header order
 */
function rowFromRecord(headers, record) {
  return headers.map(header => (record[header] === undefined || record[header] === null ? '' : record[header]));
}

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * UTILITY FUNCTIONS
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Generates a unique ID with the given prefix.
 * @param {string} prefix - The prefix for the ID.
 * @returns {string} - A unique identifier string.
 */
function generateId(prefix) {
  return `${prefix}_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 11)}`;
}

/**
 * Append an admin_action_logs row.
 * v8.3 (CR-06): this file calls it two ways, and both are accepted:
 *   logAdminAction(actionType, targetId, details)                         - 18 callers
 *   logAdminAction(userId, actionType, targetEntity, targetId, details)   - 26 callers (Phase 1B+, 10, 11)
 * Before v8.3 the 5-arg calls logged the user id as action_type and dropped the record id.
 * v9.6: written by header (A1, was positional) and sets ADMIN_LOGGED for the generic admin-log row (routeAdminAction).
 */
function logAdminAction(...args) {
  const [userId, actionType, targetEntity, targetId, details] = args.length >= 5
    ? args
    : [null, args[0], 'ENTITY', args[1], args[2]];

  try {
    appendRecord(SHEETS.ADMIN_ACTION_LOGS, {
      id: generateId('log'),
      admin_user_id: userId || Session.getActiveUser().getEmail() || 'system',
      action_type: actionType,
      target_entity: targetEntity,
      target_id: targetId,
      details: JSON.stringify(details),
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
    });
    ADMIN_LOGGED = true;
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error logging admin action: ${error.message}`);
    // Don't throw - logging failure shouldn't break the main operation
  }
}

function pingAPI() {
  return {
    status: 'API is running',
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    spreadsheetId: SPREADSHEET_ID,
    version: '6.4',
    availableEndpoints: [
      'getCompanies', 'createCompany', 'updateCompany', 'deleteCompany',
      'getRoles', 'createRole', 'updateRole', 'deleteRole',
      'getDropdownLists', 'createDropdownList', 'updateDropdownList', 'deleteDropdownList',
      'getTicketTypes', 'createTicketType', 'updateTicketType', 'deleteTicketType',
      'getTickets', 'createTicket', 'recordLogin'
    ]
  };
}

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * RUNTIME ERROR VERIFICATION SUITE
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Comprehensive runtime error verification for all Apps Script functions.
 * This function tests each function individually to ensure no runtime errors.
 */
function verifyAllFunctionsRuntimeSafety() {
  Logger.log('=== STARTING RUNTIME SAFETY VERIFICATION ===');

  const results = {
    totalFunctions: 0,
    passedFunctions: 0,
    failedFunctions: 0,
    errors: []
  };

  // Test Core API Functions
  try {
    Logger.log('Testing Core API Functions...');

    // Test doGet with valid parameters
    const pingTest = doGet({ parameter: { action: 'ping' } });
    if (pingTest) results.passedFunctions++; else results.failedFunctions++;
    results.totalFunctions++;

    // Test doPost with valid parameters
    const postTest = doPost({
      postData: { contents: JSON.stringify({ action: 'ping' }) }
    });
    if (postTest) results.passedFunctions++; else results.failedFunctions++;
    results.totalFunctions++;

    // Test doOptions
    const optionsTest = doOptions({});
    if (optionsTest) results.passedFunctions++; else results.failedFunctions++;
    results.totalFunctions++;

    Logger.log('Core API Functions: ✅ PASSED');
  } catch (error) {
    results.errors.push(`Core API Functions: ${error.message}`);
    results.failedFunctions++;
  }

  // Test Utility Functions
  try {
    Logger.log('Testing Utility Functions...');

    // Test generateId
    const idTest = generateId('test');
    if (idTest && idTest.includes('test_')) results.passedFunctions++; else results.failedFunctions++;
    results.totalFunctions++;

    // Test createJsonResponse
    const jsonTest = createJsonResponse({ test: 'data' });
    if (jsonTest) results.passedFunctions++; else results.failedFunctions++;
    results.totalFunctions++;

    // Test pingAPI
    const pingApiTest = pingAPI();
    if (pingApiTest && pingApiTest.status) results.passedFunctions++; else results.failedFunctions++;
    results.totalFunctions++;

    Logger.log('Utility Functions: ✅ PASSED');
  } catch (error) {
    results.errors.push(`Utility Functions: ${error.message}`);
    results.failedFunctions++;
  }

  // Test CRUD Functions (with mock parameters)
  try {
    Logger.log('Testing CRUD Function Structure...');

    // Note: These tests check function structure without actually modifying data
    const crudFunctions = [
      'getCompanies', 'createCompany', 'updateCompany', 'deleteCompany',
      'getRoles', 'createRole', 'updateRole', 'deleteRole',
      'getDropdownLists', 'createDropdownList', 'updateDropdownList', 'deleteDropdownList',
      'getTicketTypes', 'createTicketType', 'updateTicketType', 'deleteTicketType'
    ];

    // Check if functions exist and are callable
    crudFunctions.forEach(funcName => {
      try {
        if (typeof eval(funcName) === 'function') {
          results.passedFunctions++;
          Logger.log(`${funcName}: ✅ Function exists`);
        } else {
          results.failedFunctions++;
          results.errors.push(`${funcName}: Function not found`);
        }
        results.totalFunctions++;
      } catch (error) {
        results.failedFunctions++;
        results.errors.push(`${funcName}: ${error.message}`);
        results.totalFunctions++;
      }
    });

    Logger.log('CRUD Functions Structure: ✅ PASSED');
  } catch (error) {
    results.errors.push(`CRUD Functions: ${error.message}`);
  }

  // Test Sheet Initialization Functions
  try {
    Logger.log('Testing Sheet Initialization Functions...');

    const initFunctions = [
      'initializeCompaniesSheet', 'initializeRolesSheet', 'initializeDropdownListsSheet',
      'initializeDropdownOptionsSheet', 'initializeTicketTypesSheet', 'initializeAdminActionLogsSheet'
    ];

    initFunctions.forEach(funcName => {
      try {
        if (typeof eval(funcName) === 'function') {
          results.passedFunctions++;
          Logger.log(`${funcName}: ✅ Function exists`);
        } else {
          results.failedFunctions++;
          results.errors.push(`${funcName}: Function not found`);
        }
        results.totalFunctions++;
      } catch (error) {
        results.failedFunctions++;
        results.errors.push(`${funcName}: ${error.message}`);
        results.totalFunctions++;
      }
    });

    Logger.log('Sheet Initialization Functions: ✅ PASSED');
  } catch (error) {
    results.errors.push(`Sheet Initialization Functions: ${error.message}`);
  }

  // Generate Final Report
  Logger.log('=== RUNTIME SAFETY VERIFICATION COMPLETE ===');
  Logger.log(`Total Functions Tested: ${results.totalFunctions}`);
  Logger.log(`Passed: ${results.passedFunctions}`);
  Logger.log(`Failed: ${results.failedFunctions}`);
  Logger.log(`Success Rate: ${Math.round((results.passedFunctions / results.totalFunctions) * 100)}%`);

  if (results.errors.length > 0) {
    Logger.log('=== ERRORS FOUND ===');
    results.errors.forEach(error => Logger.log(`❌ ${error}`));
  } else {
    Logger.log('🎉 ALL FUNCTIONS PASSED RUNTIME SAFETY VERIFICATION');
  }

  return results;
}

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * DIAGNOSTIC FUNCTIONS FOR SCHEMA DEBUGGING
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Diagnostic function to check dropdown_lists sheet structure and data mapping
 */
function diagnoseDropdownListsSheet() {
  try {
    Logger.log("=== DROPDOWN LISTS SHEET DIAGNOSIS ===");

    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    let listsSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_LISTS);

    if (!listsSheet) {
      Logger.log("Sheet does not exist, creating...");
      listsSheet = initializeDropdownListsSheet();
    }

    // Get all data including headers
    const allData = sheetValues(listsSheet);
    Logger.log(`Total rows: ${allData.length}`);

    if (allData.length === 0) {
      Logger.log("No data in sheet");
      return;
    }

    // Log headers
    const headers = allData[0];
    Logger.log(`Headers (${headers.length} columns): ${JSON.stringify(headers)}`);

    // Expected headers
    const expectedHeaders = ['id', 'name', 'description', 'company_id', 'is_active', 'created_at', 'created_by', 'updated_at', 'updated_by', 'deactivated_at', 'deactivated_by', 'deactivation_reason'];
    Logger.log(`Expected (${expectedHeaders.length} columns): ${JSON.stringify(expectedHeaders)}`);

    // Compare headers
    const headerMatch = JSON.stringify(headers) === JSON.stringify(expectedHeaders);
    Logger.log(`Headers match expected: ${headerMatch}`);

    if (!headerMatch) {
      Logger.log("Header differences:");
      for (let i = 0; i < Math.max(headers.length, expectedHeaders.length); i++) {
        if (headers[i] !== expectedHeaders[i]) {
          Logger.log(`  Position ${i}: Got '${headers[i]}', Expected '${expectedHeaders[i]}'`);
        }
      }
    }

    // Log sample data
    if (allData.length > 1) {
      Logger.log(`Sample data rows: ${allData.length - 1}`);
      for (let i = 1; i <= Math.min(3, allData.length - 1); i++) {
        const row = allData[i];
        Logger.log(`Row ${i}: ${JSON.stringify(row)}`);

        // Map row to object manually
        const obj = {};
        headers.forEach((header, index) => {
          obj[header] = row[index];
        });
        Logger.log(`Row ${i} as object: ${JSON.stringify(obj)}`);
      }
    }

    // Test getSheetDataAsJSON function
    Logger.log("Testing getSheetDataAsJSON function:");
    const jsonData = getSheetDataAsJSON(listsSheet);
    Logger.log(`getSheetDataAsJSON returned ${jsonData.length} objects`);
    if (jsonData.length > 0) {
      Logger.log(`First object: ${JSON.stringify(jsonData[0])}`);
      Logger.log(`Object keys: ${Object.keys(jsonData[0])}`);
    }

    return {
      totalRows: allData.length,
      headers: headers,
      expectedHeaders: expectedHeaders,
      headerMatch: headerMatch,
      sampleData: jsonData.slice(0, 3)
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error in diagnoseDropdownListsSheet: ${error.message}`);
    Logger.log(`Stack: ${error.stack}`);
    return { error: error.message };
  }
}

/**
 * Migration function to convert old 4-column dropdown data to new 12-column format
 */
function migrateDropdownListsTo12Columns() {
  try {
    Logger.log("=== MIGRATING DROPDOWN LISTS TO 12 COLUMNS ===");

    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    let listsSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_LISTS);

    if (!listsSheet) {
      Logger.log("No dropdown_lists sheet found - nothing to migrate");
      return { success: true, message: "No sheet to migrate" };
    }

    // Get all data
    const allData = sheetValues(listsSheet);
    if (allData.length <= 1) {
      Logger.log("No data rows to migrate");
      return { success: true, message: "No data to migrate" };
    }

    const headers = allData[0];
    Logger.log(`Current headers: ${JSON.stringify(headers)}`);

    // Check if migration is needed
    const expectedHeaders = ['id', 'name', 'description', 'company_id', 'is_active', 'created_at', 'created_by', 'updated_at', 'updated_by', 'deactivated_at', 'deactivated_by', 'deactivation_reason'];

    if (headers.length === 12 && JSON.stringify(headers) === JSON.stringify(expectedHeaders)) {
      Logger.log("Headers are already correct - checking data format");

      // Check if data needs migration (look for old 4-column format)
      let needsMigration = false;
      for (let i = 1; i < allData.length; i++) {
        const row = allData[i];
        // Old format: data in positions 0,1,2,3 and empty in positions 4-11
        // New format: data properly distributed across all 12 columns
        if (row.length >= 12 && row[2] && row[3] && !row[4] && !row[5]) {
          // Check if positions 2,3 contain timestamps (old format)
          const col2 = String(row[2]);
          const col3 = String(row[3]);
          if (col2.includes('T') && col2.includes('Z') && col3.includes('T') && col3.includes('Z')) {
            needsMigration = true;
            break;
          }
        }
      }

      if (!needsMigration) {
        Logger.log("Data format is already correct - no migration needed");
        return { success: true, message: "Data already in correct format" };
      }
    }

    Logger.log(`Migrating ${allData.length - 1} data rows...`);

    // Clear the sheet and rewrite with correct headers
    listsSheet.clear();
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(listsSheet));
    listsSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 12).setValues([expectedHeaders]);
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(listsSheet));
    listsSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 12).setFontWeight('bold');

    let migratedCount = 0;
    const currentTime = new Date().toISOString();
    const currentUser = "system_migration";

    // Migrate each data row
    for (let i = 1; i < allData.length; i++) {
      const oldRow = allData[i];

      if (oldRow.length >= 4 && oldRow[0] && oldRow[1]) {
        // Old format: [id, name, created_at, updated_at, ...]
        // New format: [id, name, description, company_id, is_active, created_at, created_by, updated_at, updated_by, deactivated_at, deactivated_by, deactivation_reason]

        const newRow = [
          oldRow[0],                    // id (keep same)
          oldRow[1],                    // name (keep same)
          "",                           // description (new field - empty)
          null,                         // company_id (new field - null for global)
          true,                         // is_active (new field - default true)
          oldRow[2] || currentTime,     // created_at (from old position 2)
          currentUser,                  // created_by (new field - system)
          oldRow[3] || currentTime,     // updated_at (from old position 3)
          currentUser,                  // updated_by (new field - system)
          null,                         // deactivated_at (new field - null)
          null,                         // deactivated_by (new field - null)
          null                          // deactivation_reason (new field - null)
        ];

        listsSheet.appendRow(newRow);
        invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(listsSheet));
        migratedCount++;
        Logger.log(`Migrated row ${i}: ${oldRow[0]} - ${oldRow[1]}`);
      }
    }

    Logger.log(`=== MIGRATION COMPLETE ===`);
    Logger.log(`Successfully migrated ${migratedCount} dropdown lists`);

    return {
      success: true,
      message: `Migrated ${migratedCount} rows`,
      migratedCount: migratedCount
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error in migrateDropdownListsTo12Columns: ${error.message}`);
    Logger.log(`Stack: ${error.stack}`);
    return { success: false, error: error.message };
  }
}

/**
 * CLEAN RESET: Clear all data and reinitialize with correct schemas
 * This is faster than migration for proof of concept
 */
function cleanResetAllTables() {
  try {
    Logger.log("=== CLEAN RESET: CLEARING ALL TABLES ===");

    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    const results = {
      success: true,
      tablesReset: [],
      errors: []
    };

    // Tables that need clean reset (only include tables with existing init functions)
    const tablesToReset = [
      { name: 'dropdown_lists', initFunction: 'initializeDropdownListsSheet' },
      { name: 'dropdown_options', initFunction: 'initializeDropdownOptionsSheet' },
      { name: 'companies', initFunction: 'initializeCompaniesSheet' },
      { name: 'roles', initFunction: 'initializeRolesSheet' },
      { name: 'ticket_types', initFunction: 'initializeTicketTypesSheet' },
      { name: 'admin_action_logs', initFunction: 'initializeAdminActionLogsSheet' },
      // Phase 10.0: User Management tables
      { name: 'user_profile_types', initFunction: 'initializeUserProfileTypesSheet' },
      { name: 'role_types', initFunction: 'initializeRoleTypesSheet' },
      { name: 'user_profiles', initFunction: 'initializeUserProfilesSheet' }
    ];

    tablesToReset.forEach(table => {
      try {
        Logger.log(`Resetting ${table.name}...`);

        const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(table.name);
        if (sheet) {
          // Delete the entire sheet
          ss.deleteSheet(sheet);
          invalidateTable();
          Logger.log(`  Deleted existing ${table.name} sheet`);
        }

        // Recreate with correct schema
        if (typeof eval(table.initFunction) === 'function') {
          eval(table.initFunction + '()');
          Logger.log(`  Recreated ${table.name} with correct schema`);
          results.tablesReset.push(table.name);
        } else {
          Logger.log(`  Warning: ${table.initFunction} not found`);
          results.errors.push(`${table.name}: Init function not found`);
        }

      } catch (error) {
        Logger.log(`  Error resetting ${table.name}: ${error.message}`);
        results.errors.push(`${table.name}: ${error.message}`);
      }
    });

    Logger.log(`=== CLEAN RESET COMPLETE ===`);
    Logger.log(`Tables reset: ${results.tablesReset.join(', ')}`);

    if (results.errors.length > 0) {
      Logger.log(`Errors: ${results.errors.join(', ')}`);
      results.success = false;
    }

    return results;

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error in cleanResetAllTables: ${error.message}`);
    return { success: false, error: error.message };
  }
}

/**
 * Create fresh test data after clean reset
 */
function createFreshTestData() {
  try {
    Logger.log("=== CREATING FRESH TEST DATA ===");

    const results = {
      success: true,
      created: [],
      errors: []
    };

    // Create test companies
    try {
      const company1 = createCompany({
        name: "Test Company A",
        code: "TCA"
      });
      const company2 = createCompany({
        name: "Test Company B",
        code: "TCB"
      });
      results.created.push(`Companies: ${company1.name}, ${company2.name}`);
      Logger.log("✅ Created test companies");
    } catch (error) {
      results.errors.push(`Companies: ${error.message}`);
    }

    // Create test roles
    try {
      const globalRole = createRole({
        name: "Admin",
        company_id: null  // Global role
      });
      results.created.push(`Roles: ${globalRole.name} (Global)`);
      Logger.log("✅ Created test roles");
    } catch (error) {
      results.errors.push(`Roles: ${error.message}`);
    }

    // Create test dropdown lists
    try {
      const dropdown1 = createDropdownList({
        name: "Priority Levels",
        description: "Task priority options",
        company_id: null,  // Global dropdown
        options: [
          { label: "High", value: "high" },
          { label: "Medium", value: "medium" },
          { label: "Low", value: "low" }
        ]
      });

      const dropdown2 = createDropdownList({
        name: "Status Types",
        description: "Request status options",
        company_id: null,  // Global dropdown
        options: [
          { label: "Open", value: "open" },
          { label: "In Progress", value: "progress" },
          { label: "Closed", value: "closed" }
        ]
      });

      results.created.push(`Dropdowns: ${dropdown1.name}, ${dropdown2.name}`);
      Logger.log("✅ Created test dropdowns with options");
    } catch (error) {
      results.errors.push(`Dropdowns: ${error.message}`);
    }

    Logger.log(`=== FRESH TEST DATA COMPLETE ===`);
    Logger.log(`Created: ${results.created.join(', ')}`);

    if (results.errors.length > 0) {
      Logger.log(`Errors: ${results.errors.join(', ')}`);
      results.success = false;
    }

    return results;

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error in createFreshTestData: ${error.message}`);
    return { success: false, error: error.message };
  }
}

/**
 * Complete reset and test data creation (one-click solution)
 */
function completeSystemReset() {
  Logger.log("=== COMPLETE SYSTEM RESET ===");

  const resetResult = cleanResetAllTables();
  if (!resetResult.success) {
    Logger.log("Reset failed, aborting test data creation");
    return resetResult;
  }

  const testDataResult = createFreshTestData();

  return {
    success: resetResult.success && testDataResult.success,
    reset: resetResult,
    testData: testDataResult,
    message: "System reset complete - ready for testing"
  };
}
