/**
 * Workflow steps for a ticket type / company, in sort order.
 * v9.1: each step carries its active SLA (sla_duration, sla_unit, exclude_weekends) so the builder's edit
 * form can show it.
 * @param {string} ticketTypeId - Ticket type filter (optional)
 * @param {string} companyId - Company filter (optional)
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: steps, message }
 */
function getWorkflowSteps(ticketTypeId = null, companyId = null) {
  try {
    const table = readTable(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS);
    if (!table) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Workflow steps sheet not found' };
    }

    const slaTable = readTable(SHEETS.STEP_SLAS);
    const slaByStep = {};
    (slaTable ? slaTable.records : [])
      .filter(sla => isActiveFlag(sla.is_active) && !sla.deleted_at)
      .forEach(sla => { slaByStep[String(sla.step_id)] = sla; });

    const steps = table.records
      .filter(step => isActiveFlag(step.is_active) && !step.deleted_at && !step.deactivated_at)
      .filter(step => !ticketTypeId || sameId(step.ticket_type_id, ticketTypeId))
      .filter(step => !companyId || sameId(step.company_id, companyId))
      .sort((a, b) => (Number(a.sort_order) || 0) - (Number(b.sort_order) || 0))
      .map(step => {
        const sla = slaByStep[String(step.id)];
        return {
          ...step,
          sla_duration: sla ? sla.duration : '',
          sla_unit: sla ? sla.unit : '',
          exclude_weekends: sla ? boolFlag(sla.exclude_weekends) : false
        };
      });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: steps,
      message: `Retrieved ${steps.length} workflow steps`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getWorkflowSteps: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Create a workflow step (and its SLA when sla_duration is given).
 * v9.1: header-driven row, generateId (copying steps in one call collided on Date.now()), company_id
 * required (workflows are per company), SLA validated before anything is written.
 * @param {Object} payload - { ticket_type_id, company_id, name, status_on_reach, step_type, approver_logic,
 *   sort_order, next_ticket_type_id, external_app_url, completion_action_name, sla_duration, sla_unit,
 *   exclude_weekends, user_id }
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: step, message }
 */
function createWorkflowStep(payload) {
  try {
    const requiredFields = ['ticket_type_id', 'company_id', 'name', 'status_on_reach', 'step_type'];
    for (const field of requiredFields) {
      if (!payload[field]) {
        return { status: 'error', message: `Missing required field: ${field}` };
      }
    }

    const approverLogic = String(payload.approver_logic || 'any').toLowerCase();
    if (!['any', 'all'].includes(approverLogic)) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'approver_logic must be "any" or "all"' };
    }

    const hasSLA = payload.sla_duration !== undefined && payload.sla_duration !== null && payload.sla_duration !== '';
    if (hasSLA) {
      const slaError = validateStepSLA(payload.sla_duration, payload.sla_unit);
      if (slaError) return { status: 'error', message: slaError };
    }

    const sheet = ss().getSheetByName(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS) || ensureTableSheet(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS);
    const userId = payload.user_id || getCurrentUserId();
    const now = new Date();

    const stepData = {
      id: generateId('ws'),
      ticket_type_id: payload.ticket_type_id,
      company_id: payload.company_id,
      name: payload.name,
      status_on_reach: payload.status_on_reach,
      step_type: payload.step_type,
      approver_logic: approverLogic,
      sort_order: payload.sort_order || 1,
      next_ticket_type_id: payload.next_ticket_type_id || '',
      external_app_url: payload.external_app_url || '',
      completion_action_name: payload.completion_action_name || '',
      is_active: true,
      created_at: now,
      created_by: userId,
      updated_at: now,
      updated_by: userId
    };
    sheet.appendRow(rowFromRecord(sheetHeaders(sheet), stepData));
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));

    if (hasSLA) {
      const sla = upsertStepSLA(stepData.id, payload, userId);
      if (sla.status !== 'success') return sla;
    }

    logAdminAction(userId, 'CREATE_WORKFLOW_STEP', 'workflow_step', stepData.id, {
      ticket_type_id: payload.ticket_type_id,
      company_id: payload.company_id,
      name: payload.name,
      step_type: payload.step_type,
      sla_duration: hasSLA ? payload.sla_duration : null
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: { ...stepData, sla_duration: hasSLA ? payload.sla_duration : '', sla_unit: hasSLA ? payload.sla_unit : '' },
      message: 'Workflow step created successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in createWorkflowStep: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Update a workflow step and, when sla_duration is in the payload, its SLA (blank = remove the SLA).
 * v9.1: whole row written by header (updateRecordFields); ids compared as strings. A step can't move to
 * another ticket type or company.
 * @param {string} stepId - Step ID
 * @param {Object} payload - Fields to change + sla_duration / sla_unit / exclude_weekends + user_id
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: step, message }
 */
function updateWorkflowStep(stepId, payload) {
  try {
    if (!stepId) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'step_id is required' };
    }
    const found = findRecord(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS, stepId);
    if (!found || !isActiveFlag(found.record.is_active)) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Workflow step not found' };
    }

    const changes = pickDefined(payload, ['name', 'status_on_reach', 'step_type', 'approver_logic', 'sort_order',
      'next_ticket_type_id', 'external_app_url', 'completion_action_name']);
    if (changes.approver_logic !== undefined) {
      changes.approver_logic = String(changes.approver_logic).toLowerCase();
      if (!['any', 'all'].includes(changes.approver_logic)) {
        return { status: 'error', message: 'approver_logic must be "any" or "all"' };
      }
    }
    ['next_ticket_type_id', 'external_app_url', 'completion_action_name'].forEach(key => {
      if (changes[key] === null) changes[key] = '';
    });

    const slaGiven = payload.sla_duration !== undefined;
    const removeSLA = slaGiven && (payload.sla_duration === null || payload.sla_duration === '');
    if (slaGiven && !removeSLA) {
      const slaError = validateStepSLA(payload.sla_duration, payload.sla_unit);
      if (slaError) return { status: 'error', message: slaError };
    }

    const userId = payload.user_id || getCurrentUserId();
    const result = updateRecordFields(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS, stepId, {
      ...changes,
      updated_at: new Date(),
      updated_by: userId
    });

    if (removeSLA) {
      removeStepSLA(stepId, userId, 'SLA removed in workflow builder');
    } else if (slaGiven) {
      const sla = upsertStepSLA(stepId, payload, userId);
      if (sla.status !== 'success') return sla;
    }

    logAdminAction(userId, 'UPDATE_WORKFLOW_STEP', 'workflow_step', stepId, {
      ...changes,
      sla_duration: slaGiven ? payload.sla_duration : undefined
    });

    return { status: 'success', data: result.after, message: 'Workflow step updated successfully' };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in updateWorkflowStep: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Delete (deactivate) a workflow step and its SLA.
 * v9.1: refused while an open ticket sits on the step (it would have no step to approve).
 * @param {string} stepId - Step ID
 * @param {string} userId - Admin (optional)
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { id, deactivated }, message }
 */
function deleteWorkflowStep(stepId, userId = null) {
  try {
    if (!stepId) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'step_id is required' };
    }
    const found = findRecord(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS, stepId);
    if (!found || !isActiveFlag(found.record.is_active)) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Workflow step not found' };
    }

    const tickets = readTable(SHEETS.TICKETS);
    const openTickets = (tickets ? tickets.records : []).filter(t =>
      sameId(t.current_step_id, stepId) && isActiveFlag(t.is_active) && !t.cancelled_at &&
      !TERMINAL_TICKET_STATUSES.includes(t.status));
    if (openTickets.length > 0) {
      return {
        status: 'error',
        message: `${openTickets.length} open ticket(s) are at this step (${openTickets.slice(0, 5).map(t => t.ticket_number || t.id).join(', ')}) - move or close them first`
      };
    }

    const result = deactivateRecord(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS, stepId, 'Admin deletion');
    if (result.status !== 'success') return result;
    removeStepSLA(stepId, userId || getCurrentUserId(), 'Workflow step deleted');

    return { status: 'success', data: { id: stepId, deactivated: true }, message: 'Workflow step deleted successfully' };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in deleteWorkflowStep: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Reorder workflow steps for a ticket type
 * @param {string} ticketTypeId - Ticket type ID
 * @param {Array} stepIds - Array of step IDs in desired order
 * @returns {Object} Response confirming reorder
 */
function reorderWorkflowSteps(ticketTypeId, stepIds) {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('workflow_steps');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Workflow steps sheet not found' };
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(stepIds) || stepIds.length === 0) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Step IDs must be a non-empty array' };
    }

    const data = sheetValues(sheet);
    const headers = data[0];
    const idIndex = headers.indexOf('id');
    const ticketTypeIndex = headers.indexOf('ticket_type_id');
    const sortOrderIndex = headers.indexOf('sort_order');
    const updatedAtIndex = headers.indexOf('updated_at');
    const updatedByIndex = headers.indexOf('updated_by');

    if (idIndex === -1 || ticketTypeIndex === -1 || sortOrderIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Required columns not found' };
    }

    const currentUser = 'admin';
    const now = new Date();
    let updatedCount = 0;

    // Update sort order for each step
    stepIds.forEach((stepId, index) => {
      const newSortOrder = index + 1;

      for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
        if (data[i][idIndex] === stepId && data[i][ticketTypeIndex] === ticketTypeId) {
          sheet.getRange(i + 1, sortOrderIndex + 1).setValue(newSortOrder);
          invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
          if (updatedAtIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(i + 1, updatedAtIndex + 1).setValue(now);
          invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
          if (updatedByIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(i + 1, updatedByIndex + 1).setValue(currentUser);
          invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
          updatedCount++;
          break;
        }
      }
    });

    // Log admin action
    logAdminAction(currentUser, 'REORDER_WORKFLOW_STEPS', 'workflow_steps', ticketTypeId, {
      step_ids: stepIds,
      updated_count: updatedCount
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: { ticket_type_id: ticketTypeId, updated_count: updatedCount },
      message: `Reordered ${updatedCount} workflow steps`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in reorderWorkflowSteps: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Clone workflow steps from one ticket type to another
 * @param {string} fromTicketType - Source ticket type ID
 * @param {string} toTicketType - Target ticket type ID
 * @returns {Object} Response with cloned steps data
 */
function cloneWorkflowSteps(fromTicketType, toTicketType) {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('workflow_steps');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Workflow steps sheet not found' };
    }

    // Get source steps
    const sourceSteps = getWorkflowSteps(fromTicketType);
    if (sourceSteps.status !== 'success' || !sourceSteps.data.length) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'No workflow steps found to clone' };
    }

    const clonedSteps = [];
    const currentUser = 'admin';
    const now = new Date();

    // Clone each step
    for (const sourceStep of sourceSteps.data) {
      const newStepId = 'ws_' + Date.now() + '_' + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9);

      const clonedStep = {
        id: newStepId,
        ticket_type_id: toTicketType,
        company_id: sourceStep.company_id,
        name: sourceStep.name,
        status_on_reach: sourceStep.status_on_reach,
        step_type: sourceStep.step_type,
        approver_logic: sourceStep.approver_logic,
        sort_order: sourceStep.sort_order,
        next_ticket_type_id: sourceStep.next_ticket_type_id,
        external_app_url: sourceStep.external_app_url,
        completion_action_name: sourceStep.completion_action_name,
        is_active: true,
        created_at: now,
        created_by: currentUser,
        updated_at: now,
        updated_by: currentUser,
        deactivated_at: null,
        deactivated_by: null,
        deactivation_reason: null
      };

      // Add to sheet
      const headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
      const newRow = [];

      headers.forEach(header => {
        newRow.push(clonedStep[header] || '');
      });

      sheet.appendRow(newRow);
      invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
      clonedSteps.push(clonedStep);

      // Small delay to ensure unique timestamps
      Utilities.sleep(10);
    }

    // Log admin action
    logAdminAction(currentUser, 'CLONE_WORKFLOW_STEPS', 'workflow_steps', toTicketType, {
      from_ticket_type: fromTicketType,
      cloned_count: clonedSteps.length,
      step_ids: clonedSteps.map(step => step.id)
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: clonedSteps,
      message: `Cloned ${clonedSteps.length} workflow steps successfully`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in cloneWorkflowSteps: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Get the roles assigned as approvers of a workflow step.
 * @param {string} stepId - Step ID
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: [{ step_id, role_id }], message }
 */
function getStepApprovers(stepId) {
  try {
    const table = readTable(SHEETS.STEP_APPROVERS);
    if (!table) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Step approvers sheet not found' };
    }

    const approvers = table.records.filter(row => sameId(row.step_id, stepId));
    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: approvers,
      message: `Retrieved ${approvers.length} step approvers`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getStepApprovers: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Assign roles as approvers to a workflow step (skips roles already assigned).
 * @param {string} stepId - Step ID
 * @param {Array} roleIds - Role IDs to add
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { step_id, assigned_count }, message }
 */
function assignStepApprovers(stepId, roleIds) {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.STEP_APPROVERS) || ensureTableSheet(SHEETS.STEP_APPROVERS);

    if (!Array.isArray(roleIds) || roleIds.length === 0) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Role IDs must be a non-empty array' };
    }

    const existing = getStepApprovers(stepId);
    const existingRoleIds = (existing.data || []).map(approver => String(approver.role_id));
    const headers = sheetHeaders(sheet);
    let assignedCount = 0;

    roleIds.forEach(roleId => {
      if (!existingRoleIds.includes(String(roleId))) {
        sheet.appendRow(rowFromRecord(headers, { step_id: stepId, role_id: roleId }));
        invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
        existingRoleIds.push(String(roleId));
        assignedCount++;
      }
    });

    logAdminAction(getCurrentUserId(), 'ASSIGN_STEP_APPROVERS', 'step_approvers', stepId, {
      role_ids: roleIds,
      assigned_count: assignedCount
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: { step_id: stepId, assigned_count: assignedCount },
      message: `Assigned ${assignedCount} new approvers to step`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in assignStepApprovers: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Remove role assignments from a workflow step.
 * step_approvers is a link table (step_id, role_id) with no status column, so the link rows are removed;
 * the admin action log records which roles were removed.
 * @param {string} stepId - Step ID
 * @param {Array} roleIds - Role IDs to remove
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { step_id, removed_count }, message }
 */
function removeStepApprovers(stepId, roleIds) {
  try {
    const table = readTable(SHEETS.STEP_APPROVERS);
    if (!table) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Step approvers sheet not found' };
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(roleIds) || roleIds.length === 0) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Role IDs must be a non-empty array' };
    }

    const removeIds = roleIds.map(String);
    let removedCount = 0;

    // Bottom-up so the remaining row numbers stay valid
    for (let i = table.records.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const row = table.records[i];
      if (sameId(row.step_id, stepId) && removeIds.includes(String(row.role_id))) {
        table.sheet.deleteRow(table.rowNumbers[i]);
        invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(table.sheet));
        removedCount++;
      }
    }

    logAdminAction(getCurrentUserId(), 'REMOVE_STEP_APPROVERS', 'step_approvers', stepId, {
      role_ids: roleIds,
      removed_count: removedCount
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: { step_id: stepId, removed_count: removedCount },
      message: `Removed ${removedCount} approvers from step`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in removeStepApprovers: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Validate step approver assignments
 * @param {string} stepId - Step ID to validate
 * @returns {Object} Validation results
 */
function validateStepApprovers(stepId) {
  try {
    const approvers = getStepApprovers(stepId);
    if (approvers.status !== 'success') {
      return approvers;
    }

    const issues = [];

    if (approvers.data.length === 0) {
      issues.push('No approvers assigned to step');
    }

    // Check if all assigned roles exist and are active
    // TODO: Add role existence validation

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: {
        step_id: stepId,
        approver_count: approvers.data.length,
        issues: issues,
        is_valid: issues.length === 0
      },
      message: issues.length === 0 ? 'Step approvers are valid' : `Found ${issues.length} validation issues`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in validateStepApprovers: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Get a user's role assignments. v9.0: date-aware (isAssignmentActive) unless include_inactive.
 * @param {string} userId - User ID
 * @param {string} companyId - Company filter (optional)
 * @param {Object} options - { include_inactive: true } returns every row, including expired/deleted
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: assignments[], message }
 */
function getUserRoleAssignments(userId, companyId = null, options = {}) {
  try {
    const table = readTable(SHEETS.USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS);
    if (!table) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'User role assignments sheet not found' };
    }

    const now = new Date();
    const assignments = table.records
      .filter(a => sameId(a.user_id, userId))
      .filter(a => !companyId || sameId(a.company_id, companyId))
      .filter(a => options.include_inactive || isAssignmentActive(a, now));

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: assignments,
      message: `Retrieved ${assignments.length} role assignments`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getUserRoleAssignments: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Create a user role assignment, with the date-based fields (v9.0: header-driven, A1).
 * @param {Object} payload - user_id, role_id, company_id (required); ticket_type_id (blank = every type),
 *   assignment_type, effective_start_date, effective_end_date, validity_end_date, auto_expire_days,
 *   approval_required, assignment_notes, assigned_by_user_id
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: assignment, message }
 */
function createUserRoleAssignment(payload) {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS) || ensureTableSheet(SHEETS.USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS);

    const requiredFields = ['user_id', 'role_id', 'company_id'];
    for (const field of requiredFields) {
      if (!payload[field]) {
        return { status: 'error', message: `Missing required field: ${field}` };
      }
    }

    // v9.1 (review R-10): a company-specific role can only be assigned in its own company
    const role = findRecord(SHEETS.ROLES, payload.role_id);
    if (!role || !isActiveFlag(role.record.is_active)) {
      return { status: 'error', message: `Role ${payload.role_id} not found` };
    }
    if (role.record.company_id && !sameId(role.record.company_id, payload.company_id)) {
      return { status: 'error', message: `Role "${role.record.name}" belongs to another company` };
    }

    // An end date covers that whole day (endOfTerm); dates are stored as sent so a date-only value stays one
    const start = payload.effective_start_date ? toDate(payload.effective_start_date) : new Date();
    const end = payload.effective_end_date ? endOfTerm(payload.effective_end_date) : null;
    if (!start || (payload.effective_end_date && !end)) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Invalid effective_start_date / effective_end_date' };
    }
    if (end && end <= start) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'effective_end_date must be on or after effective_start_date' };
    }

    // Same role + ticket type + company that is current or not yet started counts as a duplicate
    const existing = getUserRoleAssignments(payload.user_id, payload.company_id, { include_inactive: true });
    const now = new Date();
    const duplicate = (existing.data || []).find(a =>
      sameId(a.role_id, payload.role_id) &&
      sameId(a.ticket_type_id || '', payload.ticket_type_id || '') &&
      isActiveFlag(a.is_active) && !a.deleted_at &&
      !(assignmentExpiry(a) && assignmentExpiry(a) <= now)
    );
    if (duplicate) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'User already has this role assignment' };
    }

    const assignment = {
      id: generateId('ura'),
      user_id: payload.user_id,
      ticket_type_id: payload.ticket_type_id || '',
      role_id: payload.role_id,
      validity_end_date: payload.validity_end_date || '',
      company_id: payload.company_id,
      assignment_type: payload.assignment_type || 'permanent',
      effective_start_date: payload.effective_start_date || start.toISOString(),
      effective_end_date: payload.effective_end_date || '',
      auto_expire_days: payload.auto_expire_days || '',
      assigned_by_user_id: payload.assigned_by_user_id || getCurrentUserId(),
      approval_required: payload.approval_required === true || String(payload.approval_required) === 'true',
      assignment_notes: payload.assignment_notes || '',
      ...createAuditFields(payload.assigned_by_user_id)
    };

    sheet.appendRow(rowFromRecord(sheetHeaders(sheet), assignment));
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));

    logAdminAction(getCurrentUserId(), 'CREATE_USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENT', 'user_role_assignment', assignment.id, {
      user_id: assignment.user_id,
      role_id: assignment.role_id,
      company_id: assignment.company_id,
      assignment_type: assignment.assignment_type,
      effective_start_date: assignment.effective_start_date,
      effective_end_date: assignment.effective_end_date
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: assignment,
      message: 'User role assignment created successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in createUserRoleAssignment: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Update a user role assignment (v9.0: dates, type, notes and is_active; whole row written by header).
 * @param {string} assignmentId - Assignment ID
 * @param {Object} payload - Fields to change
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { id, changes }, message }
 */
function updateUserRoleAssignment(assignmentId, payload) {
  try {
    const allowedFields = ['validity_end_date', 'effective_start_date', 'effective_end_date', 'auto_expire_days',
      'assignment_type', 'assignment_notes', 'approval_required', 'ticket_type_id', 'is_active'];
    const updates = {};
    allowedFields.forEach(field => {
      if (payload.hasOwnProperty(field) && payload[field] !== undefined) {
        updates[field] = field === 'is_active' ? isActiveFlag(payload[field]) : payload[field];
      }
    });

    const current = findRecord(SHEETS.USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS, assignmentId);
    if (!current) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'User role assignment not found' };
    }

    const merged = { ...current.record, ...updates };
    const start = toDate(merged.effective_start_date);
    const end = merged.effective_end_date ? endOfTerm(merged.effective_end_date) : null;
    if (start && end && end <= start) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'effective_end_date must be on or after effective_start_date' };
    }

    const result = updateRecordFields(SHEETS.USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS, assignmentId, { ...updates, ...updateAuditFields() });
    dropAllowCache(result.before.user_id); // v9.6: allow-list re-checked (dates / is_active may have changed)
    const changes = {};
    Object.keys(updates).forEach(field => {
      if (String(result.before[field]) !== String(updates[field])) {
        changes[field] = { from: result.before[field], to: updates[field] };
      }
    });

    if (Object.keys(changes).length > 0) {
      logAdminAction(getCurrentUserId(), 'UPDATE_USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENT', 'user_role_assignment', assignmentId, changes);
    }

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: { id: assignmentId, changes: changes },
      message: 'User role assignment updated successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in updateUserRoleAssignment: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Soft-delete a user role assignment (deleted_* columns, is_active false).
 * @param {string} assignmentId - Assignment ID
 * @param {string} reason - Deletion reason
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { id }, message }
 */
function deleteUserRoleAssignment(assignmentId, reason = 'Admin deletion') {
  try {
    const result = updateRecordFields(SHEETS.USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS, assignmentId, deleteAuditFields(reason));
    if (!result) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'User role assignment not found' };
    }
    dropAllowCache(result.before.user_id); // v9.6: allow-list re-checked

    logAdminAction(getCurrentUserId(), 'DELETE_USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENT', 'user_role_assignment', assignmentId, {
      reason: reason,
      user_id: result.before.user_id,
      role_id: result.before.role_id
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: { id: assignmentId },
      message: 'User role assignment deleted successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in deleteUserRoleAssignment: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Get the users who can approve a workflow step.
 * v9.0: only assignments that are active today (isAssignmentActive), and - when a ticket is given - only
 * assignments for the ticket's company and ticket type (a blank ticket_type_id covers every type).
 * @param {string} stepId - Step ID
 * @param {Object} ticket - Ticket record (optional; without it, every company/type matches)
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: [{ user_id, role_id, company_id, ticket_type_id, ... }], message }
 */
function getUsersForStep(stepId, ticket = null) {
  try {
    const stepApprovers = getStepApprovers(stepId);
    if (stepApprovers.status !== 'success') {
      return stepApprovers;
    }

    const roleIds = stepApprovers.data.map(approver => String(approver.role_id));
    // v9.5: roles an escalate_role rule granted on this visit of the step (only for the ticket's current step)
    const granted = ticket && sameId(ticket.current_step_id, stepId) ? escalationGrantedRoles(ticket) : [];
    granted.forEach(roleId => { if (!roleIds.includes(roleId)) roleIds.push(roleId); });
    if (roleIds.length === 0) {
      return {
        status: 'success',
        data: [],
        message: 'No roles assigned to this step'
      };
    }

    const table = readTable(SHEETS.USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS);
    if (!table) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'User role assignments sheet not found' };
    }

    const now = new Date();
    const eligibleUsers = table.records
      .filter(a => roleIds.includes(String(a.role_id)) && isAssignmentActive(a, now))
      .filter(a => !ticket || sameId(a.company_id, ticket.company_id))
      .filter(a => !ticket || !a.ticket_type_id || sameId(a.ticket_type_id, ticket.ticket_type_id))
      .map(a => ({
        user_id: a.user_id,
        role_id: a.role_id,
        company_id: a.company_id,
        ticket_type_id: a.ticket_type_id,
        effective_start_date: a.effective_start_date,
        effective_end_date: a.effective_end_date,
        validity_end_date: a.validity_end_date,
        escalated: granted.includes(String(a.role_id)) && !stepApprovers.data.some(s => sameId(s.role_id, a.role_id))
      }));

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: eligibleUsers,
      message: `Found ${eligibleUsers.length} users eligible for this step`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getUsersForStep: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Validate user role assignment data
 * @param {Object} payload - Assignment data to validate
 * @returns {Object} Validation results
 */
function validateUserRoleAssignment(payload) {
  try {
    const issues = [];

    // Check required fields
    const requiredFields = ['user_id', 'role_id', 'company_id'];
    requiredFields.forEach(field => {
      if (!payload[field]) {
        issues.push(`Missing required field: ${field}`);
      }
    });

    // Check validity end date format
    if (payload.validity_end_date) {
      if (!isValidDate(payload.validity_end_date)) {
        issues.push('Invalid validity_end_date format');
      } else if (new Date(payload.validity_end_date) <= new Date()) {
        issues.push('Validity end date must be in the future');
      }
    }

    // TODO: Add checks for user existence, role existence, company existence

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: {
        is_valid: issues.length === 0,
        issues: issues
      },
      message: issues.length === 0 ? 'Assignment data is valid' : `Found ${issues.length} validation issues`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in validateUserRoleAssignment: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Get SLA configuration for a workflow step (v9.0: id compared as a string, A8).
 * @param {string} stepId - Step ID
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: slas[], message }
 */
function getStepSLAs(stepId) {
  try {
    const table = readTable(SHEETS.STEP_SLAS);
    if (!table) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Step SLAs sheet not found' };
    }

    const slas = table.records.filter(sla => isActiveFlag(sla.is_active) && !sla.deleted_at && sameId(sla.step_id, stepId));
    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: slas,
      message: `Retrieved ${slas.length} SLA configurations`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getStepSLAs: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Create SLA configuration for a workflow step
 * @param {Object} payload - SLA data
 * @returns {Object} Response with created SLA data
 */
function createStepSLA(payload) {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('step_slas');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Step SLAs sheet not found' };
    }

    // Validate required fields
    const requiredFields = ['step_id', 'duration', 'unit'];
    for (const field of requiredFields) {
      if (!payload[field]) {
        return { status: 'error', message: `Missing required field: ${field}` };
      }
    }

    // Validate unit
    const validUnits = ['hours', 'days'];
    if (!validUnits.includes(payload.unit)) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Unit must be either "hours" or "days"' };
    }

    // Validate duration
    if (isNaN(payload.duration) || payload.duration <= 0) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Duration must be a positive number' };
    }

    // Check for existing SLA
    const existing = getStepSLAs(payload.step_id);
    if (existing.status === 'success' && existing.data.length > 0) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'SLA already exists for this step. Use update function instead.' };
    }

    const slaId = 'sla_' + Date.now();
    const currentUser = 'admin';
    const now = new Date();

    const slaData = {
      id: slaId,
      step_id: payload.step_id,
      duration: payload.duration,
      unit: payload.unit,
      exclude_weekends: payload.exclude_weekends || false,
      is_active: true,
      created_at: now,
      created_by: currentUser,
      updated_at: now,
      updated_by: currentUser,
      deleted_at: null,
      deleted_by: null,
      deletion_reason: null
    };

    // Add to sheet
    const headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
    const newRow = [];

    headers.forEach(header => {
      newRow.push(slaData[header] || '');
    });

    sheet.appendRow(newRow);
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));

    // Log admin action
    logAdminAction(currentUser, 'CREATE_STEP_SLA', 'step_sla', slaId, {
      step_id: payload.step_id,
      duration: payload.duration,
      unit: payload.unit
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: slaData,
      message: 'Step SLA created successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in createStepSLA: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Update SLA configuration for a workflow step
 * @param {string} slaId - SLA ID to update
 * @param {Object} payload - Updated SLA data
 * @returns {Object} Response with updated SLA data
 */
function updateStepSLA(slaId, payload) {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('step_slas');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Step SLAs sheet not found' };
    }

    const data = sheetValues(sheet);
    const headers = data[0];
    const idIndex = headers.indexOf('id');

    if (idIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'ID column not found' };
    }

    // Find the SLA
    let rowIndex = -1;
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (data[i][idIndex] === slaId) {
        rowIndex = i;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (rowIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Step SLA not found' };
    }

    const currentUser = 'admin';
    const now = new Date();

    // Validate fields if provided
    if (payload.unit && !['hours', 'days'].includes(payload.unit)) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Unit must be either "hours" or "days"' };
    }

    if (payload.duration && (isNaN(payload.duration) || payload.duration <= 0)) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Duration must be a positive number' };
    }

    // Update allowed fields
    const allowedFields = ['duration', 'unit', 'exclude_weekends'];
    const changes = {};

    allowedFields.forEach(field => {
      if (payload.hasOwnProperty(field)) {
        const colIndex = headers.indexOf(field);
        if (colIndex !== -1) {
          const oldValue = data[rowIndex][colIndex];
          sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, colIndex + 1).setValue(payload[field]);
          invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
          if (oldValue !== payload[field]) {
            changes[field] = { from: oldValue, to: payload[field] };
          }
        }
      }
    });

    // Update audit fields
    const updatedAtIndex = headers.indexOf('updated_at');
    const updatedByIndex = headers.indexOf('updated_by');

    if (updatedAtIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, updatedAtIndex + 1).setValue(now);
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
    if (updatedByIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, updatedByIndex + 1).setValue(currentUser);
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));

    // Log admin action
    if (Object.keys(changes).length > 0) {
      logAdminAction(currentUser, 'UPDATE_STEP_SLA', 'step_sla', slaId, changes);
    }

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: { id: slaId, changes: changes },
      message: 'Step SLA updated successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in updateStepSLA: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Delete SLA configuration for a workflow step
 * @param {string} slaId - SLA ID to delete
 * @returns {Object} Response confirming deletion
 */
function deleteStepSLA(slaId) {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('step_slas');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Step SLAs sheet not found' };
    }

    const data = sheetValues(sheet);
    const headers = data[0];
    const idIndex = headers.indexOf('id');

    if (idIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'ID column not found' };
    }

    // Find the SLA
    let rowIndex = -1;
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (data[i][idIndex] === slaId) {
        rowIndex = i;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (rowIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Step SLA not found' };
    }

    // Soft delete
    const currentUser = 'admin';
    const now = new Date();

    const deletedAtIndex = headers.indexOf('deleted_at');
    const deletedByIndex = headers.indexOf('deleted_by');
    const deletionReasonIndex = headers.indexOf('deletion_reason');
    const isActiveIndex = headers.indexOf('is_active');

    if (deletedAtIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, deletedAtIndex + 1).setValue(now);
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
    if (deletedByIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, deletedByIndex + 1).setValue(currentUser);
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
    if (deletionReasonIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, deletionReasonIndex + 1).setValue('Admin deletion');
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
    if (isActiveIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, isActiveIndex + 1).setValue(false);
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));

    // Log admin action
    logAdminAction(currentUser, 'DELETE_STEP_SLA', 'step_sla', slaId, {
      reason: 'Admin deletion',
      deleted_at: now
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: { id: slaId },
      message: 'Step SLA deleted successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in deleteStepSLA: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

// PHASE 1C: TICKET MANAGEMENT SYSTEM
// Purpose: Complete ticket lifecycle management with workflow progression
