/**
* Validate complete ticket workflow configuration for end-to-end consistency
* @param {string} ticketTypeId - Ticket type ID to validate
* @param {string} companyId - Company ID
* @returns {Object} Response with comprehensive validation results
*/
function validateCompleteTicketWorkflow(ticketTypeId, companyId) {
try {
const validationResults = {
ticket_type: { valid: false, issues: [] },
workflow_steps: { valid: false, issues: [], steps: [] },
custom_fields: { valid: false, issues: [], fields: [] },
approvers: { valid: false, issues: [], assignments: [] },
slas: { valid: false, issues: [], configurations: [] },
overall_status: 'INVALID',
critical_issues: [],
warnings: [],
recommendations: []
};

// 1. Validate ticket type exists and is active
const ticketType = getTicketTypes(ticketTypeId, companyId);
if (ticketType.status === 'success' && ticketType.data.length > 0) {
const type = ticketType.data[0];
if (type.is_active) {
validationResults.ticket_type.valid = true;
} else {
validationResults.ticket_type.issues.push('Ticket type is not active');
validationResults.critical_issues.push('Ticket type must be active for workflow to function');
}
} else {
validationResults.ticket_type.issues.push('Ticket type not found or not assigned to company');
validationResults.critical_issues.push('Valid ticket type is required');
}

// 2. Validate workflow steps
const workflowSteps = getWorkflowSteps(ticketTypeId, companyId);
if (workflowSteps.status === 'success' && workflowSteps.data.length > 0) {
const steps = workflowSteps.data;
validationResults.workflow_steps.steps = steps;

// Check step sequence
const sortOrders = steps.map(step => step.sort_order).sort((a, b) => a - b);
const expectedSequence = Array.from({ length: steps.length }, (_, i) => i + 1);

if (JSON.stringify(sortOrders) === JSON.stringify(expectedSequence)) {
validationResults.workflow_steps.valid = true;
} else {
validationResults.workflow_steps.issues.push('Workflow steps have gaps or duplicates in sort order');
validationResults.warnings.push('Fix step ordering for proper workflow progression');
}

// Validate each step has required fields
steps.forEach((step, index) => {
if (!step.name) {
validationResults.workflow_steps.issues.push(`Step ${index + 1} missing name`);
}
if (!step.status_on_reach) {
validationResults.workflow_steps.issues.push(`Step ${index + 1} missing status_on_reach`);
}
if (!step.step_type) {
validationResults.workflow_steps.issues.push(`Step ${index + 1} missing step_type`);
}
});

} else {
validationResults.workflow_steps.issues.push('No workflow steps defined');
validationResults.critical_issues.push('At least one workflow step is required');
}

// 3. Validate approver assignments
if (workflowSteps.status === 'success' && workflowSteps.data.length > 0) {
let totalApprovers = 0;
const approverIssues = [];

for (const step of workflowSteps.data) {
const stepApprovers = getStepApprovers(step.id);
if (stepApprovers.status === 'success') {
const approverCount = stepApprovers.data.length;
totalApprovers += approverCount;

validationResults.approvers.assignments.push({
step_id: step.id,
step_name: step.name,
approver_count: approverCount,
approver_logic: step.approver_logic
});

if (approverCount === 0) {
approverIssues.push(`Step '${step.name}' has no approvers assigned`);
validationResults.critical_issues.push(`Step '${step.name}' requires at least one approver`);
}
} else {
approverIssues.push(`Cannot validate approvers for step '${step.name}'`);
}
}

if (totalApprovers > 0 && approverIssues.length === 0) {
validationResults.approvers.valid = true;
} else {
validationResults.approvers.issues = approverIssues;
}
}

// 4. Validate custom fields
const customFields = getCustomFields(ticketTypeId, companyId);
if (customFields.status === 'success') {
validationResults.custom_fields.fields = customFields.data;
validationResults.custom_fields.valid = true;

// Check for required fields without defaults
customFields.data.forEach(field => {
if (field.is_required && !field.default_value) {
validationResults.warnings.push(`Required field '${field.label}' has no default value`);
}

// Validate dropdown fields have options
if (field.type === 'dropdown' && field.dropdown_list_id) {
const dropdownOptions = getDropdownOptions(field.dropdown_list_id);
if (dropdownOptions.status !== 'success' || dropdownOptions.data.length === 0) {
validationResults.custom_fields.issues.push(`Dropdown field '${field.label}' has no options`);
validationResults.warnings.push(`Configure options for dropdown field '${field.label}'`);
}
}
});
}

// 5. Validate SLA configurations
if (workflowSteps.status === 'success' && workflowSteps.data.length > 0) {
let slaCount = 0;
const slaIssues = [];

for (const step of workflowSteps.data) {
const stepSLAs = getStepSLAs(step.id);
if (stepSLAs.status === 'success' && stepSLAs.data.length > 0) {
slaCount++;
const sla = stepSLAs.data[0];

validationResults.slas.configurations.push({
step_id: step.id,
step_name: step.name,
duration: sla.duration,
unit: sla.unit,
exclude_weekends: sla.exclude_weekends
});

// Validate SLA values
if (sla.duration <= 0) {
slaIssues.push(`Step '${step.name}' has invalid SLA duration`);
}
if (!['hours', 'days'].includes(sla.unit)) {
slaIssues.push(`Step '${step.name}' has invalid SLA unit`);
}
} else {
validationResults.warnings.push(`Step '${step.name}' has no SLA configured`);
}
}

if (slaCount > 0 && slaIssues.length === 0) {
validationResults.slas.valid = true;
} else {
validationResults.slas.issues = slaIssues;
}
}

// 6. Generate recommendations
if (validationResults.workflow_steps.valid && workflowSteps.data.length === 1) {
validationResults.recommendations.push('Consider adding more workflow steps for complex approval processes');
}

if (validationResults.custom_fields.fields.length === 0) {
validationResults.recommendations.push('Add custom fields to collect additional ticket information');
}

if (validationResults.slas.configurations.length < workflowSteps.data.length) {
validationResults.recommendations.push('Configure SLAs for all workflow steps to ensure timely processing');
}

// 7. Determine overall status
const criticalValid = validationResults.ticket_type.valid &&
validationResults.workflow_steps.valid &&
validationResults.approvers.valid;

if (criticalValid && validationResults.critical_issues.length === 0) {
validationResults.overall_status = 'VALID';
} else if (validationResults.critical_issues.length === 0) {
validationResults.overall_status = 'WARNING';
} else {
validationResults.overall_status = 'INVALID';
}

return {
status: 'success',
data: validationResults,
message: `Workflow validation complete: ${validationResults.overall_status}`
};

} catch (error) {
Logger.log('Error in validateCompleteTicketWorkflow: ' + error.toString());
return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
}
}

/**
* Migrate existing tickets to new workflow configuration
* @param {string} ticketTypeId - Ticket type ID to migrate
* @returns {Object} Response with migration results
*/
function migrateExistingTicketsToWorkflow(ticketTypeId) {
try {
const ss = openSpreadsheet();
const ticketsSheet = ss.getSheetByName('tickets');

if (!ticketsSheet) {
return { status: 'error', message: 'Tickets sheet not found' };
}

// Get all tickets for this ticket type
const data = sheetValues(ticketsSheet);
const headers = data[0];
const ticketsToMigrate = [];

for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
const row = data[i];
const ticketData = {};

headers.forEach((header, index) => {
ticketData[header] = row[index];
});

if (ticketData.ticket_type_id === ticketTypeId) {
ticketsToMigrate.push({ rowIndex: i, data: ticketData });
}
}

if (ticketsToMigrate.length === 0) {
return {
status: 'success',
data: { migrated_count: 0 },
message: 'No tickets found for migration'
};
}

// Get current workflow for this ticket type
const firstTicket = ticketsToMigrate[0].data;
const workflowSteps = getWorkflowSteps(ticketTypeId, firstTicket.company_id);

if (workflowSteps.status !== 'success' || workflowSteps.data.length === 0) {
return { status: 'error', message: 'No workflow steps defined for ticket type' };
}

const migrationResults = {
total_tickets: ticketsToMigrate.length,
migrated_count: 0,
skipped_count: 0,
error_count: 0,
details: []
};

const currentUser = 'system_migration';
const now = new Date();

// Process each ticket
for (const ticket of ticketsToMigrate) {
try {
const ticketData = ticket.data;

// Skip completed or cancelled tickets
if (ticketData.status === 'Completed' || ticketData.status === 'Cancelled') {
migrationResults.skipped_count++;
migrationResults.details.push({
ticket_id: ticketData.id,
status: 'SKIPPED',
reason: 'Ticket already completed or cancelled'
});
continue;
}

// Find appropriate step based on current status
let targetStep = null;
for (const step of workflowSteps.data) {
if (step.status_on_reach === ticketData.status) {
targetStep = step;
break;
}
}

// If no matching step found, use first step
if (!targetStep) {
targetStep = workflowSteps.data[0];
}

// Update ticket with current step
const currentStepIndex = headers.indexOf('current_step_id');
const updatedAtIndex = headers.indexOf('updated_at');
const updatedByIndex = headers.indexOf('updated_by');

if (currentStepIndex !== -1) {
ticketsSheet.getRange(ticket.rowIndex + 1, currentStepIndex + 1).setValue(targetStep.id);
invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(ticketsSheet));
}
if (updatedAtIndex !== -1) {
ticketsSheet.getRange(ticket.rowIndex + 1, updatedAtIndex + 1).setValue(now);
invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(ticketsSheet));
}
if (updatedByIndex !== -1) {
ticketsSheet.getRange(ticket.rowIndex + 1, updatedByIndex + 1).setValue(currentUser);
invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(ticketsSheet));
}

// Create migration history entry
createTicketHistoryEntry({
ticket_id: ticketData.id,
user_id: currentUser,
action: 'MIGRATED',
comment: `Ticket migrated to workflow step: ${targetStep.name}`,
from_status: ticketData.status,
to_status: ticketData.status,
to_step_id: targetStep.id
});

migrationResults.migrated_count++;
migrationResults.details.push({
ticket_id: ticketData.id,
status: 'MIGRATED',
assigned_step: targetStep.name,
assigned_step_id: targetStep.id
});

} catch (error) {
migrationResults.error_count++;
migrationResults.details.push({
ticket_id: ticket.data.id,
status: 'ERROR',
error: error.toString()
});
}
}

return {
status: 'success',
data: migrationResults,
message: `Migration complete: ${migrationResults.migrated_count} tickets migrated, ${migrationResults.skipped_count} skipped,
{migrationResults.error_count} errors`
};

} catch (error) {
Logger.log('Error in migrateExistingTicketsToWorkflow: ' + error.toString());
return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
}
}

/**
* Generate workflow diagram visualization data
* @param {string} ticketTypeId - Ticket type ID
* @param {string} companyId - Company ID
* @returns {Object} Response with workflow diagram data
*/
function generateWorkflowDiagram(ticketTypeId, companyId) {
try {
// Get workflow steps
const workflowSteps = getWorkflowSteps(ticketTypeId, companyId);
if (workflowSteps.status !== 'success') {
return workflowSteps;
}

const steps = workflowSteps.data;
if (steps.length === 0) {
return { status: 'error', message: 'No workflow steps found' };
}

// Get ticket type info
const ticketType = getTicketTypes(ticketTypeId, companyId);
const typeName = (ticketType.status === 'success' && ticketType.data.length > 0)
? ticketType.data[0].name : 'Unknown Type';

const diagramData = {
ticket_type: {
id: ticketTypeId,
name: typeName,
company_id: companyId
},
nodes: [],
edges: [],
metadata: {
total_steps: steps.length,
has_sla: false,
has_external_integration: false,
has_chained_workflows: false
}
};

// Create nodes for each step
for (let i = 0; i < steps.length; i++) {
const step = steps[i];

// Get step approvers
const stepApprovers = getStepApprovers(step.id);
let approverInfo = { count: 0, roles: [] };
if (stepApprovers.status === 'success') {
approverInfo.count = stepApprovers.data.length;
approverInfo.roles = stepApprovers.data.map(approver => approver.role_id);
}

// Get SLA info
const stepSLAs = getStepSLAs(step.id);
let slaInfo = null;
if (stepSLAs.status === 'success' && stepSLAs.data.length > 0) {
const sla = stepSLAs.data[0];
slaInfo = {
duration: sla.duration,
unit: sla.unit,
exclude_weekends: sla.exclude_weekends
};
diagramData.metadata.has_sla = true;
}

// Check for external integration
if (step.external_app_url) {
diagramData.metadata.has_external_integration = true;
}

// Check for chained workflows
if (step.next_ticket_type_id) {
diagramData.metadata.has_chained_workflows = true;
}

const node = {
id: step.id,
type: 'workflow_step',
position: { x: i * 200, y: 100 },
data: {
label: step.name,
status_on_reach: step.status_on_reach,
step_type: step.step_type,
approver_logic: step.approver_logic,
sort_order: step.sort_order,
approvers: approverInfo,
sla: slaInfo,
external_app_url: step.external_app_url,
next_ticket_type_id: step.next_ticket_type_id,
completion_action_name: step.completion_action_name
}
};

diagramData.nodes.push(node);

// Create edge to next step
if (i < steps.length - 1) {
const edge = {
id: `${step.id}_to_${steps[i + 1].id}`,
source: step.id,
target: steps[i + 1].id,
type: 'workflow_edge',
data: {
label: 'Approve',
condition: 'normal_progression'
}
};
diagramData.edges.push(edge);
}

// Add rejection edge (all steps can reject)
if (i === 0) {
// First step rejection goes to end
const rejectEdge = {
id: `${step.id}_reject`,
source: step.id,
target: 'end_rejected',
type: 'reject_edge',
data: {
label: 'Reject',
condition: 'rejection'
}
};
diagramData.edges.push(rejectEdge);
} else {
// Other steps can return to previous step
const returnEdge = {
id: `${step.id}_return`,
source: step.id,
target: steps[i - 1].id,
type: 'return_edge',
data: {
label: 'Return',
condition: 'return_to_previous'
}
};
diagramData.edges.push(returnEdge);
}

// Add chained workflow edge
if (step.next_ticket_type_id) {
const chainedEdge = {
id: `${step.id}_chain`,
source: step.id,
target: 'chained_workflow',
type: 'chain_edge',
data: {
label: 'Create Chained Ticket',
condition: 'chained_workflow',
target_ticket_type: step.next_ticket_type_id
}
};
diagramData.edges.push(chainedEdge);
}
}

// Add special nodes for end states
diagramData.nodes.push(
{
id: 'end_completed',
type: 'end_node',
position: { x: steps.length * 200, y: 100 },
data: { label: 'Completed', status: 'final' }
},
{
id: 'end_rejected',
type: 'end_node',
position: { x: -100, y: 200 },
data: { label: 'Rejected', status: 'final' }
}
);

// Add edge from last step to completion
if (steps.length > 0) {
const lastStep = steps[steps.length - 1];
diagramData.edges.push({
id: `${lastStep.id}_complete`,
source: lastStep.id,
target: 'end_completed',
type: 'complete_edge',
data: {
label: 'Complete',
condition: 'workflow_completion'
}
});
}

return {
status: 'success',
data: diagramData,
message: `Workflow diagram generated with ${diagramData.nodes.length} nodes and ${diagramData.edges.length} edges`
};

} catch (error) {
Logger.log('Error in generateWorkflowDiagram: ' + error.toString());
return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
}
}

/**
* Audit workflow consistency and data integrity for a company
* @param {string} companyId - Company ID to audit
* @returns {Object} Response with audit results
*/
function auditWorkflowConsistency(companyId) {
try {
const auditResults = {
company_id: companyId,
audit_timestamp: new Date(),
summary: {
total_issues: 0,
critical_issues: 0,
warnings: 0,
recommendations: 0
},
ticket_types: [],
orphaned_records: [],
data_integrity: {
duplicate_ids: [],
missing_references: [],
invalid_data: []
},
performance_issues: []
};

// Get all ticket types for this company
const ticketTypes = getTicketTypes(null, companyId);
if (ticketTypes.status !== 'success') {
return { status: 'error', message: 'Cannot retrieve ticket types for audit' };
}

// Audit each ticket type
for (const ticketType of ticketTypes.data) {
const typeAudit = {
ticket_type_id: ticketType.id,
ticket_type_name: ticketType.name,
issues: [],
warnings: [],
recommendations: []
};

// 1. Validate workflow completeness
const workflowValidation = validateCompleteTicketWorkflow(ticketType.id, companyId);
if (workflowValidation.status === 'success') {
const validation = workflowValidation.data;

if (validation.overall_status === 'INVALID') {
typeAudit.issues.push(...validation.critical_issues);
auditResults.summary.critical_issues += validation.critical_issues.length;
}

typeAudit.warnings.push(...validation.warnings);
typeAudit.recommendations.push(...validation.recommendations);
auditResults.summary.warnings += validation.warnings.length;
auditResults.summary.recommendations += validation.recommendations.length;
}

// 2. Check for orphaned workflow steps
const workflowSteps = getWorkflowSteps(ticketType.id, companyId);
if (workflowSteps.status === 'success') {
for (const step of workflowSteps.data) {
// Check if step has orphaned approver assignments
const stepApprovers = getStepApprovers(step.id);
if (stepApprovers.status === 'success') {
for (const approver of stepApprovers.data) {
// Check if role exists
const role = getRoles(approver.role_id, companyId);
if (role.status !== 'success' || role.data.length === 0) {
auditResults.orphaned_records.push({
type: 'step_approver',
step_id: step.id,
role_id: approver.role_id,
issue: 'Role no longer exists'
});
}
}
}

// Check for orphaned SLAs
const stepSLAs = getStepSLAs(step.id);
if (stepSLAs.status === 'success' && stepSLAs.data.length > 1) {
typeAudit.issues.push(`Step '${step.name}' has multiple SLA configurations`);
auditResults.summary.critical_issues++;
}
}
}

// 3. Check custom field consistency
const customFields = getCustomFields(ticketType.id, companyId);
if (customFields.status === 'success') {
for (const field of customFields.data) {
if (field.type === 'dropdown' && field.dropdown_list_id) {
const dropdownOptions = getDropdownOptions(field.dropdown_list_id);
if (dropdownOptions.status !== 'success' || dropdownOptions.data.length === 0) {
typeAudit.warnings.push(`Dropdown field '${field.label}' has no options configured`);
auditResults.summary.warnings++;
}
}
}
}

// 4. Check for tickets in inconsistent states
const ss = openSpreadsheet();
const ticketsSheet = ss.getSheetByName('tickets');
if (ticketsSheet) {
const data = sheetValues(ticketsSheet);
const headers = data[0];
let inconsistentTickets = 0;

for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
const row = data[i];
const ticketData = {};
headers.forEach((header, index) => {
ticketData[header] = row[index];
});

if (ticketData.ticket_type_id === ticketType.id && ticketData.company_id === companyId) {
// Check if current step exists in workflow
if (ticketData.current_step_id) {
const stepExists = workflowSteps.data.some(step => step.id === ticketData.current_step_id);
if (!stepExists) {
inconsistentTickets++;
auditResults.data_integrity.missing_references.push({
type: 'ticket_step_reference',
ticket_id: ticketData.id,
missing_step_id: ticketData.current_step_id
});
}
}
}
}

if (inconsistentTickets > 0) {
typeAudit.issues.push(`${inconsistentTickets} tickets have invalid step references`);
auditResults.summary.critical_issues += inconsistentTickets;
}
}

auditResults.summary.total_issues += typeAudit.issues.length + typeAudit.warnings.length;
auditResults.ticket_types.push(typeAudit);
}

// 5. Check for duplicate IDs across sheets
const sheetsToCheck = ['workflow_steps', 'custom_fields', 'step_slas', 'user_role_assignments'];
for (const sheetName of sheetsToCheck) {
const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(sheetName);
if (sheet) {
const data = sheetValues(sheet);
const headers = data[0];
const idIndex = headers.indexOf('id');

if (idIndex !== -1) {
const ids = [];
for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
const id = data[i][idIndex];
if (id && ids.includes(id)) {
auditResults.data_integrity.duplicate_ids.push({
sheet: sheetName,
duplicate_id: id,
row: i + 1
});
}
if (id) ids.push(id);
}
}
}
}

// 6. Performance issue detection
if (auditResults.ticket_types.length > 50) {
auditResults.performance_issues.push('Large number of ticket types may impact performance');
}

// Calculate final summary
auditResults.summary.total_issues = auditResults.summary.critical_issues +
auditResults.summary.warnings +
auditResults.orphaned_records.length +
auditResults.data_integrity.duplicate_ids.length +
auditResults.data_integrity.missing_references.length;

return {
status: 'success',
data: auditResults,
message: `Audit complete: ${auditResults.summary.total_issues} total issues found`
};

} catch (error) {
Logger.log('Error in auditWorkflowConsistency: ' + error.toString());
return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
}
}

/**
* Get workflow performance metrics for analytics
* @param {string} ticketTypeId - Ticket type ID (optional)
* @param {string} companyId - Company ID
* @returns {Object} Response with performance metrics
*/
function getWorkflowPerformanceMetrics(ticketTypeId = null, companyId) {
try {
const ss = openSpreadsheet();
const ticketsSheet = ss.getSheetByName('tickets');

if (!ticketsSheet) {
return { status: 'error', message: 'Tickets sheet not found' };
}

const data = sheetValues(ticketsSheet);
const headers = data[0];
const now = new Date();
const thirtyDaysAgo = new Date(now.getTime() - (30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));

const metrics = {
company_id: companyId,
ticket_type_id: ticketTypeId,
analysis_period: {
start_date: thirtyDaysAgo,
end_date: now,
days: 30
},
volume_metrics: {
total_tickets: 0,
completed_tickets: 0,
cancelled_tickets: 0,
in_progress_tickets: 0,
overdue_tickets: 0
},
performance_metrics: {
avg_completion_time_hours: 0,
median_completion_time_hours: 0,
completion_rate_percent: 0,
sla_compliance_percent: 0,
avg_steps_per_ticket: 0
},
step_performance: [],
bottlenecks: [],
trends: {
daily_volume: [],
completion_trend: []
}
};

const relevantTickets = [];
const completionTimes = [];

// Collect relevant tickets
for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
const row = data[i];
const ticketData = {};

headers.forEach((header, index) => {
ticketData[header] = row[index];
});

// Filter by company and ticket type
if (ticketData.company_id !== companyId) continue;
if (ticketTypeId && ticketData.ticket_type_id !== ticketTypeId) continue;

// Filter by date range
const createdAt = new Date(ticketData.created_at);
if (createdAt < thirtyDaysAgo) continue;

relevantTickets.push(ticketData);

// Calculate completion time for completed tickets
if (ticketData.status === 'Completed' && ticketData.updated_at) {
const completionTime = new Date(ticketData.updated_at) - createdAt;
completionTimes.push(completionTime / (1000 * 60 * 60)); // Convert to hours
}
}

metrics.volume_metrics.total_tickets = relevantTickets.length;

// Calculate volume metrics
for (const ticket of relevantTickets) {
switch (ticket.status) {
case 'Completed':
metrics.volume_metrics.completed_tickets++;
break;
case 'Cancelled':
metrics.volume_metrics.cancelled_tickets++;
break;
default:
metrics.volume_metrics.in_progress_tickets++;

// Check if overdue
if (ticket.step_due_date && new Date(ticket.step_due_date) < now) {
metrics.volume_metrics.overdue_tickets++;
}
break;
}
}

// Calculate performance metrics
if (completionTimes.length > 0) {
const totalTime = completionTimes.reduce((sum, time) => sum + time, 0);
metrics.performance_metrics.avg_completion_time_hours = Math.round(totalTime / completionTimes.length * 100) / 100;

const sortedTimes = completionTimes.sort((a, b) => a - b);
const medianIndex = Math.floor(sortedTimes.length / 2);
metrics.performance_metrics.median_completion_time_hours = sortedTimes.length % 2 === 0
? (sortedTimes[medianIndex - 1] + sortedTimes[medianIndex]) / 2
: sortedTimes[medianIndex];
}

metrics.performance_metrics.completion_rate_percent = Math.round(
(metrics.volume_metrics.completed_tickets / Math.max(metrics.volume_metrics.total_tickets, 1)) * 100 * 100
) / 100;

// Calculate SLA compliance (simplified - would need more detailed SLA tracking)
const onTimeCompletions = metrics.volume_metrics.completed_tickets - metrics.volume_metrics.overdue_tickets;
metrics.performance_metrics.sla_compliance_percent = Math.round(
(onTimeCompletions / Math.max(metrics.volume_metrics.completed_tickets, 1)) * 100 * 100
) / 100;

// Get workflow steps for analysis
if (ticketTypeId) {
const workflowSteps = getWorkflowSteps(ticketTypeId, companyId);
if (workflowSteps.status === 'success') {
metrics.performance_metrics.avg_steps_per_ticket = workflowSteps.data.length;

// Analyze step performance (simplified)
for (const step of workflowSteps.data) {
const stepMetrics = {
step_id: step.id,
step_name: step.name,
tickets_processed: 0,
avg_processing_time_hours: 0,
bottleneck_score: 0
};

// Count tickets that passed through this step (simplified analysis)
let stepTicketCount = 0;
for (const ticket of relevantTickets) {
if (ticket.status === 'Completed' || ticket.current_step_id !== step.id) {
stepTicketCount++;
}
}

stepMetrics.tickets_processed = stepTicketCount;
stepMetrics.avg_processing_time_hours = stepTicketCount > 0
? Math.round((totalTime / stepTicketCount / workflowSteps.data.length) * 100) / 100
: 0;

metrics.step_performance.push(stepMetrics);
}
}
}

// Identify bottlenecks (simplified analysis)
const currentStepCounts = {};
for (const ticket of relevantTickets) {
if (ticket.status !== 'Completed' && ticket.status !== 'Cancelled' && ticket.current_step_id) {
currentStepCounts[ticket.current_step_id] = (currentStepCounts[ticket.current_step_id] || 0) + 1;
}
}

for (const [stepId, count] of Object.entries(currentStepCounts)) {
if (count > metrics.volume_metrics.in_progress_tickets * 0.3) { // More than 30% of tickets
const step = metrics.step_performance.find(s => s.step_id === stepId);
metrics.bottlenecks.push({
step_id: stepId,
step_name: step ? step.step_name : 'Unknown Step',
tickets_waiting: count,
percentage_of_total: Math.round((count / metrics.volume_metrics.in_progress_tickets) * 100)
});
}
}

return {
status: 'success',
data: metrics,
message: `Performance metrics calculated for ${metrics.volume_metrics.total_tickets} tickets`
};

} catch (error) {
Logger.log('Error in getWorkflowPerformanceMetrics: ' + error.toString());
return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
}
}

/**
* Optimize workflow step order based on historical performance
* @param {string} ticketTypeId - Ticket type ID to optimize
* @returns {Object} Response with optimization recommendations
*/
function optimizeWorkflowStepOrder(ticketTypeId) {
try {
// Get current workflow steps
const workflowSteps = getWorkflowSteps(ticketTypeId);
if (workflowSteps.status !== 'success' || workflowSteps.data.length === 0) {
return { status: 'error', message: 'No workflow steps found for optimization' };
}

const currentSteps = workflowSteps.data;
const optimization = {
ticket_type_id: ticketTypeId,
current_order: currentSteps.map(step => ({
id: step.id,
name: step.name,
sort_order: step.sort_order,
step_type: step.step_type
})),
analysis: {
total_steps: currentSteps.length,
parallel_opportunities: [],
sequential_requirements: [],
bottleneck_steps: []
},
recommendations: [],
estimated_improvement: {
time_reduction_percent: 0,
efficiency_gain: 'Low'
}
};

// Analyze step dependencies and requirements
for (let i = 0; i < currentSteps.length; i++) {
const step = currentSteps[i];

// Check for steps that could potentially run in parallel
if (step.step_type === 'approval' && i < currentSteps.length - 1) {
const nextStep = currentSteps[i + 1];

if (nextStep.step_type === 'approval') {
// Two approval steps in sequence - check if they could be parallel
const stepApprovers = getStepApprovers(step.id);
const nextStepApprovers = getStepApprovers(nextStep.id);

if (stepApprovers.status === 'success' && nextStepApprovers.status === 'success') {
const stepRoles = stepApprovers.data.map(a => a.role_id);
const nextStepRoles = nextStepApprovers.data.map(a => a.role_id);

// If different roles, could potentially be parallel
const hasOverlap = stepRoles.some(role => nextStepRoles.includes(role));
if (!hasOverlap) {
optimization.analysis.parallel_opportunities.push({
step1: { id: step.id, name: step.name },
step2: { id: nextStep.id, name: nextStep.name },
reason: 'Different approver roles - no dependency conflict'
});
}
}
}
}

// Identify potential bottlenecks
const stepApprovers = getStepApprovers(step.id);
if (stepApprovers.status === 'success') {
if (stepApprovers.data.length === 1) {
optimization.analysis.bottleneck_steps.push({
step_id: step.id,
step_name: step.name,
issue: 'Single approver - potential bottleneck',
recommendation: 'Consider adding additional approvers'
});
}
}

// Check for external integrations that might be moved earlier
if (step.external_app_url && i > 0) {
optimization.recommendations.push({
type: 'MOVE_EXTERNAL_STEP',
step_id: step.id,
step_name: step.name,
current_position: i + 1,
recommended_position: 1,
reason: 'External steps should typically be completed early to avoid delays'
});
}

// Check SLA configurations for optimization
const stepSLAs = getStepSLAs(step.id);
if (stepSLAs.status === 'success' && stepSLAs.data.length > 0) {
const sla = stepSLAs.data[0];
if (sla.duration > 24 && sla.unit === 'hours') {
optimization.recommendations.push({
type: 'OPTIMIZE_SLA',
step_id: step.id,
step_name: step.name,
current_sla: `${sla.duration} ${sla.unit}`,
recommendation: 'Consider breaking down into smaller steps or reducing SLA duration'
});
}
}
}

// Generate parallel processing recommendations
if (optimization.analysis.parallel_opportunities.length > 0) {
optimization.recommendations.push({
type: 'IMPLEMENT_PARALLEL_PROCESSING',
opportunities: optimization.analysis.parallel_opportunities.length,
potential_improvement: 'High',
description: 'Consider implementing parallel approval steps to reduce overall processing time'
});

optimization.estimated_improvement.time_reduction_percent =
Math.min(30, optimization.analysis.parallel_opportunities.length * 15);
optimization.estimated_improvement.efficiency_gain = 'High';
}

// Check for redundant steps
const stepNames = currentSteps.map(s => s.name.toLowerCase());
const duplicateNames = stepNames.filter((name, index) => stepNames.indexOf(name) !== index);
if (duplicateNames.length > 0) {
optimization.recommendations.push({
type: 'CONSOLIDATE_SIMILAR_STEPS',
duplicate_steps: duplicateNames,
recommendation: 'Review steps with similar names for potential consolidation'
});
}

// Overall recommendations
if (currentSteps.length > 7) {
optimization.recommendations.push({
type: 'SIMPLIFY_WORKFLOW',
current_steps: currentSteps.length,
recommendation: 'Consider consolidating workflow steps - complex workflows may cause delays'
});
}

if (optimization.recommendations.length === 0) {
optimization.recommendations.push({
type: 'NO_OPTIMIZATION_NEEDED',
message: 'Current workflow structure appears to be well-optimized'
});
optimization.estimated_improvement.efficiency_gain = 'Already Optimized';
}

return {
status: 'success',
data: optimization,
message: `Optimization analysis complete: ${optimization.recommendations.length} recommendations generated`
};

} catch (error) {
Logger.log('Error in optimizeWorkflowStepOrder: ' + error.toString());
return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
}
}

/**
* Monitor workflow bottlenecks across all workflows for a company
* @param {string} companyId - Company ID to monitor
* @returns {Object} Response with bottleneck analysis
*/
function monitorWorkflowBottlenecks(companyId) {
try {
const ss = openSpreadsheet();
const ticketsSheet = ss.getSheetByName('tickets');

if (!ticketsSheet) {
return { status: 'error', message: 'Tickets sheet not found' };
}

const bottleneckAnalysis = {
company_id: companyId,
analysis_timestamp: new Date(),
overall_health: 'Good',
critical_bottlenecks: [],
step_analysis: [],
user_workload: [],
recommendations: []
};

// Get all active tickets for company
const data = sheetValues(ticketsSheet);
const headers = data[0];
const activeTickets = [];

for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
const row = data[i];
const ticketData = {};

headers.forEach((header, index) => {
ticketData[header] = row[index];
});

if (ticketData.company_id === companyId &&
ticketData.is_active &&
!ticketData.cancelled_at &&
ticketData.status !== 'Completed') {
activeTickets.push(ticketData);
}
}

if (activeTickets.length === 0) {
return {
status: 'success',
data: {
...bottleneckAnalysis,
message: 'No active tickets found for analysis'
}
};
}

// Group tickets by current step
const stepGroups = {};
const overdueTickets = [];
const now = new Date();

for (const ticket of activeTickets) {
if (ticket.current_step_id) {
if (!stepGroups[ticket.current_step_id]) {
stepGroups[ticket.current_step_id] = [];
}
stepGroups[ticket.current_step_id].push(ticket);

// Check for overdue tickets
if (ticket.step_due_date && new Date(ticket.step_due_date) < now) {
overdueTickets.push(ticket);
}
}
}

// Analyze each step for bottlenecks
for (const [stepId, tickets] of Object.entries(stepGroups)) {
const stepDetails = getWorkflowStepById(stepId);
let stepName = 'Unknown Step';
let ticketTypeName = 'Unknown Type';

if (stepDetails.status === 'success') {
stepName = stepDetails.data.name;

// Get ticket type name
if (tickets.length > 0) {
const ticketType = getTicketTypes(tickets[0].ticket_type_id, companyId);
if (ticketType.status === 'success' && ticketType.data.length > 0) {
ticketTypeName = ticketType.data[0].name;
}
}
}

const avgAge = tickets.reduce((sum, ticket) => {
const age = (now - new Date(ticket.created_at)) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24); // Days
return sum + age;
}, 0) / tickets.length;

const overdueCount = tickets.filter(ticket =>
ticket.step_due_date && new Date(ticket.step_due_date) < now
).length;

const stepAnalysis = {
step_id: stepId,
step_name: stepName,
ticket_type_name: ticketTypeName,
waiting_tickets: tickets.length,
overdue_tickets: overdueCount,
avg_age_days: Math.round(avgAge * 100) / 100,
bottleneck_score: 0,
severity: 'Normal'
};

// Calculate bottleneck score
let score = 0;
score += tickets.length * 2; // Queue length
score += overdueCount * 5; // Overdue penalty
score += Math.max(0, avgAge - 3) * 2; // Age penalty (after 3 days)

stepAnalysis.bottleneck_score = Math.round(score);

// Determine severity
if (score > 20) {
stepAnalysis.severity = 'Critical';
bottleneckAnalysis.critical_bottlenecks.push(stepAnalysis);
} else if (score > 10) {
stepAnalysis.severity = 'Warning';
}

bottleneckAnalysis.step_analysis.push(stepAnalysis);
}

// Analyze user workloads
const userWorkloads = {};

for (const step of bottleneckAnalysis.step_analysis) {
const stepApprovers = getStepApprovers(step.step_id);
if (stepApprovers.status === 'success') {
for (const approver of stepApprovers.data) {
// Get users assigned to this role
const roleUsers = getUsersForStep(step.step_id);
if (roleUsers.status === 'success') {
for (const user of roleUsers.data) {
if (!userWorkloads[user.user_id]) {
userWorkloads[user.user_id] = {
user_id: user.user_id,
pending_tickets: 0,
overdue_tickets: 0,
steps_assigned: []
};
}

userWorkloads[user.user_id].pending_tickets += step.waiting_tickets;
userWorkloads[user.user_id].overdue_tickets += step.overdue_tickets;
userWorkloads[user.user_id].steps_assigned.push({
step_id: step.step_id,
step_name: step.step_name,
waiting_count: step.waiting_tickets
});
}
}
}
}
}

// Convert to array and sort by workload
bottleneckAnalysis.user_workload = Object.values(userWorkloads)
.sort((a, b) => b.pending_tickets - a.pending_tickets)
.slice(0, 10); // Top 10 users by workload

// Generate recommendations
if (bottleneckAnalysis.critical_bottlenecks.length > 0) {
bottleneckAnalysis.overall_health = 'Critical';

bottleneckAnalysis.recommendations.push({
priority: 'Critical',
type: 'ADDRESS_CRITICAL_BOTTLENECKS',
message: `${bottleneckAnalysis.critical_bottlenecks.length} critical bottlenecks require immediate attention`,
steps: bottleneckAnalysis.critical_bottlenecks.map(b => b.step_name)
});
}

if (overdueTickets.length > activeTickets.length * 0.1) { // More than 10% overdue
bottleneckAnalysis.recommendations.push({
priority: 'High',
type: 'REDUCE_OVERDUE_TICKETS',
message: `${overdueTickets.length} tickets are overdue (${Math.round(overdueTickets.length/activeTickets.length*100)}% of
ctive tickets)`,
action: 'Review SLA configurations and approver availability'
});

if (bottleneckAnalysis.overall_health === 'Good') {
bottleneckAnalysis.overall_health = 'Warning';
}
}

// Check for overloaded users
const overloadedUsers = bottleneckAnalysis.user_workload.filter(user => user.pending_tickets > 20);
if (overloadedUsers.length > 0) {
bottleneckAnalysis.recommendations.push({
priority: 'Medium',
type: 'REDISTRIBUTE_WORKLOAD',
message: `${overloadedUsers.length} users have high pending ticket counts`,
action: 'Consider adding more approvers or redistributing responsibilities'
});
}

if (bottleneckAnalysis.recommendations.length === 0) {
bottleneckAnalysis.recommendations.push({
priority: 'Info',
type: 'WORKFLOW_HEALTHY',
message: 'No significant bottlenecks detected'
});
}

return {
status: 'success',
data: bottleneckAnalysis,
message: `Bottleneck analysis complete: ${bottleneckAnalysis.step_analysis.length} steps analyzed,
{bottleneckAnalysis.critical_bottlenecks.length} critical issues found`
};

} catch (error) {
Logger.log('Error in monitorWorkflowBottlenecks: ' + error.toString());
return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
}
}

/**
* Generate workflow compliance report for audit and regulatory purposes
* @param {string} companyId - Company ID
* @param {Object} dateRange - Date range for report {start_date, end_date}
* @returns {Object} Response with compliance report data
*/
function generateWorkflowComplianceReport(companyId, dateRange) {
try {
const startDate = new Date(dateRange.start_date);
const endDate = new Date(dateRange.end_date);

const complianceReport = {
company_id: companyId,
report_period: {
start_date: startDate,
end_date: endDate,
days: Math.ceil((endDate - startDate) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24))
},
generated_at: new Date(),
executive_summary: {
total_tickets_processed: 0,
sla_compliance_rate: 0,
avg_completion_time: 0,
audit_trail_completeness: 0,
overall_compliance_score: 0
},
ticket_type_breakdown: [],
sla_performance: {
met_sla: 0,
missed_sla: 0,
no_sla_defined: 0,
compliance_by_step: []
},
audit_compliance: {
tickets_with_complete_history: 0,
tickets_with_gaps: 0,
missing_approvals: 0,
unauthorized_actions: []
},
regulatory_metrics: {
approval_chain_integrity: 100,
segregation_of_duties: 100,
audit_trail_integrity: 100
},
recommendations: []
};

// Get all tickets in date range
const ss = openSpreadsheet();
const ticketsSheet = ss.getSheetByName('tickets');

if (!ticketsSheet) {
return { status: 'error', message: 'Tickets sheet not found' };
}

const data = sheetValues(ticketsSheet);
const headers = data[0];
const relevantTickets = [];

for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
const row = data[i];
const ticketData = {};

headers.forEach((header, index) => {
ticketData[header] = row[index];
});

if (ticketData.company_id === companyId) {
const createdAt = new Date(ticketData.created_at);
if (createdAt >= startDate && createdAt <= endDate) {
relevantTickets.push(ticketData);
}
}
}

complianceReport.executive_summary.total_tickets_processed = relevantTickets.length;

if (relevantTickets.length === 0) {
return {
status: 'success',
data: complianceReport,
message: 'No tickets found in specified date range'
};
}

// Analyze tickets by type
const ticketTypeGroups = {};
let totalCompletionTime = 0;
let completedCount = 0;

for (const ticket of relevantTickets) {
if (!ticketTypeGroups[ticket.ticket_type_id]) {
ticketTypeGroups[ticket.ticket_type_id] = {
ticket_type_id: ticket.ticket_type_id,
tickets: []
};
}
ticketTypeGroups[ticket.ticket_type_id].tickets.push(ticket);

// Calculate completion time for completed tickets
if (ticket.status === 'Completed' && ticket.updated_at) {
const completionTime = (new Date(ticket.updated_at) - new Date(ticket.created_at)) / (1000 * 60 * 60);
totalCompletionTime += completionTime;
completedCount++;
}
}

complianceReport.executive_summary.avg_completion_time = completedCount > 0
? Math.round(totalCompletionTime / completedCount * 100) / 100
: 0;

// Analyze each ticket type
for (const [ticketTypeId, group] of Object.entries(ticketTypeGroups)) {
const ticketType = getTicketTypes(ticketTypeId, companyId);
const typeName = (ticketType.status === 'success' && ticketType.data.length > 0)
? ticketType.data[0].name : 'Unknown Type';

const typeMetrics = {
ticket_type_id: ticketTypeId,
ticket_type_name: typeName,
total_tickets: group.tickets.length,
completed_tickets: 0,
cancelled_tickets: 0,
in_progress_tickets: 0,
sla_compliance_rate: 0,
audit_compliance_issues: 0
};

let slaMetTickets = 0;
let slaMissedTickets = 0;

for (const ticket of group.tickets) {
// Count by status
switch (ticket.status) {
case 'Completed':
typeMetrics.completed_tickets++;
break;
case 'Cancelled':
typeMetrics.cancelled_tickets++;
break;
default:
typeMetrics.in_progress_tickets++;
break;
}

// Check SLA compliance (simplified)
if (ticket.step_due_date) {
const dueDate = new Date(ticket.step_due_date);
const completionDate = ticket.updated_at ? new Date(ticket.updated_at) : new Date();

if (ticket.status === 'Completed') {
if (completionDate <= dueDate) {
slaMetTickets++;
} else {
slaMissedTickets++;
}
}
}

// Check audit trail completeness
const historyResult = getTicketHistory(ticket.id);
if (historyResult.status === 'success') {
const history = historyResult.data;
if (history.length < 2) { // At least creation + one action
typeMetrics.audit_compliance_issues++;
}
} else {
typeMetrics.audit_compliance_issues++;
}
}

// Calculate SLA compliance rate for this type
const totalSLATickets = slaMetTickets + slaMissedTickets;
typeMetrics.sla_compliance_rate = totalSLATickets > 0
? Math.round((slaMetTickets / totalSLATickets) * 100 * 100) / 100
: 0;

complianceReport.ticket_type_breakdown.push(typeMetrics);
}

// Calculate overall SLA performance
const totalSLAMet = complianceReport.ticket_type_breakdown.reduce((sum, type) =>
sum + (type.sla_compliance_rate * type.completed_tickets / 100), 0
);
const totalCompleted = complianceReport.ticket_type_breakdown.reduce((sum, type) =>
sum + type.completed_tickets, 0
);

complianceReport.executive_summary.sla_compliance_rate = totalCompleted > 0
? Math.round((totalSLAMet / totalCompleted) * 100 * 100) / 100
: 0;

// Calculate audit trail completeness
const totalAuditIssues = complianceReport.ticket_type_breakdown.reduce((sum, type) =>
sum + type.audit_compliance_issues, 0
);
complianceReport.executive_summary.audit_trail_completeness = Math.round(
((relevantTickets.length - totalAuditIssues) / relevantTickets.length) * 100 * 100
) / 100;

// Calculate overall compliance score
const slaWeight = 0.4;
const auditWeight = 0.6;
complianceReport.executive_summary.overall_compliance_score = Math.round(
(complianceReport.executive_summary.sla_compliance_rate * slaWeight +
complianceReport.executive_summary.audit_trail_completeness * auditWeight) * 100
) / 100;

// Generate recommendations based on findings
if (complianceReport.executive_summary.sla_compliance_rate < 90) {
complianceReport.recommendations.push({
priority: 'High',
category: 'SLA Compliance',
issue: `SLA compliance rate is ${complianceReport.executive_summary.sla_compliance_rate}% (below 90% threshold)`,
recommendation: 'Review and optimize workflow steps, consider additional approvers or extended SLA timeframes'
});
}

if (complianceReport.executive_summary.audit_trail_completeness < 95) {
complianceReport.recommendations.push({
priority: 'Critical',
category: 'Audit Compliance',
issue: `${totalAuditIssues} tickets have incomplete audit trails`,
recommendation: 'Implement mandatory comment requirements and ensure all actions are properly logged'
});
}

if (complianceReport.executive_summary.overall_compliance_score >= 95) {
complianceReport.recommendations.push({
priority: 'Info',
category: 'Overall',
issue: 'Excellent compliance performance',
recommendation: 'Maintain current processes and consider sharing best practices across the organization'
});
}

return {
status: 'success',
data: complianceReport,
message: `Compliance report generated for ${relevantTickets.length} tickets with
{complianceReport.executive_summary.overall_compliance_score}% overall compliance score`
};

} catch (error) {
Logger.log('Error in generateWorkflowComplianceReport: ' + error.toString());
return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
}
}

// =================================================================================
// END OF PHASE 1D: INTEGRATION & ADMIN FUNCTIONS
// Total functions added: 8
// Version: 6.4

// =================================================================================
// PHASE 10.0: UNIVERSAL ENTITY ARCHITECTURE - USER/ROLE MANAGEMENT
// Universal entity system enabling custom fields for users, roles, and tickets
// Implementation Date: September 27, 2025
// Functions: 15 new + 3 enhanced existing functions
// =================================================================================

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * UNIVERSAL ENTITY CUSTOM FIELDS ENHANCEMENT
 * Enhanced existing custom field functions to support multiple entity types
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Custom field definitions for an entity type (ticket type, user profile type, role type).
 * v9.1: ids compared as strings (A8); a blank entity_category counts as 'ticket'; inactive fields skipped.
 * @param {string} entityTypeId - ticket_type_id column (reused for every entity category)
 * @param {string} entityCategory - 'ticket' | 'user_profile' | 'role' | 'workflow_step'
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: fields sorted by sort_order, message }
 */
function getCustomFields(entityTypeId, entityCategory = 'ticket') {
  try {
    const table = readTable(SHEETS.CUSTOM_FIELDS);
    if (!table) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Custom fields sheet not found' };
    }

    const category = entityCategory || 'ticket';
    const customFields = table.records
      .filter(field => isActiveFlag(field.is_active) && !field.deactivated_at)
      .filter(field => !entityTypeId || sameId(field.ticket_type_id, entityTypeId))
      .filter(field => (field.entity_category || 'ticket') === category)
      .sort((a, b) => (Number(a.sort_order) || 0) - (Number(b.sort_order) || 0));

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: customFields,
      message: `Retrieved ${customFields.length} custom fields for ${category} entity type ${entityTypeId}`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getCustomFields: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Create custom field for any entity type (enhanced from original)
 * @param {string} entityTypeId - ID of entity type
 * @param {Object} fieldData - Field configuration
 * @param {string} entityCategory - Category: 'ticket', 'user_profile', 'role', 'workflow_step'
 * @returns {Object} Response with created field data
 */
function createCustomField(entityTypeId, fieldData, entityCategory = 'ticket') {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.CUSTOM_FIELDS);

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Custom fields sheet not found' };
    }

    // Generate unique field ID
    const fieldId = generateId('cf');
    const currentUser = getCurrentUserId();
    const timestamp = getCurrentTimestamp();

    const newField = {
      id: fieldId,
      ticket_type_id: entityTypeId, // Reused for all entity types
      entity_category: entityCategory,
      name: fieldData.name,
      label: fieldData.label,
      type: fieldData.type,
      is_required: fieldData.is_required || false,
      is_hidden: fieldData.is_hidden || false,
      sort_order: fieldData.sort_order || 1,
      dropdown_list_id: fieldData.dropdown_list_id || null,
      depends_on_field_id: fieldData.depends_on_field_id || null,
      is_active: true,
      created_at: timestamp,
      created_by: currentUser,
      updated_at: timestamp,
      updated_by: currentUser,
      deactivated_at: null,
      deactivated_by: null,
      deactivation_reason: null
    };

    // Add to sheet
    const headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
    const newRow = headers.map(header => newField[header] || '');
    sheet.appendRow(newRow);
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));

    // Log admin action
    logAdminAction(currentUser, 'CREATE_CUSTOM_FIELD', 'custom_field', fieldId, {
      entity_category: entityCategory,
      entity_type_id: entityTypeId,
      field_name: fieldData.name,
      field_type: fieldData.type
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: newField,
      message: `Custom field created for ${entityCategory} entity type ${entityTypeId}`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in createCustomField: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Set custom field value for any entity (enhanced from original)
 * @param {string} entityId - ID of entity instance (ticket_id, user_id, role_id)
 * @param {string} fieldId - Custom field ID
 * @param {any} value - Field value
 * @param {string} entityCategory - Category: 'ticket', 'user_profile', 'role'
 * @returns {Object} Response with operation status
 */
function setCustomFieldValue(entityId, fieldId, value, entityCategory = 'ticket') {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.CUSTOM_FIELD_VALUES);

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Custom field values sheet not found' };
    }

    const data = sheetValues(sheet);
    const headers = data[0];
    const rows = data.slice(1);

    const entityIdIndex = headers.indexOf('ticket_id'); // Reused for all entities
    const entityCategoryIndex = headers.indexOf('entity_category');
    const fieldIdIndex = headers.indexOf('custom_field_id');
    const activeIndex = headers.indexOf('is_active');

    // Find existing value
    const existingRowIndex = rows.findIndex(row =>
      row[entityIdIndex] === entityId &&
      row[entityCategoryIndex] === entityCategory &&
      row[fieldIdIndex] === fieldId &&
      row[activeIndex] === true
    );

    const currentUser = getCurrentUserId();
    const timestamp = getCurrentTimestamp();

    if (existingRowIndex !== -1) {
      // Update existing value
      const rowNumber = existingRowIndex + 2; // +1 for header, +1 for 0-based index

      // Update value based on field type
      const textIndex = headers.indexOf('text_value');
      const numberIndex = headers.indexOf('number_value');
      const dateIndex = headers.indexOf('date_value');
      const startDateIndex = headers.indexOf('start_date_value');
      const endDateIndex = headers.indexOf('end_date_value');
      const dropdownIndex = headers.indexOf('dropdown_option_id');
      const updatedAtIndex = headers.indexOf('updated_at');
      const updatedByIndex = headers.indexOf('updated_by');

      // Clear all value columns first
      sheet.getRange(rowNumber, textIndex + 1).setValue('');
      invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
      sheet.getRange(rowNumber, numberIndex + 1).setValue('');
      invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
      sheet.getRange(rowNumber, dateIndex + 1).setValue('');
      invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
      sheet.getRange(rowNumber, startDateIndex + 1).setValue('');
      invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
      sheet.getRange(rowNumber, endDateIndex + 1).setValue('');
      invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
      sheet.getRange(rowNumber, dropdownIndex + 1).setValue('');
      invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));

      // Set appropriate value column based on type
      if (typeof value === 'object' && value.start_date && value.end_date) {
        // Date range value
        sheet.getRange(rowNumber, startDateIndex + 1).setValue(value.start_date);
        invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
        sheet.getRange(rowNumber, endDateIndex + 1).setValue(value.end_date);
        invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
      } else if (typeof value === 'number') {
        sheet.getRange(rowNumber, numberIndex + 1).setValue(value);
        invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
      } else if (value && value.toString().match(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/)) {
        sheet.getRange(rowNumber, dateIndex + 1).setValue(value);
        invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
      } else if (value && value.toString().startsWith('opt_')) {
        sheet.getRange(rowNumber, dropdownIndex + 1).setValue(value);
        invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
      } else {
        sheet.getRange(rowNumber, textIndex + 1).setValue(value || '');
        invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
      }

      // Update audit fields
      sheet.getRange(rowNumber, updatedAtIndex + 1).setValue(timestamp);
      invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
      sheet.getRange(rowNumber, updatedByIndex + 1).setValue(currentUser);
      invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));

    } else {
      // Create new value record
      const valueId = generateId('cfv');

      const newValue = {
        id: valueId,
        ticket_id: entityId, // Reused for all entities
        entity_category: entityCategory,
        custom_field_id: fieldId,
        text_value: '',
        number_value: '',
        date_value: '',
        start_date_value: '',
        end_date_value: '',
        dropdown_option_id: '',
        is_active: true,
        created_at: timestamp,
        created_by: currentUser,
        updated_at: timestamp,
        updated_by: currentUser,
        deleted_at: null,
        deleted_by: null,
        deletion_reason: null
      };

      // Set appropriate value based on type
      if (typeof value === 'object' && value.start_date && value.end_date) {
        newValue.start_date_value = value.start_date;
        newValue.end_date_value = value.end_date;
      } else if (typeof value === 'number') {
        newValue.number_value = value;
      } else if (value && value.toString().match(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/)) {
        newValue.date_value = value;
      } else if (value && value.toString().startsWith('opt_')) {
        newValue.dropdown_option_id = value;
      } else {
        newValue.text_value = value || '';
      }

      const newRow = headers.map(header => newValue[header] || '');
      sheet.appendRow(newRow);
      invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
    }

    return {
      status: 'success',
      message: `Custom field value set for ${entityCategory} entity ${entityId}`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in setCustomFieldValue: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * USER PROFILE TYPE MANAGEMENT
 * Functions for managing configurable user profile types
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Get user profile types.
 * @param {string} companyId - Company filter (optional)
 * @param {boolean} includeInactive - v9.0: true also returns deactivated types (admin list toggle)
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: types[], message }
 */
function getUserProfileTypes(companyId = null, includeInactive = false) {
  try {
    const table = readTable(SHEETS.USER_PROFILE_TYPES);
    if (!table) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'User profile types sheet not found' };
    }

    const profileTypes = table.records
      .filter(row => includeInactive || isActiveFlag(row.is_active))
      .filter(row => !companyId || sameId(row.company_id, companyId));

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: profileTypes,
      message: `Retrieved ${profileTypes.length} user profile types`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getUserProfileTypes: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Create a user profile type (v9.0: is_active from the request, default true; header-driven row).
 * @param {Object} profileTypeData - { name, description, code, company_id, is_active }
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: type, message }
 */
function createUserProfileType(profileTypeData) {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.USER_PROFILE_TYPES) || ensureTableSheet(SHEETS.USER_PROFILE_TYPES);

    if (!profileTypeData.name) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'User profile type name is required' };
    }

    const profileTypeId = generateId('upt');
    const newProfileType = {
      id: profileTypeId,
      name: profileTypeData.name,
      description: profileTypeData.description || '',
      code: profileTypeData.code || '',
      company_id: profileTypeData.company_id || '',
      ...createAuditFields(),
      is_active: profileTypeData.is_active === undefined ? true : isActiveFlag(profileTypeData.is_active)
    };

    sheet.appendRow(rowFromRecord(sheetHeaders(sheet), newProfileType));
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));

    logAdminAction(getCurrentUserId(), 'CREATE_USER_PROFILE_TYPE', 'user_profile_type', profileTypeId, {
      name: profileTypeData.name,
      code: profileTypeData.code,
      company_id: profileTypeData.company_id,
      is_active: newProfileType.is_active
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: newProfileType,
      message: `User profile type created: ${profileTypeData.name}`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in createUserProfileType: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Update a user profile type (v9.0: is_active writable, so an inactive type can be reactivated).
 * @param {string} profileTypeId - Profile type ID
 * @param {Object} updateData - { name, description, code, company_id, is_active }
 * @returns {Object} { status, data, message }
 */
function updateUserProfileType(profileTypeId, updateData) {
  try {
    const changes = pickDefined(updateData, ['name', 'description', 'code', 'company_id']);
    if (updateData.is_active !== undefined) {
      Object.assign(changes, isActiveFlag(updateData.is_active) ? reactivateAuditFields() : deactivateAuditFields('admin_request'));
    }

    const result = updateRecordFields(SHEETS.USER_PROFILE_TYPES, profileTypeId, { ...changes, ...updateAuditFields() });
    if (!result) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'User profile type not found' };
    }

    logAdminAction(getCurrentUserId(), 'UPDATE_USER_PROFILE_TYPE', 'user_profile_type', profileTypeId, updateData);

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: result.after,
      message: `User profile type updated: ${profileTypeId}`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in updateUserProfileType: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

      .filter(row => includeInactive || isActiveFlag(row.is_active))

    return {

      data: roleTypes,

    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getRoleTypes: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Create a role type (v9.0: is_active from the request, default true; header-driven row).
 * @param {Object} roleTypeData - { name, description, code, company_id, is_active, permission_level,
 *   can_approve_steps, max_approval_amount, requires_certification }
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: type, message }
 */
function createRoleType(roleTypeData) {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ROLE_TYPES) || ensureTableSheet(SHEETS.ROLE_TYPES);

    if (!roleTypeData.name) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Role type name is required' };
    }

    const roleTypeId = generateId('rt');
    const newRoleType = {
      id: roleTypeId,
      name: roleTypeData.name,
      description: roleTypeData.description || '',
      code: roleTypeData.code || '',
      company_id: roleTypeData.company_id || '',
      ...pickDefined(roleTypeData, ['permission_level', 'can_approve_steps', 'max_approval_amount', 'requires_certification']),
      ...createAuditFields(),
      is_active: roleTypeData.is_active === undefined ? true : isActiveFlag(roleTypeData.is_active)
    };

    sheet.appendRow(rowFromRecord(sheetHeaders(sheet), newRoleType));
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));

    logAdminAction(getCurrentUserId(), 'CREATE_ROLE_TYPE', 'role_type', roleTypeId, {
      name: roleTypeData.name,
      code: roleTypeData.code,
      company_id: roleTypeData.company_id,
      is_active: newRoleType.is_active
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: newRoleType,
      message: `Role type created: ${roleTypeData.name}`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in createRoleType: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Update a role type (v9.0: is_active writable, so an inactive type can be reactivated).
 * @param {string} roleTypeId - Role type ID
 * @param {Object} updateData - { name, description, code, company_id, is_active, permission_level, ... }
 * @returns {Object} { status, data, message }
 */
function updateRoleType(roleTypeId, updateData) {
  try {
    const changes = pickDefined(updateData, ['name', 'description', 'code', 'company_id',
      'permission_level', 'can_approve_steps', 'max_approval_amount', 'requires_certification']);
    if (updateData.is_active !== undefined) {
      Object.assign(changes, isActiveFlag(updateData.is_active) ? reactivateAuditFields() : deactivateAuditFields('admin_request'));
    }

    const result = updateRecordFields(SHEETS.ROLE_TYPES, roleTypeId, { ...changes, ...updateAuditFields() });
    if (!result) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Role type not found' };
    }

    logAdminAction(getCurrentUserId(), 'UPDATE_ROLE_TYPE', 'role_type', roleTypeId, updateData);

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: result.after,
      message: `Role type updated: ${roleTypeId}`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in updateRoleType: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * USER PROFILE MANAGEMENT
 * Functions for managing individual user profiles with approval relationships
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Get active user profiles (v9.0: a blank is_active counts as active).
 * user_profiles has no company column - a user's companies come from their role assignments.
 * @param {string} companyId - When given, only users with an active assignment in that company
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: profiles[], message }
 */
function getUserProfiles(companyId = null) {
  try {
    const table = readTable(SHEETS.USER_PROFILES);
    if (!table) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'User profiles sheet not found' };
    }

    let profiles = table.records.filter(row => isActiveFlag(row.is_active));

    if (companyId) {
      const assignments = readTable(SHEETS.USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS);
      const now = new Date();
      const userIds = new Set((assignments ? assignments.records : [])
        .filter(a => sameId(a.company_id, companyId) && isAssignmentActive(a, now))
        .map(a => String(a.user_id)));
      profiles = profiles.filter(p => userIds.has(String(p.user_id)));
    }

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: profiles,
      message: `Retrieved ${profiles.length} user profiles`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getUserProfiles: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Create user profile
 * @param {Object} profileData - User profile data
 * @returns {Object} Response with created profile data
 */
function createUserProfile(profileData) {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.USER_PROFILES || 'user_profiles');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'User profiles sheet not found' };
    }

    const auditFields = createAuditFields();

    const newProfile = {
      user_id: profileData.user_id,
      user_profile_type_id: profileData.user_profile_type_id,
      display_name: profileData.display_name,
      email: profileData.email,
      immediate_approver_id: profileData.immediate_approver_id || null,
      backup_approver_id: profileData.backup_approver_id || null,
      status: profileData.status || 'active',
      hire_date: profileData.hire_date || null,
      ...auditFields
    };

    // Add to sheet
    const headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
    const newRow = headers.map(header => newProfile[header] || '');
    sheet.appendRow(newRow);
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));

    // Log admin action
    logAdminAction(getCurrentUserId(), 'CREATE_USER_PROFILE', 'user_profile', profileData.user_id, {
      display_name: profileData.display_name,
      email: profileData.email,
      user_profile_type_id: profileData.user_profile_type_id
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: newProfile,
      message: `User profile created: ${profileData.display_name}`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in createUserProfile: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Update user profile
 * @param {string} userId - User ID to update
 * @param {Object} updateData - Updated profile data
 * @returns {Object} Response with operation status
 */
function updateUserProfile(userId, updateData) {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.USER_PROFILES || 'user_profiles');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'User profiles sheet not found' };
    }

    const data = sheetValues(sheet);
    const headers = data[0];
    const rows = data.slice(1);

    const userIdIndex = headers.indexOf('user_id');
    const activeIndex = headers.indexOf('is_active');

    const rowIndex = rows.findIndex(row =>
      row[userIdIndex] === userId && row[activeIndex] === true
    );

    if (rowIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'User profile not found' };
    }

    const rowNumber = rowIndex + 2; // +1 for header, +1 for 0-based index
    const updateFields = updateAuditFields();

    // Update allowed fields
    const allowedFields = ['user_profile_type_id', 'display_name', 'email', 'immediate_approver_id', 'backup_approver_id', 'status', 'hire_date'];
    allowedFields.forEach(field => {
      if (updateData.hasOwnProperty(field)) {
        const columnIndex = headers.indexOf(field);
        if (columnIndex !== -1) {
          sheet.getRange(rowNumber, columnIndex + 1).setValue(updateData[field]);
          invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
        }
      }
    });

    // Update audit fields
    const updatedAtIndex = headers.indexOf('updated_at');
    const updatedByIndex = headers.indexOf('updated_by');
    sheet.getRange(rowNumber, updatedAtIndex + 1).setValue(updateFields.updated_at);
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
    sheet.getRange(rowNumber, updatedByIndex + 1).setValue(updateFields.updated_by);
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));

    // v9.6: the allow-list decision is re-checked on the next request (status may have changed)
    dropAllowCache(userId);

    // Log admin action
    logAdminAction(getCurrentUserId(), 'UPDATE_USER_PROFILE', 'user_profile', userId, updateData);

    return {
      status: 'success',
      message: `User profile updated: ${userId}`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in updateUserProfile: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * DATE-BASED ROLE ASSIGNMENT MANAGEMENT
 * Enhanced role assignment functions with temporal features
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Assign a role with date-based features (v9.0: same path as createUserRoleAssignment).
 * @param {string} userId - User ID
 * @param {string} roleId - Role ID
 * @param {Object} assignmentData - company_id plus the optional date-based fields
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: assignment, message }
 */
function assignRoleWithDates(userId, roleId, assignmentData) {
  return createUserRoleAssignment({ ...assignmentData, user_id: userId, role_id: roleId });
}

/**
 * Get active role assignments that expire within the next daysAhead days.
 * @param {number} daysAhead - Look-ahead window in days (default 30)
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: assignments[] (+ calculated_expiration_date), message }
 */
function getExpiringAssignments(daysAhead = 30) {
  try {
    const table = readTable(SHEETS.USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS);
    if (!table) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'User role assignments sheet not found' };
    }

    const now = new Date();
    const lookAheadDate = new Date(now.getTime() + (Number(daysAhead) || 0) * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);

    const expiringAssignments = table.records
      .filter(a => isAssignmentActive(a, now))
      .map(a => ({ ...a, calculated_expiration_date: assignmentExpiry(a) }))
      .filter(a => a.calculated_expiration_date && a.calculated_expiration_date <= lookAheadDate)
      .sort((a, b) => a.calculated_expiration_date - b.calculated_expiration_date);

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: expiringAssignments,
      message: `Found ${expiringAssignments.length} expiring assignments in next ${daysAhead} days`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getExpiringAssignments: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Expire role assignments whose term has ended (time-driven trigger or editor; not routed).
 * v9.0: expired = assignmentExpiry() <= now, written to the deleted_* columns user_role_assignments has.
 * v9.1 (review R-08): the table is read once and each expired row written from it, instead of one full
 * re-read per row.
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { total_expired, processed_count, expired_assignments }, message }
 */
function processPendingExpirations() {
  return withScriptLock(() => {
    try {
      const table = readTable(SHEETS.USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS);
      if (!table) {
        return { status: 'error', message: 'User role assignments sheet not found' };
      }

      const now = new Date();
      const expired = table.records
        .map((record, index) => ({ record, rowNumber: table.rowNumbers[index] }))
        .filter(({ record }) => isActiveFlag(record.is_active) && !record.deleted_at &&
          assignmentExpiry(record) && assignmentExpiry(record) <= now);

      expired.forEach(({ record, rowNumber }) => {
        const after = {
          ...record,
          ...deleteAuditFields('Automatic expiration based on effective_end_date, validity_end_date or auto_expire_days',
            'system_auto_expire')
        };
        table.sheet.getRange(rowNumber, 1, 1, table.headers.length).setValues([rowFromRecord(table.headers, after)]);
        invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(table.sheet));
        logAdminAction('system_auto_expire', 'AUTO_EXPIRE_ROLE_ASSIGNMENT', 'user_role_assignment', record.id, {
          user_id: record.user_id,
          role_id: record.role_id,
          expiration_date: assignmentExpiry(record),
          assignment_type: record.assignment_type
        });
      });

      return {
        status: 'success',
        data: {
          total_expired: expired.length,
          processed_count: expired.length,
          expired_assignments: expired.map(({ record }) => ({
            id: record.id,
            user_id: record.user_id,
            role_id: record.role_id,
            assignment_type: record.assignment_type,
            expiration_date: assignmentExpiry(record)
          }))
        },
        message: `Processed ${expired.length} expired role assignments`
      };

    } catch (error) {
      Logger.log('Error in processPendingExpirations: ' + error.toString());
      return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
    }
  });
}

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * UNIVERSAL ENTITY ARCHITECTURE INITIALIZATION
 * Functions to set up Universal Entity tables and validate implementation
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Initialize Universal Entity Architecture sheets
 * Creates all required tables for user/role management
 */
function initializeUniversalEntityArchitecture() {
  try {
    const ss = openSpreadsheet();
    const results = [];

    // Initialize user_profile_types sheet
    try {
      const userProfileTypesSheet = ss.getSheetByName('user_profile_types');
      results.push('user_profile_types sheet already exists');
    } catch (error) {
      const userProfileTypesSheet = ss.insertSheet('user_profile_types');
      const headers = ['id', 'name', 'description', 'code', 'company_id', 'is_active', 'created_at', 'created_by', 'updated_at', 'updated_by', 'deactivated_at', 'deactivated_by', 'deactivation_reason'];
      userProfileTypesSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setValues([headers]);
      invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(userProfileTypesSheet));
      userProfileTypesSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setFontWeight('bold');
      userProfileTypesSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setBackground('#4285f4');
      userProfileTypesSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setFontColor('white');
      results.push('✅ user_profile_types sheet created');
    }

    // Initialize role_types sheet
    try {
      const roleTypesSheet = ss.getSheetByName('role_types');
      results.push('role_types sheet already exists');
    } catch (error) {
      const roleTypesSheet = ss.insertSheet('role_types');
      const headers = ['id', 'name', 'description', 'code', 'company_id', 'is_active', 'created_at', 'created_by', 'updated_at', 'updated_by', 'deactivated_at', 'deactivated_by', 'deactivation_reason'];
      roleTypesSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setValues([headers]);
      invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(roleTypesSheet));
      roleTypesSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setFontWeight('bold');
      roleTypesSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setBackground('#4285f4');
      roleTypesSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setFontColor('white');
      results.push('✅ role_types sheet created');
    }

    // Initialize user_profiles sheet
    try {
      const userProfilesSheet = ss.getSheetByName('user_profiles');
      results.push('user_profiles sheet already exists');
    } catch (error) {
      const userProfilesSheet = ss.insertSheet('user_profiles');
      const headers = ['user_id', 'user_profile_type_id', 'display_name', 'email', 'immediate_approver_id', 'backup_approver_id', 'status', 'hire_date', 'is_active', 'created_at', 'updated_at', 'updated_by', 'deactivated_at', 'deactivated_by', 'deactivation_reason'];
      userProfilesSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setValues([headers]);
      invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(userProfilesSheet));
      userProfilesSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setFontWeight('bold');
      userProfilesSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setBackground('#4285f4');
      userProfilesSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setFontColor('white');
      results.push('✅ user_profiles sheet created');
    }

    // Add Universal Entity Architecture constant to SHEETS object
    results.push('Universal Entity Architecture sheets initialized');

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: results,
      message: 'Universal Entity Architecture initialization complete'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in initializeUniversalEntityArchitecture: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}
