/**
 * =================================================================================
 * DATABASE SCHEMA ANALYZER FUNCTION - UPDATED September 27, 2025
 * Purpose: Analyze current Google Sheets database structure and document schema
 * Usage: Add this function to your APPSCRIPT.txt file and run analyzeCurrentSchema()
 * Output: Updates 'xSchema' sheet with complete database schema documentation
 *
 * UPDATES:
 * - Added Universal Entity Architecture tables (user_profile_types, role_types, user_profiles)
 * - Updated custom_fields and custom_field_values with entity_category column
 * - Enhanced user_role_assignments with date-based assignment features
 * - Added roles.role_type_id relationship
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Analyze current database schema and write to 'xSchema' sheet
 * This function examines all sheets in the spreadsheet and documents their structure
 */
function analyzeCurrentSchema() {
  const ss = openSpreadsheet();

  // Get the existing xSchema sheet and clear it
  let xSchemaSheet;
  try {
    xSchemaSheet = ss.getSheetByName('xSchema');
    xSchemaSheet.clear();
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(xSchemaSheet));
  } catch (error) {
    throw new Error('xSchema sheet not found. Please create the xSchema sheet first.');
  }

  // Set up headers for schema documentation
  const headers = [
    'Sheet Name',
    'Column Count',
    'Column Headers',
    'Sample Row 2',
    'Row Count',
    'Status',
    'Notes'
  ];

  xSchemaSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setValues([headers]);
  invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(xSchemaSheet));
  xSchemaSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setFontWeight('bold');
  xSchemaSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setBackground('#4285f4');
  xSchemaSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setFontColor('white');

  // Get all sheets in the spreadsheet
  const allSheets = ss.getSheets();
  const schemaData = [];

  // Define expected sheets from CLAUDE.md specification - UPDATED September 27, 2025
  // Added Universal Entity Architecture tables for user/role management
  const expectedSheets = {
    'companies': 'id|name|code|code_locked|code_locked_at|code_locked_reason|ticket_count + audit fields',
    'roles': 'id|role_type_id|name|company_id + audit fields',
    'tickets': 'id|ticket_number|title|ticket_type_id|requester_id|status|current_step_id|step_due_date|company_id + audit fields',
    'ticket_history': 'id|ticket_id|user_id|action|comment|timestamp',
    'ticket_types': 'id|transaction_id|code|name|description|is_active|require_attachment_on_create|company_id + audit fields',
    'comment_requirements': 'ticket_type_id|require_on_approve|require_on_return|require_on_reject|require_on_cancel + audit fields',
    'custom_fields': '🆕 id|ticket_type_id|entity_category|name|label|type|is_required|is_hidden|sort_order|dropdown_list_id|depends_on_field_id + audit fields',
    'custom_field_values': '🆕 id|ticket_id|entity_category|custom_field_id|text_value|number_value|date_value|start_date_value|end_date_value|dropdown_option_id + audit fields',
    'workflow_steps': 'id|ticket_type_id|company_id|name|status_on_reach|step_type|approver_logic|sort_order|next_ticket_type_id|external_app_url|completion_action_name + audit fields',
    'step_approvers': 'step_id|role_id + audit fields',
    'user_role_assignments': '🆕 id|user_id|role_id|ticket_type_id|company_id|assignment_type|effective_start_date|effective_end_date|auto_expire_days|assigned_by_user_id|approval_required|assignment_notes|validity_end_date + audit fields',
    'step_slas': 'step_id|duration|unit|exclude_weekends + audit fields',
    'step_conditions': 'id|step_id|custom_field_id|operator|value + audit fields',
    'dropdown_lists': 'id|name|description + audit fields',
    'dropdown_options': 'id|dropdown_list_id|label|value|parent_option_id|sort_order + audit fields',
    'dropdown_company_assignments': 'id|dropdown_list_id|company_id|is_global|is_active + audit fields',
    'ticket_attachments': 'id|ticket_id|uploader_id|file_name|file_url|uploaded_at + audit fields',
    'report_configurations': 'id|ticket_type_id|field_name|display_name|field_type|sort_order + audit fields',
    'ticket_links': 'id|parent_ticket_id|child_ticket_id|link_type + audit fields',
    'sequence_counters': 'id|sequence_name|last_number|company_id|ticket_type_code + audit fields',
    'ticket_action_logs': 'id|ticket_id|user_id|action_type|details|timestamp',
    'admin_action_logs': 'id|admin_user_id|action_type|target_entity|target_id|details|timestamp',
    'user_preferences': 'id|user_id|dark_mode|timezone|language|email_notifications|desktop_notifications|dashboard_layout|items_per_page|auto_refresh|refresh_interval + audit fields',
    // 🆕 NEW UNIVERSAL ENTITY ARCHITECTURE TABLES (September 27, 2025)
    'user_profile_types': '🆕 id|name|description|code|company_id + audit fields',
    'role_types': '🆕 id|name|description|code|company_id + audit fields',
    'user_profiles': '🆕 user_id|user_profile_type_id|display_name|email|immediate_approver_id|backup_approver_id|status|hire_date + audit fields'
  };

  // Analyze each sheet
  for (let i = 0; i < allSheets.length; i++) {
    const sheet = allSheets[i];
    const sheetName = sheet.getName();

    // Skip the xSchema sheet itself
    if (sheetName === 'xSchema') continue;

    try {
      const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
      const lastColumn = sheet.getLastColumn();

      let columnHeaders = '';
      let sampleRow = '';
      let status = '';
      let notes = '';

      if (lastRow >= 1 && lastColumn >= 1) {
        // Get headers (row 1)
        const headersRange = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastColumn);
        const headersValues = headersRange.getValues()[0];
        columnHeaders = headersValues.join('|');

        // Get sample data (row 2 if exists)
        if (lastRow >= 2) {
          const sampleRange = sheet.getRange(2, 1, 1, lastColumn);
          const sampleValues = sampleRange.getValues()[0];
          sampleRow = sampleValues.map(val => val ? String(val).substring(0, 20) : '').join('|');
        } else {
          sampleRow = 'No data rows';
        }

        // Determine status and check for Universal Entity Architecture updates
        if (expectedSheets[sheetName]) {
          status = '✅ Expected';

          // Special checks for Universal Entity Architecture tables
          if (sheetName === 'custom_fields') {
            if (columnHeaders.includes('entity_category')) {
              notes = '🆕 Universal Entity Architecture: entity_category column added';
            } else {
              notes = '⚠️ Missing entity_category column for Universal Entity support';
            }
          } else if (sheetName === 'custom_field_values') {
            if (columnHeaders.includes('entity_category')) {
              notes = '🆕 Universal Entity Architecture: entity_category column added';
            } else {
              notes = '⚠️ Missing entity_category column for Universal Entity support';
            }
          } else if (sheetName === 'user_role_assignments') {
            if (columnHeaders.includes('assignment_type') && columnHeaders.includes('effective_start_date')) {
              notes = '🆕 Date-based role assignments enhanced';
            } else {
              notes = '⚠️ Missing date-based assignment columns';
            }
          } else if (sheetName === 'roles') {
            if (columnHeaders.includes('role_type_id')) {
              notes = '🆕 Role types relationship added';
            } else {
              notes = '⚠️ Missing role_type_id column';
            }
          } else if (['user_profile_types', 'role_types', 'user_profiles'].includes(sheetName)) {
            notes = '🆕 NEW: Universal Entity Architecture table';
          } else {
            // General audit field check
            if (columnHeaders.includes('is_active') && columnHeaders.includes('created_at')) {
              notes = 'Has audit fields';
            } else if (sheetName === 'ticket_history' || sheetName === 'ticket_action_logs' || sheetName === 'admin_action_logs') {
              notes = 'Immutable log table (no audit fields needed)';
            } else {
              notes = '⚠️ Missing audit fields';
            }
          }
        } else {
          status = '❓ Unexpected sheet';
          notes = 'Not in CLAUDE.md specification';
        }

      } else {
        columnHeaders = 'Empty sheet';
        sampleRow = 'No data';
        status = '❌ Empty';
        notes = 'Sheet exists but has no data';
      }

      schemaData.push([
        sheetName,
        lastColumn,
        columnHeaders,
        sampleRow,
        lastRow - 1, // Subtract 1 for header row
        status,
        notes
      ]);

    } catch (error) {
      schemaData.push([
        sheetName,
        0,
        'ERROR',
        'ERROR: ' + error.toString(),
        0,
        '❌ Error',
        'Failed to analyze sheet'
      ]);
    }
  }

  // Check for missing expected sheets
  for (const expectedSheet in expectedSheets) {
    const found = schemaData.some(row => row[0] === expectedSheet);
    if (!found) {
      schemaData.push([
        expectedSheet,
        0,
        expectedSheets[expectedSheet],
        'SHEET MISSING',
        0,
        '❌ Missing',
        'Required by CLAUDE.md specification'
      ]);
    }
  }

  // Sort data: existing sheets first, then missing sheets
  schemaData.sort((a, b) => {
    if (a[4] > 0 && b[4] === 0) return -1; // Existing sheets first
    if (a[4] === 0 && b[4] > 0) return 1;  // Missing sheets last
    return a[0].localeCompare(b[0]); // Alphabetical within groups
  });

  // Write data to sheet
  if (schemaData.length > 0) {
    xSchemaSheet.getRange(2, 1, schemaData.length, headers.length).setValues(schemaData);
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(xSchemaSheet));
  }

  // Add Universal Entity Architecture summary at the bottom
  const summaryStartRow = schemaData.length + 4;
  const existingSheets = schemaData.filter(row => row[4] > 0).length;
  const missingSheets = schemaData.filter(row => row[4] === 0).length;
  const sheetsWithAuditFields = schemaData.filter(row => row[6] === 'Has audit fields').length;
  const sheetsNeedingAuditFields = schemaData.filter(row => row[6] === '⚠️ Missing audit fields').length;
  const universalEntitySheets = schemaData.filter(row => row[6].includes('🆕')).length;

  const summary = [
    ['📊 SCHEMA ANALYSIS SUMMARY - UNIVERSAL ENTITY ARCHITECTURE'],
    [''],
    ['Total Sheets Found:', existingSheets],
    ['Missing Required Sheets:', missingSheets],
    ['Sheets with Audit Fields:', sheetsWithAuditFields],
    ['Sheets Needing Audit Fields:', sheetsNeedingAuditFields],
    ['Universal Entity Enhanced Sheets:', universalEntitySheets],
    [''],
    ['🎯 UNIVERSAL ENTITY ARCHITECTURE STATUS:'],
    ['✅ Custom Fields Infrastructure: Reusable for all entity types'],
    ['✅ User Profile Types: New table for configurable user profiles'],
    ['✅ Role Types: New table for configurable role types'],
    ['✅ Date-based Assignments: Enhanced role management'],
    [''],
    ['🔄 NEXT STEPS:'],
    ['1. Execute completeSystemReset() in Google Apps Script'],
    ['2. Create missing Universal Entity tables'],
    ['3. Add entity_category columns to custom_fields tables'],
    ['4. Enhance user_role_assignments with date-based features'],
    ['5. Test Universal Entity Architecture implementation']
  ];

  xSchemaSheet.getRange(summaryStartRow, 1, summary.length, 2).setValues(summary);
  invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(xSchemaSheet));
  xSchemaSheet.getRange(summaryStartRow, 1).setFontWeight('bold');
  xSchemaSheet.getRange(summaryStartRow, 1).setFontSize(12);

  // Format the sheet
  xSchemaSheet.autoResizeColumns(1, headers.length);
  xSchemaSheet.setFrozenRows(1);

  // Add conditional formatting for status column
  const statusRange = xSchemaSheet.getRange(2, 6, schemaData.length, 1);

  // Create rules for different statuses
  const rules = [];

  // Green for expected sheets
  rules.push(SpreadsheetApp.newConditionalFormatRule()
    .whenTextContains('✅ Expected')
    .setBackground('#d4edda')
    .setRanges([statusRange])
    .build());

  // Red for missing/error sheets
  rules.push(SpreadsheetApp.newConditionalFormatRule()
    .whenTextContains('❌')
    .setBackground('#f8d7da')
    .setRanges([statusRange])
    .build());

  // Yellow for unexpected sheets
  rules.push(SpreadsheetApp.newConditionalFormatRule()
    .whenTextContains('❓')
    .setBackground('#fff3cd')
    .setRanges([statusRange])
    .build());

  xSchemaSheet.setConditionalFormatRules(rules);

  Logger.log('Universal Entity Architecture schema analysis complete. Results written to xSchema sheet.');

  return {
    status: 'success',
    message: 'Universal Entity Architecture schema analysis complete',
    totalSheets: existingSheets,
    missingSheets: missingSheets,
    sheetsWithAuditFields: sheetsWithAuditFields,
    sheetsNeedingAuditFields: sheetsNeedingAuditFields,
    universalEntitySheets: universalEntitySheets
  };
}

/**
 * Helper function to get detailed column analysis for a specific sheet
 * Enhanced for Universal Entity Architecture validation
 * @param {string} sheetName - Name of the sheet to analyze
 * @returns {Object} Detailed column analysis
 */
function analyzeSheetColumns(sheetName) {
  const ss = openSpreadsheet();

  try {
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(sheetName);
    const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
    const lastColumn = sheet.getLastColumn();

    if (lastRow < 1 || lastColumn < 1) {
      return { error: 'Sheet is empty' };
    }

    const headersRange = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastColumn);
    const headers = headersRange.getValues()[0];

    const columnAnalysis = [];
    const universalEntityChecks = {
      hasEntityCategory: false,
      hasAuditFields: false,
      hasDateBasedAssignment: false,
      hasRoleTypeRelation: false
    };

    for (let col = 0; col < headers.length; col++) {
      const columnName = headers[col];
      let sampleValues = [];
      let dataTypes = new Set();

      // Check for Universal Entity Architecture columns
      if (columnName === 'entity_category') universalEntityChecks.hasEntityCategory = true;
      if (columnName === 'is_active' || columnName === 'created_at') universalEntityChecks.hasAuditFields = true;
      if (columnName === 'assignment_type' || columnName === 'effective_start_date') universalEntityChecks.hasDateBasedAssignment = true;
      if (columnName === 'role_type_id') universalEntityChecks.hasRoleTypeRelation = true;

      // Get sample values from first 5 data rows
      const maxSampleRows = Math.min(6, lastRow); // Row 1 is headers, so 2-6 for data
      if (maxSampleRows > 1) {
        const sampleRange = sheet.getRange(2, col + 1, maxSampleRows - 1, 1);
        const values = sampleRange.getValues();

        values.forEach(([value]) => {
          if (value !== null && value !== '') {
            sampleValues.push(String(value).substring(0, 30));
            dataTypes.add(typeof value);
          }
        });
      }

      columnAnalysis.push({
        position: col + 1,
        name: columnName,
        sampleValues: sampleValues.slice(0, 3), // Show first 3 samples
        dataTypes: Array.from(dataTypes),
        isEmpty: sampleValues.length === 0
      });
    }

    return {
      sheetName: sheetName,
      totalColumns: lastColumn,
      totalRows: lastRow - 1, // Exclude header
      columns: columnAnalysis,
      universalEntityStatus: universalEntityChecks
    };

  } catch (error) {
    return { error: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Create missing Universal Entity Architecture sheets based on CLAUDE.md specification
 * Creates the new tables required for user/role management
 */
function createUniversalEntitySheets() {
  const ss = openSpreadsheet();

  const universalEntitySheets = [
    {
      name: 'user_profile_types',
      headers: ['id', 'name', 'description', 'code', 'company_id', 'is_active', 'created_at', 'created_by', 'updated_at', 'updated_by', 'deactivated_at', 'deactivated_by', 'deactivation_reason']
    },
    {
      name: 'role_types',
      headers: ['id', 'name', 'description', 'code', 'company_id', 'is_active', 'created_at', 'created_by', 'updated_at', 'updated_by', 'deactivated_at', 'deactivated_by', 'deactivation_reason']
    },
    {
      name: 'user_profiles',
      headers: ['user_id', 'user_profile_type_id', 'display_name', 'email', 'immediate_approver_id', 'backup_approver_id', 'status', 'hire_date', 'is_active', 'created_at', 'updated_at', 'updated_by', 'deactivated_at', 'deactivated_by', 'deactivation_reason']
    }
  ];

  const created = [];
  const skipped = [];

  universalEntitySheets.forEach(sheetConfig => {
    try {
      const existing = ss.getSheetByName(sheetConfig.name);
      skipped.push(sheetConfig.name + ' (already exists)');
    } catch (error) {
      // Sheet doesn't exist, create it
      const newSheet = ss.insertSheet(sheetConfig.name);

      // Add headers
      newSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheetConfig.headers.length).setValues([sheetConfig.headers]);
      invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(newSheet));
      newSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheetConfig.headers.length).setFontWeight('bold');
      newSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheetConfig.headers.length).setBackground('#4285f4');
      newSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheetConfig.headers.length).setFontColor('white');

      // Auto-resize columns
      newSheet.autoResizeColumns(1, sheetConfig.headers.length);

      created.push(sheetConfig.name);
    }
  });

  Logger.log('Universal Entity sheets created: ' + created.join(', '));
  Logger.log('Universal Entity sheets skipped: ' + skipped.join(', '));

  return {
    created: created,
    skipped: skipped,
    message: 'Universal Entity Architecture sheet creation complete. Run schema analyzer to verify structure.'
  };
}

/**
 * Validate Universal Entity Architecture implementation
 * Checks if all required columns and relationships are in place
 */
function validateUniversalEntityArchitecture() {
  const ss = openSpreadsheet();

  const validationResults = {
    status: 'success',
    checks: [],
    errors: [],
    warnings: []
  };

  // Check custom_fields has entity_category
  try {
    const customFieldsSheet = ss.getSheetByName('custom_fields');
    const headers = customFieldsSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, customFieldsSheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];

    if (headers.includes('entity_category')) {
      validationResults.checks.push('✅ custom_fields has entity_category column');
    } else {
      validationResults.errors.push('❌ custom_fields missing entity_category column');
    }
  } catch (error) {
    validationResults.errors.push('❌ custom_fields sheet not found');
  }

  // Check custom_field_values has entity_category
  try {
    const customFieldValuesSheet = ss.getSheetByName('custom_field_values');
    const headers = customFieldValuesSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, customFieldValuesSheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];

    if (headers.includes('entity_category')) {
      validationResults.checks.push('✅ custom_field_values has entity_category column');
    } else {
      validationResults.errors.push('❌ custom_field_values missing entity_category column');
    }
  } catch (error) {
    validationResults.errors.push('❌ custom_field_values sheet not found');
  }

  // Check for Universal Entity tables
  const requiredTables = ['user_profile_types', 'role_types', 'user_profiles'];
  requiredTables.forEach(tableName => {
    try {
      const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(tableName);
      validationResults.checks.push(`✅ ${tableName} table exists`);
    } catch (error) {
      validationResults.errors.push(`❌ ${tableName} table missing`);
    }
  });

  if (validationResults.errors.length > 0) {
    validationResults.status = 'failed';
  } else if (validationResults.warnings.length > 0) {
    validationResults.status = 'warning';
  }

  Logger.log('Universal Entity Architecture validation: ' + validationResults.status);

  return validationResults;
}

/**
 * Add entity_category column to custom_fields sheet
 * This enables universal entity architecture
 */
function addEntityCategoryToCustomFields() {
  const ss = openSpreadsheet();
  const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.CUSTOM_FIELDS);

  if (!sheet) {
    Logger.log('custom_fields sheet not found');
    return { success: false, message: 'custom_fields sheet not found' };
  }

  const headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];

  // Check if entity_category already exists
  if (headers.includes('entity_category')) {
    Logger.log('entity_category column already exists in custom_fields');
    return { success: true, message: 'entity_category already exists' };
  }

  // Find position after ticket_type_id (index 1 typically)
  const ticketTypeIdIndex = headers.indexOf('ticket_type_id');
  const insertPosition = ticketTypeIdIndex >= 0 ? ticketTypeIdIndex + 2 : 3; // 1-based

  // Insert new column
  sheet.insertColumnAfter(insertPosition - 1);
  sheet.getRange(1, insertPosition).setValue('entity_category');
  invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
  sheet.getRange(1, insertPosition).setFontWeight('bold');

  // Set default value 'ticket' for all existing rows
  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow > 1) {
    const range = sheet.getRange(2, insertPosition, lastRow - 1, 1);
    const values = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < lastRow - 1; i++) {
      values.push(['ticket']);
    }
    range.setValues(values);
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(range));
  }

  Logger.log('Added entity_category column to custom_fields at position ' + insertPosition);
  return { success: true, message: 'entity_category column added' };
}

/**
 * Add entity_category column to custom_field_values sheet
 * This enables universal entity architecture
 */
function addEntityCategoryToCustomFieldValues() {
  const ss = openSpreadsheet();
  const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.CUSTOM_FIELD_VALUES);

  if (!sheet) {
    Logger.log('custom_field_values sheet not found');
    return { success: false, message: 'custom_field_values sheet not found' };
  }

  const headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];

  // Check if entity_category already exists
  if (headers.includes('entity_category')) {
    Logger.log('entity_category column already exists in custom_field_values');
    return { success: true, message: 'entity_category already exists' };
  }

  // Find position after ticket_id (index 1 typically)
  const ticketIdIndex = headers.indexOf('ticket_id');
  const insertPosition = ticketIdIndex >= 0 ? ticketIdIndex + 2 : 3; // 1-based

  // Insert new column
  sheet.insertColumnAfter(insertPosition - 1);
  sheet.getRange(1, insertPosition).setValue('entity_category');
  invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
  sheet.getRange(1, insertPosition).setFontWeight('bold');

  // Set default value 'ticket' for all existing rows
  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow > 1) {
    const range = sheet.getRange(2, insertPosition, lastRow - 1, 1);
    const values = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < lastRow - 1; i++) {
      values.push(['ticket']);
    }
    range.setValues(values);
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(range));
  }

  Logger.log('Added entity_category column to custom_field_values at position ' + insertPosition);
  return { success: true, message: 'entity_category column added' };
}

/**
 * Initialize all user management tables and add entity_category columns
 * One-click setup for Universal Entity Architecture
 */
function initializeUserManagementTables() {
  Logger.log('=== INITIALIZING USER MANAGEMENT TABLES ===');

  const results = {
    success: true,
    initialized: [],
    errors: []
  };

  try {
    // Initialize new tables
    initializeUserProfileTypesSheet();
    results.initialized.push('user_profile_types');

    initializeRoleTypesSheet();
    results.initialized.push('role_types');

    initializeUserProfilesSheet();
    results.initialized.push('user_profiles');

    // Add entity_category columns to existing tables
    const cfResult = addEntityCategoryToCustomFields();
    if (cfResult.success) {
      results.initialized.push('custom_fields (entity_category)');
    } else {
      results.errors.push('custom_fields: ' + cfResult.message);
    }

    const cfvResult = addEntityCategoryToCustomFieldValues();
    if (cfvResult.success) {
      results.initialized.push('custom_field_values (entity_category)');
    } else {
      results.errors.push('custom_field_values: ' + cfvResult.message);
    }

    Logger.log('=== USER MANAGEMENT TABLES INITIALIZED ===');
    Logger.log('Initialized: ' + results.initialized.join(', '));

    if (results.errors.length > 0) {
      Logger.log('Errors: ' + results.errors.join(', '));
      results.success = false;
    }

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in initializeUserManagementTables: ' + error.message);
    results.success = false;
    results.errors.push(error.message);
  }

  return results;
}

/**
 * Create test data for user management system
 */
function createUserManagementTestData() {
  Logger.log('=== CREATING USER MANAGEMENT TEST DATA ===');

  const results = {
    success: true,
    created: [],
    errors: []
  };

  // v8.3 (CR-05): seed once - a repeat call (retry, test page) must not add duplicate EMP/CTR/APR/DEP rows
  const existingTypes = getSheetDataAsJSON(ss().getSheetByName(SHEETS.USER_PROFILE_TYPES));
  if (existingTypes.length > 0) {
    Logger.log('user_profile_types already has data - test data not created again');
    results.skipped = true;
    return results;
  }

  try {
    // Create test user profile types
    const employeeType = createUserProfileType({
      name: 'Standard Employee',
      description: 'Regular full-time employee profile',
      code: 'EMP',
      company_id: null // Global
    });
    results.created.push('User Profile Type: ' + employeeType.data.name);

    const contractorType = createUserProfileType({
      name: 'Contractor',
      description: 'External contractor or consultant',
      code: 'CTR',
      company_id: null
    });
    results.created.push('User Profile Type: ' + contractorType.data.name);

    // Create test role types
    const approvalRoleType = createRoleType({
      name: 'Approval Roles',
      description: 'Roles that can approve workflow steps',
      code: 'APR',
      company_id: null,
      permission_level: 3,
      can_approve_steps: true,
      max_approval_amount: 50000,
      requires_certification: false
    });
    results.created.push('Role Type: ' + approvalRoleType.data.name);

    const departmentRoleType = createRoleType({
      name: 'Department Roles',
      description: 'Departmental management roles',
      code: 'DEP',
      company_id: null,
      permission_level: 2,
      can_approve_steps: true,
      max_approval_amount: 10000,
      requires_certification: false
    });
    results.created.push('Role Type: ' + departmentRoleType.data.name);

    Logger.log('=== USER MANAGEMENT TEST DATA CREATED ===');
    Logger.log('Created: ' + results.created.join(', '));

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in createUserManagementTestData: ' + error.message);
    results.success = false;
    results.errors.push(error.message);
  }

  return results;
}

/**
 * Complete user management system setup (one-click solution)
 */
function completeUserManagementSetup() {
  Logger.log('=== COMPLETE USER MANAGEMENT SETUP ===');

  const initResult = initializeUserManagementTables();
  if (!initResult.success) {
    Logger.log('Initialization failed, aborting test data creation');
    return initResult;
  }

  const testDataResult = createUserManagementTestData();

  return {
    success: initResult.success && testDataResult.success,
    initialization: initResult,
    testData: testDataResult,
    message: 'User management system setup complete'
  };
}

// END OF PHASE 10.0: USER MANAGEMENT TABLE INITIALIZATION
// Total functions added: 8
// Version: 8.0

// =================================================================================
// PHASE 10.1: SCHEMA REBUILD (SAFE REPAIR)
// Added: October 17, 2026
// One authoritative column list for all 33 tables (SHEETS + TAG_SHEETS) and a
// non-destructive rebuild that creates missing tabs and repairs header drift.
// =================================================================================

/**
 * TABLE_SCHEMAS - authoritative ordered headers for every table.
 *
 * Ordering rules (some writers use positional appendRow, so order matters):
 *  1. A positional writer's appendRow order comes first and must line up exactly.
 *  2. Otherwise the existing initialize*Sheet() order is kept, so live tabs don't shift.
 *  3. Columns used only by header-driven writers (headers.map(h => obj[h])) are appended.
 *
 * Conflicts resolved (line refs are v8.0 APPSCRIPT.txt):
 *  - tickets: routed createTicket() (:1049) writes 11 positional values; initializeTicketsSheet()
 *    (:2370) used a different 19-col order. Writer order kept as prefix (also matches the Claude.md
 *    spec); description/priority/audit + cancellation fields from createTicketWithWorkflow() (:7013)
 *    and tag/collab counters read by header at :12319/:12353 are appended.
 *  - role_types: 13 cols at :11034/:11481 vs 17 cols at :12499 -> newest 17-col version.
 *  - user_profiles: 15 cols at :11048/:11485 vs 19 cols at :12530 -> newest 19-col version.
 *  - custom_fields / custom_field_values: entity_category sits after ticket_type_id / ticket_id,
 *    exactly where addEntityCategoryToCustomFields/Values() (:12549/:12594) insert it.
 *    v8.3: every custom_field_values writer is header-driven now (createTicket, setCustomFieldValue,
 *    create/update/deleteCustomFieldValue). Rows added by the rebuild get entity_category = 'ticket'
 *    from COLUMN_DEFAULTS.
 *  - v8.3: every initialize*Sheet() writes TABLE_SCHEMAS headers through ensureTableSheet(), so a
 *    reset (cleanResetAllTables / completeSystemReset) recreates tabs in this exact order.
 *  - user_role_assignments: init order (:6866) kept; assignRoleWithDates() (:10804) fields appended.
 *  - ticket_history: logTicketHistory() positional 6 cols kept; createTicketHistoryEntry() (:7721)
 *    fields appended.
 *  - sequence_counters, ticket_action_logs and the 8 tag/collaboration tables had no init function:
 *    taken from their writers (getNextSequence, logTicketAction) and DATABASE_SCHEMA_UPDATES.txt.
 */
const TABLE_SCHEMAS = {
  // --- Core tables (SHEETS) ---
  companies: ['id', 'name', 'code', 'code_locked', 'code_locked_at', 'code_locked_reason', 'ticket_count',
    'is_active', 'created_at', 'created_by', 'updated_at', 'updated_by',
    'deactivated_at', 'deactivated_by', 'deactivation_reason',
    'email_enabled', 'email_sender_name', 'email_reply_to', 'email_cc', 'email_footer'],
  roles: ['id', 'name', 'company_id', 'created_at', 'updated_at',
    'role_type_id', 'is_active', 'created_by', 'updated_by',
    'deactivated_at', 'deactivated_by', 'deactivation_reason'],
  tickets: ['id', 'ticket_number', 'title', 'ticket_type_id', 'requester_id', 'status',
    'current_step_id', 'step_due_date', 'created_at', 'updated_at', 'company_id',
    'description', 'priority', 'is_active', 'created_by', 'updated_by',
    'deactivated_at', 'deactivated_by', 'deactivation_reason',
    'cancelled_at', 'cancelled_by', 'cancellation_reason',
    'is_collaborative', 'collaboration_count', 'has_external_collaborators', 'last_tag_update', 'tag_count',
    'step_started_at',
    // v10.0: head snapshot (design §4.5, routed from v10.3) + Restricted ticket (§4.11)
    'head_approver_id', 'head_backup_id', 'is_confidential'],
  ticket_history: ['id', 'ticket_id', 'user_id', 'action', 'comment', 'timestamp',
    'from_status', 'to_status', 'from_step_id', 'to_step_id', 'field_changes'],
  ticket_types: ['id', 'transaction_id', 'code', 'name', 'description', 'is_active',
    'require_attachment_on_create', 'company_id', 'created_at', 'updated_at',
    'enable_tags', 'enable_sharing', 'sla_email',
    // v10.0: who sees the tickets (design §2.2), head step switch (§4.5), Restricted tickets (§4.11)
    'visibility', 'require_immediate_head_approval', 'confidential_mode', 'confidential_admin_access'],
  comment_requirements: ['ticket_type_id', 'require_on_approve', 'require_on_return', 'require_on_reject',
    'require_on_cancel', 'require_on_create', 'require_on_update', 'company_id',
    'is_active', 'created_at', 'created_by', 'updated_at', 'updated_by',
    'deactivated_at', 'deactivated_by', 'deactivation_reason'],
  custom_fields: ['id', 'ticket_type_id', 'entity_category', 'name', 'label', 'type', 'is_required', 'is_hidden',
    'sort_order', 'dropdown_list_id', 'depends_on_field_id',
    'is_active', 'created_at', 'created_by', 'updated_at', 'updated_by',
    'deactivated_at', 'deactivated_by', 'deactivation_reason',
    'is_confidential'], // v10.0: Restricted field (design §4.10)

/**
 * Decide how a sheet's existing columns map onto its schema.
 * Used by rebuildTable() when the header row differs from TABLE_SCHEMAS.
 *
 * @param {string[]} existingHeaders - Current header row (trimmed; may contain '' or unknown names)
 * @param {string[]} schemaHeaders - Ordered headers from TABLE_SCHEMAS
 * @returns {{headers: string[], order: number[], added: string[], extra: string[]}}
 *   headers - final header row to write
 *   order   - for each final column, the index of its source column in existingHeaders (-1 = new, empty)
 *   added   - schema columns the sheet doesn't have yet
 *   extra   - existing columns not in the schema, kept after the schema columns
 *             (blank headers renamed _unnamed_<col>, repeated headers <name>_dup_<col>)
 */
function resolveColumnMapping(existingHeaders, schemaHeaders) {
  // Schema columns first, matched by header name (first occurrence wins; -1 = not in sheet yet)
  const order = schemaHeaders.map(header => existingHeaders.indexOf(header));
  const added = schemaHeaders.filter((header, i) => order[i] === -1);
  const headers = schemaHeaders.slice();
  const extra = [];

  // KEEP policy: every other existing column is kept after the schema columns, so no data is lost.
  // Blank headers become _unnamed_<col>, repeated schema headers become <name>_dup_<col> (1-based col).
  existingHeaders.forEach((header, i) => {
    if (order.includes(i)) return;
    let name = header;
    if (!header) {
      name = `_unnamed_${i + 1}`;
    } else if (schemaHeaders.includes(header)) {
      name = `${header}_dup_${i + 1}`;
    }
    headers.push(name);
    order.push(i);
    extra.push(name);
  });

  return { headers, order, added, extra };
}

/**
 * Write the schema header row to a sheet (bold + frozen, same style as the init functions)
 * @param {Sheet} sheet - Target sheet
 * @param {string[]} headers - Header row
 */
function writeSchemaHeaders(sheet, headers) {
  sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setValues([headers]);
  invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
  sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setFontWeight('bold');
  sheet.setFrozenRows(1);
}

/**
 * Make sure a table's tab exists with its TABLE_SCHEMAS header row (v8.3, CR-04/CR-05).
 * Every initialize*Sheet() function calls this, so TABLE_SCHEMAS is the only header list.
 * - Missing tab              -> created with the schema headers
 * - Empty or header row only -> header row rewritten from TABLE_SCHEMAS
 * - Has data rows            -> left unchanged (use rebuildAllTables() to repair its headers)
 * Never clears data, so it is safe to call repeatedly.
 * @param {string} tableName - Sheet name (a TABLE_SCHEMAS key)
 * @returns {Sheet} The table's sheet
 */
function ensureTableSheet(tableName) {
  const headers = TABLE_SCHEMAS[tableName];
  if (!headers) {
    throw new Error(`No TABLE_SCHEMAS entry for '${tableName}'`);
  }

  const ss = openSpreadsheet();
  let sheet = ss.getSheetByName(tableName);
  if (!sheet) {
    sheet = ss.insertSheet(tableName);
    Logger.log(`Created ${tableName} sheet`);
  }

  if (sheet.getLastRow() > 1) {
    Logger.log(`${tableName} already has data - left unchanged`);
    return sheet;
  }

  // Replace an old header row (it may be longer than the schema)
  if (sheet.getLastColumn() > 0) {
    sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).clearContent();
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
  }
  writeSchemaHeaders(sheet, headers);

  Logger.log(`Initialized ${tableName} sheet with ${headers.length} columns`);
  return sheet;
}

/**
 * Values for existing rows when rebuildTable() adds a missing schema column (v8.3, CR-02).
 * Columns not listed here start empty. entity_category matches the old
 * addEntityCategoryToCustomFields/Values() back-fill - getCustomFields() and
 * setCustomFieldValue() skip rows whose category is blank.
 */
const COLUMN_DEFAULTS = {
  custom_fields: { entity_category: 'ticket', is_confidential: false }, // v10.0 Restricted field
  custom_field_values: { entity_category: 'ticket' },
  roles: { is_active: true }, // v9.0: getRoles hides inactive roles
  ticket_links: { enforce_blocking_rules: false, sync_status: false, notify_on_update: false }, // v9.1
  // v9.3 switches; v9.5 sla_email; v10.0 visibility (D2: existing types become private), head step, Restricted
  ticket_types: { enable_tags: false, enable_sharing: false, sla_email: 'default', visibility: 'private',
    require_immediate_head_approval: false, confidential_mode: 'off', confidential_admin_access: 'reveal_with_reason' },
  tickets: { is_confidential: false }, // v10.0
  ticket_attachments: { link_status: 'ok' }, // v9.6: broken-link reports
  companies: { email_enabled: 'default' } // v9.6: company email settings
};

/**
 * Check and (unless dryRun) repair one table against its schema.
 * @param {Spreadsheet} ss - Open spreadsheet
 * @param {string} tableName - Sheet name
 * @param {string[]} schemaHeaders - Ordered headers from TABLE_SCHEMAS
 * @param {boolean} dryRun - true = report only
 * @param {string} stamp - Timestamp suffix for backup tabs
 * @returns {Object} Report entry: { table, action: 'created'|'ok'|'repaired', columns, rows, ... }
 */
function rebuildTable(ss, tableName, schemaHeaders, dryRun, stamp) {
  const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(tableName);

  // Missing or completely empty tab: just write the headers
  if (!sheet || sheet.getLastColumn() === 0) {
    if (!dryRun) {
      writeSchemaHeaders(sheet || ss.insertSheet(tableName), schemaHeaders);
    }
    return { table: tableName, action: 'created', columns: schemaHeaders.length, rows: 0 };
  }

  const data = sheetValues(sheet);
  const existingHeaders = data[0].map(header => String(header).trim());
  const rowCount = data.length - 1;

  if (existingHeaders.join('|') === schemaHeaders.join('|')) {
    return { table: tableName, action: 'ok', columns: schemaHeaders.length, rows: rowCount };
  }

  const mapping = resolveColumnMapping(existingHeaders, schemaHeaders);

  // Schema columns already in place, only kept extras after them: nothing to rewrite (no new backup)
  if (mapping.headers.join('|') === existingHeaders.join('|')) {
    return { table: tableName, action: 'ok', columns: mapping.headers.length, rows: rowCount, extra: mapping.extra };
  }

  const moved = schemaHeaders.filter((header, i) => {
    const from = existingHeaders.indexOf(header);
    return from !== -1 && from !== i;
  });

  // New columns take their COLUMN_DEFAULTS value (v8.3, CR-02), else ''
  const defaults = COLUMN_DEFAULTS[tableName] || {};
  const defaulted = mapping.added.filter(header => defaults[header] !== undefined);

  if (!dryRun) {
    // Backup first - the repair rewrites every row
    sheet.copyTo(ss).setName(`_bak_${tableName}_${stamp}`);
    invalidateTable();

    const rows = data.slice(1).map(row => mapping.order.map((from, i) => {
      if (from !== -1) return row[from];
      const header = mapping.headers[i];
      return defaults[header] !== undefined ? defaults[header] : '';
    }));
    const output = [mapping.headers].concat(rows);

    if (sheet.getMaxColumns() < mapping.headers.length) {
      sheet.insertColumnsAfter(sheet.getMaxColumns(), mapping.headers.length - sheet.getMaxColumns());
    }
    sheet.clearContents();
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
    sheet.getRange(1, 1, output.length, mapping.headers.length).setValues(output);
    invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
    writeSchemaHeaders(sheet, mapping.headers);
  }

  return {
    table: tableName,
    action: 'repaired',
    columns: mapping.headers.length,
    rows: rowCount,
    added: mapping.added,
    defaulted: defaulted,
    moved: moved,
    extra: mapping.extra
  };
}

/**
 * Rebuild ALL tables with their full column lists - SAFE REPAIR, no data loss.
 * - Missing tab     -> created with headers
 * - Headers match   -> left alone
 * - Headers differ  -> backed up to _bak_<table>_<yyyyMMdd_HHmmss>, rows re-mapped by header name
 *
 * Run from the editor with no arguments = DRY RUN (report only).
 * Use rebuildAllTablesApply() to actually apply the changes.
 *
 * @param {boolean} dryRun - true (default) only reports; false applies changes
 * @returns {Object} { success, dryRun, created, ok, repaired, errors, report }
 */
function rebuildAllTables(dryRun = true) {
  Logger.log(`=== REBUILD ALL TABLES (${dryRun ? 'DRY RUN - no changes' : 'APPLY'}) ===`);

  const ss = openSpreadsheet();
  const stamp = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'Asia/Manila', 'yyyyMMdd_HHmmss');
  const results = {
    success: true,
    dryRun: dryRun,
    created: [],
    ok: [],
    repaired: [],
    errors: [],
    report: []
  };

  const rebuildEveryTable = () => {
    Object.keys(TABLE_SCHEMAS).forEach(tableName => {
      try {
        const entry = rebuildTable(ss, tableName, TABLE_SCHEMAS[tableName], dryRun, stamp);
        results[entry.action].push(tableName);
        results.report.push(entry);

        let line = `  ${entry.action.toUpperCase()}: ${tableName} (${entry.columns} cols, ${entry.rows} rows)`;
        if (entry.action === 'repaired') {
          line += ` | added: [${entry.added.join(', ')}] moved: [${entry.moved.join(', ')}] extra: [${entry.extra.join(', ')}]`;
          if (entry.defaulted.length > 0) {
            line += ` defaulted: [${entry.defaulted.join(', ')}]`;
          }
        }
        Logger.log(line);
      } catch (error) {
        Logger.log(`  ERROR: ${tableName}: ${error.message}`);
        results.errors.push(`${tableName}: ${error.message}`);
      }
    });
  };

  // v8.3 (CR-03): doPost writes and ticket numbering take the same lock, so none can append a row
  // between this read and clearContents(). Waits up to 30s for an in-flight write; writes that
  // arrive during the rebuild wait up to 30s, then fail with an error instead of being lost.
  if (dryRun) {
    rebuildEveryTable();
  } else {
    withScriptLock(rebuildEveryTable);
  }

  Logger.log(`=== REBUILD COMPLETE: ${results.created.length} created, ${results.ok.length} ok, ` +
    `${results.repaired.length} repaired, ${results.errors.length} errors ===`);

  if (results.errors.length > 0) {
    results.success = false;
  }

  return results;
}

/**
 * Apply the rebuild (no-argument wrapper so it can be run from the Apps Script editor)
 */
function rebuildAllTablesApply() {
  return rebuildAllTables(false);
}

// END OF PHASE 10.1: SCHEMA REBUILD

//                  rebuildAllTables, rebuildAllTablesApply (+ TABLE_SCHEMAS)
// Version: 8.2 (resolveColumnMapping implemented - KEEP policy)
// Version: 8.3 (code review fixes): + ensureTableSheet, COLUMN_DEFAULTS, withScriptLock,
//                  createHandlerResponse, rowFromRecord, routePostAction
// =================================================================================

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * PHASE 12.0: API CONTRACT + ROLES & APPROVALS (v9.0)
 * Helpers shared by the v9.0 fixes, and the handlers behind the routes the frontend
 * (src/api/googleSheet.js) called but no version routed. Plan:
 * ~/.claude/plans/plan-already-the-next-squishy-sutherland.md
 * =================================================================================
 */

/** Ticket statuses that end the workflow - no further approvals. */
const TERMINAL_TICKET_STATUSES = ['Completed', 'Rejected', 'Cancelled'];

/**
 * Compare two ids as strings (A8). null/undefined compare as ''.
 * @returns {boolean}
 */
function sameId(a, b) {
  return String(a === null || a === undefined ? '' : a) === String(b === null || b === undefined ? '' : b);
}

/**
 * is_active cell -> boolean. Only false / 'FALSE' are inactive; a blank cell (rows written before the
 * column existed, or by a positional writer) counts as active.
 * @returns {boolean}
 */
function isActiveFlag(value) {
  return !(value === false || String(value).trim().toUpperCase() === 'FALSE');
}

/** Distinct values, first occurrence order. */
function unique(values) {
  return values.filter((value, index) => values.indexOf(value) === index);
}

/** Records grouped by key (v9.4, A16): { key: [records] }, built once instead of filtering inside a loop. */
function groupBy(records, keyOf) {
  const groups = {};
  (records || []).forEach(record => {
    const key = keyOf(record);
    (groups[key] = groups[key] || []).push(record);
  });
  return groups;
}

/** The listed keys of obj whose value is not undefined. */
function pickDefined(obj, keys) {
  const out = {};
  keys.forEach(key => {
    if (obj && obj[key] !== undefined) out[key] = obj[key];
  });
  return out;
}

/**
 * Cell value -> Date, or null when blank/invalid. A date-only string (2026-10-31) is local midnight in the
 * script time zone - the same Date Sheets returns for a typed date.
 */
function toDate(value) {
  if (value === null || value === undefined || value === '') return null;
  if (value instanceof Date) return isNaN(value.getTime()) ? null : value;
  const text = String(value).trim();
  const dateOnly = text.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
  const date = dateOnly ? new Date(Number(dateOnly[1]), Number(dateOnly[2]) - 1, Number(dateOnly[3])) : new Date(text);
  return isNaN(date.getTime()) ? null : date;
}

/**
 * The moment a term ends (exclusive). An end date with no time of day (Manila midnight - what a typed date
 * becomes in Sheets) covers that whole day, so it ends at the next midnight.
 * v9.4 (A17): midnight is checked in Asia/Manila, not in the script's time zone.
 * @returns {Date|null}
 */
function endOfTerm(value) {
  const date = toDate(value);
  if (!date) return null;
  const isMidnight = Utilities.formatDate(date, 'Asia/Manila', 'HH:mm:ss') === '00:00:00' && date.getMilliseconds() === 0;
  return isMidnight ? new Date(date.getTime() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000) : date;
}

/** A sheet's header row. v9.4: from the request cache when the tab is cached (no round trip). */
function sheetHeaders(sheet) {
  const cached = cacheEnabled() && TABLE_CACHE[sheet.getName()];
  if (cached) {
    const headers = (cached[0] || []).map(h => String(h).trim());
    return headers.every(h => h === '') ? [] : headers;
  }
  const lastColumn = sheet.getLastColumn();
  return lastColumn > 0 ? sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastColumn).getValues()[0].map(h => String(h).trim()) : [];
}

/**
 * Read a table as records.
 * v9.4: served from the per-request cache (sheetValues); the records are fresh objects on every call.
 * @param {string} sheetName - Sheet name
 * @returns {Object|null} { sheet, headers, records, rowNumbers } (rowNumbers[i] = sheet row of records[i]),
 *   or null when the tab doesn't exist. Completely blank rows are skipped.
 */
function readTable(sheetName) {
  const sheet = ss().getSheetByName(sheetName);
  if (!sheet) return null;

  // v9.4: a cached tab skips the getLastColumn() round trip (an empty tab is cached as [['']] -> no headers)
  const data = (cacheEnabled() && TABLE_CACHE[sheetName]) || sheet.getLastColumn() > 0 ? sheetValues(sheet) : [[]];
  const headers = (data[0] || []).map(h => String(h).trim());
  const records = [];
  const rowNumbers = [];

  for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (data[i].every(cell => cell === '' || cell === null)) continue;
    const record = {};
    headers.forEach((header, index) => {
      if (header) record[header] = data[i][index];
    });
    records.push(record);
    rowNumbers.push(i + 1);
  }

  return { sheet, headers, records, rowNumbers };
}

/**
 * Find one record by id (string comparison, A8).
 * @returns {Object|null} { sheet, headers, rowNumber, record }
 */
function findRecord(sheetName, id, idColumn = 'id') {
  const table = readTable(sheetName);
  if (!table) return null;
  const index = table.records.findIndex(record => sameId(record[idColumn], id));
  if (index === -1) return null;
  return { sheet: table.sheet, headers: table.headers, rowNumber: table.rowNumbers[index], record: table.records[index] };
}

/**
 * Merge changes into one record and write the whole row back by header (A1).
 * Keys that aren't sheet columns are ignored; columns not in changes keep their value.
 * @returns {Object|null} { before, after, rowNumber }, or null when the record doesn't exist
 */
function updateRecordFields(sheetName, id, changes, idColumn = 'id') {
  const found = findRecord(sheetName, id, idColumn);
  if (!found) return null;

  const after = { ...found.record, ...changes };
  found.sheet.getRange(found.rowNumber, 1, 1, found.headers.length).setValues([rowFromRecord(found.headers, after)]);
  invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(found.sheet));
  return { before: found.record, after, rowNumber: found.rowNumber };
}

/**
 * When a role assignment stops being in force (exclusive): effective_end_date, else validity_end_date
 * (an end date covers that whole day - endOfTerm), else effective_start_date + auto_expire_days.
 * null = open-ended.
 * @returns {Date|null}
 */
function assignmentExpiry(assignment) {
  const end = endOfTerm(assignment.effective_end_date) || endOfTerm(assignment.validity_end_date);
  if (end) return end;
  const start = toDate(assignment.effective_start_date);
  const days = Number(assignment.auto_expire_days);
  if (start && days > 0) return new Date(start.getTime() + days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
  return null;
}

/**
 * Is a role assignment in force at `now`? Active, not deleted, started, and not expired.
 * @returns {boolean}
 */
function isAssignmentActive(assignment, now = new Date()) {
  if (!isActiveFlag(assignment.is_active) || assignment.deleted_at) return false;

  const start = toDate(assignment.effective_start_date);
  if (start && start > now) return false;

  const expiry = assignmentExpiry(assignment);
  return !expiry || now < expiry;
}

/**
 * JSON response for the v9.0 routes: like createHandlerResponse, but a { status, data } handler result is
 * unwrapped so response.data is the records the frontend method returns.
 * @param {Object} result - Handler return value
 * @returns {ContentService.TextOutput}
 */
function createDataResponse(result) {
  const failed = result && (result.status === 'error' || result.success === false);
  if (failed) {
    return createHandlerResponse(result);
  }
  const isHandlerResult = result && typeof result === 'object' && !Array.isArray(result) &&
    typeof result.status === 'string' && 'data' in result;
  return createJsonResponse(isHandlerResult
    ? { success: true, data: result.data, message: result.message }
    : { success: true, data: result });
}

/**
 * Approvals recorded during the ticket's current visit to a step (since it last arrived there),
 * in the order they happened. Used for approver_logic 'all'.
 * v9.1: shares visitApprovalsFromRows with the approver inbox; a reopen to the same step starts a new
 * visit (review R-06).
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket ID
 * @param {string} stepId - Step ID
 * @returns {Array} ticket_history rows (+ role_ids parsed from field_changes)
 */
function getStepVisitApprovals(ticketId, stepId) {
  const history = readTable(SHEETS.TICKET_HISTORY);
  return visitApprovalsFromRows(history ? history.records : [], ticketId, stepId);
}

/**
 * Put a new ticket on the first step of its company's workflow for its ticket type.
 * No steps = the ticket stays 'New' (explicit-assignment principle: no default workflow).
 * @param {string|number} ticketId - Ticket ID
 * @param {string} userId - Requester
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { started, step_id, status, step_due_date, reason } }
 */
function startTicketWorkflow(ticketId, userId) {
  const current = getTicketById(ticketId);
  if (current.status !== 'success') {
    return current;
  }

  const ticket = current.data;
  const steps = getWorkflowSteps(ticket.ticket_type_id, ticket.company_id);
  if (steps.status !== 'success' || steps.data.length === 0) {
    return { status: 'success', data: { started: false, reason: 'No workflow steps for this ticket type and company' } };
  }

  const firstStep = steps.data[0];
  const now = new Date();
  const stepDueDate = stepDueDateFor(ticket, firstStep, now); // v9.5 (D4)

  const newStatus = firstStep.status_on_reach || 'Pending';
  updateRecordFields(SHEETS.TICKETS, ticketId, {
    status: newStatus,
    current_step_id: firstStep.id,
    step_due_date: stepDueDate,
    step_started_at: now,
    is_active: true,
    created_by: ticket.created_by || userId,
    updated_at: now,
    updated_by: userId
  });

  createTicketHistoryEntry({
    ticket_id: ticketId,
    user_id: userId,
    action: 'WORKFLOW_STARTED',
    comment: `Workflow started at step: ${firstStep.name}`,
    from_status: ticket.status,
    to_status: newStatus,
    from_step_id: null,
    to_step_id: firstStep.id
  });

  return {
    status: 'success',
    data: { started: true, step_id: firstStep.id, status: newStatus, step_due_date: stepDueDate || null }
  };
}

/**
 * canUserApprove route: { user_id, step_id, ticket_id }.
 * With a ticket the step must be the ticket's current step; without one, any active assignment for one of
 * the step's approver roles counts.
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { can_approve, ... } }
 */
function canUserApproveAction(payload) {
  const userId = actorId(payload.user_id || payload.userId); // v9.4 (A15)
  const stepId = payload.step_id || payload.stepId;
  const ticketId = payload.ticket_id || payload.ticketId;

  if (!userId || !stepId) {
    return { status: 'error', message: 'user_id and step_id are required' };
  }

  if (ticketId) {
    const check = checkUserCanApproveStep(userId, ticketId);
    if (check.status !== 'success') return check;
    if (!sameId(check.data.step_id, stepId)) {
      return { status: 'success', data: { ...check.data, can_approve: false, reason: 'Step is not the ticket\'s current step' } };
    }
    return check;
  }

  const users = getUsersForStep(stepId);
  if (users.status !== 'success') return users;
  const roleIds = unique(users.data.filter(u => sameId(u.user_id, userId)).map(u => String(u.role_id)));
  return { status: 'success', data: { can_approve: roleIds.length > 0, user_id: userId, step_id: stepId, eligible_role_ids: roleIds } };
}

/**
 * submitStepApproval route: { ticket_id, step_id, user_id, decision, comment }.
 * `decision` replaces the old `action` key, which overwrote the route name in the flat POST body.
 * @returns {Object} progressTicketWorkflow result
 */
function submitStepApproval(payload) {
  const decision = payload.decision || payload.approval_action;
  if (!payload.ticket_id || !payload.user_id || !decision) {
    return { status: 'error', message: 'ticket_id, user_id and decision are required' };
  }
  return progressTicketWorkflow(payload.ticket_id, decision, {
    user_id: payload.user_id,
    comment: payload.comment || '',
    step_id: payload.step_id
  });
}

/**
 * getStepApprovals route: approval actions taken on a step of a ticket, newest first.
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket ID
 * @param {string} stepId - Step ID
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: [{ id, ticket_id, step_id, user_id, user_name, action, comment, timestamp }] }
 */
function getStepApprovals(ticketId, stepId) {
  if (!ticketId || !stepId) {
    return { status: 'error', message: 'ticket_id and step_id are required' };
  }

  const history = readTable(SHEETS.TICKET_HISTORY);
  const profiles = readTable(SHEETS.USER_PROFILES);
  const names = {};
  (profiles ? profiles.records : []).forEach(p => { names[String(p.user_id)] = p.display_name || p.email; });

  const actionNames = { APPROVED: 'approve', PARTIAL_APPROVAL: 'approve', REJECTED: 'reject', RETURNED: 'return', COMPLETED: 'complete' };
  const approvals = (history ? history.records : [])
    .filter(row => sameId(row.ticket_id, ticketId) && sameId(row.from_step_id, stepId) && actionNames[row.action])
    .map(row => ({
      id: row.id,
      ticket_id: row.ticket_id,
      step_id: row.from_step_id,
      user_id: row.user_id,
      user_name: names[String(row.user_id)] || row.user_id,
      action: actionNames[row.action],
      partial: row.action === 'PARTIAL_APPROVAL',
      comment: row.comment,
      timestamp: row.timestamp
    }))
    .reverse();

  return { status: 'success', data: approvals, message: `Retrieved ${approvals.length} approvals` };
}

/**
 * A user's active role assignments with the role names attached.
 * @param {string} userId - User ID
 * @param {string} companyId - Company filter (optional)
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: [{ ...assignment, assignment_id, role_name }] }
 */
function getUserRoleList(userId, companyId = null) {
  if (!userId) {
    return { status: 'error', message: 'userId is required' };
  }
  const assignments = getUserRoleAssignments(userId, companyId);
  if (assignments.status !== 'success') return assignments;

  const roles = readTable(SHEETS.ROLES);
  const roleById = {};
  (roles ? roles.records : []).forEach(r => { roleById[String(r.id)] = r; });

  return {
    status: 'success',
    data: assignments.data.map(a => ({
      ...a,
      assignment_id: a.id,
      role_name: (roleById[String(a.role_id)] || {}).name || a.role_id
    })),
    message: `Retrieved ${assignments.data.length} roles`
  };
}

/**
 * getUserProfile route: the profile plus the user's active roles, companies and general role, in the shape
 * UserContext.fetchUserData() reads ({ roles, companies }). null when the user has neither a profile nor
 * any assignment (UserContext then shows "no roles").
 * @param {string} userId - User ID (Firebase uid)
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: profile | null }
 */
function getUserProfileWithAccess(userId) {
  if (!userId) {
    return { status: 'error', message: 'userId is required' };
  }

  const found = findRecord(SHEETS.USER_PROFILES, userId, 'user_id');
  const roleList = getUserRoleList(userId);
  const roles = roleList.status === 'success' ? roleList.data : [];

  if (!found && roles.length === 0) {
    return { status: 'success', data: null, message: 'No profile or role assignments for this user' };
  }

  const companies = readTable(SHEETS.COMPANIES);
  const companyIds = unique(roles.map(r => String(r.company_id)));
  const userCompanies = (companies ? companies.records : [])
    .filter(c => companyIds.includes(String(c.id)) && isActiveFlag(c.is_active))
    .map(c => ({ id: c.id, name: c.name, code: c.code }));

  const general = getUserGeneralRole(userId);

  return {
    status: 'success',
    data: {
      ...(found ? found.record : { user_id: userId }),
      has_profile: Boolean(found),
      roles: roles.map(r => ({
        id: r.role_id,
        name: r.role_name,
        company_id: r.company_id,
        ticket_type_id: r.ticket_type_id,
        assignment_id: r.id,
        assignment_type: r.assignment_type,
        effective_start_date: r.effective_start_date,
        effective_end_date: r.effective_end_date
      })),
      companies: userCompanies,
      general_role: general.status === 'success' ? general.data.general_role : 'viewer'
    },
    message: 'User profile retrieved'
  };
}

/**
 * Classify a user as maker / approver / both / viewer from their assignments in force today.
 *   approver - holds a role that is an approver of at least one workflow step (in companyId, if given)
 *   maker    - holds any other role, or has an active profile and no roles (any employee can file tickets)
 *   both     - both of the above
 *   viewer   - neither (no active profile and no roles in force)
 * @param {string} userId - User ID
 * @param {string} companyId - Company filter (optional)
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { user_id, general_role, approver_role_ids, other_role_ids } }
 */
function getUserGeneralRole(userId, companyId = null) {
  if (!userId) {
    return { status: 'error', message: 'userId is required' };
  }

  const assignments = getUserRoleAssignments(userId, companyId);
  if (assignments.status !== 'success') return assignments;

  const profile = findRecord(SHEETS.USER_PROFILES, userId, 'user_id');
  const result = classifyGeneralRole(
    assignments.data.map(a => String(a.role_id)),
    getApproverRoleIds(companyId),
    Boolean(profile && isActiveFlag(profile.record.is_active))
  );

  return {
    status: 'success',
    data: { user_id: userId, ...result },
    message: `User is ${result.general_role}`
  };
}

/**
 * Role ids that approve at least one active workflow step (in companyId, if given).
 * @returns {Set<string>}
 */
function getApproverRoleIds(companyId = null) {
  const steps = readTable(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS);
  const stepIds = new Set((steps ? steps.records : [])
    .filter(s => isActiveFlag(s.is_active) && !s.deleted_at && (!companyId || sameId(s.company_id, companyId)))
    .map(s => String(s.id)));
  const approvers = readTable(SHEETS.STEP_APPROVERS);
  return new Set((approvers ? approvers.records : [])
    .filter(a => stepIds.has(String(a.step_id)))
    .map(a => String(a.role_id)));
}

/**
 * The general-role rule (see getUserGeneralRole), shared by the per-user and the list routes.
 * @param {string[]} heldRoleIds - Role ids of the user's assignments in force
 * @param {Set<string>} approverRoleIds - getApproverRoleIds()
 * @param {boolean} hasActiveProfile - The user has an active user_profiles row
 * @returns {Object} { general_role, approver_role_ids, other_role_ids }
 */
function classifyGeneralRole(heldRoleIds, approverRoleIds, hasActiveProfile) {
  const held = unique(heldRoleIds.map(String));
  const approverRoles = held.filter(id => approverRoleIds.has(id));
  const otherRoles = held.filter(id => !approverRoleIds.has(id));
  const isApprover = approverRoles.length > 0;
  const isMaker = otherRoles.length > 0 || (held.length === 0 && hasActiveProfile);
  const generalRole = isApprover && isMaker ? 'both' : isApprover ? 'approver' : isMaker ? 'maker' : 'viewer';
  return { general_role: generalRole, approver_role_ids: approverRoles, other_role_ids: otherRoles };
}

/**
 * getUsers route: active user profiles with their roles in force and general role, computed in one pass
 * (each tab read once, not once per user).
 * @param {string} companyId - When given, only users with an active assignment in that company
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: [{ ...profile, id, name, roles: [{ id, name, company_id, ... }], general_role }] }
 */
function getUsersWithAccess(companyId = null) {
  const profiles = getUserProfiles(companyId);
  if (profiles.status !== 'success') return profiles;

  const assignments = readTable(SHEETS.USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS);
  const roles = readTable(SHEETS.ROLES);
  const roleById = {};
  (roles ? roles.records : []).forEach(r => { roleById[String(r.id)] = r; });
  const approverRoleIds = getApproverRoleIds(companyId);
  const now = new Date();

  const byUser = {};
  (assignments ? assignments.records : [])
    .filter(a => isAssignmentActive(a, now) && (!companyId || sameId(a.company_id, companyId)))
    .forEach(a => {
      (byUser[String(a.user_id)] = byUser[String(a.user_id)] || []).push(a);
    });

  const users = profiles.data.map(p => {
    const held = byUser[String(p.user_id)] || [];
    return {
      ...p,
      id: p.user_id,
      name: p.display_name || p.email,
      roles: held.map(a => ({
        id: a.role_id,
        name: (roleById[String(a.role_id)] || {}).name || a.role_id,
        company_id: a.company_id,
        ticket_type_id: a.ticket_type_id,
        assignment_id: a.id
      })),
      general_role: classifyGeneralRole(held.map(a => String(a.role_id)), approverRoleIds, true).general_role
    };
  });

  return { status: 'success', data: users, message: `Retrieved ${users.length} users` };
}

/**
 * assignUserRole route: { userId | user_id, roleId | role_id, companyId | company_id } + optional
 * date-based fields (ticket_type_id, assignment_type, effective_start_date, effective_end_date, ...).
 * @returns {Object} createUserRoleAssignment result
 */
function assignUserRoleAction(payload) {
  return createUserRoleAssignment({
    ...payload,
    user_id: payload.user_id || payload.userId,
    role_id: payload.role_id || payload.roleId,
    company_id: payload.company_id || payload.companyId
  });
}

/**
 * removeUserRole route: { userId, roleId, companyId (optional) } - soft-deletes the user's assignments of
 * that role (in that company).
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { removed_count, assignment_ids } }
 */
function removeUserRoleAction(payload) {
  const userId = payload.user_id || payload.userId;
  const roleId = payload.role_id || payload.roleId;
  const companyId = payload.company_id || payload.companyId || null;
  if (!userId || !roleId) {
    return { status: 'error', message: 'userId and roleId are required' };
  }

  const assignments = getUserRoleAssignments(userId, companyId, { include_inactive: true });
  if (assignments.status !== 'success') return assignments;

  const targets = assignments.data.filter(a => sameId(a.role_id, roleId) && isActiveFlag(a.is_active) && !a.deleted_at);
  if (targets.length === 0) {
    return { status: 'error', message: 'User does not have this role' };
  }

  targets.forEach(a => deleteUserRoleAssignment(a.id, payload.reason || 'Role removed by admin'));
  return {
    status: 'success',
    data: { removed_count: targets.length, assignment_ids: targets.map(a => a.id) },
    message: `Removed ${targets.length} role assignment(s)`
  };
}

/**
 * user_profiles.user_id for an email (case-insensitive), or null.
 */
function findUserIdByEmail(email) {
  if (!email) return null;
  const profiles = readTable(SHEETS.USER_PROFILES);
  const match = (profiles ? profiles.records : [])
    .find(p => String(p.email).trim().toLowerCase() === String(email).trim().toLowerCase());
  return match ? match.user_id : null;
}

/**
 * bulkAssignRole route (BulkRoleAssignmentDialog): { user_email, company_id, ticket_type_id, role_id,
 * validity_end_date } - adds the role for the user with that email.
 * @returns {Object} createUserRoleAssignment result
 */
function bulkAssignRole(payload) {
  const userId = findUserIdByEmail(payload.user_email);
  if (!userId) {
    return { status: 'error', message: `No user profile with email ${payload.user_email}` };
  }
  return createUserRoleAssignment({
    user_id: userId,
    role_id: payload.role_id,
    company_id: payload.company_id,
    ticket_type_id: payload.ticket_type_id,
    validity_end_date: payload.validity_end_date,
    assignment_notes: 'Bulk role assignment'
  });
}

/**
 * replaceUserRoles route: same payload as bulkAssignRole. Soft-deletes the user's current assignments in
 * the same company and ticket-type scope, then assigns role_id.
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { removed_count, assignment } }
 */
function replaceUserRoles(payload) {
  const userId = findUserIdByEmail(payload.user_email);
  if (!userId) {
    return { status: 'error', message: `No user profile with email ${payload.user_email}` };
  }
  if (!payload.company_id || !payload.role_id) {
    return { status: 'error', message: 'company_id and role_id are required' };
  }

  const current = getUserRoleAssignments(userId, payload.company_id, { include_inactive: true });
  if (current.status !== 'success') return current;
  const inScope = current.data.filter(a =>
    isActiveFlag(a.is_active) && !a.deleted_at && sameId(a.ticket_type_id || '', payload.ticket_type_id || ''));

  inScope.forEach(a => deleteUserRoleAssignment(a.id, 'Replaced by replaceUserRoles'));
  const created = createUserRoleAssignment({
    user_id: userId,
    role_id: payload.role_id,
    company_id: payload.company_id,
    ticket_type_id: payload.ticket_type_id,
    validity_end_date: payload.validity_end_date,
    assignment_notes: 'Replaced role assignment'
  });
  if (created.status !== 'success') return created;

  return {
    status: 'success',
    data: { removed_count: inScope.length, assignment: created.data },
    message: `Replaced ${inScope.length} assignment(s)`
  };
}

/**
 * setStepApprovers route: { step_id, role_ids } - makes the step's approver roles exactly role_ids
 * (an empty list removes them all).
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { step_id, role_ids, added, removed } }
 */
function setStepApprovers(stepId, roleIds) {
  if (!stepId) {
    return { status: 'error', message: 'step_id is required' };
  }
  if (!Array.isArray(roleIds)) {
    return { status: 'error', message: 'role_ids must be an array' };
  }

  const current = getStepApprovers(stepId);
  if (current.status !== 'success') return current;

  const wanted = unique(roleIds.map(String));
  const existing = current.data.map(a => String(a.role_id));
  const toRemove = existing.filter(id => !wanted.includes(id));
  const toAdd = wanted.filter(id => !existing.includes(id));

  if (toRemove.length) {
    const removed = removeStepApprovers(stepId, toRemove);
    if (removed.status !== 'success') return removed;
  }
  if (toAdd.length) {
    const added = assignStepApprovers(stepId, toAdd);
    if (added.status !== 'success') return added;
  }

  return {
    status: 'success',
    data: { step_id: stepId, role_ids: wanted, added: toAdd.length, removed: toRemove.length },
    message: `Step approvers set (${toAdd.length} added, ${toRemove.length} removed)`
  };
}

/**
 * updateTicketStatus route: { id, status, comment, user_id, step_id }.
 * Only cancel and reopen are direct status changes; approve/reject/return go through submitStepApproval so
 * the workflow rules apply.
 * v9.1 (review R-05, spec 3.4): user_id is required. Cancel = the requester or an admin; reopen a rejected or
 * cancelled ticket = an admin; resubmit a ticket returned to the requester = the requester or an admin.
 * @returns {Object} cancelTicketWithReason / reopenTicketToStep result
 */
function updateTicketStatusAction(payload) {
  const ticketId = payload.id || payload.ticket_id;
  const status = String(payload.status || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  const userId = actorId(payload.user_id || payload.userId); // v9.4 (A15)

  if (!ticketId || !status) {
    return { status: 'error', message: 'id and status are required' };
  }
  if (!userId) {
    return { status: 'error', message: 'user_id is required' };
  }

  const current = getTicketById(ticketId);
  if (current.status !== 'success') return current;
  const ticket = current.data;
  const isRequester = sameId(ticket.requester_id, userId);

  if (status === 'cancelled' || status === 'canceled') {
    if (!isRequester && !isAdminUser(userId)) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Only the requester or an admin can cancel this ticket' };
    }
    return cancelTicketWithReason(ticketId, payload.comment || payload.reason || '', userId);
  }
  if (status === 'reopened' || status === 'reopen') {
    const resubmit = ticket.status === 'Returned to Requester';
    if (resubmit ? !(isRequester || isAdminUser(userId)) : !isAdminUser(userId)) {
      return { status: 'error', message: resubmit ? 'Only the requester or an admin can resubmit this ticket' : 'Only an admin can reopen a ticket' };
    }
    return reopenTicketToStep(ticketId, payload.step_id || null, userId);
  }
  return {
    status: 'error',
    message: `Status '${payload.status}' can't be set directly - use submitStepApproval (approve / reject / return)`
  };
}

/**
 * getSystemHealth route: API version, server time and a schema check (rebuildAllTables dry run - read-only).
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { status: 'healthy' | 'degraded', api_version, server_time, tables, issues } }
 */
function getSystemHealth() {
  const report = rebuildAllTables(true);
  const issues = []
    .concat(report.created.map(t => `missing tab: ${t}`))
    .concat(report.repaired.map(t => `schema differs: ${t}`))
    .concat(report.errors.map(e => `error: ${e}`));

  return {
    status: 'success',
    data: {
      status: issues.length === 0 ? 'healthy' : 'degraded',
      api_version: API_VERSION,
      server_time: new Date().toISOString(),
      tables: {
        ok: report.ok.length,
        missing: report.created.length,
        schema_differs: report.repaired.length,
        errors: report.errors.length
      },
      issues: issues
    },
    message: issues.length === 0 ? 'All tables match TABLE_SCHEMAS' : `${issues.length} schema issue(s) - run rebuildAllTables() from the editor`
  };
}

// =================================================================================
// END OF PHASE 12.0 (v9.0)
// =================================================================================
/**
 * =================================================================================
 * PHASE 13.0: CORE TICKET LOOP + WORKFLOW ADMIN (v9.1)
 * Handlers behind the v9.1 routes: ticket numbers, workflow step admin (create / copy / default), custom
 * field get, ticket links, company code lock, comment requirements, ticket history and the approver inbox.
 * Plan: ~/.claude/plans/plan-already-the-next-squishy-sutherland.md (Release 2)
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Cell value -> boolean: only true / 'TRUE' count. Unlike isActiveFlag a blank is false, and so is anything
 * else - e.g. the dates the pre-v9.1 updateCompany wrote into code_locked.
 * @returns {boolean}
 */
function boolFlag(value) {
  return value === true || String(value).trim().toUpperCase() === 'TRUE';
}

/** Is a company's code locked? (boolFlag - a date left in code_locked by the old bug reads as unlocked) */
function isLockedFlag(value) {
  return boolFlag(value);
}

/**
 * Check an SLA before it is written.
 * @returns {string|null} Error message, or null when valid
 */
function validateStepSLA(duration, unit) {
  const value = Number(duration);
  if (!isFinite(value) || value <= 0) return 'SLA duration must be a positive number';
  if (!['hours', 'days'].includes(String(unit || '').toLowerCase())) return 'SLA unit must be "hours" or "days"';
  return null;
}

/**
 * Create or update a step's SLA (one active SLA per step).
 * @param {string} stepId - Step ID
 * @param {Object} payload - { sla_duration, sla_unit, exclude_weekends }
 * @param {string} userId - Acting user
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: sla, message }
 */
function upsertStepSLA(stepId, payload, userId) {
  const error = validateStepSLA(payload.sla_duration, payload.sla_unit);
  if (error) return { status: 'error', message: error };

  const now = new Date();
  const values = {
    duration: Number(payload.sla_duration),
    unit: String(payload.sla_unit).toLowerCase(),
    exclude_weekends: boolFlag(payload.exclude_weekends),
    updated_at: now,
    updated_by: userId
  };

  const existing = getStepSLAs(stepId);
  if (existing.status === 'success' && existing.data.length > 0) {
    const result = updateRecordFields(SHEETS.STEP_SLAS, existing.data[0].id, values);
    return { status: 'success', data: result.after, message: 'SLA updated' };
  }

  const sheet = ss().getSheetByName(SHEETS.STEP_SLAS) || ensureTableSheet(SHEETS.STEP_SLAS);
  const sla = { id: generateId('sla'), step_id: stepId, ...values, is_active: true, created_at: now, created_by: userId };
  sheet.appendRow(rowFromRecord(sheetHeaders(sheet), sla));
  invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
  return { status: 'success', data: sla, message: 'SLA created' };
}

/**
 * Soft-delete a step's active SLAs (deleted_* columns, as step_slas has).
 * @returns {number} How many SLAs were removed
 */
function removeStepSLA(stepId, userId, reason) {
  const existing = getStepSLAs(stepId);
  const slas = existing.status === 'success' ? existing.data : [];
  const now = new Date();
  slas.forEach(sla => updateRecordFields(SHEETS.STEP_SLAS, sla.id, {
    is_active: false,
    deleted_at: now,
    deleted_by: userId,
    deletion_reason: reason,
    updated_at: now,
    updated_by: userId
  }));
  return slas.length;
}

/**
 * Is the user an admin? Same rule as the frontend (UserContext: a role whose name contains "admin"), over
 * the user's assignments that are active today.
 * @param {string} userId - User ID
 * @returns {boolean}
 */
function isAdminUser(userId) {
  if (!userId) return false;
  const assignments = readTable(SHEETS.USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS);
  const roles = readTable(SHEETS.ROLES);
  const now = new Date();
  const roleIds = (assignments ? assignments.records : [])
    .filter(a => sameId(a.user_id, userId) && isAssignmentActive(a, now))
    .map(a => String(a.role_id));
  return (roles ? roles.records : []).some(r =>
    roleIds.includes(String(r.id)) && isActiveFlag(r.is_active) && String(r.name || '').toLowerCase().includes('admin'));
}

// --- Ticket numbers ----------------------------------------------------------

/**
 * Company and ticket type codes for numbering.
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { company, ticketType } }
 */
function resolveNumberingCodes(companyId, ticketTypeId) {
  if (!companyId || !ticketTypeId) {
    return { status: 'error', message: 'company_id and ticket_type_id are required' };
  }
  const company = findRecord(SHEETS.COMPANIES, companyId);
  const ticketType = findRecord(SHEETS.TICKET_TYPES, ticketTypeId);
  if (!company) return { status: 'error', message: `Company ${companyId} not found` };
  if (!ticketType) return { status: 'error', message: `Ticket type ${ticketTypeId} not found` };
  if (!company.record.code || !ticketType.record.code) {
    return { status: 'error', message: 'Company and ticket type both need a code for ticket numbering' };
  }
  return { status: 'success', data: { company: company.record, ticketType: ticketType.record } };
}

/**
 * getNextTicketNumber route: the number the next ticket would get, without reserving it (read-only).
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { ticket_number, sequence_name, next_sequence } }
 */
function peekTicketNumber(companyId, ticketTypeId) {
  const codes = resolveNumberingCodes(companyId, ticketTypeId);
  if (codes.status !== 'success') return codes;

  const sequenceName = `${codes.data.company.code}-${codes.data.ticketType.code}`;
  const counters = readTable(SHEETS.SEQUENCE_COUNTERS);
  const counter = (counters ? counters.records : []).find(c => String(c.sequence_name) === sequenceName);
  const next = (counter ? parseInt(counter.last_number, 10) || 0 : 0) + 1;
  const year = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'Asia/Manila', 'yyyy');

  return {
    status: 'success',
    data: {
      ticket_number: `${sequenceName}-${year}-${String(next).padStart(8, '0')}`,
      sequence_name: sequenceName,
      next_sequence: next
    }
  };
}

/**
 * generateTicketNumber route: reserve the next number (increments the counter).
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { ticket_number } }
 */
function generateTicketNumberAction(companyId, ticketTypeId) {
  const codes = resolveNumberingCodes(companyId, ticketTypeId);
  if (codes.status !== 'success') return codes;
