    if (!isFinite(threshold) || threshold < 1 || threshold > 100) return { error: 'warning_threshold must be 1-100 (%)' };
    values.warning_threshold = threshold;
  } else if (!partial) {
    values.warning_threshold = 75;
  }
  ['exclude_weekends', 'exclude_holidays', 'escalation_enabled'].forEach(key => {
    if (!partial || has(key)) values[key] = boolFlag(payload[key]);
  });
  ['escalation_delay', 'escalation_unit', 'business_hours_start', 'business_hours_end', 'timezone'].forEach(key => {
    if (!partial || has(key)) values[key] = payload[key] === undefined || payload[key] === null ? '' : payload[key];
  });
  ['escalation_recipients', 'business_days'].forEach(key => {
    if (!partial || has(key)) values[key] = JSON.stringify(Array.isArray(payload[key]) ? payload[key] : listParam(payload[key]));
  });
  if (!partial && !values.timezone) values.timezone = 'Asia/Manila';
  return { values };
}

/** getCompanySLARules route: live rules of a company (+ ticket type when given). */
function getCompanySLARules(companyId, ticketTypeId = null) {
  if (!companyId) return { status: 'error', message: 'company_id is required' };
  const rules = liveRecords(SHEETS.COMPANY_SLA_RULES)
    .filter(r => sameId(r.company_id, companyId) && (!ticketTypeId || sameId(r.ticket_type_id, ticketTypeId)))
    .map(companySLARuleView);
  return { status: 'success', data: rules };
}

/** createCompanySLARule route. One live rule per (company, ticket type, step name). */
function createCompanySLARule(payload) {
  if (!payload.company_id || !findRecord(SHEETS.COMPANIES, payload.company_id)) return { status: 'error', message: `Company ${payload.company_id} not found` };
  if (!payload.ticket_type_id || !findRecord(SHEETS.TICKET_TYPES, payload.ticket_type_id)) return { status: 'error', message: `Ticket type ${payload.ticket_type_id} not found` };
  const checked = companySLARuleValues(payload, false);
  if (checked.error) return { status: 'error', message: checked.error };
  const duplicate = liveRecords(SHEETS.COMPANY_SLA_RULES).some(r => sameId(r.company_id, payload.company_id) &&
    sameId(r.ticket_type_id, payload.ticket_type_id) && String(r.step_name).toLowerCase() === checked.values.step_name.toLowerCase());
  if (duplicate) return { status: 'error', message: `There is already a rule for step "${checked.values.step_name}"` };

  const actor = payload.user_id || getCurrentUserId();
  const now = new Date();
  const rule = appendRecord(SHEETS.COMPANY_SLA_RULES, {
    id: generateId('csla'), company_id: payload.company_id, ticket_type_id: payload.ticket_type_id, ...checked.values,
    is_active: true, created_at: now, created_by: actor, updated_at: now, updated_by: actor
  });
  logAdminAction(actor, 'CREATE_COMPANY_SLA_RULE', 'company_sla_rule', rule.id, { company_id: rule.company_id, step_name: rule.step_name });
  return { status: 'success', data: companySLARuleView(rule), message: 'SLA rule created' };
}

/** updateCompanySLARule route: only the fields sent change. */
function updateCompanySLARule(ruleId, payload) {
  const found = ruleId ? findRecord(SHEETS.COMPANY_SLA_RULES, ruleId) : null;
  if (!found || !liveRecord(found.record)) return { status: 'error', message: `SLA rule ${ruleId} not found` };
  const checked = companySLARuleValues(payload, true);
  if (checked.error) return { status: 'error', message: checked.error };
  const actor = payload.user_id || getCurrentUserId();
  const updated = updateRecordFields(SHEETS.COMPANY_SLA_RULES, ruleId, { ...checked.values, updated_at: new Date(), updated_by: actor });
  logAdminAction(actor, 'UPDATE_COMPANY_SLA_RULE', 'company_sla_rule', ruleId, checked.values);
  return { status: 'success', data: companySLARuleView(updated.after), message: 'SLA rule updated' };
}

/** deleteCompanySLARule route: soft delete. */
function deleteCompanySLARule(ruleId, userId = null, reason = 'admin_request') {
  const found = ruleId ? findRecord(SHEETS.COMPANY_SLA_RULES, ruleId) : null;
  if (!found || !liveRecord(found.record)) return { status: 'error', message: `SLA rule ${ruleId} not found` };
  const actor = userId || getCurrentUserId();
  const now = new Date();
  updateRecordFields(SHEETS.COMPANY_SLA_RULES, ruleId, { is_active: false, deactivated_at: now, deactivated_by: actor,
    deactivation_reason: reason || 'admin_request', updated_at: now, updated_by: actor });
  logAdminAction(actor, 'DELETE_COMPANY_SLA_RULE', 'company_sla_rule', ruleId, { reason: reason });
  return { status: 'success', data: { id: ruleId, deleted: true }, message: 'SLA rule deleted' };
}

// --- Configuration export / import / backup ------------------------------------------

/** Include flags of an export / import request (missing = included). */
function configIncludes(params) {
  const flag = key => params[key] === undefined || params[key] === null || params[key] === '' ? true : boolFlag(params[key]);
  return {
    workflows: flag('include_workflows'),
    custom_fields: flag('include_custom_fields'),
    sla_rules: flag('include_sla_rules'),
    roles: flag('include_roles'),
    dropdowns: flag('include_dropdowns')
  };
}

/**
 * exportConfiguration route (admins only, read-only): one company's configuration as JSON - for the given
 * ticket types (all when none): workflow steps + approvers + SLAs + routes, custom fields, comment and
 * report settings, company SLA rules, roles, and the dropdown lists the fields use or the company can use.
 * @param {Object} params - { user_id, company_id, ticket_type_ids, include_workflows, include_custom_fields,
 *   include_sla_rules, include_roles, include_dropdowns }
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: export document }
 */
function exportConfiguration(params = {}) {
  const denied = requireAdmin(actorId(params.user_id), 'export the configuration');
  if (denied) return denied;
  const company = params.company_id ? findRecord(SHEETS.COMPANIES, params.company_id) : null;
  if (!company) return { status: 'error', message: `Company ${params.company_id} not found` };
  const companyId = String(company.record.id);
  const include = configIncludes(params);
  const typeFilter = listParam(params.ticket_type_ids);
  const wantType = id => typeFilter.length === 0 || typeFilter.some(t => sameId(t, id));

  const ticketTypes = liveRecords(SHEETS.TICKET_TYPES).filter(t => wantType(t.id));
  const typeIds = ticketTypes.map(t => String(t.id));
  const inTypes = id => typeIds.includes(String(id));

  const doc = {
    format: 'jarvis-config',
    api_version: API_VERSION,
    exported_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    exported_by: params.user_id,
    company: { id: companyId, name: company.record.name, code: company.record.code },
    includes: include,
    ticket_types: ticketTypes
  };

  if (include.workflows) {
    const steps = liveRecords(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS).filter(s => sameId(s.company_id, companyId) && inTypes(s.ticket_type_id));
    const stepIds = steps.map(s => String(s.id));
    const approverTable = readTable(SHEETS.STEP_APPROVERS);
    doc.workflow_steps = steps;
    doc.step_approvers = (approverTable ? approverTable.records : []).filter(a => stepIds.includes(String(a.step_id)));
    doc.step_slas = liveRecords(SHEETS.STEP_SLAS).filter(s => stepIds.includes(String(s.step_id)));
    doc.step_conditions = liveRecords(SHEETS.STEP_CONDITIONS).filter(c => stepIds.includes(String(c.step_id)));
  }
  if (include.custom_fields) {
    doc.custom_fields = liveRecords(SHEETS.CUSTOM_FIELDS).filter(f => inTypes(f.ticket_type_id));
    doc.comment_requirements = liveRecords(SHEETS.COMMENT_REQUIREMENTS)
      .filter(r => inTypes(r.ticket_type_id) && (!r.company_id || sameId(r.company_id, companyId)));
    doc.report_configurations = liveRecords(SHEETS.REPORT_CONFIGURATIONS)
      .filter(r => inTypes(r.ticket_type_id) && (!r.company_id || sameId(r.company_id, companyId)));
  }
  if (include.sla_rules) {
    doc.company_sla_rules = liveRecords(SHEETS.COMPANY_SLA_RULES).filter(r => sameId(r.company_id, companyId) && inTypes(r.ticket_type_id));
    // v9.5: escalation rules travel with the SLA rules; a rule for a step carries the step's name
    const stepNames = recordsById(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS);
    doc.sla_escalation_rules = liveRecords(SHEETS.SLA_ESCALATION_RULES)
      .filter(r => sameId(r.company_id, companyId) && inTypes(r.ticket_type_id))
      .map(r => ({ ...slaEscalationRuleView(r), step_name: r.step_id ? ((stepNames[String(r.step_id)] || {}).name || '') : '' }));
  }
  if (include.roles) {
    doc.roles = liveRecords(SHEETS.ROLES).filter(r => !r.company_id || sameId(r.company_id, companyId));
  }
  if (include.dropdowns) {
    const allAssignments = readTable(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_COMPANY_ASSIGNMENTS);
    const assignmentRows = allAssignments ? allAssignments.records : [];
    const usedByFields = liveRecords(SHEETS.CUSTOM_FIELDS).filter(f => inTypes(f.ticket_type_id) && f.dropdown_list_id).map(f => String(f.dropdown_list_id));
    const lists = liveRecords(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_LISTS)
      .filter(l => usedByFields.includes(String(l.id)) || dropdownAvailableTo(dropdownAvailability(l, assignmentRows), companyId));
    const listIds = lists.map(l => String(l.id));
    const optionTable = readTable(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_OPTIONS);
    doc.dropdown_lists = lists;
    doc.dropdown_options = (optionTable ? optionTable.records : []).filter(o => listIds.includes(String(o.dropdown_list_id)));
    doc.dropdown_company_assignments = assignmentRows.filter(r => liveRecord(r) && listIds.includes(String(r.dropdown_list_id)));
  }

  const counts = {};
  Object.keys(doc).forEach(key => { if (Array.isArray(doc[key])) counts[key] = doc[key].length; });
  doc.counts = counts;
  return { status: 'success', data: doc, message: `Exported ${company.record.name}` };
}

/**
 * createConfigurationBackup route (admins only): save the company's full export as a JSON file in the
 * script owner's Google Drive. (The first run after deploying v9.3 asks the owner to allow Drive access.)
 * @param {Object} params - { user_id, company_id, backup_name }
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { file_id, url, name, size, counts } }
 */
function createConfigurationBackup(params = {}) {
  const exported = exportConfiguration({ user_id: params.user_id, company_id: params.company_id });
  if (exported.status !== 'success') return exported;
  const safeName = String(params.backup_name || 'backup').replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9 _.-]/g, '_').slice(0, 80);
  const stamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, '-');
  const name = `jarvis-config-${exported.data.company.code}-${safeName}-${stamp}.json`;
  const json = JSON.stringify(exported.data, null, 2);
  const file = DriveApp.createFile(name, json, 'application/json');
  logAdminAction(params.user_id, 'CREATE_CONFIG_BACKUP', 'company', exported.data.company.id, { file_id: file.getId(), name: name });
  return {
    status: 'success',
    data: { file_id: file.getId(), url: file.getUrl(), name: name, size: json.length, counts: exported.data.counts },
    message: `Backup saved to Drive: ${name}`
  };
}

/**
 * importConfiguration route (admins only): copy configuration from one company to another. dry_run (the
 * default) only reports what would happen. Additive - existing target configuration is kept - except
 * workflows with overwrite_existing, where the target's steps for that ticket type are retired first
 * (refused while one of its open tickets is on them).
 * - workflows: copyWorkflowStepsFromCompany per ticket type; with include_roles, approver roles that belong
 *   to the source company are switched to the target role of the same name
 * - custom fields belong to the ticket type (shared by every company): nothing to copy
 * - company SLA rules: copied unless the target has a rule for the same step
 * - roles: the source company's own roles, unless the target has one of the same name
 * - dropdowns: the target gets access to the lists the source company is assigned
 * @param {Object} params - { user_id, source_company_id, target_company_id, ticket_type_ids, include_*,
 *   overwrite_existing, dry_run }
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { dry_run, actions: [{ area, ticket_type_id, action, detail }], counts } }
 */
function importConfiguration(params = {}) {
  const denied = requireAdmin(actorId(params.user_id), 'import configuration');
  if (denied) return denied;
  const sourceId = params.source_company_id;
  const targetId = params.target_company_id;
  if (!sourceId || !findRecord(SHEETS.COMPANIES, sourceId)) return { status: 'error', message: `Source company ${sourceId} not found` };
  if (!targetId || !findRecord(SHEETS.COMPANIES, targetId)) return { status: 'error', message: `Target company ${targetId} not found` };
  if (sameId(sourceId, targetId)) return { status: 'error', message: 'Source and target company are the same' };

  const dryRun = params.dry_run === undefined || params.dry_run === null || params.dry_run === '' ? true : boolFlag(params.dry_run);
  const overwrite = boolFlag(params.overwrite_existing);
  const include = configIncludes(params);
  const actor = params.user_id;
  const now = new Date();
  const typeFilter = listParam(params.ticket_type_ids);
  const types = liveRecords(SHEETS.TICKET_TYPES).filter(t => typeFilter.length === 0 || typeFilter.some(id => sameId(id, t.id)));
  const actions = [];
  const note = (area, ticketTypeId, action, detail) => actions.push({ area, ticket_type_id: ticketTypeId || '', action, detail });

  // Roles first, so copied approver rows can be switched to the target's roles
  const roleMap = {};
  if (include.roles) {
    const roles = liveRecords(SHEETS.ROLES);
    const targetByName = {};
    roles.filter(r => sameId(r.company_id, targetId)).forEach(r => { targetByName[String(r.name).toLowerCase()] = r; });
    roles.filter(r => sameId(r.company_id, sourceId)).forEach(role => {
      const existing = targetByName[String(role.name).toLowerCase()];
      if (existing) {
        roleMap[String(role.id)] = String(existing.id);
        note('roles', '', 'skip', `Role "${role.name}" already exists in the target`);
        return;
      }
      if (dryRun) {
        note('roles', '', 'copy', `Role "${role.name}"`);
        return;
      }
      const copy = appendRecord(SHEETS.ROLES, { ...role, id: generateId('role'), company_id: targetId, is_active: true,
        created_at: now, updated_at: now, created_by: actor, updated_by: actor });
      roleMap[String(role.id)] = String(copy.id);
      note('roles', '', 'copy', `Role "${role.name}"`);
    });
  }

  if (include.workflows) {
    const steps = liveRecords(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS);
    const tickets = liveRecords(SHEETS.TICKETS);
    types.forEach(type => {
      const sourceSteps = steps.filter(s => sameId(s.ticket_type_id, type.id) && sameId(s.company_id, sourceId));
      const targetSteps = steps.filter(s => sameId(s.ticket_type_id, type.id) && sameId(s.company_id, targetId));
      if (sourceSteps.length === 0) {
        note('workflows', type.id, 'skip', `${type.name}: the source company has no workflow`);
        return;
      }
      if (targetSteps.length > 0 && !overwrite) {
        note('workflows', type.id, 'skip', `${type.name}: the target already has ${targetSteps.length} step(s) (tick overwrite to replace)`);
        return;
      }
      if (targetSteps.length > 0) {
        const targetStepIds = targetSteps.map(s => String(s.id));
        const busy = tickets.filter(t => targetStepIds.includes(String(t.current_step_id)) && !TERMINAL_TICKET_STATUSES.includes(t.status));
        if (busy.length > 0) {
          note('workflows', type.id, 'refused', `${type.name}: ${busy.length} open ticket(s) are on the target's steps`);
          return;
        }
      }
      if (dryRun) {
        note('workflows', type.id, targetSteps.length ? 'replace' : 'copy', `${type.name}: ${sourceSteps.length} step(s)`);
        return;
      }
      targetSteps.forEach(s => {
        updateRecordFields(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS, s.id, { is_active: false, deactivated_at: now, deactivated_by: actor,
          deactivation_reason: 'replaced_by_import', updated_at: now, updated_by: actor });
        removeStepSLA(s.id, actor, 'replaced_by_import');
      });
      const copied = copyWorkflowStepsFromCompany(type.id, sourceId, targetId, actor);
      if (copied.status !== 'success') {
        note('workflows', type.id, 'error', `${type.name}: ${copied.message}`);
        return;
      }
      // Approver roles of the source company -> the target's role of the same name
      const newStepIds = liveRecords(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS)
        .filter(s => sameId(s.ticket_type_id, type.id) && sameId(s.company_id, targetId)).map(s => String(s.id));
      const approvers = readTable(SHEETS.STEP_APPROVERS);
      let remapped = 0;
      let foreign = 0;
      const roleById = recordsById(SHEETS.ROLES);
      (approvers ? approvers.records : []).forEach((a, i) => {
        if (!newStepIds.includes(String(a.step_id))) return;
        const role = roleById[String(a.role_id)];
        if (!role || !role.company_id || !sameId(role.company_id, sourceId)) return;
        if (roleMap[String(a.role_id)]) {
          writeTableRow(approvers, i, { ...a, role_id: roleMap[String(a.role_id)] });
          remapped++;
        } else {
          foreign++;
        }
      });
      note('workflows', type.id, targetSteps.length ? 'replace' : 'copy',
        `${type.name}: ${sourceSteps.length} step(s)` + (remapped ? `, ${remapped} approver role(s) switched to the target's roles` : '') +
        (foreign ? `, ${foreign} approver role(s) still belong to the source company - include roles to switch them` : ''));
    });
  }

  if (include.custom_fields) {
    note('custom_fields', '', 'skip', 'Custom fields belong to the ticket type and are shared by every company - nothing to copy');
  }

  if (include.sla_rules) {
    const rules = liveRecords(SHEETS.COMPANY_SLA_RULES);
    rules.filter(r => sameId(r.company_id, sourceId) && types.some(t => sameId(t.id, r.ticket_type_id))).forEach(rule => {
      const exists = rules.some(r => sameId(r.company_id, targetId) && sameId(r.ticket_type_id, rule.ticket_type_id) &&
        String(r.step_name).toLowerCase() === String(rule.step_name).toLowerCase());
      if (exists) {
        note('sla_rules', rule.ticket_type_id, 'skip', `SLA rule "${rule.step_name}" already exists in the target`);
        return;
      }
      if (!dryRun) {
        appendRecord(SHEETS.COMPANY_SLA_RULES, { ...rule, id: generateId('csla'), company_id: targetId, is_active: true,
          created_at: now, created_by: actor, updated_at: now, updated_by: actor, deactivated_at: '', deactivated_by: '', deactivation_reason: '' });
      }
      note('sla_rules', rule.ticket_type_id, 'copy', `SLA rule "${rule.step_name}"`);
    });

    // v9.5: escalation rules; a rule for a step goes to the target step with the same name
    const targetSteps = (readTable(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS) || { records: [] }).records.filter(st => sameId(st.company_id, targetId) && isActiveFlag(st.is_active));
    const sourceStepNames = recordsById(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS);
    liveRecords(SHEETS.SLA_ESCALATION_RULES)
      .filter(r => sameId(r.company_id, sourceId) && types.some(t => sameId(t.id, r.ticket_type_id)))
      .forEach(rule => {
        let targetStepId = '';
        if (rule.step_id) {
          const name = String((sourceStepNames[String(rule.step_id)] || {}).name || '').trim().toLowerCase();
          const match = targetSteps.find(st => sameId(st.ticket_type_id, rule.ticket_type_id) && String(st.name || '').trim().toLowerCase() === name);
          if (!match) {
            note('sla_rules', rule.ticket_type_id, 'skip', `Escalation level ${rule.level}: the target has no step "${name}"`);
            return;
          }
          targetStepId = String(match.id);
        }
        if (slaRuleLevelTaken(targetId, rule.ticket_type_id, targetStepId, rule.level)) {
          note('sla_rules', rule.ticket_type_id, 'skip', `Escalation level ${rule.level} already exists in the target`);
          return;
        }
        if (!dryRun) {
          appendRecord(SHEETS.SLA_ESCALATION_RULES, { ...rule, id: generateId('sler'), company_id: targetId, step_id: targetStepId, is_active: true,
            created_at: now, created_by: actor, updated_at: now, updated_by: actor, deactivated_at: '', deactivated_by: '', deactivation_reason: '' });
        }
        note('sla_rules', rule.ticket_type_id, 'copy', `Escalation level ${rule.level} (${rule.trigger})`);
      });
  }

  if (include.dropdowns) {
    const allRows = (readTable(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_COMPANY_ASSIGNMENTS) || { records: [] }).records;
    liveRecords(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_LISTS).forEach(list => {
      const availability = dropdownAvailability(list, allRows);
      if (availability.mode !== 'companies' || !availability.company_ids.some(id => sameId(id, sourceId))) return;
      if (availability.company_ids.some(id => sameId(id, targetId))) {
        note('dropdowns', '', 'skip', `"${list.name}" is already available to the target`);
        return;
      }
      if (!dryRun) assignDropdownToCompany(list.id, targetId, actor);
      note('dropdowns', '', 'copy', `Give the target access to "${list.name}"`);
    });
  }

  const counts = {};
  actions.forEach(a => { counts[a.action] = (counts[a.action] || 0) + 1; });
  if (!dryRun) {
    logAdminAction(actor, 'IMPORT_CONFIGURATION', 'company', targetId, { source_company_id: sourceId, counts: counts });
  }
  return {
    status: 'success',
    data: { dry_run: dryRun, source_company_id: sourceId, target_company_id: targetId, actions: actions, counts: counts },
    message: dryRun ? 'Dry run - nothing was changed' : 'Configuration imported'
  };
}

// --- Per-ticket-type switches: tags and sharing ----------------------------------------

/**
 * Is a ticket type's tags / sharing in use? (A live tag on, or a live share of, one of its tickets.)
 * Once in use the switch can't be turned off, so nothing already tagged or shared is orphaned.
 * @param {string} ticketTypeId - Ticket type ID
 * @returns {Object} { tags, sharing } booleans
 */
function ticketTypeFeatureUse(ticketTypeId) {
  const ticketIds = {};
  liveRecords(SHEETS.TICKETS).filter(t => sameId(t.ticket_type_id, ticketTypeId)).forEach(t => { ticketIds[String(t.id)] = true; });
  return {
    tags: liveRecords(SHEETS.TICKET_TAG_ASSIGNMENTS).some(a => ticketIds[String(a.ticket_id)]),
    sharing: liveRecords(SHEETS.TICKET_COLLABORATIONS).some(c => ticketIds[String(c.ticket_id)])
  };
}

/** The switches of a ticket type with their lock state. */
function ticketTypeFeatures(ticketType) {
  const use = ticketTypeFeatureUse(ticketType.id);
  return {
    enable_tags: boolFlag(ticketType.enable_tags),
    enable_sharing: boolFlag(ticketType.enable_sharing),
    tags_locked: boolFlag(ticketType.enable_tags) && use.tags,
    sharing_locked: boolFlag(ticketType.enable_sharing) && use.sharing,
    sla_email: SLA_EMAIL_MODES.includes(String(ticketType.sla_email)) ? String(ticketType.sla_email) : 'default',
    visibility: ticketTypeVisibility(ticketType) // v10.0 (decision 2): no lock - it can change any time
  };
}

/**
 * setTicketTypeFeatures route: turn tags / sharing on or off for a ticket type. Off is refused once the
 * feature is in use (user decision 19 Oct 2026: free until first use, then locked on - no override).
 * @param {Object} payload - { ticket_type_id, enable_tags, enable_sharing, user_id }
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { ticket_type_id, enable_tags, enable_sharing, tags_locked, sharing_locked } }
 */
function setTicketTypeFeatures(payload) {
  const found = payload.ticket_type_id ? findRecord(SHEETS.TICKET_TYPES, payload.ticket_type_id) : null;
  if (!found) return { status: 'error', message: `Ticket type ${payload.ticket_type_id} not found` };
  const current = ticketTypeFeatures(found.record);
  const changes = {};
  if (payload.enable_tags !== undefined) {
    const on = boolFlag(payload.enable_tags);
    if (!on && current.tags_locked) return { status: 'error', message: "Tags are in use on this ticket type and can't be turned off" };
    changes.enable_tags = on;
  }
  if (payload.enable_sharing !== undefined) {
    const on = boolFlag(payload.enable_sharing);
    if (!on && current.sharing_locked) return { status: 'error', message: "Sharing is in use on this ticket type and can't be turned off" };
    changes.enable_sharing = on;
  }
  if (payload.sla_email !== undefined) {
    // v9.5: email for SLA notices - 'default' follows the global setting (Script Property SLA_EMAIL_DEFAULT)
    if (!SLA_EMAIL_MODES.includes(String(payload.sla_email))) return { status: 'error', message: `sla_email must be one of ${SLA_EMAIL_MODES.join(', ')}` };
    changes.sla_email = String(payload.sla_email);
  }
  if (payload.visibility !== undefined) {
    // v10.0 (decision 2): private = requesters see their own + the ones they're involved in, approvers see all;
    // collaborative = any role on the type + company sees all
    const visibility = String(payload.visibility).toLowerCase();
    if (!TICKET_VISIBILITY_MODES.includes(visibility)) return { status: 'error', message: `visibility must be one of ${TICKET_VISIBILITY_MODES.join(', ')}` };
    const outdated = requireColumns(SHEETS.TICKET_TYPES, ['visibility']);
    if (outdated) return outdated;
    changes.visibility = visibility;
  }
  const actor = actorId(payload.user_id) || getCurrentUserId();
  const updated = updateRecordFields(SHEETS.TICKET_TYPES, found.record.id, { ...changes, updated_at: new Date() });
  logAdminAction(actor, 'SET_TICKET_TYPE_FEATURES', 'ticket_type', found.record.id, changes);
  return { status: 'success', data: { ticket_type_id: String(found.record.id), ...ticketTypeFeatures(updated.after) }, message: 'Ticket type features saved' };
}

/**
 * The ticket and its type, refusing when the type has the feature off.
 * @returns {Object} { error } or { ticket, ticketType }
 */
function ticketWithFeature(ticketId, feature) {
  const found = ticketId ? findRecord(SHEETS.TICKETS, ticketId) : null;
  if (!found || !isActiveFlag(found.record.is_active)) return { error: `Ticket ${ticketId} not found` };
  const type = findRecord(SHEETS.TICKET_TYPES, found.record.ticket_type_id);
  const on = type && boolFlag(type.record[feature === 'tags' ? 'enable_tags' : 'enable_sharing']);
  if (!on) return { error: feature === 'tags' ? 'Tags are turned off for this ticket type' : 'Sharing is turned off for this ticket type' };
  return { ticket: found.record, ticketType: type.record };
}

// --- Tags (Phase 11 design: docs/reference/DATABASE_SCHEMA_UPDATES.txt) ------------------------

const DEFAULT_TAG_COLOR = '#3B82F6';

/** A tag as the frontend reads it. */
function tagView(tag) {
  return { ...tag, id: String(tag.id), is_global: boolFlag(tag.is_global) || !tag.company_id, usage_count: Number(tag.usage_count) || 0 };
}

/** Can a ticket of this company use the tag? */
function tagAvailableTo(tag, companyId) {
  return boolFlag(tag.is_global) || !tag.company_id || sameId(tag.company_id, companyId);
}

/** getTags route: live tags, all or those a company can use (its own + global). */
function getTagsAction(params = {}) {
  const companyId = params.company_id || params.companyId || null;
  const tags = liveRecords(SHEETS.TICKET_TAGS)
    .filter(t => !companyId || tagAvailableTo(t, companyId))
    .map(tagView)
    .sort((a, b) => String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)));
  return { status: 'success', data: tags };
}

/** createTag route: name unique (case-insensitive) within its scope; blank company = global. */
function createTagAction(payload) {
  const name = String(payload.name || '').trim();
  if (!name) return { status: 'error', message: 'Tag name is required' };
  if (name.length > 50) return { status: 'error', message: 'Tag names are at most 50 characters' };
  const color = String(payload.color || DEFAULT_TAG_COLOR);
  if (!/^#[0-9A-Fa-f]{6}$/.test(color)) return { status: 'error', message: 'Color must be like #3B82F6' };
  const companyId = payload.company_id || '';
  if (companyId && !findRecord(SHEETS.COMPANIES, companyId)) return { status: 'error', message: `Company ${companyId} not found` };
  const clash = liveRecords(SHEETS.TICKET_TAGS).some(t => String(t.name).toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase() &&
    (companyId ? (sameId(t.company_id, companyId) || !t.company_id) : true));
  if (clash) return { status: 'error', message: `A tag named "${name}" already exists` };

  const actor = payload.user_id || getCurrentUserId();
  const now = new Date();
  const tag = appendRecord(SHEETS.TICKET_TAGS, {
    id: generateId('tag'), name: name, color: color, description: payload.description || '', tag_category: payload.tag_category || '',
    parent_tag_id: payload.parent_tag_id || '', company_id: companyId, is_global: !companyId, usage_count: 0, created_by_user_id: actor,
    is_active: true, created_at: now, created_by: actor, updated_at: now, updated_by: actor
  });
  logAdminAction(actor, 'CREATE_TAG', 'ticket_tag', tag.id, { name: name, company_id: companyId });
  return { status: 'success', data: tagView(tag), message: 'Tag created' };
}

/** updateTag route: name / color / description / category. */
function updateTagAction(payload) {
  const tagId = payload.tag_id || payload.id;
  const found = tagId ? findRecord(SHEETS.TICKET_TAGS, tagId) : null;
  if (!found || !liveRecord(found.record)) return { status: 'error', message: `Tag ${tagId} not found` };
  const changes = pickDefined(payload, ['description', 'tag_category']);
  if (payload.name !== undefined) {
    const name = String(payload.name).trim();
    if (!name || name.length > 50) return { status: 'error', message: 'Tag names are 1-50 characters' };
    const clash = liveRecords(SHEETS.TICKET_TAGS).some(t => !sameId(t.id, tagId) && String(t.name).toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase() &&
      (sameId(t.company_id, found.record.company_id) || !t.company_id || !found.record.company_id));
    if (clash) return { status: 'error', message: `A tag named "${name}" already exists` };
    changes.name = name;
  }
  if (payload.color !== undefined) {
    if (!/^#[0-9A-Fa-f]{6}$/.test(String(payload.color))) return { status: 'error', message: 'Color must be like #3B82F6' };
    changes.color = payload.color;
  }
  const actor = payload.user_id || getCurrentUserId();
  const updated = updateRecordFields(SHEETS.TICKET_TAGS, tagId, { ...changes, updated_at: new Date(), updated_by: actor });
  logAdminAction(actor, 'UPDATE_TAG', 'ticket_tag', tagId, changes);
  return { status: 'success', data: tagView(updated.after), message: 'Tag updated' };
}

/** deleteTag route: soft delete; tickets keep their history but no longer show the tag. */
function deleteTagAction(tagId, userId = null, reason = 'admin_request') {
  const found = tagId ? findRecord(SHEETS.TICKET_TAGS, tagId) : null;
  if (!found || !liveRecord(found.record)) return { status: 'error', message: `Tag ${tagId} not found` };
  const actor = userId || getCurrentUserId();
  const now = new Date();
  updateRecordFields(SHEETS.TICKET_TAGS, tagId, { is_active: false, deactivated_at: now, deactivated_by: actor,
    deactivation_reason: reason || 'admin_request', updated_at: now, updated_by: actor });
  logAdminAction(actor, 'DELETE_TAG', 'ticket_tag', tagId, { reason: reason });
  return { status: 'success', data: { id: String(tagId), deleted: true }, message: 'Tag deleted' };
}

/** getTicketTags route: a ticket's live tags (+ assignment id / when / who). */
function getTicketTagsAction(ticketId) {
  if (!ticketId) return { status: 'error', message: 'ticket_id is required' };
  const tags = recordsById(SHEETS.TICKET_TAGS);
  const data = liveRecords(SHEETS.TICKET_TAG_ASSIGNMENTS)
    .filter(a => sameId(a.ticket_id, ticketId) && tags[String(a.tag_id)] && liveRecord(tags[String(a.tag_id)]))
    .map(a => ({ ...tagView(tags[String(a.tag_id)]), assignment_id: a.id, assigned_at: isoOrBlank(a.created_at),
      assigned_by_user_id: a.assigned_by_user_id, assignment_notes: a.assignment_notes || '' }));
  return { status: 'success', data: data };
}

/** assignTags route: add tags to a ticket (type must have tags on; tags must be available to its company). */
function assignTagsAction(payload) {
  const checked = ticketWithFeature(payload.ticket_id, 'tags');
  if (checked.error) return { status: 'error', message: checked.error };
  const tagIds = listParam(payload.tag_ids);
  if (tagIds.length === 0) return { status: 'error', message: 'tag_ids is required' };
  const tags = recordsById(SHEETS.TICKET_TAGS);
  for (const tagId of tagIds) {
    const tag = tags[tagId];
    if (!tag || !liveRecord(tag)) return { status: 'error', message: `Tag ${tagId} not found` };
    if (!tagAvailableTo(tag, checked.ticket.company_id)) return { status: 'error', message: `Tag "${tag.name}" belongs to another company` };
  }

  const actor = payload.user_id || getCurrentUserId();
  const now = new Date();
  const already = liveRecords(SHEETS.TICKET_TAG_ASSIGNMENTS).filter(a => sameId(a.ticket_id, checked.ticket.id)).map(a => String(a.tag_id));
  const added = tagIds.filter(id => !already.includes(id));
  added.forEach(tagId => {
    appendRecord(SHEETS.TICKET_TAG_ASSIGNMENTS, { id: generateId('tta'), ticket_id: checked.ticket.id, tag_id: tagId,
      assigned_by_user_id: actor, assignment_notes: payload.notes || '', is_active: true,
      created_at: now, created_by: actor, updated_at: now, updated_by: actor });
    updateRecordFields(SHEETS.TICKET_TAGS, tagId, { usage_count: (Number(tags[tagId].usage_count) || 0) + 1 });
  });
  if (added.length) {
    createTicketActionLog({ ticket_id: checked.ticket.id, user_id: actor, action_type: 'TAGS_ADDED',
      details: { tags: added.map(id => tags[id].name) } });
  }
  const result = getTicketTagsAction(checked.ticket.id);
  result.message = added.length ? `Added ${added.length} tag(s)` : 'Those tags were already on the ticket';
  return result;
}

/** removeTags route: take tags off a ticket (the assignment rows are kept, marked deleted). */
function removeTagsAction(payload) {
  const ticketId = payload.ticket_id;
  const tagIds = listParam(payload.tag_ids);
  if (!ticketId || tagIds.length === 0) return { status: 'error', message: 'ticket_id and tag_ids are required' };
  const table = readTable(SHEETS.TICKET_TAG_ASSIGNMENTS);
  if (!table) return { status: 'error', message: 'The ticket has no tags' };
  const actor = payload.user_id || getCurrentUserId();
  const now = new Date();
  const removed = [];
  table.records.forEach((a, i) => {
    if (!sameId(a.ticket_id, ticketId) || !tagIds.includes(String(a.tag_id)) || !liveRecord(a)) return;
    writeTableRow(table, i, { ...a, is_active: false, deleted_at: now, deleted_by: actor,
      deletion_reason: payload.reason || 'removed', updated_at: now, updated_by: actor });
    removed.push(String(a.tag_id));
  });
  if (removed.length) {
    const tags = recordsById(SHEETS.TICKET_TAGS);
    createTicketActionLog({ ticket_id: ticketId, user_id: actor, action_type: 'TAGS_REMOVED',
      details: { tags: removed.map(id => (tags[id] || {}).name || id) } });
  }
  const result = getTicketTagsAction(ticketId);
  result.message = `Removed ${removed.length} tag(s)`;
  return result;
}

/**
 * searchTicketsByTags route: ids of the tickets with ANY (OR) or ALL (AND) of tag_ids and none of
 * exclude_tag_ids; company_id narrows to one company.
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { ticket_ids, operator } }
 */
function searchTicketsByTagsAction(params = {}) {
  const wanted = listParam(params.tag_ids);
  const excluded = listParam(params.exclude_tag_ids);
  const operator = String(params.operator || 'OR').toUpperCase() === 'AND' ? 'AND' : 'OR';
  const tagsByTicket = {};
  liveRecords(SHEETS.TICKET_TAG_ASSIGNMENTS).forEach(a => {
    (tagsByTicket[String(a.ticket_id)] = tagsByTicket[String(a.ticket_id)] || []).push(String(a.tag_id));
  });
  const scope = requestScope(params.user_id || params.userId, 'searchTicketsByTags'); // v10.0: visible tickets only
  const ids = liveRecords(SHEETS.TICKETS)
    .filter(t => (!params.company_id || sameId(t.company_id, params.company_id)) && canViewTicket(scope, t) !== 'none')
    .map(t => String(t.id))
    .filter(id => {
      const tags = tagsByTicket[id] || [];
      if (excluded.some(tag => tags.includes(tag))) return false;
      if (wanted.length === 0) return excluded.length > 0;
      return operator === 'AND' ? wanted.every(tag => tags.includes(tag)) : wanted.some(tag => tags.includes(tag));
    });
  return { status: 'success', data: { ticket_ids: ids, operator: operator } };
}

// --- Sharing (collaboration) ----------------------------------------------------------------

const SHARE_PERMISSIONS = ['view', 'comment'];

/** Is a share in force now (live and not expired)? */
function shareInForce(share, now = new Date()) {
  if (!liveRecord(share)) return false;
  const expires = share.expiration_date ? endOfTerm(share.expiration_date) : null;
  return !expires || now < expires;
}

/** A share with display names. */
function shareView(share, profiles, companies) {
  const profile = profiles[String(share.shared_with_user_id)] || {};
  const sharer = profiles[String(share.shared_by_user_id)] || {};
  return {
    ...share,
    id: String(share.id),
    can_view: true,
    can_comment: boolFlag(share.can_comment),
    can_edit: false,
    can_approve: false,
    shared_at: isoOrBlank(share.created_at),
    expiration_date: share.expiration_date ? isoOrBlank(share.expiration_date) : '',
    shared_with_user_name: share.shared_with_user_id ? (profile.display_name || profile.email || String(share.shared_with_user_id)) : '',
    shared_with_company_name: share.shared_with_company_id ? ((companies[String(share.shared_with_company_id)] || {}).name || String(share.shared_with_company_id)) : '',
    shared_by_user_name: sharer.display_name || sharer.email || String(share.shared_by_user_id || '')
  };
}

/**
 * shareTicket route: give one user or one company read access (view, or view + comment) to a ticket,
 * optionally until a date. Only the requester or an admin can share; approving stays with workflow roles.
 * @param {Object} payload - { ticket_id, shared_with_user_id | shared_with_company_id, permission_level,
 *   expiration_date, share_reason, user_id }
 */
function shareTicketAction(payload) {
  const checked = ticketWithFeature(payload.ticket_id, 'sharing');
  if (checked.error) return { status: 'error', message: checked.error };
  const actor = actorId(payload.user_id); // v9.4 (A15)
  if (!actor) return { status: 'error', message: 'user_id is required' };
  if (!sameId(checked.ticket.requester_id, actor) && !isAdminUser(actor)) {
    return { status: 'error', message: 'Only the requester or an admin can share this ticket' };
  }
  const userTarget = payload.shared_with_user_id || '';
  const companyTarget = payload.shared_with_company_id || '';
  if (Boolean(userTarget) === Boolean(companyTarget)) return { status: 'error', message: 'Share with one user or one company' };
  if (userTarget && sameId(userTarget, checked.ticket.requester_id)) return { status: 'error', message: 'The requester can already see this ticket' };
  if (companyTarget && !findRecord(SHEETS.COMPANIES, companyTarget)) return { status: 'error', message: `Company ${companyTarget} not found` };
  const permission = String(payload.permission_level || 'view').toLowerCase();
  if (!SHARE_PERMISSIONS.includes(permission)) {
    return { status: 'error', message: 'Shares give view or comment access - approving stays with the workflow roles' };
  }
  const expiration = payload.expiration_date ? toDate(payload.expiration_date) : null;
  if (payload.expiration_date && (!expiration || endOfTerm(payload.expiration_date) <= new Date())) {
    return { status: 'error', message: 'The expiration date must be in the future' };
  }
  const duplicate = liveRecords(SHEETS.TICKET_COLLABORATIONS).some(c => sameId(c.ticket_id, checked.ticket.id) && shareInForce(c) &&
    (userTarget ? sameId(c.shared_with_user_id, userTarget) : sameId(c.shared_with_company_id, companyTarget)));
  if (duplicate) return { status: 'error', message: 'The ticket is already shared with them' };

  const now = new Date();
  const share = appendRecord(SHEETS.TICKET_COLLABORATIONS, {
    id: generateId('share'), ticket_id: checked.ticket.id, shared_by_user_id: actor, shared_with_user_id: userTarget,
    shared_with_company_id: companyTarget, permission_level: permission, can_view: true, can_comment: permission === 'comment',
    can_edit: false, can_approve: false, expiration_date: payload.expiration_date || '', share_reason: payload.share_reason || '',
    is_active: true, created_at: now, created_by: actor, updated_at: now, updated_by: actor
  });
  if (userTarget) {
    appendRecord(SHEETS.COLLABORATION_NOTIFICATIONS, { id: generateId('cn'), collaboration_id: share.id, notification_type: 'ticket_shared',
      recipient_user_id: userTarget, message: `Ticket ${checked.ticket.ticket_number} was shared with you`, is_read: false,
      is_active: true, created_at: now, updated_at: now });
  }
  createTicketActionLog({ ticket_id: checked.ticket.id, user_id: actor, action_type: 'TICKET_SHARED',
    details: { with_user: userTarget, with_company: companyTarget, permission: permission, expires: payload.expiration_date || '' } });
  return { status: 'success', data: shareView(share, recordsById(SHEETS.USER_PROFILES, 'user_id'), recordsById(SHEETS.COMPANIES)), message: 'Ticket shared' };
}

/** getTicketShares route: the shares of a ticket that are in force. */
function getTicketSharesAction(ticketId) {
  if (!ticketId) return { status: 'error', message: 'ticket_id is required' };
  const profiles = recordsById(SHEETS.USER_PROFILES, 'user_id');
  const companies = recordsById(SHEETS.COMPANIES);
  const now = new Date();
  const data = liveRecords(SHEETS.TICKET_COLLABORATIONS)
    .filter(c => sameId(c.ticket_id, ticketId) && shareInForce(c, now))
    .map(c => shareView(c, profiles, companies));
  return { status: 'success', data: data };
}

/** revokeTicketShare route: the sharer, the requester or an admin ends a share. */
function revokeTicketShareAction(payload) {
  const shareId = payload.collaboration_id || payload.id;
  const table = readTable(SHEETS.TICKET_COLLABORATIONS);
  const index = table ? table.records.findIndex(c => sameId(c.id, shareId)) : -1;
  if (index === -1 || !liveRecord(table.records[index])) return { status: 'error', message: `Share ${shareId} not found` };
  const share = table.records[index];
  const actor = actorId(payload.user_id); // v9.4 (A15)
  const ticket = findRecord(SHEETS.TICKETS, share.ticket_id);
  const allowed = actor && (sameId(share.shared_by_user_id, actor) || (ticket && sameId(ticket.record.requester_id, actor)) || isAdminUser(actor));
  if (!allowed) return { status: 'error', message: 'Only the person who shared it, the requester or an admin can remove this share' };
  const now = new Date();
  writeTableRow(table, index, { ...share, is_active: false, revoked_at: now, revoked_by: actor,
    revocation_reason: payload.reason || 'revoked', updated_at: now, updated_by: actor });
  createTicketActionLog({ ticket_id: share.ticket_id, user_id: actor, action_type: 'TICKET_SHARE_REVOKED',
    details: { collaboration_id: share.id, reason: payload.reason || '' } });
  return { status: 'success', data: { id: String(share.id), revoked: true }, message: 'Share removed' };
}

/**
 * getSharedTickets route: tickets shared with the user directly, or with a company where the user has a
 * role in force, with how they were shared.
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: [ticket + { share: { id, permission_level, can_comment, shared_by_user_id,
 *   expiration_date, via } }] }
 */
function getSharedTicketsAction(userId) {
  if (!userId) return { status: 'error', message: 'user_id is required' };
  const now = new Date();
  const assignments = readTable(SHEETS.USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS);
  const myCompanies = unique((assignments ? assignments.records : [])
    .filter(a => sameId(a.user_id, userId) && isAssignmentActive(a, now)).map(a => String(a.company_id)).filter(Boolean));
  const tickets = recordsById(SHEETS.TICKETS);
  const seen = {};
  const data = [];
  liveRecords(SHEETS.TICKET_COLLABORATIONS)
    .filter(c => shareInForce(c, now) && (sameId(c.shared_with_user_id, userId) ||
      (c.shared_with_company_id && myCompanies.includes(String(c.shared_with_company_id)))))
    .forEach(c => {
      const ticket = tickets[String(c.ticket_id)];
      if (!ticket || !isActiveFlag(ticket.is_active) || seen[String(c.ticket_id)]) return;
      seen[String(c.ticket_id)] = true;
      data.push({ ...ticket, share: { id: String(c.id), permission_level: c.permission_level, can_comment: boolFlag(c.can_comment),
        shared_by_user_id: c.shared_by_user_id, expiration_date: c.expiration_date ? isoOrBlank(c.expiration_date) : '',
        via: c.shared_with_user_id ? 'user' : 'company' } });
    });
  return { status: 'success', data: data };
}

// =================================================================================
// END OF PHASE 15.0 (v9.3)
// =================================================================================

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * v9.5: SLA ESCALATION, NOTIFICATIONS AND BUSINESS CALENDAR (Phase 17.0)
 * Plan: docs/planning/PLAN_SLAEscalation_20261030.md v1.1 (decisions D1-D5 + session 7e008704)
 * Rules: compliance A14-A19 - one read per tab per run, real UTC, Manila only through formatDate,
 * idempotent trigger runs (event key = ticket + step + visit + rule), tryLock skip, dry run first.
 * =================================================================================
 */

const MANILA_TZ = 'Asia/Manila';
const MANILA_OFFSET = '+08:00'; // Asia/Manila has no daylight saving time, so a fixed offset is exact
const DAY_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
const SLA_TRIGGERS = ['percent_elapsed', 'before_due', 'after_due'];
const SLA_TRIGGER_UNITS = ['minutes', 'hours', 'days', 'business_hours', 'business_days'];
const SLA_ACTIONS = ['notify', 'escalate_role'];
const SLA_EMAIL_MODES = ['default', 'on', 'off'];
const SLA_MAX_NOTIFICATIONS_PER_RUN = 200;
const SLA_EMAIL_QUOTA_RESERVE = 10; // daily MailApp quota kept for other mail

// --- Business calendar (A17) ---------------------------------------------------------------

/** ISO weekday in Manila: 1 = Monday ... 7 = Sunday. */
function manilaWeekday(date) {
  return Number(Utilities.formatDate(date, MANILA_TZ, 'u'));
}

/** HH:mm in Manila. */
function manilaClock(date) {
  return Utilities.formatDate(date, MANILA_TZ, 'HH:mm');
}

/** The moment `clock` (HH:mm) happens on the Manila calendar day `day` (yyyy-MM-dd). */
function manilaAt(day, clock) {
  return new Date(`${day}T${clock}:00${MANILA_OFFSET}`);
}

/** The Manila calendar day after `day`. */
function nextManilaDay(day) {
  return phDay(new Date(manilaAt(day, '12:00').getTime() + DAY_MS));
}

/** HH:mm from a cell: a time string ('8:00', '08:00') or a Date (Sheets turns typed times into dates). */
function normalizeClock(value, fallback) {
  if (value instanceof Date && !isNaN(value.getTime())) return manilaClock(value);
  const match = /^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/.exec(String(value || '').trim());
  return match ? `${match[1].padStart(2, '0')}:${match[2]}` : fallback;
}

/** yyyy-MM-dd from a holiday cell (a date string, or a Date when Sheets converted it). */
function holidayDay(value) {
  if (value instanceof Date && !isNaN(value.getTime())) return phDay(value);
  // A leading apostrophe keeps the cell as text in Sheets (it isn't returned there, but older rows / tests may have it)
  const match = /^'?(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})/.exec(String(value || '').trim());
  return match ? match[1] : '';
}

/** Holiday days (yyyy-MM-dd) of a company: its own rows plus the rows for every company (blank company_id). */
function holidayDaysFor(companyId, holidayRows = null) {
  const rows = holidayRows || liveRecords(SHEETS.COMPANY_HOLIDAYS);
  return new Set(rows.filter(h => !h.company_id || sameId(h.company_id, companyId)).map(h => holidayDay(h.date)).filter(Boolean));
}

/**
 * Calendar for SLA time: business hours and days from a company SLA rule (business_days use the JavaScript
 * convention of CompanySLAManager: 0 = Sunday ... 6 = Saturday), weekends and holidays.
 * @param {Object|null} rule - company_sla_rules row (null: plain wall-clock time)
 * @param {Set} holidays - Holiday days of the ticket's company
 */
function slaCalendar(rule, holidays) {
  const view = rule ? companySLARuleView(rule) : {};
  return {
    start: normalizeClock(view.business_hours_start, '08:00'),
    end: normalizeClock(view.business_hours_end, '17:00'),
    business_days: view.business_days && view.business_days.length ? view.business_days.map(Number) : [1, 2, 3, 4, 5],
    exclude_weekends: rule ? boolFlag(view.exclude_weekends) : false,
    exclude_holidays: rule ? boolFlag(view.exclude_holidays) : false,
    holidays: holidays || new Set()
  };
}

/** Does this Manila day count? Business units follow business_days; plain days skip weekends when asked. */
function slaDayCounts(day, cal, business) {
  const isoWeekday = manilaWeekday(manilaAt(day, '12:00'));
  if (business) {
    if (!cal.business_days.includes(isoWeekday % 7)) return false;
  } else if (cal.exclude_weekends && isoWeekday >= 6) {
    return false;
  }
  return !(cal.exclude_holidays && cal.holidays.has(day));
}

/**
 * start + amount of SLA time (v9.5, A17). minutes / hours: wall clock. days: calendar days (weekends / holidays
 * skipped when the calendar says so), same time of day. business_days: the Nth business day, time of day kept
 * inside business hours. business_hours: only time inside business hours on business days counts.
 * @param {Date} start - Start
 * @param {number} amount - Amount
 * @param {string} unit - One of SLA_TRIGGER_UNITS
 * @param {Object} cal - slaCalendar() (optional)
 * @returns {Date}
 */
function addSlaTime(start, amount, unit, cal = {}) {
  const begin = new Date(start);
  const n = Number(amount) || 0;
  const calendar = { start: '08:00', end: '17:00', business_days: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5], exclude_weekends: false,
    exclude_holidays: false, holidays: new Set(), ...cal };
  switch (unit) {
    case 'minutes': return new Date(begin.getTime() + n * 60 * 1000);
    case 'days': return addSlaDays(begin, n, calendar, false);
    case 'business_days': return addSlaDays(begin, n, calendar, true);
    case 'business_hours': return addBusinessMinutes(begin, n * 60, calendar);
    default: return new Date(begin.getTime() + n * 60 * 60 * 1000);
  }
}

function addSlaDays(begin, n, cal, business) {
  let day = phDay(begin);
  let advanced = 0;
  let counted = 0;
  while (counted < n && advanced < 3660) {
    day = nextManilaDay(day);
    advanced++;
    if (slaDayCounts(day, cal, business)) counted++;
  }
  if (!business) return new Date(begin.getTime() + advanced * DAY_MS); // exact time of day
  const clock = manilaClock(begin);
  return manilaAt(day, clock < cal.start ? cal.start : (clock > cal.end ? cal.end : clock));
}

function addBusinessMinutes(begin, minutes, cal) {
  let remaining = minutes;
  let cursor = begin;
  let day = phDay(begin);
  for (let guard = 0; guard < 3660; guard++) {
    if (slaDayCounts(day, cal, true)) {
      const open = manilaAt(day, cal.start);
      const close = manilaAt(day, cal.end);
      const from = cursor > open ? cursor : open;
      if (from < close) {
        const available = (close.getTime() - from.getTime()) / 60000;
        if (available >= remaining) return new Date(from.getTime() + remaining * 60000);
        remaining -= available;
      }
    }
    day = nextManilaDay(day);
    cursor = manilaAt(day, '00:00');
  }
  return new Date(begin.getTime() + minutes * 60000); // no working time at all in the calendar
}

// --- Due dates (decision D4: a matching company SLA rule wins over the step SLA) ------------------

/** The live company SLA rule for a company, ticket type and step name (case-insensitive), or null. */
function companySLARuleFor(companyId, ticketTypeId, stepName, ruleRows = null) {
  if (!stepName) return null;
  const name = String(stepName).trim().toLowerCase();
  return (ruleRows || liveRecords(SHEETS.COMPANY_SLA_RULES)).find(r => sameId(r.company_id, companyId) &&
    sameId(r.ticket_type_id, ticketTypeId) && String(r.step_name || '').trim().toLowerCase() === name) || null;
}

/**
 * Due date of a step visit that starts at `start` (v9.5): the company SLA rule for the step's name when there is
 * one (business hours, business days, holidays), otherwise the step SLA. '' when the step has no SLA.
 * @param {Object} ticket - { company_id, ticket_type_id }
 * @param {Object} step - workflow step { id, name }
 * @param {Date} start - When the ticket reached the step
 * @returns {Date|string}
 */
function stepDueDateFor(ticket, step, start) {
  const rule = companySLARuleFor(ticket.company_id, ticket.ticket_type_id, step.name);
  if (rule) {
    const view = companySLARuleView(rule);
    return addSlaTime(start, view.duration, view.unit, slaCalendar(rule, holidayDaysFor(ticket.company_id)));
  }
  const stepSLA = getStepSLAs(step.id);
  if (stepSLA.status === 'success' && stepSLA.data.length > 0) {
    const sla = stepSLA.data[0];
    return calculateSLADueDate(start, sla.duration, sla.unit, sla.exclude_weekends);
  }
  return '';
}

// --- Step visits and escalation grants ---------------------------------------------------------------

/** Same moment? Cells may hold a Date or an ISO string. */
function sameInstant(a, b) {
  const x = toDate(a);
  const y = toDate(b);
  return Boolean(x && y && x.getTime() === y.getTime());
}

/**
 * When the ticket reached its current step: tickets.step_started_at (v9.5), else - tickets from before v9.5 -
 * the newest history row arriving at the step (the visitApprovalsFromRows rule), else created_at (stable).
 * @returns {Date|null}
 */
function visitStartedAt(ticket, historyRows = null) {
  const stored = toDate(ticket.step_started_at);
  if (stored) return stored;
  const rows = historyRows || ((readTable(SHEETS.TICKET_HISTORY) || { records: [] }).records);
  const arrival = rows.filter(h => sameId(h.ticket_id, ticket.id) && sameId(h.to_step_id, ticket.current_step_id) &&
    h.action !== 'PARTIAL_APPROVAL').pop();
  // created_at, not updated_at: updated_at moves with unrelated edits (tags, shares), which changed the visit key and
  // re-fired every rule - review CR2-08
  return (arrival && toDate(arrival.timestamp)) || toDate(ticket.created_at);
}

/** A JSON list cell as an array of strings. */
function jsonList(value) {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(String);
  if (value === '' || value === null || value === undefined) return [];
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(value);
    return (Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : [parsed]).map(String).filter(Boolean);
  } catch (error) {
    return String(value).split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  }
}

/**
 * Role ids an escalate_role event granted on the ticket's current visit (v9.5). Holders of these roles may
 * approve, reject or return the step until it moves on (a new visit has a new key, so the grant ends).
 * @returns {string[]}
 */
function escalationGrantedRoles(ticket, eventRows = null, historyRows = null) {
  if (!ticket || !ticket.current_step_id) return [];
  const rows = eventRows || ((readTable(SHEETS.SLA_ESCALATION_EVENTS) || { records: [] }).records);
  const mine = rows.filter(e => sameId(e.ticket_id, ticket.id) && String(e.escalation_action) === 'escalate_role' && sameId(e.step_id, ticket.current_step_id));
  if (!mine.length) return [];
  const visit = visitStartedAt(ticket, historyRows);
  return unique([].concat(...mine.filter(e => sameInstant(e.visit_started_at, visit)).map(e => jsonList(e.granted_role_ids))));
}

// --- Escalation rules: CRUD (admin, A15) --------------------------------------------------------------

const SLA_RULE_FIELDS = ['step_id', 'level', 'name', 'trigger', 'trigger_value', 'trigger_unit', 'escalation_action',
  'notify_current_approvers', 'notify_requester', 'notify_role_ids', 'notify_emails', 'message'];

/** An escalation rule as the frontend reads it. */
function slaEscalationRuleView(r) {
  return {
    ...r,
    id: String(r.id),
    step_id: r.step_id ? String(r.step_id) : '',
    level: Number(r.level) || 1,
    trigger_value: Number(r.trigger_value) || 0,
    notify_current_approvers: boolFlag(r.notify_current_approvers),
    notify_requester: boolFlag(r.notify_requester),
    notify_role_ids: jsonList(r.notify_role_ids),
    notify_emails: jsonList(r.notify_emails)
  };
}

/** Validate an escalation rule (all fields, or the given ones when partial). @returns {Object} { error } or { values } */
function slaEscalationRuleValues(payload, partial, existing = {}) {
  const has = key => payload[key] !== undefined;
  const pick = key => (has(key) ? payload[key] : existing[key]);
  const values = {};
  const level = Number(pick('level'));
  if (!Number.isInteger(level) || level < 1) return { error: 'level must be a whole number from 1' };
  const trigger = String(pick('trigger') || '');
  if (!SLA_TRIGGERS.includes(trigger)) return { error: `trigger must be one of ${SLA_TRIGGERS.join(', ')}` };
  const value = Number(pick('trigger_value'));
  if (!isFinite(value) || value <= 0) return { error: 'trigger_value must be a positive number' };
  if (trigger === 'percent_elapsed' && value > 100) return { error: 'percent_elapsed must be 1-100' };
  const unit = trigger === 'percent_elapsed' ? 'percent' : String(pick('trigger_unit') || 'hours');
  if (trigger !== 'percent_elapsed' && !SLA_TRIGGER_UNITS.includes(unit)) return { error: `trigger_unit must be one of ${SLA_TRIGGER_UNITS.join(', ')}` };
  if (trigger === 'before_due' && unit.indexOf('business') === 0) return { error: 'before_due counts back in minutes, hours or days' };
  // escalation_action, not action: a payload key `action` would replace the route name (A13)
  const action = String(pick('escalation_action') || 'notify');
  if (!SLA_ACTIONS.includes(action)) return { error: `escalation_action must be one of ${SLA_ACTIONS.join(', ')}` };
  const roleIds = jsonList(pick('notify_role_ids'));
  const emails = jsonList(pick('notify_emails'));
  if (emails.some(e => !/^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$/.test(e))) return { error: 'notify_emails must be email addresses' };
  const approvers = boolFlag(pick('notify_current_approvers'));
  const requester = boolFlag(pick('notify_requester'));
  if (action === 'escalate_role' && !roleIds.length) return { error: 'escalate_role needs at least one role in notify_role_ids' };
  if (!approvers && !requester && !roleIds.length && !emails.length) return { error: 'Choose at least one recipient' };

  Object.assign(values, {
    level, trigger, trigger_value: value, trigger_unit: unit, escalation_action: action,
    notify_current_approvers: approvers, notify_requester: requester,
    notify_role_ids: JSON.stringify(roleIds), notify_emails: JSON.stringify(emails),
    name: String(pick('name') || `Level ${level}`).trim(), message: String(pick('message') || '').trim(),
    step_id: pick('step_id') ? String(pick('step_id')) : ''
  });
  if (partial) Object.keys(values).forEach(key => { if (!has(key) && !SLA_RULE_FIELDS.includes(key)) delete values[key]; });
  return { values };
}

/** getSLAEscalationRules route: live rules of a company (+ ticket type, + step incl. "all steps" rows). */
function getSLAEscalationRules(params = {}) {
  const companyId = params.company_id || params.companyId;
  if (!companyId) return { status: 'error', message: 'company_id is required' };
  const typeId = params.ticket_type_id || params.ticketTypeId;
  const stepId = params.step_id || params.stepId;
  const rules = liveRecords(SHEETS.SLA_ESCALATION_RULES)
    .filter(r => sameId(r.company_id, companyId) && (!typeId || sameId(r.ticket_type_id, typeId)) &&
      (!stepId || !r.step_id || sameId(r.step_id, stepId)))
    .map(slaEscalationRuleView)
    .sort((a, b) => String(a.ticket_type_id).localeCompare(String(b.ticket_type_id)) || a.step_id.localeCompare(b.step_id) || a.level - b.level);
  return { status: 'success', data: rules, message: `${rules.length} escalation rule(s)` };
}

/** Is another live rule already on this company + type + step + level? */
function slaRuleLevelTaken(companyId, typeId, stepId, level, exceptId = null) {
  return liveRecords(SHEETS.SLA_ESCALATION_RULES).some(r => !sameId(r.id, exceptId) && sameId(r.company_id, companyId) &&
    sameId(r.ticket_type_id, typeId) && String(r.step_id || '') === String(stepId || '') && Number(r.level) === Number(level));
}

/** A step id must belong to the rule's ticket type and company. @returns {string|null} error */
function slaRuleStepError(stepId, companyId, typeId) {
  if (!stepId) return null;
  const step = findRecord(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS, stepId);
  if (!step || !sameId(step.record.company_id, companyId) || !sameId(step.record.ticket_type_id, typeId)) {
    return `Step ${stepId} is not in this company's workflow for the ticket type`;
  }
  return null;
}

function createSLAEscalationRule(payload) {
  const actor = actorId(payload.user_id);
  const denied = requireAdmin(actor, 'manage SLA escalation rules');
  if (denied) return denied;
  if (!payload.company_id || !findRecord(SHEETS.COMPANIES, payload.company_id)) return { status: 'error', message: `Company ${payload.company_id} not found` };
  if (!payload.ticket_type_id || !findRecord(SHEETS.TICKET_TYPES, payload.ticket_type_id)) return { status: 'error', message: `Ticket type ${payload.ticket_type_id} not found` };
  const checked = slaEscalationRuleValues(payload, false);
  if (checked.error) return { status: 'error', message: checked.error };
  const stepError = slaRuleStepError(checked.values.step_id, payload.company_id, payload.ticket_type_id);
  if (stepError) return { status: 'error', message: stepError };
  if (slaRuleLevelTaken(payload.company_id, payload.ticket_type_id, checked.values.step_id, checked.values.level)) {
    return { status: 'error', message: `Level ${checked.values.level} already exists for this step` };
  }
  const now = new Date();
  const rule = appendRecord(SHEETS.SLA_ESCALATION_RULES, {
    id: generateId('sler'), company_id: payload.company_id, ticket_type_id: payload.ticket_type_id, ...checked.values,
    is_active: true, created_at: now, created_by: actor, updated_at: now, updated_by: actor
  });
  logAdminAction(actor, 'CREATE_SLA_ESCALATION_RULE', 'sla_escalation_rule', rule.id, checked.values);
  return { status: 'success', data: slaEscalationRuleView(rule), message: 'Escalation rule created' };
}

function updateSLAEscalationRule(payload) {
  const actor = actorId(payload.user_id);
  const denied = requireAdmin(actor, 'manage SLA escalation rules');
  if (denied) return denied;
  const ruleId = payload.rule_id || payload.id;
  const found = ruleId ? findRecord(SHEETS.SLA_ESCALATION_RULES, ruleId) : null;
  if (!found || !liveRecord(found.record)) return { status: 'error', message: `Escalation rule ${ruleId} not found` };
  const checked = slaEscalationRuleValues(payload, false, slaEscalationRuleView(found.record));
  if (checked.error) return { status: 'error', message: checked.error };
  const stepError = slaRuleStepError(checked.values.step_id, found.record.company_id, found.record.ticket_type_id);
  if (stepError) return { status: 'error', message: stepError };
  if (slaRuleLevelTaken(found.record.company_id, found.record.ticket_type_id, checked.values.step_id, checked.values.level, ruleId)) {
    return { status: 'error', message: `Level ${checked.values.level} already exists for this step` };
  }
  const updated = updateRecordFields(SHEETS.SLA_ESCALATION_RULES, ruleId, { ...checked.values, updated_at: new Date(), updated_by: actor });
  logAdminAction(actor, 'UPDATE_SLA_ESCALATION_RULE', 'sla_escalation_rule', ruleId, checked.values);
  return { status: 'success', data: slaEscalationRuleView(updated.after), message: 'Escalation rule saved' };
}

function deleteSLAEscalationRule(payload) {
  const actor = actorId(payload.user_id);
  const denied = requireAdmin(actor, 'manage SLA escalation rules');
  if (denied) return denied;
  const ruleId = payload.rule_id || payload.id;
  const found = ruleId ? findRecord(SHEETS.SLA_ESCALATION_RULES, ruleId) : null;
  if (!found || !liveRecord(found.record)) return { status: 'error', message: `Escalation rule ${ruleId} not found` };
  const now = new Date();
  updateRecordFields(SHEETS.SLA_ESCALATION_RULES, ruleId, { is_active: false, deactivated_at: now, deactivated_by: actor,
    deactivation_reason: payload.reason || 'admin_request', updated_at: now, updated_by: actor });
  logAdminAction(actor, 'DELETE_SLA_ESCALATION_RULE', 'sla_escalation_rule', ruleId, { reason: payload.reason || 'admin_request' });
  return { status: 'success', data: { id: String(ruleId) }, message: 'Escalation rule removed' };
}

/** getSLAEscalationEvents route: what fired on a ticket (newest first), for the ticket timeline. */
function getSLAEscalationEvents(params = {}) {
  const ticketId = params.ticket_id || params.ticketId;
  if (!ticketId) return { status: 'error', message: 'ticket_id is required' };
  const rules = recordsById(SHEETS.SLA_ESCALATION_RULES);
  const events = ((readTable(SHEETS.SLA_ESCALATION_EVENTS) || { records: [] }).records)
    .filter(e => sameId(e.ticket_id, ticketId))
    .map(e => ({
      ...e, id: String(e.id), level: Number(e.level) || 0, fired_at: isoOrBlank(e.fired_at), due_at: isoOrBlank(e.due_at),
      visit_started_at: isoOrBlank(e.visit_started_at), recipient_user_ids: jsonList(e.recipient_user_ids),
      granted_role_ids: jsonList(e.granted_role_ids), rule_name: (rules[String(e.rule_id)] || {}).name || ''
    }))
    .sort((a, b) => String(b.fired_at).localeCompare(String(a.fired_at)));
  return { status: 'success', data: events, message: `${events.length} escalation event(s)` };
}

// --- Notifications (in-app, D1) ---------------------------------------------------------------------

/** getNotifications route: the caller's own notifications, newest first. */
function getNotifications(params = {}) {
  const userId = actorId(params.user_id || params.userId);
  if (!userId) return { status: 'error', message: 'user_id is required' };
  const unreadOnly = String(params.unread_only) === 'true';
  const limit = Math.min(Math.max(Number(params.limit) || 50, 1), 200);
  const mine = ((readTable(SHEETS.NOTIFICATIONS) || { records: [] }).records)
    .filter(n => sameId(n.user_id, userId) && isActiveFlag(n.is_active));
  const unread = mine.filter(n => !boolFlag(n.is_read)).length;
  const items = mine
    .filter(n => !unreadOnly || !boolFlag(n.is_read))
    .map(n => ({ ...n, id: String(n.id), is_read: boolFlag(n.is_read), created_at: isoOrBlank(n.created_at), read_at: isoOrBlank(n.read_at) }))
    .sort((a, b) => String(b.created_at).localeCompare(String(a.created_at)))
    .slice(0, limit);
  return { status: 'success', data: { items, unread_count: unread, total: mine.length }, message: `${unread} unread` };
}

/** markNotificationsRead route: the caller's own notifications (ids, or all: true). */
function markNotificationsRead(payload = {}) {
  const userId = actorId(payload.user_id || payload.userId);
  if (!userId) return { status: 'error', message: 'user_id is required' };
  const ids = listParam(payload.ids);
  const all = boolFlag(payload.all);
  if (!all && !ids.length) return { status: 'error', message: 'Give ids or all: true' };
  const table = readTable(SHEETS.NOTIFICATIONS);
  if (!table) return { status: 'success', data: { marked: 0 }, message: 'No notifications' };
  const now = new Date();
  const changes = {};
  table.records.forEach(n => {
    if (!sameId(n.user_id, userId) || boolFlag(n.is_read)) return;
    if (!all && !ids.some(id => sameId(id, n.id))) return;
    changes[String(n.id)] = { is_read: true, read_at: now, updated_at: now };
  });
  // One write per contiguous run of rows (review CR2-07: one setValues per notification held the lock for seconds)
  const marked = writeRecordsById(SHEETS.NOTIFICATIONS, changes);
  return { status: 'success', data: { marked }, message: `${marked} notification(s) marked read` };
}

// --- Holidays (D4) ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

/** Easter Sunday (Gregorian, anonymous algorithm) as yyyy-MM-dd. */
function easterSunday(year) {
  const a = year % 19;
  const b = Math.floor(year / 100);
  const c = year % 100;
  const d = Math.floor(b / 4);
  const e = b % 4;
  const f = Math.floor((b + 8) / 25);
  const g = Math.floor((b - f + 1) / 3);
  const h = (19 * a + b - d - g + 15) % 30;
  const i = Math.floor(c / 4);
  const k = c % 4;
  const l = (32 + 2 * e + 2 * i - h - k) % 7;
  const m = Math.floor((a + 11 * h + 22 * l) / 451);
  const month = Math.floor((h + l - 7 * m + 114) / 31);
  const day = ((h + l - 7 * m + 114) % 31) + 1;
  return `${year}-${String(month).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(day).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

/** yyyy-MM-dd plus n days (calendar arithmetic, no time zone). */
function shiftDay(day, n) {
  const [y, m, d] = day.split('-').map(Number);
  const moved = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d + n));
  return `${moved.getUTCFullYear()}-${String(moved.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(moved.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

/**
 * Philippine regular holidays and the usual special non-working days of a year. Eid'l Fitr / Eid'l Adha and
 * Chinese New Year are proclaimed each year: add them by hand. Check the yearly proclamation - dates can move.
 */
function philippineHolidays(year) {
  const day = (m, d) => `${year}-${String(m).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(d).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  const easter = easterSunday(year);
  let heroes = day(8, 31);
  while (new Date(`${heroes}T12:00:00Z`).getUTCDay() !== 1) heroes = shiftDay(heroes, -1);
  return [
    { date: day(1, 1), name: "New Year's Day" },
    { date: shiftDay(easter, -3), name: 'Maundy Thursday' },
    { date: shiftDay(easter, -2), name: 'Good Friday' },
    { date: shiftDay(easter, -1), name: 'Black Saturday' },
    { date: day(4, 9), name: 'Araw ng Kagitingan' },
    { date: day(5, 1), name: 'Labor Day' },
    { date: day(6, 12), name: 'Independence Day' },
    { date: day(8, 21), name: 'Ninoy Aquino Day' },
    { date: heroes, name: 'National Heroes Day' },
    { date: day(11, 1), name: "All Saints' Day" },
    { date: day(11, 30), name: 'Bonifacio Day' },
    { date: day(12, 8), name: 'Feast of the Immaculate Conception' },
    { date: day(12, 24), name: 'Christmas Eve' },
    { date: day(12, 25), name: 'Christmas Day' },
    { date: day(12, 30), name: 'Rizal Day' },
    { date: day(12, 31), name: 'Last Day of the Year' }
  ].sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date));
}

/** getCompanyHolidays route: a company's holidays plus the ones for every company (optionally one year). */
function getCompanyHolidays(params = {}) {
  const companyId = params.company_id || params.companyId || '';
  const year = params.year ? String(params.year) : '';
  const rows = liveRecords(SHEETS.COMPANY_HOLIDAYS)
    .filter(h => (!companyId ? true : (!h.company_id || sameId(h.company_id, companyId))))
    .map(h => ({ ...h, id: String(h.id), company_id: h.company_id || '', date: holidayDay(h.date) }))
    .filter(h => h.date && (!year || h.date.indexOf(year) === 0))
    .sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date));
  return { status: 'success', data: rows, message: `${rows.length} holiday(s)` };
}

function createCompanyHoliday(payload) {
  const actor = actorId(payload.user_id);
  const denied = requireAdmin(actor, 'manage holidays');
  if (denied) return denied;
  const date = holidayDay(payload.date);
  if (!date) return { status: 'error', message: 'date must be yyyy-MM-dd' };
  if (!String(payload.name || '').trim()) return { status: 'error', message: 'name is required' };
  const companyId = payload.company_id || '';
  if (companyId && !findRecord(SHEETS.COMPANIES, companyId)) return { status: 'error', message: `Company ${companyId} not found` };
  if (liveRecords(SHEETS.COMPANY_HOLIDAYS).some(h => String(h.company_id || '') === String(companyId) && holidayDay(h.date) === date)) {
    return { status: 'error', message: `${date} is already a holiday${companyId ? ' for this company' : ''}` };
  }
  const now = new Date();
  // The date is stored as text ('2026-12-25'), so Sheets can't turn it into a midnight in another time zone
  const holiday = appendRecord(SHEETS.COMPANY_HOLIDAYS, { id: generateId('hol'), company_id: companyId, date: `'${date}`,
    name: String(payload.name).trim(), is_active: true, created_at: now, created_by: actor, updated_at: now, updated_by: actor });
  logAdminAction(actor, 'CREATE_COMPANY_HOLIDAY', 'company_holiday', holiday.id, { company_id: companyId, date, name: holiday.name });
  return { status: 'success', data: { ...holiday, date }, message: 'Holiday added' };
}

function deleteCompanyHoliday(payload) {
  const actor = actorId(payload.user_id);
  const denied = requireAdmin(actor, 'manage holidays');
  if (denied) return denied;
  const found = payload.id ? findRecord(SHEETS.COMPANY_HOLIDAYS, payload.id) : null;
  if (!found || !liveRecord(found.record)) return { status: 'error', message: `Holiday ${payload.id} not found` };
  const now = new Date();
  updateRecordFields(SHEETS.COMPANY_HOLIDAYS, payload.id, { is_active: false, deactivated_at: now, deactivated_by: actor,
    deactivation_reason: payload.reason || 'admin_request', updated_at: now, updated_by: actor });
  logAdminAction(actor, 'DELETE_COMPANY_HOLIDAY', 'company_holiday', payload.id, {});
  return { status: 'success', data: { id: String(payload.id) }, message: 'Holiday removed' };
}

/** addPhilippineHolidays route: a year's PH holidays for a company (blank = every company). Dry run by default. */
function addPhilippineHolidays(payload) {
  const actor = actorId(payload.user_id);
  const denied = requireAdmin(actor, 'manage holidays');
  if (denied) return denied;
  const year = Number(payload.year);
  if (!Number.isInteger(year) || year < 2000 || year > 2100) return { status: 'error', message: 'year must be 2000-2100' };
  const dryRun = !(payload.dry_run === false || String(payload.dry_run) === 'false');
  const companyId = payload.company_id || '';
  const existing = new Set(liveRecords(SHEETS.COMPANY_HOLIDAYS)
    .filter(h => String(h.company_id || '') === String(companyId)).map(h => holidayDay(h.date)));
  const holidays = philippineHolidays(year).map(h => ({ ...h, exists: existing.has(h.date) }));
  const toAdd = holidays.filter(h => !h.exists);
  if (!dryRun && toAdd.length) {
    const now = new Date();
    appendRecords(SHEETS.COMPANY_HOLIDAYS, toAdd.map(h => ({ id: generateId('hol'), company_id: companyId, date: `'${h.date}`,
      name: h.name, is_active: true, created_at: now, created_by: actor, updated_at: now, updated_by: actor })));
    logAdminAction(actor, 'ADD_PHILIPPINE_HOLIDAYS', 'company_holiday', companyId || 'all', { year, added: toAdd.length });
  }
  return { status: 'success', data: { dry_run: dryRun, year, company_id: companyId, holidays, added: dryRun ? 0 : toAdd.length },
    message: dryRun ? `${toAdd.length} holiday(s) would be added` : `${toAdd.length} holiday(s) added` };
}

// --- Email settings (D1: in-app first; email global default + per ticket type) -----------------------

/** Email for a ticket type's SLA notices: its sla_email ('on' / 'off'), or the global default for 'default'. */
function slaEmailEnabled(ticketType) {
  const mode = SLA_EMAIL_MODES.includes(String((ticketType || {}).sla_email)) ? String(ticketType.sla_email) : 'default';
  if (mode !== 'default') return mode === 'on';
  return String(scriptProperty('SLA_EMAIL_DEFAULT', 'off')).toLowerCase() === 'on';
}

function getSLANotificationSettings() {
  const types = liveRecords(SHEETS.TICKET_TYPES).map(t => ({ ticket_type_id: String(t.id), name: t.name,
    sla_email: SLA_EMAIL_MODES.includes(String(t.sla_email)) ? String(t.sla_email) : 'default', email_on: slaEmailEnabled(t) }));
  return { status: 'success', data: { email_default: String(scriptProperty('SLA_EMAIL_DEFAULT', 'off')).toLowerCase() === 'on' ? 'on' : 'off',
    ticket_types: types, quota_remaining: mailQuotaRemaining() }, message: 'SLA notification settings' };
}

function saveSLANotificationSettings(payload) {
  const actor = actorId(payload.user_id);
  const denied = requireAdmin(actor, 'change SLA notification settings');
  if (denied) return denied;
  const changes = {};
  if (payload.email_default !== undefined) {
    const value = String(payload.email_default).toLowerCase() === 'on' || payload.email_default === true ? 'on' : 'off';
    PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty('SLA_EMAIL_DEFAULT', value);
    changes.email_default = value;
  }
  const perType = payload.ticket_types && typeof payload.ticket_types === 'object' ? payload.ticket_types : {};
  Object.keys(perType).forEach(typeId => {
    const mode = String(perType[typeId]);
    if (!SLA_EMAIL_MODES.includes(mode)) return;
    if (updateRecordFields(SHEETS.TICKET_TYPES, typeId, { sla_email: mode, updated_at: new Date() })) changes[typeId] = mode;
  });
  logAdminAction(actor, 'SAVE_SLA_NOTIFICATION_SETTINGS', 'settings', 'sla_email', changes);
  return getSLANotificationSettings();
}

/** MailApp's remaining daily quota (0 when mail isn't available). */
function mailQuotaRemaining() {
  try {
    return MailApp.getRemainingDailyQuota();
  } catch (error) {
    return 0;
  }
}

// --- Engine ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

/** Append many records in one write (one setValues), creating the tab from TABLE_SCHEMAS when missing. */
function appendRecords(sheetName, records) {
  if (!records.length) return records;
  const sheet = ss().getSheetByName(sheetName) || ensureTableSheet(sheetName);
  const headers = sheetHeaders(sheet);
  sheet.getRange(sheet.getLastRow() + 1, 1, records.length, headers.length).setValues(records.map(r => rowFromRecord(headers, r)));
  invalidateTable(cacheNameOf(sheet));
  return records;
}

/**
 * Merge changes into records by id and write them back with one setValues per run of contiguous rows (v9.5,
 * review CR2-07). Keys that aren't columns are ignored. @returns {number} records written
 */
function writeRecordsById(sheetName, changesById) {
  const ids = Object.keys(changesById || {});
  if (!ids.length) return 0;
  const table = readTable(sheetName);
  if (!table) return 0;
  const rows = [];
  table.records.forEach((record, index) => {
    const changes = changesById[String(record.id)];
    if (changes) rows.push({ rowNumber: table.rowNumbers[index], values: rowFromRecord(table.headers, { ...record, ...changes }) });
  });
  rows.sort((a, b) => a.rowNumber - b.rowNumber);
  let start = 0;
  for (let i = 1; i <= rows.length; i++) {
    if (i === rows.length || rows[i].rowNumber !== rows[i - 1].rowNumber + 1) {
      table.sheet.getRange(rows[start].rowNumber, 1, i - start, table.headers.length).setValues(rows.slice(start, i).map(r => r.values));
      start = i;
    }
  }
  invalidateTable(sheetName);
  return rows.length;
}

/** When a rule fires for a visit that started at `start` and is due at `due`. null when it can't. */
function slaFireTime(rule, start, due, cal) {
  if (!due) return null;
  const value = Number(rule.trigger_value) || 0;
  switch (String(rule.trigger)) {
    case 'percent_elapsed':
      return start && due > start ? new Date(start.getTime() + (due.getTime() - start.getTime()) * value / 100) : null;
    case 'before_due': {
