      // Wall-clock look-back (validation allows minutes / hours / days only). Counting forward from the due date with
      // the calendar made weekends after it stretch the span - review CR2-04
      const unitMs = { minutes: 60 * 1000, hours: 60 * 60 * 1000, days: DAY_MS }[rule.trigger_unit] || 60 * 60 * 1000;
      return new Date(due.getTime() - value * unitMs);
    }
    case 'after_due':
      return addSlaTime(due, value, rule.trigger_unit, cal);
    default:
      return null;
  }
}

/** The rules for a ticket's current step: per level, a rule for the step wins over an "all steps" rule. */
function slaRulesForTicket(ticket, rulesByCompanyType) {
  const candidates = rulesByCompanyType[`${ticket.company_id}|${ticket.ticket_type_id}`] || [];
  const byLevel = {};
  candidates.forEach(rule => {
    if (rule.step_id && !sameId(rule.step_id, ticket.current_step_id)) return;
    const level = Number(rule.level) || 1;
    if (!byLevel[level] || (rule.step_id && !byLevel[level].step_id)) byLevel[level] = rule;
  });
  return Object.keys(byLevel).map(Number).sort((a, b) => a - b).map(level => byLevel[level]);
}

/**
 * Fire the SLA escalation rules that are due (v9.5). Runs from a 15-minute trigger (installSLAEscalationTrigger),
 * from runSLAEscalationsNow (admin) and from the tests with an injected `now`. Idempotent: an event row per
 * ticket + step + visit + rule, so a rule fires once per visit and a second run fires nothing new. Takes the
 * script lock with tryLock and skips when busy (A19). At most 200 notifications per run; the rest go next run.
 * @param {Object} options - { dryRun, now }
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { dry_run, skipped, checked, fired: [...], notifications, emails_sent,
 *   emails_skipped, capped } }
 */
function runSLAEscalations(options = {}) {
  const dryRun = options.dryRun === true || options.dry_run === true || String(options.dry_run) === 'true';
  const now = options.now ? new Date(options.now) : new Date();
  let lock = null;
  if (!dryRun && !scriptLockHeld) {
    lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
    if (!lock.tryLock(5000)) {
      return { status: 'success', data: { dry_run: dryRun, skipped: true, reason: 'busy' }, message: 'Another run or a write holds the lock - skipped (the next run catches up)' };
    }
    scriptLockHeld = true;
  }
  let result;
  try {
    // Events, notices (email_status 'queued') and logs are written here, under the lock - the idempotency record
    // exists before any email goes out (review CR2-03)
    result = withRequestScope(() => slaEscalationPass(now, dryRun));
  } finally {
    if (lock) {
      scriptLockHeld = false;
      lock.releaseLock();
    }
  }
  // Email outside the lock, so user writes don't queue behind MailApp (review CR2-05). When an admin runs it from
  // runSLAEscalationsNow, the request's own write lock is still held - a rare, admin-started run
  const pending = result.pendingMail;
  delete result.pendingMail;
  if (pending && !dryRun) sendSLAEmails(pending, result.data);
  return result;
}

/**
 * Send the digests of a run and record each notice's email_status (sent | quota | error) in one batch (v9.5).
 * v9.6: one digest per company + address, with the company's sender name / Reply-To / CC / footer (sendCompanyEmail).
 * @param {Object} pending - { mail: { '<company_id>|<address>': [lines] }, noticeEmail: { notificationId: same key } }
 * @param {Object} summary - The run summary (emails_sent / emails_skipped are updated)
 */
function sendSLAEmails(pending, summary) {
  const keys = Object.keys(pending.mail);
  if (!keys.length) return;
  const companies = recordsById(SHEETS.COMPANIES);
  const sent = {};
  keys.forEach(key => {
    const split = key.indexOf('|');
    const companyId = key.slice(0, split);
    const address = key.slice(split + 1);
    const result = sendCompanyEmail(companies[companyId], { to: address, subject: `[JARVIS] SLA notices (${pending.mail[key].length})`,
      body: pending.mail[key].join('\n\n') + '\n\nOpen the app to act on these tickets.' });
    sent[key] = result.status;
    if (result.status === 'sent') summary.emails_sent++;
    else summary.emails_skipped++;
  });
  const now = new Date();
  const changes = {};
  Object.keys(pending.noticeEmail).forEach(id => { changes[id] = { email_status: sent[pending.noticeEmail[id]] || 'error', updated_at: now }; });
  withScriptLock(() => withRequestScope(() => writeRecordsById(SHEETS.NOTIFICATIONS, changes)));
}
function slaEscalationPass(now, dryRun) {
  const rules = liveRecords(SHEETS.SLA_ESCALATION_RULES);
  const summary = { dry_run: dryRun, skipped: false, checked: 0, fired: [], notifications: 0, emails_sent: 0, emails_skipped: 0, capped: false };
  if (!rules.length) return { status: 'success', data: summary, message: 'No escalation rules' };

  const rulesByCompanyType = groupBy(rules, r => `${r.company_id}|${r.ticket_type_id}`);
  const tickets = ((readTable(SHEETS.TICKETS) || { records: [] }).records).filter(t => t.current_step_id && t.step_due_date &&
    isActiveFlag(t.is_active) && !t.cancelled_at && !TERMINAL_TICKET_STATUSES.includes(t.status));
  const eventRows = (readTable(SHEETS.SLA_ESCALATION_EVENTS) || { records: [] }).records;
  const fired = new Set(eventRows.map(e => `${e.ticket_id}|${e.step_id}|${isoOrBlank(e.visit_started_at)}|${e.rule_id}`));
  const historyRows = (readTable(SHEETS.TICKET_HISTORY) || { records: [] }).records;
  const approverRoles = groupBy((readTable(SHEETS.STEP_APPROVERS) || { records: [] }).records, a => String(a.step_id));
  const assignments = ((readTable(SHEETS.USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS) || { records: [] }).records).filter(a => isAssignmentActive(a, now));
  const profiles = recordsById(SHEETS.USER_PROFILES, 'user_id');
  const preferences = recordsById(SHEETS.USER_PREFERENCES, 'user_id');
  const ticketTypes = recordsById(SHEETS.TICKET_TYPES);
  const companies = recordsById(SHEETS.COMPANIES); // v9.6: company email settings
  const steps = recordsById(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS);
  const companySla = liveRecords(SHEETS.COMPANY_SLA_RULES);
  const holidayRows = liveRecords(SHEETS.COMPANY_HOLIDAYS);

  const holders = (roleIds, ticket) => assignments.filter(a => roleIds.includes(String(a.role_id)) && sameId(a.company_id, ticket.company_id) &&
    (!a.ticket_type_id || sameId(a.ticket_type_id, ticket.ticket_type_id))).map(a => String(a.user_id));

  const newEvents = [];
  const newNotifications = [];
  const newLogs = [];
  const mail = {}; // v9.6: '<company_id>|<email>' -> [lines] (one digest per company sender settings)

  for (const ticket of tickets) {
    summary.checked++;
    const ticketRules = slaRulesForTicket(ticket, rulesByCompanyType);
    if (!ticketRules.length) continue;
    const step = steps[String(ticket.current_step_id)] || { name: '' };
    const start = visitStartedAt(ticket, historyRows);
    const due = toDate(ticket.step_due_date);
    const companyRule = companySLARuleFor(ticket.company_id, ticket.ticket_type_id, step.name, companySla);
    const cal = slaCalendar(companyRule, holidayDaysFor(ticket.company_id, holidayRows));
    const visit = isoOrBlank(start);

    const dueRules = ticketRules.filter(rule => {
      const at = slaFireTime(rule, start, due, cal);
      return at && now >= at && !fired.has(`${ticket.id}|${ticket.current_step_id}|${visit}|${rule.id}`);
    });
    if (!dueRules.length) continue;

    // One notice per recipient for the highest level that came due; every due level is recorded
    const top = dueRules[dueRules.length - 1];
    const grantedNow = unique([].concat(...dueRules.filter(r => r.escalation_action === 'escalate_role').map(r => jsonList(r.notify_role_ids))));
    const alreadyGranted = escalationGrantedRoles(ticket, eventRows, historyRows);
    let recipients = [];
    if (boolFlag(top.notify_current_approvers)) {
      const stepRoles = (approverRoles[String(ticket.current_step_id)] || []).map(a => String(a.role_id));
      recipients = recipients.concat(holders(unique(stepRoles.concat(alreadyGranted)), ticket));
    }
    if (boolFlag(top.notify_requester) && ticket.requester_id) recipients.push(String(ticket.requester_id));
    recipients = unique(recipients.concat(holders(jsonList(top.notify_role_ids), ticket)));
    const extraEmails = jsonList(top.notify_emails);

    if (summary.notifications + recipients.length > SLA_MAX_NOTIFICATIONS_PER_RUN && summary.notifications > 0) {
      summary.capped = true;
      break;
    }

    const overdue = now >= due;
    const type = top.escalation_action === 'escalate_role' ? 'sla_escalated' : (overdue ? 'sla_overdue' : 'sla_warning');
    const label = ticket.ticket_number || `#${ticket.id}`;
    const title = type === 'sla_escalated' ? `Escalated: ${label}` : (overdue ? `Overdue: ${label}` : `SLA warning: ${label}`);
    const dueText = Utilities.formatDate(due, MANILA_TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm');
    const message = (top.message ? `${top.message} - ` : '') + `"${ticket.title || ''}" at step ${step.name || ticket.current_step_id}, due ${dueText} (Manila)` +
      (grantedNow.length ? '. You can now act on this step.' : '');
    // v9.6: the company's email_enabled (on / off) overrides the ticket type's sla_email
    const emailOn = companyEmailEnabled(companies[String(ticket.company_id)], ticketTypes[String(ticket.ticket_type_id)]);
    const mailKey = address => `${ticket.company_id}|${address}`;

    recipients.forEach(userId => {
      const profile = profiles[userId] || {};
      const pref = preferences[userId];
      let emailStatus = 'none';
      if (emailOn) {
        if (pref && pref.email_notifications !== undefined && !boolFlag(pref.email_notifications)) emailStatus = 'pref_off';
        else if (!profile.email) emailStatus = 'no_email';
        else {
          emailStatus = 'queued';
          (mail[mailKey(profile.email)] = mail[mailKey(profile.email)] || []).push(`${title}: ${message}`);
        }
      }
      newNotifications.push({ id: generateId('ntf'), user_id: userId, company_id: ticket.company_id, ticket_id: ticket.id, type, title,
        message, link: `/tickets?ticket=${ticket.id}`, is_read: false, read_at: '', email_status: emailStatus, is_active: true,
        created_at: now, updated_at: now });
    });
    if (emailOn) extraEmails.forEach(email => { (mail[mailKey(email)] = mail[mailKey(email)] || []).push(`${title}: ${message}`); });
    summary.notifications += recipients.length;

    dueRules.forEach(rule => {
      newEvents.push({ id: generateId('sle'), rule_id: rule.id, ticket_id: ticket.id, company_id: ticket.company_id, step_id: ticket.current_step_id,
        visit_started_at: visit, level: Number(rule.level) || 1, escalation_action: rule.escalation_action, due_at: due, fired_at: now,
        recipient_user_ids: JSON.stringify(rule === top ? recipients : []),
        granted_role_ids: JSON.stringify(rule.escalation_action === 'escalate_role' ? jsonList(rule.notify_role_ids) : []),
        notification_count: rule === top ? recipients.length : 0, is_active: true, created_at: now });
      fired.add(`${ticket.id}|${ticket.current_step_id}|${visit}|${rule.id}`);
    });
    newLogs.push({ id: generateId('tal'), ticket_id: ticket.id, user_id: 'system', action_type: 'SLA_ESCALATION',
      details: JSON.stringify({ levels: dueRules.map(r => Number(r.level)), type, recipients: recipients.length, granted_role_ids: grantedNow, step_id: ticket.current_step_id }),
      timestamp: now });
    summary.fired.push({ ticket_id: String(ticket.id), ticket_number: ticket.ticket_number || '', step_id: String(ticket.current_step_id),
      levels: dueRules.map(r => Number(r.level)), type, recipients, granted_role_ids: grantedNow });
  }

  // Record first (idempotency, review CR2-03); runSLAEscalations sends the mail after releasing the lock (CR2-05)
  const noticeEmail = {};
  newNotifications.forEach(n => {
    if (n.email_status === 'queued') noticeEmail[String(n.id)] = `${n.company_id}|${(profiles[String(n.user_id)] || {}).email}`;
  });
  if (!dryRun) {
    appendRecords(SHEETS.SLA_ESCALATION_EVENTS, newEvents);
    appendRecords(SHEETS.NOTIFICATIONS, newNotifications);
    appendRecords(SHEETS.TICKET_ACTION_LOGS, newLogs);
  }
  summary.emails_planned = Object.keys(mail).length;
  Logger.log(`SLA escalations${dryRun ? ' (dry run)' : ''}: checked ${summary.checked}, fired ${summary.fired.length}, notices ${summary.notifications}, emails ${summary.emails_sent}`);
  return { status: 'success', data: summary, message: `${summary.fired.length} ticket(s) escalated${dryRun ? ' (dry run)' : ''}`,
    pendingMail: dryRun ? null : { mail, noticeEmail } };
}

/** runSLAEscalationsNow route (admin): a dry run unless dry_run is false. */
function runSLAEscalationsNow(payload = {}) {
  const denied = requireAdmin(actorId(payload.user_id), 'run SLA escalations');
  if (denied) return denied;
  const dryRun = !(payload.dry_run === false || String(payload.dry_run) === 'false');
  return runSLAEscalations({ dryRun });
}

/** testSLAEscalations route (admin, read-only): what would fire now. */
function testSLAEscalations(params = {}) {
  const denied = requireAdmin(actorId(params.user_id), 'test SLA escalations');
  if (denied) return denied;
  return runSLAEscalations({ dryRun: true });
}

// --- Editor-only (A19): trigger, dry run, migration --------------------------------------------------------

/** Install the 15-minute trigger (removes duplicates first). Run once from the editor; asks for permissions. */
function installSLAEscalationTrigger() {
  removeSLAEscalationTrigger();
  ScriptApp.newTrigger('runSLAEscalationsTrigger').timeBased().everyMinutes(15).create();
  Logger.log('SLA escalation trigger installed (every 15 minutes)');
  return { installed: true };
}

function removeSLAEscalationTrigger() {
  let removed = 0;
  ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().forEach(trigger => {
    if (trigger.getHandlerFunction() === 'runSLAEscalationsTrigger') {
      ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(trigger);
      removed++;
    }
  });
  Logger.log(`Removed ${removed} SLA escalation trigger(s)`);
  return { removed };
}

/** The trigger's handler. */
function runSLAEscalationsTrigger() {
  return runSLAEscalations({});
}

/** Editor: what would fire now, without writing or sending anything. */
function runSLAEscalationsDryRun() {
  const result = runSLAEscalations({ dryRun: true });
  Logger.log(JSON.stringify(result.data, null, 2));
  return result;
}

/**
 * Editor: turn the escalation fields of company SLA rules (warning_threshold, escalation_*) into escalation rules
 * (level 1: percent_elapsed warning to the current approvers; level 2: after_due to the recipients). Dry run.
 */
function migrateCompanyEscalationsToRules(dryRun = true) {
  return withRequestScope(() => {
    const now = new Date();
    const planned = [];
    const stepsTable = (readTable(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS) || { records: [] }).records;
    liveRecords(SHEETS.COMPANY_SLA_RULES).forEach(rule => {
      const view = companySLARuleView(rule);
      const step = stepsTable.find(s => sameId(s.company_id, rule.company_id) && sameId(s.ticket_type_id, rule.ticket_type_id) &&
        String(s.name || '').trim().toLowerCase() === String(rule.step_name || '').trim().toLowerCase());
      const stepId = step ? String(step.id) : '';
      const base = { company_id: rule.company_id, ticket_type_id: rule.ticket_type_id, step_id: stepId };
      if (view.warning_threshold) {
        planned.push({ ...base, level: 1, name: `${view.warning_threshold}% warning`, trigger: 'percent_elapsed',
          trigger_value: view.warning_threshold, trigger_unit: 'percent', escalation_action: 'notify', notify_current_approvers: true,
          notify_requester: false, notify_role_ids: '[]', notify_emails: '[]', message: '', source_rule: String(rule.id) });
      }
      if (view.escalation_enabled && Number(view.escalation_delay) > 0) {
        const recipients = view.escalation_recipients;
        const emails = recipients.filter(r => r.indexOf('@') !== -1);
        planned.push({ ...base, level: 2, name: 'Escalation', trigger: 'after_due', trigger_value: Number(view.escalation_delay),
          trigger_unit: SLA_TRIGGER_UNITS.includes(String(view.escalation_unit)) ? String(view.escalation_unit) : 'hours', escalation_action: 'notify',
          notify_current_approvers: true, notify_requester: false, notify_role_ids: '[]', notify_emails: JSON.stringify(emails),
          message: 'SLA breached', source_rule: String(rule.id) });
      }
    });
    const toCreate = planned.filter(p => !slaRuleLevelTaken(p.company_id, p.ticket_type_id, p.step_id, p.level));
    if (!dryRun && toCreate.length) {
      appendRecords(SHEETS.SLA_ESCALATION_RULES, toCreate.map(p => {
        const { source_rule, ...rule } = p;
        return { id: generateId('sler'), ...rule, is_active: true, created_at: now, created_by: 'migration', updated_at: now, updated_by: 'migration' };
      }));
    }
    const result = { dry_run: dryRun, planned: planned.length, created: dryRun ? 0 : toCreate.length, skipped_existing: planned.length - toCreate.length, rules: toCreate };
    Logger.log(JSON.stringify(result, null, 2));
    return result;
  });
}

function migrateCompanyEscalationsToRulesApply() {
  return migrateCompanyEscalationsToRules(false);
}


/**
 * =================================================================================
 * v9.6: ADMIN CHECKS, SIGN-IN ALLOW-LIST, INVITES, ATTACHMENT LINKS, COMPANY EMAIL
 * (docs/planning/HANDOFF_v9.6_20261031.md, PLAN_FeatureGaps_Volume_20261031.md §4.12 / §4.13,
 *  REVIEW_AppScriptReco_20261030.md §4 "S1 part 3")
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * doPost actions only admins may call (S1 part 3). Checked in doPost before the handler runs:
 * AUTH_MODE enforce refuses a non-admin (code ADMIN_ONLY), warn logs and continues, off doesn't check.
 * While no active admin assignment exists (bootstrap), any verified user may call them (logged ADMIN_BOOTSTRAP).
 * Kept open on purpose: tags (users tag tickets), createTicket, setCustomFieldValue, generateTicketNumber,
 * submitStepApproval, updateTicket(Status), updateUserPreferences, recordLogin, shareTicket / revokeTicketShare,
 * markNotificationsRead and the v9.6 link routes (their own requester / approver / admin rule).
 */
const ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS = ['createCompany', 'updateCompany', 'deleteCompany', 'reactivateCompany',
  'createRole', 'updateRole', 'deleteRole',
  'createDropdownList', 'updateDropdownList', 'deleteDropdownList', 'testMinimalDropdownCreate',
  'createTicketType', 'updateTicketType', 'deleteTicketType',
  'createUserProfileType', 'updateUserProfileType', 'deleteUserProfileType',
  'createRoleType', 'updateRoleType', 'deleteRoleType',
  'createUserProfile', 'updateUserProfile',
  'createCustomField', 'updateCustomField', 'deleteCustomField',
  'initializeUserManagementTables', 'completeUserManagementSetup',
  'setStepApprovers', 'assignUserRole', 'removeUserRole', 'bulkAssignRole', 'replaceUserRoles',
  'updateUserRoleAssignment', 'deleteUserRoleAssignment', 'getExpiringAssignments',
  'createWorkflowStep', 'updateWorkflowStep', 'deleteWorkflowStep', 'copyWorkflowSteps', 'createDefaultWorkflow',
  'lockCompany', 'unlockCompany', 'saveCommentRequirements', 'saveStepConditions', 'saveReportConfig',
  'assignDropdownToCompany', 'assignDropdownGlobally', 'removeDropdownCompanyAssignment', 'updateDropdownCompanyAssignments',
  'createCompanySLARule', 'updateCompanySLARule', 'deleteCompanySLARule',
  'createConfigurationBackup', 'setTicketTypeFeatures', 'createTicketLink', 'deleteTicketLink',
  // v9.6 routes
  'createUserInvite', 'resendUserInvite', 'revokeUserInvite', 'saveCompanyEmailSettings', 'sendCompanyTestEmail'];

/** ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS that only read: no generic admin-log row for them. */
const ADMIN_ONLY_READS = ['getExpiringAssignments'];

/** Payload keys that name the record an admin write touched, in priority order (generic admin-log row). */
const ADMIN_LOG_ID_KEYS = ['invite_id', 'assignment_id', 'step_id', 'field_id', 'rule_id', 'link_id', 'user_profile_type_id',
  'role_type_id', 'role_id', 'dropdown_list_id', 'ticket_type_id', 'id', 'company_id', 'user_id'];

const INVITE_STATUSES = ['pending', 'accepted', 'revoked', 'expired'];
const INVITE_EXPIRY_DAYS = 14;
const ALLOW_CACHE_SECONDS = 600; // 10 minutes
const INACTIVE_PROFILE_STATUSES = ['inactive', 'terminated', 'suspended', 'disabled'];
const LINK_STATUSES = ['ok', 'reported_broken'];
const COMPANY_EMAIL_MODES = ['default', 'on', 'off'];
const EMAIL_PATTERN = /^[^\s@,;<>]+@[^\s@,;<>]+\.[^\s@,;<>]+$/;
const LINK_URL_PATTERN = /^https?:\/\/[^\s<>"']+$/i;
// v9.7: scheme + host of a URL. The host may not contain '@' or ':' - so docs.google.com@evil.com doesn't match.
const LINK_HOST_PATTERN = /^(https?):\/\/([^\/?#@:\s]+)(?::443)?(?:[\/?#]|$)/i;
const DEFAULT_LINK_ALLOWED_HOSTS = 'docs.google.com,drive.google.com';

// --- Admin gate (S1 part 3) ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------

/**
 * Is the bootstrap window open? True while no active assignment of an active admin role exists, so the first
 * admin can be set up. Closed as soon as one exists.
 * @returns {boolean}
 */
function adminBootstrapOpen() {
  const roles = readTable(SHEETS.ROLES);
  const adminRoleIds = new Set((roles ? roles.records : [])
    .filter(r => isActiveFlag(r.is_active) && String(r.name || '').toLowerCase().includes('admin')).map(r => String(r.id)));
  if (!adminRoleIds.size) return true;
  const assignments = readTable(SHEETS.USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS);
  const now = new Date();
  return !(assignments ? assignments.records : []).some(a => adminRoleIds.has(String(a.role_id)) && isAssignmentActive(a, now));
}

/**
 * Admin rule for the v9.6 handlers: an admin, or any signed-in user while bootstrap is open.
 * @param {string} userId - Actor (from actorId)
 * @param {string} what - "invite users" etc. for the message
 * @returns {Object|null} error response or null
 */
function requireAdminOrBootstrap(userId, what) {
  if (isAdminUser(userId)) return null;
  if (userId && adminBootstrapOpen()) return null;
  return { status: 'error', code: 'ADMIN_ONLY', message: `Only admins can ${what}` };
}

/**
 * The admin check doPost runs before an ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS handler. The actor is the verified uid
 * (actorId(null)): on admin routes a body user_id often names the target (assignee, profile), not the caller.
 * @param {string} action - Action name
 * @returns {Object} { denied: error response | null, bootstrap: boolean, actor }
 */
function checkAdminOnly(action) {
  const mode = REQUEST_AUTH.mode;
  if (mode === 'off' || !ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS.includes(action)) return { denied: null, bootstrap: false, actor: null };
  const actor = actorId(null);
  if (actor && isAdminUser(actor)) return { denied: null, bootstrap: false, actor };
  if (actor && adminBootstrapOpen()) {
    console.log(`ADMIN_BOOTSTRAP: ${action} by ${actor} (no admin assigned yet)`);
    return { denied: null, bootstrap: true, actor };
  }
  if (mode === 'enforce') {
    return { denied: { success: false, status: 'error', code: 'ADMIN_ONLY', error: `Only admins can ${action}` }, bootstrap: false, actor };
  }
  console.log(`AUTH warn: ${action} by non-admin ${actor || '(no verified user)'}`);
  return { denied: null, bootstrap: false, actor };
}

/** The parsed body of a TextOutput (null when it isn't JSON). */
function responseBody(output) {
  try {
    return JSON.parse(output.getContent());
  } catch (error) {
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * Run an ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS route and make sure the write left an admin_action_logs row: when it succeeded and
 * the handler logged nothing, one generic row (action_type = the action, target id from the payload's id keys,
 * details = the payload keys, not their values). Runs inside the script lock.
 * @returns {ContentService.TextOutput}
 */
function routeAdminAction(action, data, gate) {
  ADMIN_LOGGED = false;
  const output = routePostAction(action, data);
  const body = responseBody(output);
  const succeeded = body && body.success === true;
  if (succeeded && !ADMIN_LOGGED && !ADMIN_ONLY_READS.includes(action)) {
    const payload = data.payload && typeof data.payload === 'object' ? { ...data, ...data.payload } : data;
    const idKey = ADMIN_LOG_ID_KEYS.find(key => payload[key] !== undefined && payload[key] !== null && payload[key] !== '');
    const targetId = idKey ? payload[idKey] : (body.data && (body.data.id || body.data.user_id)) || '';
    logAdminAction(gate.actor || getCurrentUserId(), action, 'action', String(targetId),
      { generic: true, keys: Object.keys(payload).filter(k => k !== 'action' && k !== 'payload').sort() });
  }
  if (succeeded && gate.bootstrap) {
    logAdminAction(gate.actor, 'ADMIN_BOOTSTRAP', 'action', action, { note: 'no active admin yet - assign the admin role' });
  }
  return output;
}

// --- Sign-in allow-list + invites (plan §4.12) ---------------------------------------------------------------

/** The script cache, or null where CacheService isn't available (editor without the service, old test fakes). */
function scriptCache() {
  try {
    return CacheService.getScriptCache();
  } catch (error) {
    return null;
  }
}

function allowCacheKey(uid) {
  return `allow:${uid}`;
}

/** Forget that a user passed the allow-list (profile deactivated, role removed). Safe to call for any id. */
function dropAllowCache(uid) {
  if (!uid) return;
  const cache = scriptCache();
  try {
    if (cache) cache.remove(allowCacheKey(uid));
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`allow cache remove failed: ${error.message}`);
  }
}

/** Is a user_profiles row usable? Live and not in an inactive status. */
function profileIsActive(profile) {
  return !!profile && liveRecord(profile) && !INACTIVE_PROFILE_STATUSES.includes(String(profile.status || '').trim().toLowerCase());
}

/**
 * Who may use the app: an active user_profiles row, or (no profile row at all) an active role assignment.
 * A profile that exists but is deactivated refuses, even with roles.
 * @returns {string} 'allowed' | 'disabled' | 'unknown'
 */
function allowListStatus(uid) {
  const profiles = readTable(SHEETS.USER_PROFILES);
  const rows = (profiles ? profiles.records : []).filter(p => sameId(p.user_id, uid));
  if (rows.length) return rows.some(profileIsActive) ? 'allowed' : 'disabled';
  const assignments = readTable(SHEETS.USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS);
  const now = new Date();
  return (assignments ? assignments.records : []).some(a => sameId(a.user_id, uid) && isAssignmentActive(a, now)) ? 'allowed' : 'unknown';
}

/**
 * The sign-in allow-list, run by doGet / doPost after authenticateRequest. Only verified requests are checked
 * (an unverified one was already refused in enforce). Pass → `allow:<uid>` cached for 10 minutes, so most
 * requests read nothing (A16). No profile and no role → a pending invite for the verified email is accepted;
 * else NOT_INVITED. Skipped while bootstrap is open. enforce refuses, warn logs and continues.
 * @param {string} action - Action name
 * @returns {Object|null} error response, or null to continue
 */
function checkAllowList(action) {
  const auth = REQUEST_AUTH;
  if (auth.mode === 'off' || action === 'ping' || !auth.verified || !auth.uid) return null;
  const cache = scriptCache();
  try {
    if (cache && cache.get(allowCacheKey(auth.uid))) return null;
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`allow cache read failed: ${error.message}`);
  }

  let status = allowListStatus(auth.uid);
  if (status === 'unknown' && adminBootstrapOpen()) return null;
  if (status === 'unknown' && auth.email) {
    status = withScriptLock(() => {
      // Another request of the same first sign-in may have accepted the invite while this one waited for the lock:
      // read the tabs again (this request's cache is from before the lock), then accept only if still unknown
      [SHEETS.USER_PROFILES, SHEETS.USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS, SHEETS.USER_INVITES].forEach(invalidateTable);
      const again = allowListStatus(auth.uid);
      if (again !== 'unknown') return again;
      return acceptPendingInvite(auth.uid, auth.email) ? 'allowed' : 'unknown';
    });
  }
  if (status === 'allowed') {
    try {
      if (cache) cache.put(allowCacheKey(auth.uid), '1', ALLOW_CACHE_SECONDS);
    } catch (error) {
      Logger.log(`allow cache write failed: ${error.message}`);
    }
    return null;
  }

  const refusal = status === 'disabled'
    ? { success: false, status: 'error', code: 'ACCOUNT_DISABLED', error: 'Your account is deactivated. Ask your administrator.' }
    : { success: false, status: 'error', code: 'NOT_INVITED', error: 'Your account isn\'t set up yet. Ask your administrator for an invite.' };
  if (auth.mode === 'enforce') return refusal;
  console.log(`AUTH warn: ${action} by ${auth.uid} - ${refusal.code}`);
  return null;
}

/** An invite's status as of now: a pending invite past expires_at reads as expired. */
function inviteStatus(invite, now = new Date()) {
  const status = INVITE_STATUSES.includes(String(invite.status)) ? String(invite.status) : 'pending';
  if (status !== 'pending') return status;
  const expires = toDate(invite.expires_at);
  return expires && expires <= now ? 'expired' : 'pending';
}

function normalizeEmail(value) {
  return String(value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
}

/** role_grants cell -> [{ role_id, company_id, ticket_type_id, effective_start_date, effective_end_date }] */
function inviteRoleGrants(value) {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value;
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(String(value || '[]'));
    return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : [];
  } catch (error) {
    return [];
  }
}

/**
 * Accept the pending, unexpired invite for a verified email: write the profile and role assignments with the
 * real uid, mark the invite accepted. Caller holds the script lock.
 * @returns {boolean} true when an invite was accepted
 */
function acceptPendingInvite(uid, email) {
  const address = normalizeEmail(email);
  if (!uid || !address) return false;
  const now = new Date();
  const invites = readTable(SHEETS.USER_INVITES);
  const invite = (invites ? invites.records : [])
    .filter(i => normalizeEmail(i.email) === address && inviteStatus(i, now) === 'pending' && isActiveFlag(i.is_active))
    .sort((a, b) => (toDate(b.created_at) || 0) - (toDate(a.created_at) || 0))[0];
  if (!invite) return false;

  const by = invite.invited_by || 'system';
  appendRecord(SHEETS.USER_PROFILES, {
    user_id: uid, user_profile_type_id: invite.user_profile_type_id || '', display_name: invite.display_name || address,
    email: address, immediate_approver_id: invite.immediate_approver_id || '', backup_approver_id: invite.backup_approver_id || '',
    status: 'active', is_active: true, created_at: now, created_by: by, updated_at: now, updated_by: by
  });
  const grants = inviteRoleGrants(invite.role_grants);
  const grantResults = grants.map(g => {
    const res = createUserRoleAssignment({ user_id: uid, role_id: g.role_id, company_id: g.company_id || invite.company_id,
      ticket_type_id: g.ticket_type_id || '', effective_start_date: g.effective_start_date || '',
      effective_end_date: g.effective_end_date || '', assigned_by_user_id: by, assignment_notes: `Invite ${invite.id}` });
    return { role_id: g.role_id, status: res.status, message: res.status === 'success' ? '' : res.message };
  });
  updateRecordFields(SHEETS.USER_INVITES, invite.id, { status: 'accepted', accepted_uid: uid, accepted_at: now, updated_at: now, updated_by: uid });
  logAdminAction(uid, 'INVITE_ACCEPTED', 'user_invite', invite.id, { email: address, roles: grantResults });
  return true;
}

/** user_invites record -> the shape the admin screen shows (effective status, parsed grants). */
function inviteView(invite, now = new Date()) {
  return { ...invite, id: String(invite.id), status: inviteStatus(invite, now), role_grants: inviteRoleGrants(invite.role_grants),
    expires_at: isoOrBlank(invite.expires_at), accepted_at: isoOrBlank(invite.accepted_at), created_at: isoOrBlank(invite.created_at),
    last_sent_at: isoOrBlank(invite.last_sent_at), send_count: Number(invite.send_count) || 0 };
}

/** getUserInvites (GET, admin): invites, newest first; status = pending | accepted | revoked | expired | all (default pending). */
function getUserInvites(params = {}) {
  const denied = requireAdminOrBootstrap(actorId(params.user_id), 'see invites');
  if (denied) return denied;
  const wanted = String(params.status || 'pending');
  const now = new Date();
  const table = readTable(SHEETS.USER_INVITES);
  const list = (table ? table.records : []).map(i => inviteView(i, now))
    .filter(i => (wanted === 'all' || i.status === wanted) && (!params.company_id || sameId(i.company_id, params.company_id)))
    .sort((a, b) => String(b.created_at).localeCompare(String(a.created_at)));
  return { status: 'success', data: list, message: `${list.length} invite(s)` };
}

/**
 * createUserInvite (admin): { email, display_name, company_id, user_profile_type_id, role_grants: [{ role_id,
 * ticket_type_id?, company_id?, effective_start_date?, effective_end_date? }], immediate_approver_id,
 * backup_approver_id, send_email (default true) }. No token in the email: the Google sign-in is the proof.
 */
function createUserInvite(payload = {}) {
  const actor = actorId(payload.user_id);
  const denied = requireAdminOrBootstrap(actor, 'invite users');
  if (denied) return denied;
  const email = normalizeEmail(payload.email);
  if (!EMAIL_PATTERN.test(email)) return { status: 'error', message: 'A valid email is required' };
  if (!payload.company_id) return { status: 'error', message: 'company_id is required' };
  const companies = recordsById(SHEETS.COMPANIES);
  const company = companies[String(payload.company_id)];
  if (!company || !liveRecord(company)) return { status: 'error', message: `Company ${payload.company_id} not found` };
  if (findUserIdByEmail(email)) return { status: 'error', message: `${email} already has a user profile` };
  const now = new Date();
  const invites = readTable(SHEETS.USER_INVITES);
  if ((invites ? invites.records : []).some(i => normalizeEmail(i.email) === email && inviteStatus(i, now) === 'pending')) {
    return { status: 'error', message: `${email} already has a pending invite - resend it instead` };
  }
  const grants = inviteRoleGrants(payload.role_grants).map(g => ({ role_id: String(g.role_id || ''),
    company_id: String(g.company_id || payload.company_id), ticket_type_id: String(g.ticket_type_id || ''),
    effective_start_date: g.effective_start_date || '', effective_end_date: g.effective_end_date || '' }));
  const roles = recordsById(SHEETS.ROLES);
  for (const g of grants) {
    const role = roles[g.role_id];
    if (!role || !isActiveFlag(role.is_active)) return { status: 'error', message: `Role ${g.role_id} not found` };
    if (role.company_id && !sameId(role.company_id, g.company_id)) return { status: 'error', message: `Role "${role.name}" belongs to another company` };
  }

  const invite = { id: generateId('inv'), email, display_name: String(payload.display_name || '').trim() || email,
    company_id: String(payload.company_id), user_profile_type_id: payload.user_profile_type_id || '', role_grants: JSON.stringify(grants),
    immediate_approver_id: payload.immediate_approver_id || '', backup_approver_id: payload.backup_approver_id || '', status: 'pending',
    expires_at: new Date(now.getTime() + INVITE_EXPIRY_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000), invited_by: actor || getCurrentUserId(),
    accepted_uid: '', accepted_at: '', last_sent_at: '', send_count: 0, is_active: true, created_at: now, created_by: actor || getCurrentUserId(),
    updated_at: now, updated_by: actor || getCurrentUserId() };
  appendRecord(SHEETS.USER_INVITES, invite);
  const sendEmail = !(payload.send_email === false || String(payload.send_email) === 'false');
  const mail = sendEmail ? sendInviteEmail(invite, company) : { status: 'not_sent' };
  if (mail.status === 'sent') updateRecordFields(SHEETS.USER_INVITES, invite.id, { last_sent_at: now, send_count: 1 });
  logAdminAction(actor, 'INVITE_CREATED', 'user_invite', invite.id, { email, company_id: invite.company_id, roles: grants.length, email_status: mail.status });
  return { status: 'success', data: { ...inviteView({ ...invite, ...(mail.status === 'sent' ? { last_sent_at: now, send_count: 1 } : {}) }, now), email_status: mail.status, email_error: mail.error || '' },
    message: `Invite created for ${email}` + (mail.status === 'sent' ? ' (email sent)' : '') };
}

/** resendUserInvite (admin): { invite_id } - a pending or expired invite gets a fresh 14 days and the email again. */
function resendUserInvite(payload = {}) {
  const actor = actorId(payload.user_id);
  const denied = requireAdminOrBootstrap(actor, 'resend invites');
  if (denied) return denied;
  const found = findRecord(SHEETS.USER_INVITES, payload.invite_id || payload.id);
  if (!found) return { status: 'error', message: 'Invite not found' };
  const now = new Date();
  const status = inviteStatus(found.record, now);
  if (status !== 'pending' && status !== 'expired') return { status: 'error', message: `This invite is ${status}` };
  const email = normalizeEmail(found.record.email);
  if (findUserIdByEmail(email)) return { status: 'error', message: `${email} already has a user profile - revoke this invite` };
  const invites = readTable(SHEETS.USER_INVITES);
  if ((invites ? invites.records : []).some(i => !sameId(i.id, found.record.id) && normalizeEmail(i.email) === email && inviteStatus(i, now) === 'pending')) {
    return { status: 'error', message: `${email} has a newer pending invite - resend that one` };
  }
  const company = recordsById(SHEETS.COMPANIES)[String(found.record.company_id)] || {};
  const changes = { status: 'pending', expires_at: new Date(now.getTime() + INVITE_EXPIRY_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000), updated_at: now, updated_by: actor };
  const mail = sendInviteEmail({ ...found.record, ...changes }, company);
  if (mail.status === 'sent') Object.assign(changes, { last_sent_at: now, send_count: (Number(found.record.send_count) || 0) + 1 });
  const result = updateRecordFields(SHEETS.USER_INVITES, found.record.id, changes);
  logAdminAction(actor, 'INVITE_RESENT', 'user_invite', found.record.id, { email: found.record.email, email_status: mail.status });
  return { status: 'success', data: { ...inviteView(result.after, now), email_status: mail.status, email_error: mail.error || '' },
    message: mail.status === 'sent' ? 'Invite sent again' : `Invite extended; email ${mail.status}` };
}

/** revokeUserInvite (admin): { invite_id, reason } - soft; an accepted invite can't be revoked (deactivate the user). */
function revokeUserInvite(payload = {}) {
  const actor = actorId(payload.user_id);
  const denied = requireAdminOrBootstrap(actor, 'revoke invites');
  if (denied) return denied;
  const found = findRecord(SHEETS.USER_INVITES, payload.invite_id || payload.id);
  if (!found) return { status: 'error', message: 'Invite not found' };
  const status = inviteStatus(found.record);
  if (status === 'accepted') return { status: 'error', message: 'This invite was already accepted - deactivate the user instead' };
  if (status === 'revoked') return { status: 'error', message: 'This invite is already revoked' };
  const now = new Date();
  const result = updateRecordFields(SHEETS.USER_INVITES, found.record.id, { status: 'revoked', revoked_at: now, revoked_by: actor,
    revocation_reason: payload.reason || 'Revoked by admin', updated_at: now, updated_by: actor });
  logAdminAction(actor, 'INVITE_REVOKED', 'user_invite', found.record.id, { email: found.record.email, reason: payload.reason || '' });
  return { status: 'success', data: inviteView(result.after, now), message: 'Invite revoked' };
}

/** The app's address for emails: Script Property APP_URL. */
function appUrl() {
  return String(scriptProperty('APP_URL', 'https://jarvis-by-erp.vercel.app'));
}

/** Send (or try to send) the invite email with the company's sender settings. @returns {Object} { status, error? } */
function sendInviteEmail(invite, company) {
  const companyName = (company && company.name) || 'your company';
  const expires = toDate(invite.expires_at);
  const body = [`Hello ${invite.display_name || invite.email},`, '',
    `You have been invited to JARVIS (${companyName}).`,
    `Sign in with the Google account ${invite.email} at:`, appUrl(), '',
    expires ? `The invite is valid until ${Utilities.formatDate(expires, MANILA_TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd')} (Manila).` : '',
    'No password or link code is needed: signing in with that Google account accepts the invite.'].join('\n');
  return sendCompanyEmail(company, { to: invite.email, subject: `[JARVIS] You're invited to ${companyName}`, body });
}

/**
 * The v9.6 columns a write needs that the tab doesn't have yet (updateRecordFields skips unknown columns, so a
 * write before the rebuild apply would report success and store nothing). @returns {Object|null} error response
 */
function requireColumns(sheetName, columns) {
  const table = readTable(sheetName);
  const missing = columns.filter(c => !(table ? table.headers : []).includes(c));
  return missing.length
    ? { status: 'error', code: 'SCHEMA_OUTDATED', message: `The ${sheetName} tab has no ${missing.join(', ')} column(s) yet: run the v9.6 schema rebuild (JARVIS Admin -> 1. Schema -> Repair tables)` }
    : null;
}

// --- Company email settings ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------

/** A company's email settings with defaults. */
function companyEmailSettings(company) {
  const c = company || {};
  return { company_id: String(c.id || ''), company_name: c.name || '',
    email_enabled: COMPANY_EMAIL_MODES.includes(String(c.email_enabled)) ? String(c.email_enabled) : 'default',
    email_sender_name: String(c.email_sender_name || ''), email_reply_to: String(c.email_reply_to || ''),
    email_cc: String(c.email_cc || ''), email_footer: String(c.email_footer || '') };
}

/** Email for an SLA notice: the company's email_enabled (on / off) wins; 'default' → the ticket type / global rule. */
function companyEmailEnabled(company, ticketType) {
  const mode = companyEmailSettings(company).email_enabled;
  if (mode !== 'default') return mode === 'on';
  return slaEmailEnabled(ticketType);
}

/** Comma / semicolon separated addresses -> list (lower-case, trimmed). */
function emailList(value) {
  return String(value || '').split(/[,;]/).map(normalizeEmail).filter(Boolean);
}

/**
 * Send one email with a company's sender name, Reply-To, CC and footer (MailApp name / replyTo / cc - mail still
 * goes out from the script owner's address; no new permission). Keeps the quota reserve for SLA notices.
 * @returns {Object} { status: 'sent' | 'quota' | 'error', error? }
 */
function sendCompanyEmail(company, message) {
  const settings = companyEmailSettings(company);
  if (mailQuotaRemaining() <= SLA_EMAIL_QUOTA_RESERVE) return { status: 'quota', error: 'Daily email quota reached' };
  const mail = { to: message.to, subject: message.subject,
    body: message.body + (settings.email_footer ? `\n\n--\n${settings.email_footer}` : '') };
  if (settings.email_sender_name) mail.name = settings.email_sender_name;
  if (settings.email_reply_to) mail.replyTo = settings.email_reply_to;
  if (settings.email_cc) mail.cc = emailList(settings.email_cc).join(',');
  try {
    MailApp.sendEmail(mail);
    return { status: 'sent' };
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Email to ${message.to} failed: ${error.message}`);
    return { status: 'error', error: error.message };
  }
}

/** getCompanyEmailSettings (GET, admin): { company_id }. */
function getCompanyEmailSettings(params = {}) {
  const denied = requireAdminOrBootstrap(actorId(params.user_id), 'see company email settings');
  if (denied) return denied;
  const company = recordsById(SHEETS.COMPANIES)[String(params.company_id || params.id || '')];
  if (!company) return { status: 'error', message: `Company ${params.company_id} not found` };
  return { status: 'success', data: { ...companyEmailSettings(company), global_default: String(scriptProperty('SLA_EMAIL_DEFAULT', 'off')).toLowerCase() === 'on' ? 'on' : 'off',
    quota_remaining: mailQuotaRemaining() }, message: 'Company email settings' };
}

/**
 * saveCompanyEmailSettings (admin): { company_id, email_enabled (default | on | off), email_sender_name, email_reply_to,
 * email_cc (up to 5, comma separated), email_footer }. Only the keys sent change.
 */
function saveCompanyEmailSettings(payload = {}) {
  const actor = actorId(payload.user_id);
  const denied = requireAdminOrBootstrap(actor, 'change company email settings');
  if (denied) return denied;
  const companyId = payload.company_id || payload.id;
  const company = recordsById(SHEETS.COMPANIES)[String(companyId || '')];
  if (!company) return { status: 'error', message: `Company ${companyId} not found` };
  const outdated = requireColumns(SHEETS.COMPANIES, ['email_enabled', 'email_sender_name', 'email_reply_to', 'email_cc', 'email_footer']);
  if (outdated) return outdated;
  const changes = {};
  if (payload.email_enabled !== undefined) {
    if (!COMPANY_EMAIL_MODES.includes(String(payload.email_enabled))) return { status: 'error', message: 'email_enabled must be default, on or off' };
    changes.email_enabled = String(payload.email_enabled);
  }
  if (payload.email_sender_name !== undefined) {
    const name = String(payload.email_sender_name || '').trim();
    if (name.length > 100 || /[\r\n<>"]/.test(name)) return { status: 'error', message: 'Sender name: up to 100 characters, no quotes, <, > or line breaks' };
    changes.email_sender_name = name;
  }
  if (payload.email_reply_to !== undefined) {
    const replyTo = normalizeEmail(payload.email_reply_to);
    if (replyTo && !EMAIL_PATTERN.test(replyTo)) return { status: 'error', message: 'Reply-To must be one email address' };
    changes.email_reply_to = replyTo;
  }
  if (payload.email_cc !== undefined) {
    const cc = emailList(payload.email_cc);
    if (cc.length > 5) return { status: 'error', message: 'CC: up to 5 addresses' };
    const bad = cc.find(a => !EMAIL_PATTERN.test(a));
    if (bad) return { status: 'error', message: `CC: "${bad}" is not an email address` };
    changes.email_cc = cc.join(', ');
  }
  if (payload.email_footer !== undefined) {
    const footer = String(payload.email_footer || '');
    if (footer.length > 1000) return { status: 'error', message: 'Footer: up to 1000 characters' };
    changes.email_footer = footer;
  }
  const result = updateRecordFields(SHEETS.COMPANIES, company.id, { ...changes, updated_at: new Date(), updated_by: actor });
  logAdminAction(actor, 'SAVE_COMPANY_EMAIL_SETTINGS', 'company', String(company.id), changes);
  return { status: 'success', data: companyEmailSettings(result.after), message: 'Email settings saved' };
}

/** sendCompanyTestEmail (admin): { company_id, to? } - to defaults to the admin's own email. */
function sendCompanyTestEmail(payload = {}) {
  const actor = actorId(payload.user_id);
  const denied = requireAdminOrBootstrap(actor, 'send test emails');
  if (denied) return denied;
  const company = recordsById(SHEETS.COMPANIES)[String(payload.company_id || payload.id || '')];
  if (!company) return { status: 'error', message: `Company ${payload.company_id} not found` };
  const profile = recordsById(SHEETS.USER_PROFILES, 'user_id')[String(actor)] || {};
  const to = normalizeEmail(payload.to || REQUEST_AUTH.email || profile.email);
  if (!EMAIL_PATTERN.test(to)) return { status: 'error', message: 'No address to send to: enter one' };
  const settings = companyEmailSettings(company);
  const result = sendCompanyEmail(company, { to, subject: `[JARVIS] Test email - ${company.name || company.id}`,
    body: `This is a test of the ${company.name || 'company'} email settings.\n\nSender name: ${settings.email_sender_name || '(default)'}\n` +
      `Reply-To: ${settings.email_reply_to || '(none)'}\nCC: ${settings.email_cc || '(none)'}\nSLA email: ${settings.email_enabled}` });
  logAdminAction(actor, 'SEND_COMPANY_TEST_EMAIL', 'company', String(company.id), { to, status: result.status });
  if (result.status !== 'sent') return { status: 'error', message: `Test email not sent: ${result.error || result.status}` };
  return { status: 'success', data: { to, email_status: 'sent' }, message: `Test email sent to ${to}` };
}

// --- Attachment links (REF_SessionLog 7e008704, plan §4.13) --------------------------------------------------

/** Is the ticket closed (Completed / Rejected / Cancelled)? */
function ticketIsClosed(ticket) {
  return TERMINAL_TICKET_STATUSES.includes(String(ticket.status)) || !!ticket.cancelled_at;
}

/**
 * May the user add / remove / fix links on this ticket? The requester, a current approver of the step, or an admin.
 * @returns {boolean}
 */
function canManageTicketLinks(userId, ticket) {
  if (!userId || !ticket) return false;
  if (sameId(ticket.requester_id, userId) || isAdminUser(userId)) return true;
  if (!ticket.current_step_id) return false;
  const check = checkUserCanApproveStep(userId, ticket.id, ticket);
  return check.status === 'success' && !!check.data.can_act;
}

/**
 * May the user see a ticket's links (and report one broken)? v10.0: whoever may see the ticket (canViewTicket) - the
 * v9.6 interim rule (any role in the company) is gone (build plan C2).
 * @returns {boolean}
 */
function mayReadTicket(userId, ticket) {
  return !!userId && !!ticket && canViewTicket(buildActorScope(userId), ticket) !== 'none';
}

/** A ticket record by id, or null. */
function ticketRecord(ticketId) {
  const found = findRecord(SHEETS.TICKETS, ticketId);
  return found && isActiveFlag(found.record.is_active) ? found.record : null;
}

/** ticket_attachments record -> the shape TicketDetail shows. */
function attachmentView(a) {
  return { id: String(a.id), ticket_id: String(a.ticket_id), file_name: a.file_name || '', file_url: a.file_url || '',
    file_type: a.file_type || '', description: a.description || '', step_id: a.step_id ? String(a.step_id) : '',
    uploader_id: a.uploader_id ? String(a.uploader_id) : '', created_at: isoOrBlank(a.created_at || a.upload_timestamp),
    link_status: LINK_STATUSES.includes(String(a.link_status)) ? String(a.link_status) : 'ok',
    reported_by: a.reported_by ? String(a.reported_by) : '', reported_at: isoOrBlank(a.reported_at), report_note: a.report_note || '',
    // v9.7: false for a link saved before on a host that isn't allowed now - the app doesn't make it clickable
    link_allowed: linkHostAllowed(a.file_url) };
}

/** v9.7: the allowed link hosts (Script Property LINK_ALLOWED_HOSTS, lower-case). ['*'] = any http(s) host. */
function linkAllowedHosts() {
  return String(scriptProperty('LINK_ALLOWED_HOSTS', DEFAULT_LINK_ALLOWED_HOSTS)).split(',')
    .map(h => h.trim().toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean);
}

/** v9.7: may this URL be stored / opened? https + an allowed host (exact match), or '*' = any http(s) URL. */
function linkHostAllowed(url, hosts = linkAllowedHosts()) {
  const value = String(url || '').trim();
  if (hosts.includes('*')) return LINK_URL_PATTERN.test(value);
  const match = value.match(LINK_HOST_PATTERN);
  return !!match && match[1].toLowerCase() === 'https' && LINK_URL_PATTERN.test(value) && hosts.includes(match[2].toLowerCase());
}

/**
 * Check a link's URL and name. v9.7: the host must be allowed (LINK_ALLOWED_HOSTS) and the name is required.
 * @returns {string|null} error message
 */
function linkInputError(url, name) {
  const value = String(url || '').trim();
  if (!value) return 'A URL is required';
  if (value.length > 2000) return 'The URL is too long (2000 characters max)';
  if (!LINK_URL_PATTERN.test(value)) return 'Only http:// or https:// links can be added';
  const hosts = linkAllowedHosts();
  if (!linkHostAllowed(value, hosts)) return `Only links to ${hosts.join(', ')} can be added (https)`;
  if (!String(name || '').trim()) return 'A name is required for the link';
  if (String(name).trim().length > 200) return 'The name is too long (200 characters max)';
  return null;
}

/** getTicketAttachments (GET): { ticket_id } -> { ticket_id, can_manage, closed, attachments: [...] } (live rows only). */
function getTicketAttachments(params = {}) {
  const ticketId = params.ticket_id || params.id;
  const ticket = ticketRecord(ticketId);
  if (!ticket) return { status: 'error', message: `Ticket ${ticketId} not found` };
  const actor = actorId(params.user_id);
  if (!mayReadTicket(actor, ticket)) return { status: 'error', message: `Ticket ${ticketId} not found` }; // v10.0: same as missing
  const table = readTable(SHEETS.TICKET_ATTACHMENTS);
  const list = (table ? table.records : []).filter(a => sameId(a.ticket_id, ticket.id) && liveRecord(a)).map(attachmentView)
    .sort((a, b) => String(a.created_at).localeCompare(String(b.created_at)));
  return { status: 'success', data: { ticket_id: String(ticket.id), can_manage: canManageTicketLinks(actor, ticket), closed: ticketIsClosed(ticket),
    attachments: list }, message: `${list.length} attachment(s)` };
}

/** addTicketAttachmentLink: { ticket_id, file_url, file_name, description?, step_id? } (requester / current approver / admin). */
function addTicketAttachmentLink(payload = {}) {
  const actor = actorId(payload.user_id);
  const ticket = ticketRecord(payload.ticket_id);
  if (!ticket || !mayReadTicket(actor, ticket)) return { status: 'error', message: `Ticket ${payload.ticket_id} not found` };
  if (!canManageTicketLinks(actor, ticket)) return { status: 'error', message: 'Only the requester, the current approvers or an admin can add links' };
  if (ticketIsClosed(ticket) && !isAdminUser(actor)) return { status: 'error', message: 'This ticket is closed: only an admin can add links' };
  const inputError = linkInputError(payload.file_url, payload.file_name);
  if (inputError) return { status: 'error', message: inputError };
  const url = String(payload.file_url).trim();
  const now = new Date();
  const record = { id: generateId('att'), ticket_id: ticket.id, uploader_id: actor, file_name: String(payload.file_name).trim(),
    file_url: url, file_size: '', file_type: 'link', upload_timestamp: now, step_id: payload.step_id || ticket.current_step_id || '',
    description: String(payload.description || '').trim().slice(0, 1000), company_id: ticket.company_id, is_active: true,
    created_at: now, created_by: actor, updated_at: now, updated_by: actor, link_status: 'ok' };
  appendRecord(SHEETS.TICKET_ATTACHMENTS, record);
  logTicketHistory(ticket.id, actor, 'LINK_ADDED', `Link added: ${record.file_name}`);
  logTicketAction(ticket.id, actor, 'LINK_ADDED', { attachment_id: record.id, file_name: record.file_name, file_url: url });
  return { status: 'success', data: attachmentView(record), message: 'Link added' };
}

/** The live attachment and its ticket, or an error response. */
function attachmentContext(payload) {
  const found = findRecord(SHEETS.TICKET_ATTACHMENTS, payload.attachment_id || payload.id);
  if (!found || !liveRecord(found.record)) return { error: { status: 'error', message: 'Attachment not found' } };
  const ticket = ticketRecord(found.record.ticket_id);
  if (!ticket) return { error: { status: 'error', message: `Ticket ${found.record.ticket_id} not found` } };
  return { attachment: found.record, ticket };
}

/** removeTicketAttachment: { attachment_id, reason? } - soft delete (requester / current approver / admin). */
function removeTicketAttachment(payload = {}) {
  const ctx = attachmentContext(payload);
  if (ctx.error) return ctx.error;
  const { attachment, ticket } = ctx;
  const actor = actorId(payload.user_id);
  if (!mayReadTicket(actor, ticket)) return { status: 'error', message: 'Attachment not found' }; // v10.0: same as missing
  if (!canManageTicketLinks(actor, ticket)) return { status: 'error', message: 'Only the requester, the current approvers or an admin can remove links' };
  if (ticketIsClosed(ticket) && !isAdminUser(actor)) return { status: 'error', message: 'This ticket is closed: only an admin can remove links' };
  updateRecordFields(SHEETS.TICKET_ATTACHMENTS, attachment.id, deactivateAuditFields(payload.reason || 'Removed', actor));
  logTicketHistory(ticket.id, actor, 'LINK_REMOVED', `Link removed: ${attachment.file_name}`);
  logTicketAction(ticket.id, actor, 'LINK_REMOVED', { attachment_id: String(attachment.id), file_name: attachment.file_name, reason: payload.reason || '' });
  return { status: 'success', data: { id: String(attachment.id) }, message: 'Link removed' };
}

/**
 * reportAttachmentLinkBroken: { attachment_id, note } - anyone signed in who can see the ticket. Notifies the
 * person who added the link and the requester (bell). Reporting an already reported link updates the note.
 */
function reportAttachmentLinkBroken(payload = {}) {
  const ctx = attachmentContext(payload);
  if (ctx.error) return ctx.error;
  const { attachment, ticket } = ctx;
  const actor = actorId(payload.user_id);
  if (!actor) return { status: 'error', message: 'Sign in to report a link' };
  if (!mayReadTicket(actor, ticket)) return { status: 'error', message: 'Attachment not found' }; // v10.0: same as missing
  const outdated = requireColumns(SHEETS.TICKET_ATTACHMENTS, ['link_status', 'reported_by', 'reported_at', 'report_note']);
  if (outdated) return outdated;
  const note = String(payload.note || '').trim().slice(0, 500);
  const already = String(attachment.link_status) === 'reported_broken';
  const now = new Date();
  const result = updateRecordFields(SHEETS.TICKET_ATTACHMENTS, attachment.id, { link_status: 'reported_broken', reported_by: actor,
    reported_at: now, report_note: note, updated_at: now, updated_by: actor });
  if (!already) {
    const label = ticket.ticket_number || `#${ticket.id}`;
    const recipients = unique([attachment.uploader_id, ticket.requester_id].filter(Boolean).map(String)).filter(u => !sameId(u, actor));
    appendRecords(SHEETS.NOTIFICATIONS, recipients.map(userId => ({ id: generateId('ntf'), user_id: userId, company_id: ticket.company_id,
      ticket_id: ticket.id, type: 'link_reported', title: `Broken link: ${label}`,
      message: `"${attachment.file_name}" was reported broken${note ? `: ${note}` : ''}`, link: `/tickets?ticket=${ticket.id}`,
      is_read: false, read_at: '', email_status: 'none', is_active: true, created_at: now, updated_at: now })));
  }
  logTicketHistory(ticket.id, actor, 'LINK_REPORTED', `Link reported broken: ${attachment.file_name}${note ? ` - ${note}` : ''}`);
  logTicketAction(ticket.id, actor, 'LINK_REPORTED', { attachment_id: String(attachment.id), note, again: already });
  return { status: 'success', data: attachmentView(result.after), message: already ? 'Report updated' : 'Link reported broken' };
}

/**
 * updateAttachmentLink: { attachment_id, file_url, file_name?, description? } - only while the link is reported
 * broken, by anyone who can add links; also on closed tickets (logged). In place; the action log keeps from → to;
 * saving clears the report.
 */
function updateAttachmentLink(payload = {}) {
  const ctx = attachmentContext(payload);
  if (ctx.error) return ctx.error;
  const { attachment, ticket } = ctx;
  const actor = actorId(payload.user_id);
  if (!mayReadTicket(actor, ticket)) return { status: 'error', message: 'Attachment not found' }; // v10.0: same as missing
  if (String(attachment.link_status) !== 'reported_broken') {
    return { status: 'error', message: 'Only a link reported broken can be edited - remove it and add a new one instead' };
  }
  if (!canManageTicketLinks(actor, ticket)) return { status: 'error', message: 'Only the requester, the current approvers or an admin can fix links' };
  const inputError = linkInputError(payload.file_url, payload.file_name);
  if (inputError) return { status: 'error', message: inputError };
  const next = { file_url: String(payload.file_url).trim(), file_name: String(payload.file_name).trim() };
  if (payload.description !== undefined) next.description = String(payload.description || '').trim().slice(0, 1000);
  const changes = Object.keys(next).filter(field => String(attachment[field] || '') !== String(next[field]))
    .map(field => ({ field, from: String(attachment[field] || ''), to: next[field] }));
  const now = new Date();
  const result = updateRecordFields(SHEETS.TICKET_ATTACHMENTS, attachment.id, { ...next, link_status: 'ok', reported_by: '', reported_at: '',
    report_note: '', updated_at: now, updated_by: actor });
  const closed = ticketIsClosed(ticket);
  logTicketHistory(ticket.id, actor, 'LINK_FIXED', `Link fixed: ${result.after.file_name}${closed ? ' (closed ticket)' : ''}`);
  logTicketAction(ticket.id, actor, 'LINK_FIXED', { attachment_id: String(attachment.id), changes, closed_ticket: closed });
  return { status: 'success', data: attachmentView(result.after), message: 'Link fixed' };
}

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * PHASE 19.0: TICKET ACCESS (v10.0)
 * Design: docs/planning/PLAN_FeatureGaps_Volume_20261031.md §2 decisions 1-2, §4.2; build plan
 * docs/planning/PLAN_v10.0_Build_20261031.md M1. Who may see and create tickets is decided here, on the server (F1),
 * from the acting user (actorId, A15), with each table read once per request (A16).
 * =================================================================================
 */

const TICKET_VISIBILITY_MODES = ['private', 'collaborative'];

/** A ticket type's visibility: 'collaborative' only when set so; blank / anything else is 'private' (D2). */
function ticketTypeVisibility(ticketType) {
  return ticketType && String(ticketType.visibility || '').toLowerCase() === 'collaborative' ? 'collaborative' : 'private';
}

/**
 * What the actor may reach, built once per request (A16) and dropped by any write (invalidateTable).
 * - isAdmin: an active role whose name contains "admin" (isAdminUser)
 * - roleScopes: 'company|ticket_type' (or 'company|*' for an assignment without a ticket type) where the actor holds a
 *   role in force
 * - approverScopes: 'company|ticket_type' where one of those roles approves a step of that type + company
 * - participantTicketIds (ticket_participants rows), sharedTicketIds (shares in force to the user or one of their
 *   companies), grantedTicketIds (v9.5 escalate_role grants to one of their roles)
 * - unscoped: there is no actor at all (AUTH_MODE warn / off without a user) - D1: reads stay unfiltered
 * @param {string|null} actor - User id (from actorId)
 * @returns {Object} scope
 */
function buildActorScope(actor) {
  const key = actor ? String(actor) : '';
  if (ACTOR_SCOPES[key]) return ACTOR_SCOPES[key];
  const scope = { actor: actor ? String(actor) : null, unscoped: !actor, isAdmin: false, roleScopes: {}, approverScopes: {},
    myCompanies: {}, participantTicketIds: {}, sharedTicketIds: {}, grantedTicketIds: {}, typeVisibility: {} };
  ((readTable(SHEETS.TICKET_TYPES) || {}).records || []).forEach(t => { scope.typeVisibility[String(t.id)] = ticketTypeVisibility(t); });
  if (!actor) {
    ACTOR_SCOPES[key] = scope;
    return scope;
  }

  const now = new Date();
  const roles = recordsById(SHEETS.ROLES);
  const assignments = ((readTable(SHEETS.USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS) || {}).records || [])
    .filter(a => sameId(a.user_id, actor) && isAssignmentActive(a, now) && roles[String(a.role_id)] && isActiveFlag(roles[String(a.role_id)].is_active));
  scope.isAdmin = assignments.some(a => String(roles[String(a.role_id)].name || '').toLowerCase().includes('admin'));
  assignments.forEach(a => {
    if (!a.company_id) return;
    scope.myCompanies[String(a.company_id)] = true;
    scope.roleScopes[`${a.company_id}|${a.ticket_type_id || '*'}`] = true;
  });

  // Approver scopes: the step's approver roles x the actor's assignments (same matching as getTicketsForApproval)
  const rolesByStep = groupBy(((readTable(SHEETS.STEP_APPROVERS) || {}).records || []), r => String(r.step_id));
  ((readTable(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS) || {}).records || []).filter(s => isActiveFlag(s.is_active)).forEach(step => {
    const stepRoles = (rolesByStep[String(step.id)] || []).map(r => String(r.role_id));
    assignments.forEach(a => {
      if (!stepRoles.includes(String(a.role_id)) || !a.company_id) return;
      if (step.company_id && !sameId(step.company_id, a.company_id)) return;
      if (a.ticket_type_id && !sameId(a.ticket_type_id, step.ticket_type_id)) return;
      scope.approverScopes[`${a.company_id}|${step.ticket_type_id}`] = true;
    });
  });

  ((readTable(SHEETS.TICKET_PARTICIPANTS) || {}).records || []).forEach(p => {
    if (p.user_id && sameId(p.user_id, actor) && isActiveFlag(p.is_active)) scope.participantTicketIds[String(p.ticket_id)] = true;
  });
  liveRecords(SHEETS.TICKET_COLLABORATIONS).forEach(c => {
    if (!shareInForce(c, now)) return;
    if (sameId(c.shared_with_user_id, actor) || (c.shared_with_company_id && scope.myCompanies[String(c.shared_with_company_id)])) {
      scope.sharedTicketIds[String(c.ticket_id)] = true;
    }
  });
  ((readTable(SHEETS.SLA_ESCALATION_EVENTS) || {}).records || []).forEach(e => {
    if (String(e.escalation_action) !== 'escalate_role') return;
    const granted = jsonList(e.granted_role_ids);
    if (assignments.some(a => granted.includes(String(a.role_id)) && sameId(a.company_id, e.company_id))) scope.grantedTicketIds[String(e.ticket_id)] = true;
  });

  ACTOR_SCOPES[key] = scope;
  return scope;
}

/**
 * The scope of the user a request acts for (actorId). With no actor at all it logs "AUTH warn: unscoped <what>" (D1).
 * @param {string} claimed - user_id the request sent
 * @param {string} what - action name for the log
 */
function requestScope(claimed, what) {
  const actor = actorId(claimed);
  if (!actor && REQUEST_AUTH.mode !== 'off') console.log(`AUTH warn: unscoped ${what}`);
  return buildActorScope(actor);
}

/**
 * May the scope's actor see the ticket? (design §4.2) 'full' or 'none' (M4 adds 'stub' for Restricted tickets).
 * Admin; requester; immediate head (snapshot); participant (acted / shared / watching); a share in force; an
 * escalate_role grant; on a 'collaborative' type any role on its company + type; on a 'private' type the approver roles
 * of that company + type.
 * @param {Object} scope - buildActorScope()
 * @param {Object} ticket - ticket record
 * @returns {string} 'full' | 'none'
 */
function canViewTicket(scope, ticket) {
  if (!ticket) return 'none';
  if (scope.unscoped || scope.isAdmin) return 'full';
  const id = String(ticket.id);
  if (sameId(ticket.requester_id, scope.actor) || (ticket.head_approver_id && sameId(ticket.head_approver_id, scope.actor))) return 'full';
  if (scope.participantTicketIds[id] || scope.sharedTicketIds[id] || scope.grantedTicketIds[id]) return 'full';
  const key = `${ticket.company_id}|${ticket.ticket_type_id}`;
  if (scope.approverScopes[key]) return 'full';
  if (scope.typeVisibility[String(ticket.ticket_type_id)] === 'collaborative' &&
    (scope.roleScopes[key] || scope.roleScopes[`${ticket.company_id}|*`])) return 'full';
  return 'none';
}

/** May the scope's actor create a ticket of this type in this company? (decision 1: a role on that type + company) */
function canCreateTicket(scope, companyId, ticketTypeId) {
  if (scope.unscoped || scope.isAdmin) return true;
  return !!(scope.roleScopes[`${companyId}|${ticketTypeId}`] || scope.roleScopes[`${companyId}|*`]);
}

/**
 * Route guard for one-ticket reads: null when the caller may see the ticket, else "Ticket not found" - a hidden
 * ticket answers like a missing one, so the answer doesn't tell that it exists (design §4.2).
 * @param {Object} request - request params / payload (user_id)
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket id
 * @param {string} what - action name
 * @returns {Object|null} error result or null
 */
function ticketReadGuard(request, ticketId, what) {
  const scope = requestScope(request && (request.user_id || request.userId), what);
  const found = ticketId ? findRecord(SHEETS.TICKETS, ticketId) : null;
  if (found && canViewTicket(scope, found.record) !== 'none') return null;
  return { status: 'error', message: 'Ticket not found' };
}

/**
 * getCreatableTicketTypes (GET): the active ticket types the user may create, each with the companies where they may
 * (decision 1; the New Ticket form lists only these - the server decides, F1).
 * @param {Object} params - { user_id, company_id? }
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: [ticket type + { creatable_company_ids }] }
 */
function getCreatableTicketTypes(params = {}) {
  const scope = requestScope(params.user_id || params.userId, 'getCreatableTicketTypes');
  const companies = ((readTable(SHEETS.COMPANIES) || {}).records || []).filter(c => isActiveFlag(c.is_active) && !c.deactivated_at);
  const types = ((readTable(SHEETS.TICKET_TYPES) || {}).records || []).filter(t => isActiveFlag(t.is_active));
  const data = types.map(type => {
    const companyIds = companies
      .filter(c => (!type.company_id || sameId(type.company_id, c.id)) && (!params.company_id || sameId(c.id, params.company_id)))
      .filter(c => canCreateTicket(scope, c.id, type.id))
      .map(c => String(c.id));
    return { ...type, id: String(type.id), visibility: ticketTypeVisibility(type), creatable_company_ids: companyIds };
  }).filter(t => t.creatable_company_ids.length > 0);
  return { status: 'success', data: data, message: `${data.length} ticket type(s) you can create` };
}
